# ============================================================
# 巡田最速適｜CELL 4 程式版本說明
# 目前版本：v65.64.19 TEST
#
# 基礎：v65.60.5 既有正式演算法，後續修正整合於本定稿版。
# 本版唯一新增的進場道路規則：
#   地址若可解析出最後一段道路，且 OSM 找得到名稱完全一致的道路，
#   則以該地址道路作為車輛進場道路優先選擇。
#
# 例如：
#   仁和路二段190巷99號 → 仁和路二段190巷
#
# 此規則的意義：
#   門牌／建築物座標是建築物代表位置，不直接等同車輛入口位置。
#   地址道路是車輛進場道路的主要語意依據。
#   附近更接近的其他巷、弄或無名道路，不得在地址道路已找到時取代它。
#
# 本版不修改：
#   Cell 2、既有道路成本、路線最佳化、分日邏輯、Crossing/U-turn、
#   汽車水體避讓，以及 Cell 4 → Cell 5 資料交接。
#
# 若地址道路在 OSM 找不到，才沿用既有道路接點處理，避免因單一案例
# 直接改變整體路線系統。
# ============================================================

# V65.63.1：MOTOR 普通重型機車快速公路分流；不再把所有 trunk/trunk_link 一律排除。
# V65.45：承接 v65.40 的正式演算法與 v65.45 profiler；僅將 road_loopback_cost 的純計算核心改為 NumPy/Numba JIT kernel，不改任何接受條件或路線決策。
# v65.15：快速篩選＋少量實際 OSM 驗證的跨日點位交換（單次涉及查核點最多 5 點）
# V65.13：承接 v65.13；跨日候選先用 TIME_D 快速估算，再只對前 5 組執行實際 OSM 驗證，降低計算時間。
#          單次涉及查核點總數最多 5 點；相鄰工作日；每日 5 小時上限；全日總工時不得增加。
# V65.11.1：修正承接問題；完整保留 v65.10「每日地理集中最佳化／跨日分界小幅調整」。
#          單次最多交換 5 點；候選以 OSM TIME_D 之地理接近程度預選，最終以實際 OSM 閉合工時判斷。
#          並新增交換前後每日總工時診斷，避免將不同基準分組誤判為改善。
# V65.10：承接 v65.9.12；不追求每日點數平均，而是以實際 OSM 閉合路線時間，
#         對相鄰工作日的「連續分界」進行小幅前後移動，降低跨區移動。
#         保留：全域近→遠、每日回固定起點、每日 5 小時上限、方向切換最低改善 2 分鐘、
#         OSM／水路快取、CAR 水體二次避讓及既有每日路線最佳化。
# V65.9.3：修正 V65.9.2 每日分界前缺少 visit_order 定義；承接全域連續區段 DP、取消跨日搬移／交換。
# V65.9：承接 V65.7 單日實際路線比較；CAR 最終 geometry 對水體鄰近 service/track 做二次檢查與避讓重算。
# V65.9：固定外勤查核方向採「由近到遠」；保留原有 OSM 路線最佳化，只在閉合路線最佳化完成後選擇較近查核點作為首站。
# V65.9.2：每日分界僅調整連續切點，不改變查核點跨日歸屬的順序。
# 前置條件：Cell 2 已產生 /content/result.xlsx；/content/input_route_config.xlsx 控制交通工具與工時
# 沿用既有全域候選路線與每日工時最佳化邏輯；每日 5 小時為絕對上限；每日分界以全域連續區段 DP 同時考量日間可行性及反向重跑風險；修正 OSM geometry 座標順序，確保路線與箭頭正確顯示
# 第一筆有效座標固定為起點；起點座標從 result.xlsx 自動取得；不在本 Cell 重新安裝 Python 套件。
# ============================================================
# ============================================================
# V65.64.13 TEST：Virtual anchor ownership / isolation + 同道路實際幾何連線修正。
#   1) virtual anchor 僅屬於建立它的案件；後續案件不得拿它當道路路口／接點。
#   2) 附近道路診斷也排除 virtual split edge，避免前一案件污染下一案件的主要道路判斷。
#   3) 同一實際 OSM 道路上的多個 virtual anchor，補接時改用原道路幾何的實際子線段，
#      不再用兩 anchor 座標的直線連接；雙向道路同時建立反向連線。
#   4) 追加 owner / source edge 驗證，將「不當共用 virtual anchor」列為硬性錯誤。
#   5) 本版仍不修改每日 5 小時／每日點數的規則；本輪判斷以全域路線正確性為主。

# V65.64.23 TEST：將 Virtual anchor＋一般案件實際物理通過順序硬性限制完整納入「全點一次方案」
#   1) 全點一次 seed 先做 Virtual anchor 可行化。
#   2) relocate / swap 每一個候選若造成 forward-crossing，直接拒絕。
#   3) 最後由近到遠方向整理後，再做一次 Virtual anchor 最終硬性驗證。
#   4) 本版不修改每日工時、每日點數、每日分界與跨日交換。

# H. 以下開始原本的 OSM 本機路線最佳化
# ============================================================

# ============================================================
# 台灣地籍點 → 本機 OSM 路網 → 機車／汽車路線最佳化
#
# 不需要：
#   - Google Maps API Key
#   - Valhalla API
#   - OSRM
#
# 使用：
#   OpenStreetMap
#   OSMnx
#   NetworkX / Dijkstra
#
# 核心：
#   1. 讀取 /content/result.xlsx
#   2. 讀取 /content/input_route_config.xlsx
#   3. 第一筆固定起點
#   4. 依交通工具從 OSM 下載一般汽車或機車取向道路圖
#   5. 依交通工具套用對應 access 規則與路徑成本
#   5. 以 OSM explicit access tags 排除：
#        access=no
#        vehicle=no
#        motor_vehicle=no
#        motor_vehicle=private
#        motorcycle=no
#        motorcycle=private
#      以及明顯非機動車道路
#   5. motorcycle=designated / motor_vehicle=designated 優先
#   6. service / track 等道路提高汽車成本，但不一律排除
#   7. 汽車模式新增道路適用性分級：明確汽車禁行＝排除；conditional 禁止＝高成本並列入診斷；疑似低汽車適用道路＝提高成本
#   8. 特別辨識「motor_vehicle / motorcar / vehicle 明確禁止，但 motorcycle 明確允許」的疑似機車專用型道路，汽車模式直接排除
#   9. 每個地籍點 snap 到最近道路節點
#   10. 106 個 snap node 建立本機道路距離矩陣
#   11. 106 個點全部一起做全域路線最佳化，最後依每日工時切割（每日至少/最多不以固定10點為限）
#  11. Nearest Neighbor + 2-opt
#  12. Crossing / U-turn 懲罰
#  13. 每日工時切割與每日回起點
#  14. 真正使用 OSM 路網 geometry 輸出路線
#  15. RoadIndex 估計道路重複
#
# 注意：
#   機車模式：採 OSM drive-compatible network + explicit motorcycle / motor_vehicle access restrictions + motorcycle-oriented edge weights。
#   汽車模式：採一般 OSM drive network；明確汽車禁行排除，conditional / 低汽車適用道路提高成本，
#   並額外辨識「汽車禁止＋機車明確允許」型道路。
#
#   這不是法律層面的「保證機車合法通行」，
#   最終仍應依現場標誌確認。
#
# 輸出：依交通工具自動加後綴 _MOTOR 或 _CAR，例如：
#   /content/route_order_MOTOR.xlsx
#   /content/route_order_CAR.xlsx
#   /content/route_summary_MOTOR.xlsx
#   /content/route_summary_CAR.xlsx
#   /content/route_points_MOTOR.csv / route_points_CAR.csv
#   /content/route_MOTOR.geojson / route_CAR.geojson
#   /content/route_errors_MOTOR.xlsx / route_errors_CAR.xlsx
#   /content/gmap_MOTOR.csv / gmap_CAR.csv
#   /content/route_map_MOTOR.html / route_map_CAR.html
#   /content/osm_graph_MOTOR.graphml / osm_graph_CAR.graphml
#
# ============================================================

    if _drive_root_candidate:
        _drive_root_candidate = Path(str(_drive_root_candidate))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# V65.50.1：雙座標進場道路判斷＋道路末端／無法貫通的道路誤吸附修正
#
# v65.48 原本使用「最近 OSM node」。若地籍點旁邊恰有一條短道路末端，
# 道路末端端點可能比真正主要道路的 OSM node 更近，造成路線被帶進無法貫通的道路。
# 本版不改路線最佳化，只改「地籍點如何接到道路網」：
#   1. 先找地籍點真正最近的 OSM edge（道路幾何）。
#   2. 原最近 edge 若屬接近無尾端的 dead-end branch，且附近有合理的
#      非 dead-end edge，優先改用附近非 dead-end edge。
#   3. 最後仍以既有 OSM node 作為矩陣 anchor，避免大改 CSR / Dijkstra 架構。
#   4. MOTOR / CAR 均使用車種完成後的 G，各自重新 snap；CAR 禁行道路移除
#      與既有水體規則不變。
#
# 本版為 Cell 4 定稿版；不覆寫 Cell 1～3。

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# V65.63.9：MOTOR「普通重型機車」台66主線／側車道／匝道分流模型
#
# 核心原則：
#   1. motorway / motorway_link：普通重型機車一律禁止。
#   2. trunk：不再只看 highway 類型。
#      名稱明確為「快速路○段」的台66平面側車道 → 保留。
#      ref=66 且不是側車道命名的 trunk → 視為台66主線排除。
#   3. trunk_link：除非有「快速路○段」側車道正面證據，否則視為快速公路匝道排除；
#      因此「平鎮二交流道」這類沒有 ref=66 的 link 也不會漏放。
#   4. ref=66 的未命名 trunk 沒有側車道正面證據 → 排除。
#   5. 非台66的一般 trunk，若沒有明確受管制標籤，仍可供 MOTOR 使用。
#   5. 一般 service / track / 未命名道路仍依既有 MOTOR 適用性權重處理。
#   6. V65.63.9：台66「快速路○段」平面側車道若標為 primary／tertiary／service，改採側車道優先成本。
#
# 台66的官方資料會區分「快速道路／主線」與「側車道」，
# 桃園沿線側車道並以「快速路○段」作道路名稱；本版因此以名稱作正面側車道證據，
# 避免把主線與側車道因同樣的 highway=trunk 而混為一談。
# 注意：OSM 標籤不是法律保證；最終仍應以現場標誌、道路管理機關公告為準。
# ------------------------------------------------------------
MOTOR_PROFILE_NAME = "普通重型機車"
MOTOR_FASTROAD_FORBID_ENABLED = True
MOTOR_ALWAYS_FORBIDDEN_HIGHWAY_TYPES = {
    "motorway", "motorway_link",
}
MOTOR_CONDITIONAL_FASTROAD_HIGHWAY_TYPES = {
    "trunk", "trunk_link",
}
MOTOR_FASTROAD_MOTORROAD_VALUES = {"yes", "true", "1", "designated"}
MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_RESTRICTION_VALUES = {"no", "use_sidepath"}
MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_NAME_RE = re.compile(
    r"(^|;)\s*快速路[一二三四五六七八九十百零〇\d]+段\s*(?:;|$)",
    flags=re.IGNORECASE,
)
MOTOR_FASTROAD_MAINLINE_NAME_HINTS = (
    "東西向快速公路",
    "東西向快速公路觀音大溪線",
    "東西向快速公路 觀音大溪線",
    "西部濱海快速公路",
)
MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_REFS = {"66"}

# D：OSM 明確禁止汽車 → 直接排除

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# V65.3：汽車防止誤走「水利／防汛／堤防管理道路」
#
# OSM 有些道路沒有完整標示 motor_vehicle=no / motorcar=no，
# 但實務上可能是水利、防汛、堤防或灌排設施管理道路。
# 這類道路若被標成 service / track，舊版只會列 B 級、提高成本，
# 仍有可能被 CAR 最短時間路徑選入。
#
# 本版沿用「多訊號交叉判斷」：
#   1. 道路名稱含防汛／防洪／堤防等關鍵字
#   2. highway=path（依臺灣 OSM tagging 指引，不視為一般四輪車道路）
#   3. 水利／堤防相關 OSM 標籤
#   4. service / track 類低階道路，同時緊鄰 OSM 水路／水體
#
# 只有在沒有明確汽車允許標示時，才套用此「疑似禁行」推定。
# 這是路線規劃上的保守防誤走規則，不是法律資格判定。
# ------------------------------------------------------------
CAR_SPECIAL_MANAGEMENT_ROAD_ENABLED = True

    "maintenance road", "flood", "floodway",
    "floodcontrol", "flood control", "levee", "dyke", "dike",
)

# ============================================================
# 1A. 載入外部路線規劃設定
# ============================================================
# 必要參數：
#   交通工具：只算機車／只算汽車／機汽車皆計算
#   每點停留時間
#   每日可勘查時間
#   機車平均速度
#   汽車平面道路平均速度
#   汽車高速公路平均速度
#   汽車快速公路平均速度

def load_route_config(path):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"找不到路線設定檔：{path}\n"
            "請將 INPUT_ROUTE_CONFIG.XLSX 放入 /content/，再執行本 Cell。"
        )

    try:
        raw = pd.read_excel(path, sheet_name="RouteConfig", header=None)
    except Exception as e:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"無法讀取路線設定檔：{path}\n"
            "請確認工作表名稱為 RouteConfig。\n"
            f"原始錯誤：{e}"
        ) from e

    header_row = None
    for i in range(len(raw)):
        vals = {str(v).strip() for v in raw.iloc[i].tolist() if pd.notna(v)}
        if "參數名稱" in vals and "設定值" in vals:
            header_row = i
            break

    if header_row is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "INPUT_ROUTE_CONFIG.XLSX 格式錯誤：找不到包含「參數名稱」與「設定值」的欄位標題列。"
        )

    cfg = raw.iloc[header_row + 1:].copy()
    cfg.columns = raw.iloc[header_row].tolist()
    cfg = cfg.reset_index(drop=True)

    if "參數名稱" not in cfg.columns or "設定值" not in cfg.columns:
        raise ValueError(
            "INPUT_ROUTE_CONFIG.XLSX 格式錯誤：需包含「參數名稱」與「設定值」欄位。"
        )

    params = {}
    for _, row in cfg.iterrows():
        name = str(row.get("參數名稱", "")).strip()
        if not name or name == "nan":
            continue
        params[name] = row.get("設定值")

    required = {
        "交通工具",
        "每點停留時間",
        "每日可勘查時間",
        "機車平均速度",
        "汽車平面道路平均速度",
        "汽車高速公路平均速度",
        "汽車快速公路平均速度",
    }
    missing = required - set(params)
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(
            "INPUT_ROUTE_CONFIG.XLSX 缺少必要參數：" + "、".join(sorted(missing))
        )

    vehicle_raw = str(params["交通工具"]).strip()
    vehicle_key = vehicle_raw.lower().replace(" ", "")
    vehicle_alias = {
        "只算機車": "MOTOR",
        "機車": "MOTOR",
        "摩托車": "MOTOR",
        "motor": "MOTOR",
        "motorcycle": "MOTOR",
        "只算汽車": "CAR",
        "汽車": "CAR",
        "小客車": "CAR",
        "car": "CAR",
        "drive": "CAR",
        "機汽車皆計算": "BOTH",
        "機汽車": "BOTH",
        "機車＋汽車": "BOTH",
        "機車+汽車": "BOTH",
        "both": "BOTH",
    }
    if vehicle_key not in vehicle_alias:
        raise ValueError(
            "INPUT_ROUTE_CONFIG.XLSX 的「交通工具」只能設定為「只算機車」、「只算汽車」或「機汽車皆計算」。"
        )

    print("路線設定檔關鍵值檢查：")
    for _k in [
        "交通工具", "每點停留時間", "每日可勘查時間",
        "機車平均速度", "汽車平面道路平均速度",
        "汽車高速公路平均速度", "汽車快速公路平均速度"
    ]:
        print(f"  {_k} = {params.get(_k)!r}")

    numeric = {}
    try:
        for key in [
            "每點停留時間",
            "每日可勘查時間",
            "機車平均速度",
            "汽車平面道路平均速度",
            "汽車高速公路平均速度",
            "汽車快速公路平均速度",
        ]:
            numeric[key] = float(params[key])
    except Exception as e:
        raise ValueError(
            "INPUT_ROUTE_CONFIG.XLSX 的速度、停留時間與每日可勘查時間必須是數字。"
        ) from e

    if not np.isfinite(numeric["每點停留時間"]) or numeric["每點停留時間"] < 0:
        raise ValueError(
            f"每點停留時間設定無效：讀到 {params.get('每點停留時間')!r}；"
            "請填寫 0 或正數（分鐘）。"
        )
    if numeric["每日可勘查時間"] <= 0 or not np.isfinite(numeric["每日可勘查時間"]):
        raise ValueError("每日可勘查時間必須大於 0 小時。")
    for key in ["機車平均速度", "汽車平面道路平均速度", "汽車高速公路平均速度", "汽車快速公路平均速度"]:
        if numeric[key] <= 0 or not np.isfinite(numeric[key]):
            raise ValueError(f"{key} 必須大於 0 km/h。")

    if vehicle_alias[vehicle_key] == "MOTOR":
        runs = [("MOTOR", "機車")]
    elif vehicle_alias[vehicle_key] == "CAR":
        runs = [("CAR", "汽車")]
    else:
        runs = [("MOTOR", "機車"), ("CAR", "汽車")]

    return {
        "VEHICLE_SELECTION": vehicle_alias[vehicle_key],
        "VEHICLE_RUNS": runs,
        "POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES": numeric["每點停留時間"],
        "DAILY_MAX_HOURS": numeric["每日可勘查時間"],
        "DAILY_TARGET_HOURS": numeric["每日可勘查時間"],
        "MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH": numeric["機車平均速度"],
        "CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH": numeric["汽車平面道路平均速度"],
        "CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH": numeric["汽車高速公路平均速度"],
        "CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH": numeric["汽車快速公路平均速度"],
    }


def _normalize_manual_text(value):
    if value is None or (isinstance(value, float) and np.isnan(value)):
        return ""
    return str(value).strip()


def _is_manual_enabled(value):
    text = _normalize_manual_text(value).lower()
    return text in {"y", "yes", "true", "1", "是", "啟用", "開"}


def _parse_manual_osm_ids(value):
    """將 OSM_ID 欄位拆成整數集合；支援 123 / 123;456 / [123,456]。"""
    text = _normalize_manual_text(value)
    if not text:
        return set()
    nums = re.findall(r"\d+", text)
    return {int(x) for x in nums}


def load_car_manual_controls(path):
    """
    載入汽車人工禁行／避開路段設定。

    檔案不存在：直接略過，回傳空設定，不讓主程式失敗。
    支援方式：OSM_ID、座標、道路名稱。
    處理方式：禁止、避免。
    """
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        print(f"汽車人工路段設定檔：找不到 {path} → 略過")
        return []

    try:
        xls = pd.ExcelFile(path)
        sheet_name = "CARForbidden" if "CARForbidden" in xls.sheet_names else xls.sheet_names[0]
        raw = pd.read_excel(path, sheet_name=sheet_name, header=None)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"汽車人工路段設定檔：讀取失敗 → 不使用這份設定 | 原因：{e}")
        return []

    header_row = None
    for i in range(len(raw)):
        vals = {_normalize_manual_text(v) for v in raw.iloc[i].tolist() if pd.notna(v)}
        if "方式" in vals and ("處理方式" in vals or "控制方式" in vals):
            header_row = i
            break
    if header_row is None:
        print("汽車人工路段設定檔：找不到「方式」與「處理方式」欄位 → 略過")
        return []

    cfg = raw.iloc[header_row + 1:].copy()
    cfg.columns = [_normalize_manual_text(v) for v in raw.iloc[header_row].tolist()]
    cfg = cfg.reset_index(drop=True)

    controls = []
    for row_no, row in cfg.iterrows():
        if "啟用" in cfg.columns and not _is_manual_enabled(row.get("啟用")):
            continue
        method = _normalize_manual_text(row.get("方式"))
        action = _normalize_manual_text(row.get("處理方式") if "處理方式" in cfg.columns else row.get("控制方式"))
        if not method or not action:
            continue
        if method not in {"OSM_ID", "OSMID", "OSM ID", "座標", "COORD", "道路名稱", "路名"}:
            print(f"  略過第 {row_no + header_row + 2} 列：不支援方式「{method}」")
            continue
        if action not in {"禁止", "避免"}:
            print(f"  略過第 {row_no + header_row + 2} 列：不支援處理方式「{action}」")
            continue
        controls.append({
            "row_no": row_no + header_row + 2,
            "enabled": True,
            "method": method,
            "action": action,
            "osm_ids": _parse_manual_osm_ids(row.get("OSM_ID")),
            "start_lon": pd.to_numeric(row.get("起點經度"), errors="coerce") if "起點經度" in cfg.columns else np.nan,
            "start_lat": pd.to_numeric(row.get("起點緯度"), errors="coerce") if "起點緯度" in cfg.columns else np.nan,
            "end_lon": pd.to_numeric(row.get("終點經度"), errors="coerce") if "終點經度" in cfg.columns else np.nan,
            "end_lat": pd.to_numeric(row.get("終點緯度"), errors="coerce") if "終點緯度" in cfg.columns else np.nan,
            "road_name": _normalize_manual_text(row.get("道路名稱")),
            "note": _normalize_manual_text(row.get("備註")),
        })

    print(f"汽車人工路段設定檔：已載入 {len(controls)} 筆有效設定")
    return controls


def _edge_osmid_set(attrs):
    """標準化 OSM edge 的 osmid 欄位。"""
    value = attrs.get("osmid")
    if value is None:
        return set()
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
        out = set()
        for x in value:
            try:
                out.add(int(x))
            except Exception:
                pass
        return out
    try:
        return {int(value)}
    except Exception:
        return set()


def _edge_name_text(attrs):
    value = attrs.get("name")
    if value is None:
        return ""
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
        return ";".join(str(x).strip().lower() for x in value)
    return str(value).strip().lower()


def _fro_local_xy_to_lonlat(x, y):
    """Inverse of this Cell 4's fixed local transform; input/output are x,y / lon,lat."""
    _x = float(x)
    _y = float(y)
    if not math.isfinite(_x) or not math.isfinite(_y):
        raise ValueError("Non-finite local XY coordinate")
    _scale_x = 111320.0 * math.cos(math.radians(23.7))
    if not math.isfinite(_scale_x) or _scale_x <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("Invalid local XY transform scale")
    return (_x / _scale_x + 120.0, _y / 110540.0 + 23.0)


def _fro_normalize_lonlat_coords(coords):
    """Normalize coordinates to lon/lat; recognize only this project's local XY domain."""
    _raw = [(float(p[0]), float(p[1])) for p in coords]
    if len(_raw) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Geometry requires at least two coordinates")
    if not all(math.isfinite(x) and math.isfinite(y) for x, y in _raw):
        raise ValueError("Geometry contains non-finite coordinates")
    # Already in the ordinary WGS84 degree domain: preserve exactly as supplied.
    if all(-180.0 <= x <= 180.0 and -90.0 <= y <= 90.0 for x, y in _raw):
        return _raw
    # The local transform is specific to FRO's Taiwan routing domain. Convert only
    # when the entire sequence maps back into a plausible Taiwan bounding box.
    _converted = [_fro_local_xy_to_lonlat(x, y) for x, y in _raw]
    if not all(119.0 <= x <= 123.5 and 21.0 <= y <= 27.5 for x, y in _converted):
        raise ValueError("Coordinates are neither WGS84 lon/lat nor recognizable FRO local XY")
    return _converted

def _edge_geometry_lonlat(graph, u, v, attrs):
    # Return WGS84 geometry consistently oriented from graph node u to v.
    # Reject unusable coordinate domains or endpoint gaps above 5 m; the 5 m
    # limit is a corruption guard, not a snapping tolerance.
    try:
        node_u = (float(graph.nodes[int(u)]["x"]), float(graph.nodes[int(u)]["y"]))
        node_v = (float(graph.nodes[int(v)]["x"]), float(graph.nodes[int(v)]["y"]))
    except Exception:
        return None

    def _fro_gap_m(a, b):
        # Local equirectangular distance only for endpoint-quality comparison.
        lat0 = math.radians((float(a[1]) + float(b[1])) / 2.0)
        dx = (float(a[0]) - float(b[0])) * 111320.0 * math.cos(lat0)
        dy = (float(a[1]) - float(b[1])) * 110540.0
        return math.hypot(dx, dy)

    geom = attrs.get("geometry") if isinstance(attrs, dict) else None
    if geom is not None:
        try:
            raw_coords = [(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in geom.coords]
            coords = _fro_normalize_lonlat_coords(raw_coords)
            if len(coords) >= 2:
                forward_u = _fro_gap_m(node_u, coords[0])
                forward_v = _fro_gap_m(node_v, coords[-1])
                reverse_u = _fro_gap_m(node_u, coords[-1])
                reverse_v = _fro_gap_m(node_v, coords[0])
                forward_score = forward_u + forward_v
                reverse_score = reverse_u + reverse_v
                if reverse_score + 1e-6 < forward_score:
                    coords = list(reversed(coords))
                    chosen_gaps = (reverse_u, reverse_v)
                else:
                    chosen_gaps = (forward_u, forward_v)
                # A line that is far from either graph endpoint is stale or was
                # cut using fractions from a different direction/parent edge.
                if max(chosen_gaps) <= 5.0:
                    return LineString(coords)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Explicit fallback: never let a mismatched parent/child geometry leak into
    # routing or Folium output. The graph endpoints define a valid directed chord.
    try:
        return LineString([node_u, node_v])
    except Exception:
        return None


def _manual_control_edges(graph, controls):
    """解析人工 CAR 路段設定，回傳 edge 控制映射與每條規則診斷。"""
    if not controls:
        return {}, []

    edge_control = {}  # (u,v,k) -> list of rule indexes
    diagnostics = []

    # 先建立容易查找的 edge metadata。
    edge_rows = []
    for u, v, k, attrs in graph.edges(keys=True, data=True):
        edge_rows.append((u, v, k, attrs, _edge_osmid_set(attrs), _edge_name_text(attrs)))

    for rule_idx, rule in enumerate(controls, start=1):
        method = rule["method"]
        method_norm = method.upper().replace(" ", "")
        matched = set()

        if method_norm in {"OSM_ID", "OSMID"}:
            wanted = set(rule["osm_ids"])
            if wanted:
                for u, v, k, attrs, osmids, names in edge_rows:
                    if osmids & wanted:
                        matched.add((u, v, k))

        elif method_norm in {"道路名稱", "路名"}:
            wanted_name = rule["road_name"].strip().lower()
            if wanted_name:
                for u, v, k, attrs, osmids, names in edge_rows:
                    if names == wanted_name or wanted_name in {x.strip() for x in names.split(";") if x.strip()}:
                        matched.add((u, v, k))

        elif method_norm in {"座標", "COORD"}:
            try:
                slon, slat = float(rule["start_lon"]), float(rule["start_lat"])
                elon, elat = float(rule["end_lon"]), float(rule["end_lat"])
                if not all(np.isfinite([slon, slat, elon, elat])):
                    raise ValueError("起訖座標不完整")
            except Exception:
                print(f"  人工規則 {rule_idx}：座標不完整 → 略過")
                diagnostics.append({
                    "規則編號": rule_idx,
                    "設定檔列": rule["row_no"],
                    "方式": method,
                    "處理方式": rule["action"],
                    "匹配edge數": 0,
                    "備註": rule["note"],
                    "結果": "座標不完整，略過",
                })
                continue

            lat0 = (slat + elat) / 2.0
            m_per_deg_lat = 111320.0
            m_per_deg_lon = max(111320.0 * math.cos(math.radians(lat0)), 1.0)
            buf_lat = CAR_MANUAL_COORD_MATCH_BUFFER_M / m_per_deg_lat
            buf_lon = CAR_MANUAL_COORD_MATCH_BUFFER_M / m_per_deg_lon
            min_lon, max_lon = min(slon, elon) - buf_lon, max(slon, elon) + buf_lon
            min_lat, max_lat = min(slat, elat) - buf_lat, max(slat, elat) + buf_lat
            target = LineString([(slon, slat), (elon, elat)])
            target_buf = target.buffer(max(buf_lon, buf_lat))

            for u, v, k, attrs, osmids, names in edge_rows:
                geom = _edge_geometry_lonlat(graph, u, v, attrs)
                if geom is None:
                    continue
                eb = geom.bounds
                if eb[2] < min_lon or eb[0] > max_lon or eb[3] < min_lat or eb[1] > max_lat:
                    continue
                try:
                    if geom.intersects(target_buf):
                        matched.add((u, v, k))
                except Exception:
                    continue

        for edge_key in matched:
            edge_control.setdefault(edge_key, []).append(rule_idx)

        diagnostics.append({
            "規則編號": rule_idx,
            "設定檔列": rule["row_no"],
            "方式": method,
            "處理方式": rule["action"],
            "匹配edge數": len(matched),
            "OSM_ID": ";".join(map(str, sorted(rule["osm_ids"]))) if rule["osm_ids"] else "",
            "道路名稱": rule["road_name"],
            "備註": rule["note"],
            "結果": "已套用" if matched else "找不到符合道路",
        })

    return edge_control, diagnostics


def _vehicle_output_name(filename: str, suffix: str) -> str:
    """檔名含 route 時放 route 後；沒有 route 時放檔名前；BOTH 模式會依序輸出 MOTOR 與 CAR。"""
    directory = "/content"
    stem, ext = os.path.splitext(filename)
    m = re.match(r"^(route)(.*)$", stem, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    if m:
        return f"{directory}/{m.group(1)}_{suffix}{m.group(2)}{ext}"
    return f"{directory}/{suffix}_{stem}{ext}"


def _car_road_speed_class(data):
    """依 OSM highway / motorroad 將汽車道路分成三類：
    MOTORWAY＝高速公路；EXPRESSWAY＝快速公路；SURFACE＝一般平面道路。
    判定優先順序：MOTORWAY > EXPRESSWAY > SURFACE。
    """
    highway = data.get("highway")
    if isinstance(highway, (list, tuple, set)):
        values = {str(x).strip().lower() for x in highway}
    else:
        values = {str(highway).strip().lower()} if highway is not None else set()
    if values & {"motorway", "motorway_link"}:
        return "MOTORWAY"
    if values & {"trunk", "trunk_link"}:
        return "EXPRESSWAY"
    motorroad = str(data.get("motorroad", "")).strip().lower()
    if motorroad in {"yes", "true", "1", "designated"}:
        return "EXPRESSWAY"
    return "SURFACE"

def _car_road_speed_kmh(data, surface_speed_kmh, motorway_speed_kmh, expressway_speed_kmh):
    """依 OSM 道路分類回傳汽車旅行速度。"""
    speed_class = _car_road_speed_class(data)
    if speed_class == "MOTORWAY":
        return float(motorway_speed_kmh)
    if speed_class == "EXPRESSWAY":
        return float(expressway_speed_kmh)
    return float(surface_speed_kmh)

def _cache_hash(payload):
    """將快取條件轉成穩定 SHA-1 短碼。"""
    raw = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    return hashlib.sha1(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]


def _osm_cache_paths(cache_kind, cache_key):
    """回傳 OSM / 水路快取檔與 metadata 路徑。"""
    global OSM_CACHE_DIR, CELL4_DRIVE_MOUNTED
    try:
        os.makedirs(OSM_CACHE_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    except Exception as _cache_exc:
        # Drive 異常時立即回退到 /content，不影響其他計算。
        if CELL4_DRIVE_MOUNTED:
            print("⚠️ Google Drive 快取建立失敗：", repr(_cache_exc))
            print("⚠️ OSM 快取改回 /content/CELL4_OSM_CACHE")
            CELL4_DRIVE_MOUNTED = False
            OSM_CACHE_DIR = "/content/CELL4_OSM_CACHE"
            os.makedirs(OSM_CACHE_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        else:
            raise

    stem = f"{cache_kind}_{cache_key}"
    return (
        os.path.join(OSM_CACHE_DIR, stem + ".pkl.gz"),
        os.path.join(OSM_CACHE_DIR, stem + ".json"),
    )


def _atomic_pickle_gzip_dump(obj, path):
    """以暫存檔 + replace 寫入，降低中途中斷留下壞快取的機率。"""
    tmp = path + ".tmp"
    with gzip.open(tmp, "wb", compresslevel=3) as f:
        pickle.dump(obj, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def _pickle_gzip_load(path):
    with gzip.open(path, "rb") as f:
        return pickle.load(f)

def _file_sha1_for_checkpoint(path):
    h = hashlib.sha1()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        while True:
            chunk = f.read(1024 * 1024)
            if not chunk:
                break
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def _build_cell4_run_signature(route_cfg):
    payload = {
        "cell4_version": CELL4_VERSION,
        "result_xlsx_sha1": _file_sha1_for_checkpoint(INPUT_XLSX)
            if os.path.exists(INPUT_XLSX) else "MISSING",
        "route_config_sha1": _file_sha1_for_checkpoint(INPUT_ROUTE_CONFIG_XLSX)
            if os.path.exists(INPUT_ROUTE_CONFIG_XLSX) else "MISSING",
        "vehicle_selection": str(route_cfg.get("VEHICLE_SELECTION", "")),
        "point_service_minutes": float(route_cfg.get("POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES", 0)),
        "daily_max_hours": float(route_cfg.get("DAILY_MAX_HOURS", 0)),
        "motor_avg_speed_kmh": float(route_cfg.get("MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH", 0)),
        "motor_profile_name": MOTOR_PROFILE_NAME,
        "motor_fastroad_forbid_enabled": bool(MOTOR_FASTROAD_FORBID_ENABLED),
        "motor_always_forbidden_highway_types": sorted(MOTOR_ALWAYS_FORBIDDEN_HIGHWAY_TYPES),
        "motor_conditional_fastroad_highway_types": sorted(MOTOR_CONDITIONAL_FASTROAD_HIGHWAY_TYPES),
        "motor_fastroad_motorroad_values": sorted(MOTOR_FASTROAD_MOTORROAD_VALUES),
        "motor_fastroad_side_restriction_values": sorted(MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_RESTRICTION_VALUES),
        "motor_fastroad_side_road_name_regex": MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_NAME_RE.pattern,
        "motor_fastroad_mainline_name_hints": list(MOTOR_FASTROAD_MAINLINE_NAME_HINTS),
        "motor_fastroad_side_road_refs": sorted(MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_REFS),
        "motor_fastroad_side_road_factor": float(MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_FACTOR),
        "motor_fastroad_side_exit_penalty_enabled": bool(MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_EXIT_PENALTY_ENABLED),
        "motor_fastroad_side_exit_penalty_m": float(MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_EXIT_PENALTY_M),
        "motor_fastroad_side_exit_lower_class_types": sorted(MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_EXIT_LOWER_CLASS_TYPES),
        "motor_trunk_link_requires_side_road_name": True,
        "car_surface_speed_kmh": float(route_cfg.get("CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH", 0)),
        "car_motorway_speed_kmh": float(route_cfg.get("CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH", 0)),
        "car_expressway_speed_kmh": float(route_cfg.get("CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH", 0)),
        # V65.52.1：道路接點演算法設定納入執行簽章，避免修改 snap / 主要道路進場規則後
        # 避免誤用舊演算法產生的交通工具續跑進度。
        "virtual_anchor_route_order_constraint_enabled": bool(VIRTUAL_ANCHOR_ROUTE_ORDER_CONSTRAINT_ENABLED),
        "snap_logic": {
            "edge_first_enabled": bool(SNAP_EDGE_FIRST_ENABLED),
            "edge_search_radius_m": float(SNAP_EDGE_SEARCH_RADIUS_M),
            "edge_candidate_node_k": int(SNAP_EDGE_CANDIDATE_NODE_K),
            "dead_end_avoid_enabled": bool(SNAP_DEAD_END_AVOID_ENABLED),
            "dead_end_alt_margin_m": float(SNAP_DEAD_END_ALT_MARGIN_M),
            "dead_end_max_chain_m": float(SNAP_DEAD_END_MAX_CHAIN_M),
            "dead_end_any_class_enabled": bool(SNAP_DEAD_END_ANY_CLASS_ENABLED),
            "branch_approach_enabled": bool(SNAP_BRANCH_APPROACH_ENABLED),
            "branch_trace_max_m": float(SNAP_BRANCH_TRACE_MAX_M),
            "branch_point_max_m": float(SNAP_BRANCH_POINT_MAX_M),
            "branch_main_distance_gap_m": float(SNAP_BRANCH_MAIN_DISTANCE_GAP_M),
            "virtual_anchor_enabled": bool(SNAP_VIRTUAL_ANCHOR_ENABLED),
            "virtual_anchor_trigger_m": float(SNAP_VIRTUAL_ANCHOR_TRIGGER_M),
            "main_road_ingress_enabled": bool(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_ENABLED),
            "main_road_ingress_search_radius_m": float(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_SEARCH_RADIUS_M),
            "main_road_ingress_max_edge_distance_m": float(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_MAX_EDGE_DISTANCE_M),
            "main_road_ingress_max_low_edge_length_m": float(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_MAX_LOW_EDGE_LENGTH_M),
            "main_road_ingress_max_extra_distance_m": float(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_MAX_EXTRA_DISTANCE_M),
            "main_road_ingress_min_rank": int(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_MIN_RANK),
            "main_road_ingress_force_virtual_anchor": bool(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_FORCE_VIRTUAL_ANCHOR),
            "virtual_anchor_isolation_version": "v65.64.12",
            "virtual_anchor_real_geometry_links": True,
            "virtual_anchor_bidirectional_links": True,
            "main_road_ingress_topology_only": bool(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_TOPOLOGY_ONLY),
            "main_road_ingress_apply_modes": sorted(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_APPLY_MODES),
            "motor_geometric_main_road_ingress_enabled": bool(SNAP_MOTOR_GEOMETRIC_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_ENABLED),
            "motor_geometric_main_road_max_distance_m": float(SNAP_MOTOR_GEOMETRIC_MAIN_ROAD_MAX_DISTANCE_M),
            "motor_geometric_main_road_min_advantage_m": float(SNAP_MOTOR_GEOMETRIC_MAIN_ROAD_MIN_ADVANTAGE_M),
            "motor_named_low_road_cluster_protect_enabled": bool(SNAP_MOTOR_NAMED_LOW_ROAD_CLUSTER_PROTECT_ENABLED),
            "motor_named_low_road_cluster_point_max_m": float(SNAP_MOTOR_NAMED_LOW_ROAD_CLUSTER_POINT_MAX_M),
            "motor_named_low_road_cluster_edge_max_m": float(SNAP_MOTOR_NAMED_LOW_ROAD_CLUSTER_EDGE_MAX_M),
            "motor_named_low_road_cluster_main_extra_max_m": float(SNAP_MOTOR_NAMED_LOW_ROAD_CLUSTER_MAIN_EXTRA_MAX_M),
            "nearby_main_road_search_enabled": bool(SNAP_NEARBY_ROAD_DIAGNOSTIC_ENABLED),
            "nearby_main_road_search_radius_m": float(SNAP_NEARBY_ROAD_DIAGNOSTIC_RADIUS_M),
            "nearby_main_road_search_k": int(SNAP_NEARBY_ROAD_DIAGNOSTIC_K),
            "motor_manual_ingress_points": SNAP_MOTOR_MANUAL_INGRESS_POINTS,
            "motor_manual_ingress_max_osm_distance_m": float(SNAP_MOTOR_MANUAL_INGRESS_MAX_OSM_DISTANCE_M),
            "branch_low_class_types": sorted(SNAP_BRANCH_LOW_CLASS_TYPES),
            "branch_main_class_types": sorted(SNAP_BRANCH_MAIN_CLASS_TYPES),
            "main_road_ingress_highway_types": sorted(SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_HIGHWAY_TYPES),
        },
    }
    raw = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    return hashlib.sha1(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:20], payload


def _checkpoint_path(vehicle_mode):
    return os.path.join(
        CELL4_CHECKPOINT_DIR,
        f"CELL4_{vehicle_mode}_{CELL4_VERSION}_checkpoint.pkl",
    )


def _atomic_pickle_dump(obj, path):
    tmp = path + ".tmp"
    with open(tmp, "wb") as f:
        pickle.dump(obj, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def _save_vehicle_checkpoint(vehicle_payload, run_signature, signature_payload):
    mode = str(vehicle_payload["VEHICLE_MODE"])
    record = {
        "CELL4_VERSION": CELL4_VERSION,
        "run_signature": run_signature,
        "signature_payload": signature_payload,
        "VEHICLE_MODE": mode,
        "saved_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "payload": vehicle_payload,
    }
    path = _checkpoint_path(mode)

    try:
        _atomic_pickle_dump(record, path)
        return path
    except Exception as _e:
        # Google Drive 暫時不可寫時，不讓已完成交通工具模式因此失敗；
        # 先退回本 Runtime 的 /content 續跑進度檔。
        fallback_dir = "/content/CELL4_CHECKPOINTS"
        fallback_path = os.path.join(
            fallback_dir,
            f"CELL4_{mode}_{CELL4_VERSION}_checkpoint.pkl",
        )
        os.makedirs(fallback_dir, exist_ok=True)

        if CELL4_DRIVE_MOUNTED:
            print(
                "⚠️ Google Drive 續跑進度檔 寫入失敗：",
                repr(_e),
            )
            print(
                "⚠️ 暫時回退 /content 續跑進度檔：",
                fallback_path,
            )
            _atomic_pickle_dump(record, fallback_path)
            return fallback_path

        raise


def _load_vehicle_checkpoint(vehicle_mode, run_signature):
    path = _checkpoint_path(vehicle_mode)
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return None
    try:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            record = pickle.load(f)
        if record.get("CELL4_VERSION") != CELL4_VERSION:
            return None
        if record.get("run_signature") != run_signature:
            return None
        payload = record.get("payload")
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            return None
        if payload.get("VEHICLE_MODE") != vehicle_mode:
            return None
        return payload
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"續跑進度讀取失敗：{path} | {e!r}")
        return None


def _save_combined_cell4_results(cell4_results):
    # Cell 5 仍使用 /content/cell4_map_data.pkl，
    # 另外在 Google Drive 保留一份完整交接備份。
    path = "/content/cell4_map_data.pkl"
    _atomic_pickle_dump(cell4_results, path)

    if CELL4_DRIVE_MOUNTED:
        try:
            drive_path = os.path.join(
                str(CELL4_DRIVE_ROOT),
                "cell4_map_data.pkl",
            )
            _atomic_pickle_dump(cell4_results, drive_path)
            print(f"Cell 4 完整交接備份：{drive_path}")
        except Exception as _e:
            print("⚠️ Google Drive 完整交接備份失敗（不影響 /content）：", repr(_e))

    return path


def _build_osm_graph_cache_key(bounds, network_type):
    west, south, east, north = [round(float(v), 7) for v in bounds]
    payload = {
        "cache_version": OSM_CACHE_VERSION,
        "kind": "osm_graph",
        "bounds": [west, south, east, north],
        "network_type": str(network_type),
        "download_mode": str(DOWNLOAD_MODE),
        "osmnx_version": str(ox.__version__),
    }
    return _cache_hash(payload), payload


def _build_water_cache_key(download_polygon):
    try:
        polygon_wkb = download_polygon.wkb_hex
    except Exception:
        polygon_wkb = str(download_polygon)
    payload = {
        "cache_version": OSM_CACHE_VERSION,
        "kind": "water",
        "polygon_wkb_sha1": hashlib.sha1(polygon_wkb.encode("ascii", errors="ignore")).hexdigest(),
        "water_tags": {"waterway": True, "natural": "water"},
        "osmnx_version": str(ox.__version__),
    }
    return _cache_hash(payload), payload


def _load_cached_osm_graph(bounds, network_type):
    """讀取符合目前範圍/網路類型的 OSM 原始路網；不存在或損壞則回傳 None。"""
    if FORCE_REFRESH_OSM_CACHE:
        print("OSM 快取：FORCE_REFRESH_OSM_CACHE=True → 忽略既有路網快取，重新下載。")
        return None
    if not ENABLE_PERSISTENT_OSM_CACHE:
        return None
    cache_key, meta = _build_osm_graph_cache_key(bounds, network_type)
    data_path, meta_path = _osm_cache_paths("osm_graph", cache_key)
    if not os.path.exists(data_path):
        return None
    try:
        with open(meta_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            saved_meta = json.load(f)
        if saved_meta != meta:
            print("OSM 快取 metadata 不符，重新下載。")
            return None
        G = _pickle_gzip_load(data_path)
        if G is None or len(G.nodes) == 0:
            print("OSM 快取內容為空，重新下載。")
            return None
        print(f"使用既有 OSM 路網快取：{data_path}")
        print(f"快取 OSM 圖：節點={len(G.nodes):,} | 道路段={len(G.edges):,}")
        return G
    except Exception as e:
        print("OSM 快取讀取失敗，改為重新下載：", repr(e))
        return None


def _save_cached_osm_graph(G, bounds, network_type):
    """將原始 OSM 路網寫入快取。"""
    if not ENABLE_PERSISTENT_OSM_CACHE or G is None:
        return
    cache_key, meta = _build_osm_graph_cache_key(bounds, network_type)
    data_path, meta_path = _osm_cache_paths("osm_graph", cache_key)
    try:
        _atomic_pickle_gzip_dump(G, data_path)
        with open(meta_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(meta, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        print(f"OSM 路網已寫入快取：{data_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        print("OSM 路網快取寫入失敗（不影響本次執行）：", repr(e))


def _load_cached_water(download_polygon):
    """讀取符合目前 polygon 的 OSM 水路/水體快取。"""
    if FORCE_REFRESH_OSM_CACHE:
        print("水路快取：FORCE_REFRESH_OSM_CACHE=True → 忽略既有水路／水體快取，重新下載。")
        return None, False
    if not ENABLE_PERSISTENT_OSM_CACHE:
        return None, False
    cache_key, meta = _build_water_cache_key(download_polygon)
    data_path, meta_path = _osm_cache_paths("water", cache_key)
    if not os.path.exists(data_path):
        return None, False
    try:
        with open(meta_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            saved_meta = json.load(f)
        if saved_meta != meta:
            print("水路快取 metadata 不符，重新下載。")
            return None, False
        payload = _pickle_gzip_load(data_path)
        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or "water_union" not in payload:
            print("水路快取格式無法辨識，重新下載。")
            return None, False
        water_union = payload.get("water_union")
        count = int(payload.get("geometry_count", 0) or 0)
        print(f"使用既有 OSM 水路／水體快取：{data_path}")
        if water_union is not None:
            print(f"快取水路／水體圖形資料筆數：{count}")
        else:
            print("快取內沒有有效的水路／水體圖形資料。")
        return water_union, True
    except Exception as e:
        print("水路快取讀取失敗，改為重新下載：", repr(e))
        return None, False


def _save_cached_water(water_union, download_polygon, geometry_count=0):
    """將 OSM 水路/水體 union 寫入快取，即使本次為空 geometry 也記錄已完成下載。"""
    if not ENABLE_PERSISTENT_OSM_CACHE:
        return
    cache_key, meta = _build_water_cache_key(download_polygon)
    data_path, meta_path = _osm_cache_paths("water", cache_key)
    try:
        payload = {
            "water_union": water_union,
            "geometry_count": int(geometry_count or 0),
        }
        _atomic_pickle_gzip_dump(payload, data_path)
        with open(meta_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(meta, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        print(f"OSM 水路／水體已寫入快取：{data_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        print("水路／水體快取寫入失敗（不影響本次執行）：", repr(e))

def _build_water_buffer_cache_key(download_polygon, buffer_m):
    """建立 CAR 水體鄰近衍生幾何的長期快取 key。"""
    water_cache_key, water_meta = _build_water_cache_key(download_polygon)
    payload = {
        "cache_version": DERIVED_WATER_CACHE_VERSION,
        "kind": "water_buffer_wgs84",
        "source_water_cache_key": water_cache_key,
        "source_water_cache_meta": water_meta,
        "buffer_m": float(buffer_m),
        "source_crs": DERIVED_WATER_BUFFER_SOURCE_CRS,
        "output_crs": DERIVED_WATER_BUFFER_OUTPUT_CRS,
        "shapely_version": str(getattr(shapely, "__version__", "")),
    }
    return _cache_hash(payload), payload


def _load_cached_water_buffer(download_polygon, buffer_m):
    """讀取既有 water_union 對應的 40m 水體鄰近衍生 geometry。"""
    if FORCE_REFRESH_OSM_CACHE or not ENABLE_DERIVED_WATER_GEOMETRY_CACHE:
        return None, False
    if not ENABLE_PERSISTENT_OSM_CACHE:
        return None, False
    cache_key, meta = _build_water_buffer_cache_key(download_polygon, buffer_m)
    data_path, meta_path = _osm_cache_paths("water_buffer", cache_key)
    if not os.path.exists(data_path) or not os.path.exists(meta_path):
        return None, False
    try:
        with open(meta_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            saved_meta = json.load(f)
        if saved_meta != meta:
            return None, False
        payload = _pickle_gzip_load(data_path)
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            return None, False
        geom = payload.get("water_buffer_wgs84")
        if geom is None or getattr(geom, "is_empty", True):
            return None, False
        print(f"使用既有 CAR 水體鄰近資料快取：{data_path}")
        return geom, True
    except Exception as e:
        print("CAR 水體鄰近衍生幾何快取讀取失敗，重新建立：", repr(e))
        return None, False


def _save_cached_water_buffer(water_buffer_wgs84, download_polygon, buffer_m):
    """儲存 CAR 水體鄰近衍生 geometry。"""
    if not ENABLE_PERSISTENT_OSM_CACHE or not ENABLE_DERIVED_WATER_GEOMETRY_CACHE:
        return
    if water_buffer_wgs84 is None or getattr(water_buffer_wgs84, "is_empty", True):
        return
    cache_key, meta = _build_water_buffer_cache_key(download_polygon, buffer_m)
    data_path, meta_path = _osm_cache_paths("water_buffer", cache_key)
    try:
        _atomic_pickle_gzip_dump({"water_buffer_wgs84": water_buffer_wgs84}, data_path)
        with open(meta_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(meta, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        print(f"CAR 水體鄰近衍生幾何已寫入快取：{data_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        print("CAR 水體鄰近衍生幾何快取寫入失敗（不影響本次執行）：", repr(e))



def _prepare_shared_osm_context():
    """
    BOTH 模式共用前置資料：
      - 讀取 /content/result.xlsx 並取得有效經緯度
      - 建立 OSM download polygon / 矩形範圍
      - OSM drive_service 原始路網只下載一次
      - OSM 水路 / 水體只下載一次

    後續 MOTOR / CAR 各自以 G_base.copy() 建立獨立圖，
    因此 CAR 排除禁行 edge 不會影響 MOTOR。
    """
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("BOTH 模式共用前置資料：OSM / 水路一次下載")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "OSM／水路快取模式："
        + ("強制重新下載" if FORCE_REFRESH_OSM_CACHE else "優先使用既有快取")
    )

    if not os.path.exists(INPUT_XLSX):
        raise FileNotFoundError(INPUT_XLSX)

    df = pd.read_excel(INPUT_XLSX)
    candidate_pairs = [
        ("經度", "緯度"),
        ("Longitude", "Latitude"),
        ("lon", "lat"),
        ("lng", "lat"),
    ]
    lon_col = None
    lat_col = None
    for a, b in candidate_pairs:
        if a in df.columns and b in df.columns:
            lon_col = a
            lat_col = b
            break
    if lon_col is None:
        raise ValueError("找不到經度 / 緯度欄位。")

    lon = pd.to_numeric(df[lon_col], errors="coerce")
    lat = pd.to_numeric(df[lat_col], errors="coerce")
    valid_mask = (
        lon.notna() & lat.notna()
        & lon.between(119, 123)
        & lat.between(21, 26)
    )
    coords = np.column_stack([
        lon.loc[valid_mask].astype(float).to_numpy(),
        lat.loc[valid_mask].astype(float).to_numpy(),
    ])
    if len(coords) == 0:
        raise ValueError("沒有有效座標。")

    def make_download_polygon(coords_array, buffer_m):
        from shapely.geometry import MultiPoint
        import geopandas as gpd
        mp = MultiPoint([(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in coords_array])
        gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(geometry=[mp], crs="EPSG:4326")
        gdf_m = gdf.to_crs("EPSG:32651")
        buffered = gdf_m.geometry.iloc[0].buffer(buffer_m)
        buffered_gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(geometry=[buffered], crs="EPSG:32651")
        return buffered_gdf.to_crs("EPSG:4326").geometry.iloc[0]

    download_polygon = make_download_polygon(coords, OSM_BUFFER_M)
    bounds = download_polygon.bounds
    print("共用 OSM 下載範圍：", bounds)
    print(f"buffer：{OSM_BUFFER_M} m")

    ox.settings.requests_timeout = OVERPASS_REQUEST_TIMEOUT
    ox.settings.overpass_rate_limit = True
    ox.settings.use_cache = True

    G = _load_cached_osm_graph(bounds, "drive_service")
    download_errors = []

    if G is None:
        print("找不到既有 OSM 路網快取，開始下載共用 OSM 道路圖...")
        for endpoint in OVERPASS_ENDPOINTS:
            print("嘗試共用 Overpass：", endpoint)
            ox.settings.overpass_url = endpoint
            endpoint_ok = False
            for attempt in range(1, OVERPASS_RETRY_PER_ENDPOINT + 1):
                try:
                    west, south, east, north = bounds
                    print(f"  第 {attempt}/{OVERPASS_RETRY_PER_ENDPOINT} 次：矩形範圍")
                    G = ox.graph.graph_from_bbox(
                        (west, south, east, north),
                        network_type="drive_service",
                        simplify=True,
                        retain_all=True,
                        truncate_by_edge=True,
                    )
                    print("  矩形範圍 下載成功。")
                    endpoint_ok = True
                    break
                except Exception as e:
                    msg = f"{endpoint} 矩形範圍 attempt={attempt}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
                    download_errors.append(msg)
                    print("  矩形範圍 失敗：", repr(e))
            if endpoint_ok:
                break
            if ALLOW_POLYGON_FALLBACK:
                try:
                    print("  矩形範圍 失敗，改用 區域範圍備用方式。")
                    G = ox.graph.graph_from_polygon(
                        download_polygon,
                        network_type="drive_service",
                        simplify=True,
                        retain_all=True,
                        truncate_by_edge=True,
                    )
                    print("  區域範圍備用方式 下載成功。")
                    endpoint_ok = True
                    break
                except Exception as e:
                    msg = f"{endpoint} 區域範圍備用方式: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
                    download_errors.append(msg)
                    print("  區域範圍備用方式 失敗：", repr(e))
            time.sleep(1)

        if G is None:
            for err in download_errors:
                print(" -", err)
            raise RuntimeError("BOTH 模式共用 OSM 道路圖下載失敗。")

        _save_cached_osm_graph(G, bounds, "drive_service")
    else:
        print("本次不下載 OSM，直接使用既有路網快取。")

    water_union = None
    if ENABLE_WATER_BARRIER_CHECK:
        water_union, water_cache_hit = _load_cached_water(download_polygon)
        if water_cache_hit:
            print("本次不下載 OSM 水路／水體，直接使用既有快取。")
        else:
            try:
                print("下載共用 OSM 水路 / 水體資料...")
                water_features = ox.features.features_from_polygon(
                    download_polygon,
                    tags={"waterway": True, "natural": "water"},
                )
                water_geometries = []
                if water_features is not None and len(water_features) > 0:
                    for geom in water_features.geometry:
                        if geom is not None and not geom.is_empty:
                            water_geometries.append(geom)
                if water_geometries:
                    water_union = unary_union(water_geometries)
                    print(f"共用 OSM 水路／水體圖形資料筆數：{len(water_geometries)}")
                else:
                    print("沒有有效的共用 OSM 水路／水體圖形資料。")
                _save_cached_water(water_union, download_polygon, len(water_geometries))
            except Exception as e:
                print("共用 OSM 水路 / 水體下載失敗，後續各模式略過水路輔助：", repr(e))
                water_union = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V52：BOTH 模式把 snap（水路修正後）只做一次。
    # snap 使用原始 OSM graph，不使用 CAR 的禁行後圖；
    # 若某車種後續因禁行邊移除而使 snap node 消失，車種內再對該筆做 fallback。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    print("共用 OSM：計算地籍點的道路接點（機車／汽車共用）...")
    shared_nearest_nodes, shared_snap_distances = ox.distance.nearest_nodes(
        G,
        X=coords[:, 0],
        Y=coords[:, 1],
        return_dist=True,
    )
    shared_nearest_nodes = np.asarray(shared_nearest_nodes, dtype=np.int64)
    shared_snap_distances = np.asarray(shared_snap_distances, dtype=float)

    # 水路輔助檢查沿用原程式邏輯，但只在共用 OSM 圖執行一次。
    if water_union is not None:
        def _shared_lonlat_to_xy(lon, lat):
            ref_lat = 23.7
            x = (lon - 120.0) * 111320.0 * math.cos(math.radians(ref_lat))
            y = (lat - 23.0) * 110540.0
            return x, y

        graph_nodes = list(G.nodes)
        graph_node_xy = np.array([
            _shared_lonlat_to_xy(float(G.nodes[n]["x"]), float(G.nodes[n]["y"]))
            for n in graph_nodes
        ])
        node_tree = cKDTree(graph_node_xy)

        def _shared_access_crosses_water(lon, lat, node):
            try:
                node_lon = float(G.nodes[node]["x"])
                node_lat = float(G.nodes[node]["y"])
                line = LineString([(float(lon), float(lat)), (node_lon, node_lat)])
                return bool(line.intersects(water_union))
            except Exception:
                return False

        adjusted_count = 0
        k = min(WATER_BARRIER_CANDIDATE_NODES, len(graph_nodes))
        for i in range(len(coords)):
            lon = float(coords[i, 0])
            lat = float(coords[i, 1])
            original_node = int(shared_nearest_nodes[i])
            if not _shared_access_crosses_water(lon, lat, original_node):
                continue

            px, py = _shared_lonlat_to_xy(lon, lat)
            candidate_distances, candidate_positions = node_tree.query([px, py], k=k)
            candidate_distances = np.atleast_1d(candidate_distances)
            candidate_positions = np.atleast_1d(candidate_positions)
            candidates = []
            for d, pos in zip(candidate_distances, candidate_positions):
                d = float(d)
                if d > WATER_BARRIER_MAX_ACCESS_M:
                    continue
                node = int(graph_nodes[int(pos)])
                crosses = _shared_access_crosses_water(lon, lat, node)
                candidates.append((0 if not crosses else 1, d, node))
            if candidates:
                candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
                cross_flag, distance_m, best_node = candidates[0]
                if cross_flag == 0:
                    shared_nearest_nodes[i] = best_node
                    shared_snap_distances[i] = distance_m
                    adjusted_count += 1
        print(f"共用 OSM 水路輔助調整道路接點：{adjusted_count} 筆")

    print(
        f"共用 snap：平均 {float(np.mean(shared_snap_distances)):.1f} m | "
        f"最大 {float(np.max(shared_snap_distances)):.1f} m | "
        f">500m {int(np.sum(shared_snap_distances > 500))} 筆"
    )

    print(f"共用 OSM 原始路網：節點={len(G.nodes):,} | 道路段={len(G.edges):,}")
    return {
        "G_base": G,
        "water_union": water_union,
        "shared_nearest_nodes": shared_nearest_nodes,
        "shared_snap_distances": shared_snap_distances,
        # V52：BOTH 模式可重用相同保留拓樸下的 ROAD_D。
        "road_matrix_cache": {},
        "road_matrix_cache_lock": threading.Lock(),
    }


def run_vehicle_mode(
    vehicle_mode,
    vehicle_label,
    vehicle_suffix,
    point_service_minutes,
    daily_max_hours,
    motor_avg_speed_kmh,
    car_surface_speed_kmh,
    car_motorway_speed_kmh,
    car_expressway_speed_kmh,
    shared_context=None,
):
    """執行單一交通工具模式；BOTH 模式可共用原始 OSM / 水路資料並平行執行。"""
    VEHICLE_MODE = vehicle_mode
    VEHICLE_LABEL = vehicle_label
    VEHICLE_SUFFIX = vehicle_suffix
    POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES = float(point_service_minutes)
    DAILY_TARGET_HOURS = float(daily_max_hours)
    DAILY_MAX_HOURS = float(daily_max_hours)
    MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH = float(motor_avg_speed_kmh)
    CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH = float(car_surface_speed_kmh)
    CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH = float(car_motorway_speed_kmh)
    CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH = float(car_expressway_speed_kmh)

    # V65.64.6：MOTOR 關閉 Crossing / U-turn 限制。
    # 仍保留實際 Crossing / U-turn 次數統計，但不納入任何路線最佳化
    # 或每日分界目標函數；CAR 維持原有懲罰。
    _ROUTE_CROSSING_PENALTY_M = (
        0.0 if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else float(CROSSING_PENALTY_M)
    )
    _ROUTE_UTURN_PENALTY_M = (
        0.0 if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else float(UTURN_PENALTY_M)
    )

    if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
        print("MOTOR Crossing / U-turn 限制：關閉（不納入路線最佳化目標函數）")

    # V65.22：延續效能計時器；只記錄時間，不改任何計算邏輯。
    _perf_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    _perf = {}
    _perf_labels = {
        "osm_copy_water_snap_and_graph_ready": "共用/模式 OSM、水路、snap 前置",
        "edge_weighting_and_car_suitability": ("道路 edge weighting／MOTOR 適用性" if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else "道路 edge weighting／CAR 適用性"),
        "route_time_csr_and_route_dijkstra": "ROUTE/TIME CSR 建立＋Route Dijkstra",
        "global_route_optimization": "全域路線最佳化",
        "road_matrix_dijkstra": "實際道路距離矩陣／ROAD Dijkstra",
        "time_matrix_dijkstra": "旅行時間矩陣／TIME Dijkstra",
        "all_in_one_optimization": "全點一次跑完方案",
        "daily_partition_and_cross_day_optimization": "每日 DP／分界／跨日最佳化",
        "daily_final_osm_geometry": "每日最終 OSM 實際道路路線",
        "all_in_one_final_osm_geometry": "全點一次跑完最終 OSM 實際道路路線",
    }
    def _perf_mark(name, started=None, emit=False):
        value = time.perf_counter() - (_perf_t0 if started is None else started)
        _perf[name] = value
        # 最終版不在 Colab 執行畫面輸出效能測試訊息。
        return value

    _perf_mark("mode_start")

    # 人工 CAR 路段控制只在 CAR 模式套用；MOTOR 完全忽略。
    CAR_MANUAL_CONTROLS = _CAR_MANUAL_CONTROLS if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else []

    OUTPUT_ROUTE_ORDER = _vehicle_output_name("route_order.xlsx", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_SUMMARY = _vehicle_output_name("route_summary.xlsx", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_POINTS = _vehicle_output_name("route_points.csv", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_GEOJSON = _vehicle_output_name("route.geojson", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_ERRORS = _vehicle_output_name("route_errors.xlsx", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_GMAP = _vehicle_output_name("gmap.csv", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_HTML = _vehicle_output_name("route_map.html", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_GRAPHML = _vehicle_output_name("osm_graph.graphml", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)

    # V50：獨立的「所有點位一次跑完」路線輸出。
    OUTPUT_ALL_IN_ONE_ORDER = _vehicle_output_name("route_all_in_one_order.xlsx", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_ALL_IN_ONE_SUMMARY = _vehicle_output_name("route_all_in_one_summary.xlsx", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_ALL_IN_ONE_POINTS = _vehicle_output_name("route_all_in_one_points.csv", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_ALL_IN_ONE_GEOJSON = _vehicle_output_name("route_all_in_one.geojson", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)
    OUTPUT_ALL_IN_ONE_GMAP = _vehicle_output_name("all_in_one_gmap.csv", VEHICLE_SUFFIX)

    # 2. 套件 / OSMnx 設定
    # ============================================================

    print("=" * 70)
    print("OSMnx 版本：", ox.__version__)
    print("NetworkX 版本：", nx.__version__)
    print("=" * 70)

    # OSMnx drive network。
    # 保持有向圖，保留 one-way。
    NETWORK_TYPE = "drive_service"  # 機車、汽車皆使用含 service 道路的有向車道路網；汽車不套用機車權重
    print(f"道路網類型：{NETWORK_TYPE}（包含服務道路）")


    # ============================================================
    # 3. 讀 Excel
    # ============================================================

    if not os.path.exists(INPUT_XLSX):
        raise FileNotFoundError(INPUT_XLSX)

    df = pd.read_excel(INPUT_XLSX)

    print("原始資料筆數：", len(df))


    # ============================================================
    # 4. 找座標
    # ============================================================

    candidate_pairs = [
        ("經度", "緯度"),
        ("Longitude", "Latitude"),
        ("lon", "lat"),
        ("lng", "lat"),
    ]

    lon_col = None
    lat_col = None

    for a, b in candidate_pairs:

        if a in df.columns and b in df.columns:
            lon_col = a
            lat_col = b
            break

    if lon_col is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "找不到經度 / 緯度欄位。"
        )


    # ============================================================
    # 5. 清洗
    # ============================================================

    df["_原始_df_index"] = df.index

    df[lon_col] = pd.to_numeric(
        df[lon_col],
        errors="coerce"
    )

    df[lat_col] = pd.to_numeric(
        df[lat_col],
        errors="coerce"
    )

    valid_mask = (
        df[lon_col].notna()
        & df[lat_col].notna()
        & df[lon_col].between(119, 123)
        & df[lat_col].between(21, 26)
    )

    work_df = (
        df.loc[valid_mask]
        .copy()
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    if len(work_df) == 0:
        raise ValueError("沒有有效座標。")

    N = len(work_df)


    # ============================================================
    # 6. 名稱／顯示名稱
#    統一使用 Cell 2 建立的「顯示名稱」；不再自行以「縣市-地段-原始地號」組名。
    # ============================================================

    def make_point_display_name(row, idx):
        """Cell 2 → Cell 4 統一顯示名稱。

        地號案件：縣市＋鄉鎮市區＋地段＋8碼地號。
        地址案件：優先沿用 Cell 2 的顯示名稱／名稱。
        """
        query_mode = str(row.get("查詢方式", "") or "").strip()
        display_name = str(row.get("顯示名稱", "") or "").strip()

        if query_mode == "地址":
            if display_name:
                return display_name
            name = str(row.get("名稱", "") or "").strip()
            if name:
                return name
            parts = [
                str(row.get("縣市", "") or "").strip(),
                str(row.get("鄉鎮市區", "") or "").strip(),
                str(row.get("地址", "") or "").strip(),
            ]
            parts = [p for p in parts if p]
            return "".join(parts) if parts else f"點位_{idx + 1}"

        city = str(row.get("縣市", "") or "").strip()
        area = str(row.get("鄉鎮市區", "") or "").strip()
        section = str(row.get("地段", "") or "").strip()

        landno = str(
            row.get("查詢地號", row.get("地號", "")) or ""
        ).strip()
        if landno.endswith(".0") and landno[:-2].isdigit():
            landno = landno[:-2]
        if landno.isdigit():
            landno = landno.zfill(8)

        parts = [city, area, section, landno]
        parts = [p for p in parts if p]
        if parts:
            return "".join(parts)

        return display_name or f"點位_{idx + 1}"


    # v65.16：正式顯示名稱與 Cell 2 統一。
    work_df["顯示名稱"] = [
        make_point_display_name(
            row,
            i
        )
        for i, (_, row)
        in enumerate(work_df.iterrows())
    ]

    # 舊欄位「名稱」保留供既有路線輸出相容，但內容同步採正式顯示名稱。
    work_df["名稱"] = work_df["顯示名稱"].copy()



    # ============================================================
    # 7. 座標
    # ============================================================

    coords = (
        work_df[
            [
                lon_col,
                lat_col,
            ]
        ]
        .astype(float)
        .values
    )

    # V65.50：主要定位座標＝Cell 2 經度／緯度。
    # 地址座標另存於「地址經度／地址緯度」，只作道路進場判斷輔助。
    address_coords = np.full((len(work_df), 2), np.nan, dtype=float)
    if SNAP_ADDRESS_COORD_ENABLED and "地址經度" in work_df.columns and "地址緯度" in work_df.columns:
        address_coords[:, 0] = pd.to_numeric(work_df["地址經度"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
        address_coords[:, 1] = pd.to_numeric(work_df["地址緯度"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
    # V65.61：地址道路名稱稍後在道路輔助函式定義完成後建立，
    # 避免 nested function 尚未建立就被提前呼叫。
    address_road_names = [""] * len(work_df)

    # 舊版 result.xlsx 沒有雙座標欄位時：地址案件本來就是以地址定位，
    # 直接把主要座標視為地址座標；地號案件則維持空白，不自行猜地址位置。
    if SNAP_ADDRESS_COORD_ENABLED:
        _addr_mode = work_df.get("查詢方式", pd.Series([""] * len(work_df), index=work_df.index)).astype(str).str.strip().eq("地址").to_numpy()
        _main_ok = np.isfinite(coords[:, 0]) & np.isfinite(coords[:, 1])
        _addr_missing = ~(np.isfinite(address_coords[:, 0]) & np.isfinite(address_coords[:, 1]))
        address_coords[_addr_mode & _main_ok & _addr_missing] = coords[_addr_mode & _main_ok & _addr_missing]

    # ================================================================
    # 固定起點：自動取「第一筆有效座標」
    # ================================================================
    # 不寫死任何經緯度。
    # Cell 2 每次換不同 result.xlsx 時，
    # 第一筆有效資料會自動成為固定起點。
    #
    # FIXED_START_INDEX 預設為 0，因此代表 work_df 的第一筆。
    # work_df 已依 result.xlsx 原始順序建立，不會重新排序。
    # ================================================================

    if not (0 <= FIXED_START_INDEX < N):
        raise IndexError(
            f"FIXED_START_INDEX={FIXED_START_INDEX} 超出有效資料範圍 0~{N-1}"
        )

    start_idx = FIXED_START_INDEX

    start_name = (
        work_df.iloc[
            start_idx
        ]["名稱"]
    )

    # 從當次 result.xlsx 實際座標自動取得起點
    start_lon = float(
        coords[
            start_idx,
            0
        ]
    )

    start_lat = float(
        coords[
            start_idx,
            1
        ]
    )

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("自動取得固定起點")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "有效點數：",
        N
    )

    print(
        "固定起點：",
        start_name
    )

    print(
        "固定起點經度：",
        f"{start_lon:.8f}"
    )

    print(
        "固定起點緯度：",
        f"{start_lat:.8f}"
    )

    print(
        "固定起點座標：[",
        f"{start_lon:.8f}, {start_lat:.8f}"
        "]"
    )


    # ============================================================
    # 8. Haversine
    # ============================================================

    EARTH_RADIUS_M = 6371008.8


    def haversine_matrix(arr):

        lon = np.radians(
            arr[:, 0]
        )

        lat = np.radians(
            arr[:, 1]
        )

        lon1 = lon[:, None]
        lon2 = lon[None, :]

        lat1 = lat[:, None]
        lat2 = lat[None, :]

        dlon = lon2 - lon1
        dlat = lat2 - lat1

        a = (
            np.sin(
                dlat / 2
            ) ** 2
            +
            np.cos(lat1)
            *
            np.cos(lat2)
            *
            np.sin(
                dlon / 2
            ) ** 2
        )

        a = np.clip(
            a,
            0,
            1
        )

        return (
            EARTH_RADIUS_M
            *
            2
            *
            np.arcsin(
                np.sqrt(a)
            )
        )


    EUCLIDEAN_M = (
        haversine_matrix(
            coords
        )
    )


    # ============================================================
    # 9. Local XY
    # ============================================================

    def lonlat_to_xy(
        lon,
        lat
    ):

        ref_lat = 23.7

        x = (
            (lon - 120.0)
            * 111320.0
            * math.cos(
                math.radians(
                    ref_lat
                )
            )
        )

        y = (
            (lat - 23.0)
            * 110540.0
        )

        return x, y


    XY = np.array([
        lonlat_to_xy(
            lon,
            lat
        )
        for lon, lat in coords
    ])


    # ============================================================
    # 10. 建立 OSM download polygon
    # ============================================================

    def make_download_polygon(
        coords_array,
        buffer_m
    ):
        """
        將所有點建立 MultiPoint，
        以公尺為單位 buffer。
        """

        from shapely.geometry import MultiPoint
        import geopandas as gpd

        mp = MultiPoint([
            (float(x), float(y))
            for x, y in coords_array
        ])

        gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(
            geometry=[mp],
            crs="EPSG:4326"
        )

        # 投影到適合台灣的 UTM 51N
        gdf_m = gdf.to_crs(
            "EPSG:32651"
        )

        buffered = gdf_m.geometry.iloc[
            0
        ].buffer(
            buffer_m
        )

        buffered_gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(
            geometry=[buffered],
            crs="EPSG:32651"
        )

        return (
            buffered_gdf
            .to_crs("EPSG:4326")
            .geometry.iloc[0]
        )


    download_polygon = (
        make_download_polygon(
            coords,
            OSM_BUFFER_M
        )
    )

    bounds = download_polygon.bounds

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("OSM 下載範圍")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "west/south/east/north：",
        bounds
    )
    print(
        f"buffer：{OSM_BUFFER_M} m"
    )
    print("下載方式：先用矩形範圍；失敗時改用區域範圍")


    # V65.48：OSM／水路／snap 前置純計時起點。
    _perf_osm_t0 = time.perf_counter()

    # ============================================================
    # 11. 下載 OSM drive graph
    # ============================================================

    if shared_context is not None:
        # BOTH 模式：原始 OSM 圖只下載一次；MOTOR/CAR 各自 copy 後再套用權重。
        print()
        print("=" * 70)
        print("共用 OSM 原始道路圖（BOTH 模式；本模式使用獨立 copy）")
        print("=" * 70)
        G = shared_context["G_base"].copy()
        download_errors = []
        print(
            f"沿用共用 OSM 圖：節點={len(G.nodes):,} | 道路段={len(G.edges):,}"
        )
    else:
        # 原模式：單獨下載。
        print()
        print("=" * 70)
        print("下載 OpenStreetMap 道路圖（v35：矩形範圍 優先；快取可由 FORCE_REFRESH_OSM_CACHE 控制）")
        print("=" * 70)

        ox.settings.requests_timeout = OVERPASS_REQUEST_TIMEOUT
        ox.settings.overpass_rate_limit = True
        ox.settings.use_cache = True

        G = _load_cached_osm_graph(bounds, NETWORK_TYPE)
        download_errors = []

        if G is not None:
            print("本次不下載 OSM，直接使用既有路網快取。")

        for endpoint in OVERPASS_ENDPOINTS:
            if G is not None:
                break

            print()
            print("嘗試 Overpass：", endpoint)

            ox.settings.overpass_url = endpoint

            endpoint_ok = False

            for attempt in range(1, OVERPASS_RETRY_PER_ENDPOINT + 1):

                try:
                    if DOWNLOAD_MODE == "矩形範圍":
                        print(
                            f"  第 {attempt}/{OVERPASS_RETRY_PER_ENDPOINT} 次：矩形範圍（v35 優先，避免 polygon 裁切瓶頸）"
                        )

                        west, south, east, north = bounds
                        G = ox.graph.graph_from_bbox(
                            (west, south, east, north),
                            network_type=NETWORK_TYPE,
                            simplify=True,
                            retain_all=True,
                            truncate_by_edge=True,
                        )
                        print("  矩形範圍 下載成功。")
                    else:
                        print(
                            f"  第 {attempt}/{OVERPASS_RETRY_PER_ENDPOINT} 次：polygon"
                        )
                        G = ox.graph.graph_from_polygon(
                            download_polygon,
                            network_type=NETWORK_TYPE,
                            simplify=True,
                            retain_all=True,
                            truncate_by_edge=True,
                        )
                        print("  polygon 下載成功。")

                    endpoint_ok = True
                    break

                except Exception as e:
                    mode = "矩形範圍" if DOWNLOAD_MODE == "矩形範圍" else "polygon"
                    msg = (
                        f"{endpoint} {mode} attempt={attempt}: "
                        f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
                    )
                    download_errors.append(msg)
                    print(f"  {mode} 失敗：", repr(e))
                    if attempt < OVERPASS_RETRY_PER_ENDPOINT:
                        time.sleep(2)

            if endpoint_ok:
                break

            if ALLOW_POLYGON_FALLBACK and DOWNLOAD_MODE == "矩形範圍":
                try:
                    print("  矩形範圍 失敗，改用 區域範圍備用方式。")
                    G = ox.graph.graph_from_polygon(
                        download_polygon,
                        network_type=NETWORK_TYPE,
                        simplify=True,
                        retain_all=True,
                        truncate_by_edge=True,
                    )
                    print("  區域範圍備用方式 下載成功。")
                    endpoint_ok = True
                    break
                except Exception as e:
                    msg = (
                        f"{endpoint} 區域範圍備用方式: "
                        f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
                    )
                    download_errors.append(msg)
                    print("  區域範圍備用方式 失敗：", repr(e))

            time.sleep(1)

        if G is None:
            print()
            print("=" * 70)
            print("所有 Overpass endpoint 都無法下載 OSM。")
            print("=" * 70)
            print()
            for err in download_errors:
                print(" -", err)
            raise RuntimeError(
                "OSM 道路圖下載失敗："
                "所有公開 Overpass endpoint 都無法連線。"
                "目前尚未進入路線最佳化階段。"
            )

        # 第一次成功下載後建立長期快取；下次執行相同範圍直接讀取，不再向 Overpass 請求。
        _save_cached_osm_graph(G, bounds, NETWORK_TYPE)

    # ============================================================
    # 11A. OSM 水路 / 水體資料：在 CAR V64 判斷前就準備完成
    #      BOTH 模式直接沿用共用 context；單一模式則命中快取時不下載，
    #      快取不存在才向 OSM 取得一次。後面的 snap 區段只重複使用此變數。
    # ============================================================
    water_union = None
    water_cache_hit = False
    if shared_context is not None:
        water_union = shared_context.get("water_union")
        if water_union is not None:
            print("沿用共用 OSM 水路 / 水體資料。")
        else:
            print("共用 OSM 沒有有效的水路／水體圖形資料，不進行水路輔助檢查。")
    elif ENABLE_WATER_BARRIER_CHECK:
        water_union, water_cache_hit = _load_cached_water(download_polygon)
        if water_cache_hit:
            print("本次不下載 OSM 水路／水體，直接使用既有快取。")
        else:
            try:
                print(
                    "下載 OSM 水路 / 水體資料，"
                    "檢查地籍點與道路之間是否隔水..."
                )
                water_features = ox.features.features_from_polygon(
                    download_polygon,
                    tags={
                        "waterway": True,
                        "natural": "water"
                    }
                )
                water_geometries = []
                if water_features is not None and len(water_features) > 0:
                    for geom in water_features.geometry:
                        if geom is not None and not geom.is_empty:
                            water_geometries.append(geom)
                if water_geometries:
                    water_union = unary_union(water_geometries)
                    print(
                        "OSM 水路 / 水體 geometry：",
                        len(water_geometries)
                    )
                else:
                    print(
                        "沒有有效 OSM 水路 / 水體 geometry，"
                        "後續略過水路輔助檢查。"
                    )
                _save_cached_water(
                    water_union,
                    download_polygon,
                    len(water_geometries),
                )
            except Exception as e:
                print(
                    "OSM 水路 / 水體下載失敗，"
                    "後續略過水路輔助檢查：",
                    repr(e),
                )
                water_union = None
    else:
        print("水路／水體檢查未啟用，略過 OSM 水路 / 水體資料。")

    # ============================================================
    # 12. OSM access helpers
    # ============================================================

    def tag_string(value):

        if value is None:
            return ""

        if isinstance(
            value,
            (list, tuple, set)
        ):
            return ";".join(
                str(x)
                for x in value
            ).lower()

        return str(value).lower()


    def _car_tag_bundle(data):
        """V65.48：一次建立 CAR suitability 反覆使用的 tag／管理文字快取。

        將 special-management 反覆使用的 context、OSM 管理標籤、名稱存在性與
        width/maxwidth 原值一起快取；只減少重複解析，不改變既有判斷值。
        """
        name_keys = ("name", "official_name", "alt_name", "loc_name", "short_name", "ref")
        return {
            "highway": tag_string(data.get("highway")),
            "surface": tag_string(data.get("surface")),
            "tracktype": tag_string(data.get("tracktype")),
            "access": tag_string(data.get("access")),
            "vehicle": tag_string(data.get("vehicle")),
            "motor_vehicle": tag_string(data.get("motor_vehicle")),
            "motorcar": tag_string(data.get("motorcar")),
            "motorcycle": tag_string(data.get("motorcycle")),
            "service": tag_string(data.get("service")),
            "narrow": tag_string(data.get("narrow")),
            "man_made": tag_string(data.get("man_made")),
            "barrier": tag_string(data.get("barrier")),
            "embankment": tag_string(data.get("embankment")),
            "context_text": _car_special_context_text(data),
            "has_name": any(_clean_nonempty(data.get(k)) for k in name_keys),
            "width_raw": data.get("width", ""),
            "maxwidth_raw": data.get("maxwidth", ""),
        }


    def _conditional_has_restriction(value):
        """粗略判讀 OSM conditional access 是否含 no/private/agricultural/forestry。

        例如：
          no @ (Mo-Fr 07:00-09:00)
          private @ (Sa-Su)

        這裡只做 routing risk 判斷，不宣稱符合/違反實際交通法規。
        """
        text = tag_string(value)
        if not text:
            return False
        # 常見 conditional 語法為「值 @ 條件」；只要條件式前段出現限制值，
        # 即視為存在 conditional restriction。
        clauses = [c.strip() for c in text.split(";") if c.strip()]
        restricted_values = {"no", "private", "agricultural", "forestry"}
        for clause in clauses:
            left = clause.split("@", 1)[0].strip()
            tokens = [t.strip() for t in re.split(r"[,|]", left) if t.strip()]
            if any(t in restricted_values for t in tokens):
                return True
        # 保留較寬鬆 fallback，避免不同資料格式漏判。
        return bool(re.search(r"(^|[;\s])(no|private|agricultural|forestry)(\s*@|[;\s]|$)", text))


    def _is_explicit_motorcycle_allowed(data, parsed_tags=None):
        """判斷 OSM 是否明確標示 motorcycle 可通行。"""
        if parsed_tags is None:
            motorcycle = tag_string(data.get("motorcycle"))
        else:
            motorcycle = parsed_tags["motorcycle"]
        return motorcycle in {"yes", "designated", "permissive"}


    def _is_explicit_car_restricted(data, parsed_tags=None):
        """判斷 OSM 是否明確限制一般汽車／汽車類機動車。"""
        parsed = parsed_tags if parsed_tags is not None else _car_tag_bundle(data)
        access = parsed["access"]
        vehicle = parsed["vehicle"]
        motor_vehicle = parsed["motor_vehicle"]
        motorcar = parsed["motorcar"]

        forbidden = {"no", "private", "agricultural", "forestry"}
        if access in {"no", "private"}:
            return True
        if vehicle in forbidden:
            return True
        if motor_vehicle in forbidden:
            return True
        if motorcar in forbidden:
            return True
        return False


    def _is_motorcycle_only_pattern(data, parsed_tags=None):
        """辨識「汽車禁止、但 motorcycle 明確允許」的 OSM 標籤組合。"""
        parsed = parsed_tags if parsed_tags is not None else _car_tag_bundle(data)
        motorcycle_allowed = _is_explicit_motorcycle_allowed(data, parsed)
        if not motorcycle_allowed:
            return False

        access = parsed["access"]
        vehicle = parsed["vehicle"]
        motor_vehicle = parsed["motor_vehicle"]
        motorcar = parsed["motorcar"]
        forbidden = {"no", "private", "agricultural", "forestry"}
        if motorcar in forbidden:
            return True
        if motor_vehicle in forbidden:
            return True
        if vehicle in forbidden:
            return True
        if access == "no" and motorcycle_allowed:
            return True
        return False


    def _is_explicit_car_allowed(data, parsed_tags=None):
        """判斷 OSM 是否有「汽車本身」的明確允許訊號。"""
        parsed = parsed_tags if parsed_tags is not None else _car_tag_bundle(data)
        for key in ("vehicle", "motor_vehicle", "motorcar"):
            if parsed[key] in CAR_SPECIAL_EXPLICIT_ALLOW_VALUES:
                return True
        return False


    def _car_special_context_text(data):
        """彙整道路名稱／說明／營運單位等文字，供防汛管理道路關鍵字判斷。"""
        fields = (
            "name",
            "official_name",
            "alt_name",
            "loc_name",
            "short_name",
            "description",
            "operator",
            "ref",
        )
        parts = []
        for key in fields:
            value = data.get(key)
            if value is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
                parts.extend(str(x).strip().lower() for x in value if str(x).strip())
            else:
                text = str(value).strip().lower()
                if text:
                    parts.append(text)
        return " ".join(parts)


    def _clean_nonempty(value):
        if value is None:
            return False
        text = str(value).strip()
        return bool(text) and text.lower() not in {"nan", "none", "null"}


    def _car_special_management_road_reason(data, parsed_tags=None):
        """回傳 V64 疑似水利／防汛／堤防管理道路原因；否則回傳空字串。"""
        if not CAR_SPECIAL_MANAGEMENT_ROAD_ENABLED:
            return ""
        parsed = parsed_tags if parsed_tags is not None else _car_tag_bundle(data)
        if _is_explicit_car_allowed(data, parsed):
            return ""

        highway = parsed["highway"]
        service = parsed["service"]
        context_text = parsed.get("context_text", "")

        if highway in CAR_SPECIAL_RESTRICTED_HIGHWAY_TYPES:
            return "highway=path：不視為一般四輪車道路"
