        if service == "emergency_access":
            return "highway=service + service=emergency_access，且未明確標示一般汽車允許"

        for kw in CAR_SPECIAL_NAME_KEYWORDS:
            if kw in context_text:
                return f"名稱／說明／營運單位含水利管理關鍵字：{kw}"

        for key in ("man_made", "barrier"):
            value = parsed.get(key, "")
            if value in CAR_SPECIAL_OSM_VALUES:
                return f"{key}={value}"

        embankment = parsed.get("embankment", "")
        if embankment in {"yes", "true", "1"} and highway in {"track", "service"}:
            if bool(data.get("_car_v64_water_adjacent", False)):
                return "embankment=yes + service/track 緊鄰水體"

        if (
            highway in {"service", "track"}
            and bool(data.get("_car_v64_water_adjacent", False))
            and parsed["motorcycle"] in {"designated", "yes"}
        ):
            return (
                "service/track 緊鄰 OSM 水路／水體，且 motorcycle=designated；"
                "未明確標示汽車允許"
            )

        if highway == "track" and bool(data.get("_car_v64_water_adjacent", False)):
            return (
                f"track 緊鄰 OSM 水路／水體（≤{CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_BUFFER_M:g}m），"
                "且無明確汽車允許標示"
            )

        if highway == "service" and bool(data.get("_car_v64_water_adjacent", False)):
            service_value = service.strip().lower()
            width_m = _parse_width_m(parsed.get("width_raw"))
            if width_m is None:
                width_m = _parse_width_m(parsed.get("maxwidth_raw"))

            if service_value in CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_SERVICE_VALUES:
                return f"service={service_value} + 緊鄰 OSM 水路／水體"

            water_name_hits = (
                "大圳", "圳路", "圳堤", "灌排", "灌溉", "水利", "渠道",
                "防汛", "防洪", "堤防", "河堤", "堤頂"
            )
            for kw in water_name_hits:
                if kw in context_text:
                    return f"service 緊鄰 OSM 水路／水體，且名稱／說明含水利管理關鍵字：{kw}"

            has_name = bool(parsed.get("has_name", False))
            surface = parsed["surface"]
            narrow_tag = parsed["narrow"]
            if (not has_name) and (
                (width_m is not None and width_m <= CAR_SPECIAL_UNNAMED_NARROW_SERVICE_WIDTH_M)
                or surface in {"unpaved", "gravel", "dirt", "ground", "sand", "mud"}
                or narrow_tag in {"yes", "true", "1"}
                or embankment in {"yes", "true", "1"}
            ):
                return (
                    "無名稱／無ref之狹窄或非鋪面 service 緊鄰 OSM 水路／水體，"
                    "且無明確汽車允許標示"
                )
        return ""


    # V65.62.4.1：診斷 helper 提前定義。
    # 原 v65.62.4 將 _v65_8_is_soft_water_candidate 定義在 edge weighting
    # 診斷區塊之後，導致 CAR 建立道路權重時先呼叫、後定義而產生 UnboundLocalError。
    # 本 helper 本身不改任何路線規則。
    # CAR 水體二次避讓的 soft-water 候選採保守條件：
    #   1. track：若未被 V65 特殊管理規則攔截，仍保留為 soft 候選；
    #   2. service：必須再具備「無名稱且有狹窄/非鋪面/embankment」等風險證據，
    #      或 service 本身屬特殊水利型 service；
    #   3. 有正式道路名稱的 service=alley，不因鄰水 alone 就列為 soft。
    def _v65_8_is_soft_water_candidate(attrs):
        if VEHICLE_MODE != "CAR" or not CAR_WATER_REROUTE_ENABLED:
            return False
        if not bool(attrs.get("_car_v64_water_adjacent", False)):
            return False
        highway = tag_string(attrs.get("highway"))
        if highway not in {"service", "track"}:
            return False
        if str(attrs.get("_car_v64_special_management_reason", "")):
            return False
        if _is_explicit_car_allowed(attrs):
            return False

        parsed = _car_tag_bundle(attrs)
        service = str(parsed.get("service", "") or "").strip().lower()
        has_name = bool(parsed.get("has_name", False))
        surface = str(parsed.get("surface", "") or "").strip().lower()
        narrow_tag = str(parsed.get("narrow", "") or "").strip().lower()
        embankment = str(parsed.get("embankment", "") or "").strip().lower()

        # 特殊水利型 service 已在第一層管理規則處理；保留此處邏輯以避免漏網。
        if highway == "service" and service in CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_SERVICE_VALUES:
            return True

        if highway == "track":
            return True

        # 一般 service 只有在沒有道路名稱，且存在額外風險證據時才 soft。
        if highway == "service":
            if has_name:
                return False
            width_m = _parse_width_m(parsed.get("width_raw"))
            if width_m is None:
                width_m = _parse_width_m(parsed.get("maxwidth_raw"))
            return bool(
                (width_m is not None and width_m <= CAR_SPECIAL_UNNAMED_NARROW_SERVICE_WIDTH_M)
                or surface in {"unpaved", "gravel", "dirt", "ground", "sand", "mud"}
                or narrow_tag in {"yes", "true", "1"}
                or embankment in {"yes", "true", "1"}
            )

        return False


    def _parse_width_m(value):
        """讀取 OSM width/maxwidth 等可能帶單位的數值（公尺）。"""
        if value is None:
            return None
        text = str(value).strip().lower()
        if not text:
            return None
        # 支援 "3.2", "3.2 m", "10 ft"；多值只取第一個數值。
        m = re.search(r"[-+]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+)", text)
        if not m:
            return None
        try:
            num = float(m.group(0))
        except Exception:
            return None
        if "ft" in text or "feet" in text:
            num *= 0.3048
        if num <= 0 or num > 50:
            return None
        return num


    def car_road_suitability(data, special_reason=None, parsed_tags=None):
        """回傳汽車道路適用性分級、原因及建議成本倍率。

        V65.22：集中 tag 解析，並只在 track / 未鋪面 / service 需要時解析 width。
        判斷順序與既有回傳值不變。
        """
        parsed = parsed_tags if parsed_tags is not None else _car_tag_bundle(data)
        highway = parsed["highway"]
        surface = parsed["surface"]
        tracktype = parsed["tracktype"]
        access = parsed["access"]
        service = parsed["service"]
        narrow_tag = parsed["narrow"]

        width_m = None
        def _lazy_width_m():
            nonlocal width_m
            if width_m is None:
                width_m = _parse_width_m(parsed.get("width_raw"))
                if width_m is None:
                    width_m = _parse_width_m(parsed.get("maxwidth_raw"))
            return width_m

        if _is_explicit_car_restricted(data, parsed):
            if _is_motorcycle_only_pattern(data, parsed):
                return {
                    "grade": "D",
                    "label": "汽車明確禁止／疑似機車專用型",
                    "reason": "OSM 顯示 car/motor_vehicle/vehicle 禁止，但 motorcycle 明確允許",
                    "factor": float("inf"),
                    "explicit_motorcycle_only": True,
                    "v64_special_management": False,
                }
            return {
                "grade": "D",
                "label": "汽車明確禁止",
                "reason": "OSM access / vehicle / motor_vehicle / motorcar 明確禁止",
                "factor": float("inf"),
                "explicit_motorcycle_only": False,
                "v64_special_management": False,
            }

        if special_reason is None:
            special_reason = _car_special_management_road_reason(data, parsed)
        if special_reason:
            return {
                "grade": "D",
                "label": "疑似汽車禁行／水利防汛管理道路",
                "reason": special_reason,
                "factor": float("inf"),
                "explicit_motorcycle_only": bool(_is_motorcycle_only_pattern(data, parsed)),
                "v64_special_management": True,
            }

        conditional_hits = []
        for key in CAR_CONDITIONAL_KEYS:
            if _conditional_has_restriction(data.get(key)):
                conditional_hits.append(key)
        if conditional_hits:
            return {
                "grade": "C",
                "label": "汽車條件式限制",
                "reason": "、".join(conditional_hits) + " 含限制條件",
                "factor": CAR_CONDITIONAL_RESTRICTION_FACTOR,
                "explicit_motorcycle_only": False,
                "v64_special_management": False,
            }

        if highway == "track":
            factor = CAR_TRACK_SUITABILITY_FACTOR
            reason = "highway=track"
            if tracktype in {"grade4", "grade5"}:
                factor *= 1.30
                reason += f" + tracktype={tracktype}"
            if narrow_tag in {"yes", "true", "1"}:
                factor *= 1.25
                reason += " + narrow=yes"
            width_val = _lazy_width_m()
            if width_val is not None and width_val < CAR_NARROW_WIDTH_M:
                factor *= CAR_NARROW_SERVICE_FACTOR
                reason += f" + width={width_val:.1f}m"
            return {
                "grade": "B",
                "label": "疑似低汽車適用道路",
                "reason": reason,
                "factor": factor,
                "explicit_motorcycle_only": False,
                "v64_special_management": False,
            }

        if surface in {"unpaved", "gravel", "dirt", "ground", "sand", "mud"}:
            factor = CAR_UNPAVED_SUITABILITY_FACTOR
            reason = f"surface={surface}"
            width_val = _lazy_width_m()
            if width_val is not None and width_val < CAR_NARROW_WIDTH_M:
                factor *= 1.50
                reason += f" + width={width_val:.1f}m"
            return {
                "grade": "B",
                "label": "未鋪面／低汽車適用道路",
                "reason": reason,
                "factor": factor,
                "explicit_motorcycle_only": False,
            }

        if highway == "service":
            if service == "alley":
                factor = CAR_SERVICE_ALLEY_FACTOR
                reason = "highway=service + service=alley"
            elif service == "driveway":
                factor = CAR_SERVICE_DRIVEWAY_FACTOR
                reason = "highway=service + service=driveway"
            elif service == "parking_aisle":
                factor = CAR_SERVICE_PARKING_AISLE_FACTOR
                reason = "highway=service + service=parking_aisle"
            else:
                factor = CAR_SERVICE_SUITABILITY_FACTOR
                reason = "highway=service"
            if narrow_tag in {"yes", "true", "1"}:
                factor *= 1.25
                reason += " + narrow=yes"
            width_val = _lazy_width_m()
            if width_val is not None and width_val < CAR_NARROW_WIDTH_M:
                factor *= CAR_NARROW_SERVICE_FACTOR
                reason += f" + width={width_val:.1f}m"
            return {
                "grade": "B",
                "label": "服務性／低汽車適用道路",
                "reason": reason,
                "factor": factor,
                "explicit_motorcycle_only": False,
                "v64_special_management": False,
            }

        if highway == "living_street":
            return {
                "grade": "B",
                "label": "低速生活道路",
                "reason": "highway=living_street",
                "factor": CAR_LIVING_STREET_SUITABILITY_FACTOR,
                "explicit_motorcycle_only": False,
                "v64_special_management": False,
            }

        if access == "destination":
            return {
                "grade": "B",
                "label": "目的地通行道路",
                "reason": "access=destination",
                "factor": CAR_DESTINATION_SUITABILITY_FACTOR,
                "explicit_motorcycle_only": False,
                "v64_special_management": False,
            }

        return {
            "grade": "A",
            "label": "一般汽車道路",
            "reason": "未發現明確限制或低適用性標籤",
            "factor": 1.0,
            "explicit_motorcycle_only": False,
        }


    def _motor_fastroad_side_road_name(data):
        """判斷是否有明確「快速路○段」側車道名稱。"""
        name = _edge_name_text(data)
        if not name:
            return False
        return bool(MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_NAME_RE.search(name))


    def _motor_fastroad_restriction_reason(data):
        """判定普通重型機車是否必須避開高速／快速公路主線或匝道。"""
        if not MOTOR_FASTROAD_FORBID_ENABLED:
            return ""

        highway = tag_string(data.get("highway"))
        if highway in MOTOR_ALWAYS_FORBIDDEN_HIGHWAY_TYPES:
            return f"MOTOR普通重型機車禁止進入高速公路道路類型：highway={highway}"

        if highway in MOTOR_CONDITIONAL_FASTROAD_HIGHWAY_TYPES:
            motorroad = tag_string(data.get("motorroad"))
            if motorroad in MOTOR_FASTROAD_MOTORROAD_VALUES:
                return f"MOTOR普通重型機車禁止進入受管制快速公路：highway={highway}, motorroad={motorroad}"

            for _tag_name in ("motorcycle", "motor_vehicle", "vehicle"):
                _tag_value = tag_string(data.get(_tag_name))
                if _tag_value in MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_RESTRICTION_VALUES:
                    return (
                        "MOTOR普通重型機車禁止使用此快速公路路段："
                        f"highway={highway}, {_tag_name}={_tag_value}"
                    )

            name = _edge_name_text(data)
            ref = tag_string(data.get("ref"))
            is_named_side_road = _motor_fastroad_side_road_name(data)
            is_mainline_name = any(
                _hint in name for _hint in MOTOR_FASTROAD_MAINLINE_NAME_HINTS
            )

            # 台66平面側車道：名稱為「快速路○段」時保留。
            if ref in MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_REFS and is_named_side_road:
                return ""

            # trunk_link 預設視為快速公路匝道；只有「快速路○段」側車道正面證據才保留。
            # 這能攔住「平鎮二交流道」這類沒有 ref=66 的 link。
            if highway == "trunk_link":
                return (
                    "MOTOR普通重型機車禁止進入快速公路匝道："
                    f"highway={highway}"
                    + (f", ref={ref}" if ref else ", ref=（無ref）")
                    + (f", name={name}" if name else ", name=（未命名）")
                )

            # 台66主線：沒有側車道名稱正面證據就排除。
            if ref in MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_REFS:
                return (
                    "MOTOR普通重型機車禁止進入台66受管制主線："
                    f"highway={highway}, ref=66"
                    + (f", name={name}" if name else ", name=（未命名）")
                )

            # 即使缺少 ref，只要名稱明確是快速公路主線就排除。
            if is_mainline_name:
                return (
                    "MOTOR普通重型機車禁止進入快速公路主線："
                    f"highway={highway}, name={name}"
                )

        return ""


    def is_explicitly_forbidden_motor(
        data
    ):
        access = tag_string(data.get("access"))
        vehicle = tag_string(data.get("vehicle"))
        motor_vehicle = tag_string(data.get("motor_vehicle"))
        motorcar = tag_string(data.get("motorcar"))
        motorcycle = tag_string(data.get("motorcycle"))

        # V65.63.2：MOTOR = 普通重型機車；高速公路一律禁止，台66主線／匝道排除、快速路○段側車道保留。
        if _motor_fastroad_restriction_reason(data):
            return True

        forbidden = {"no", "private", "agricultural", "forestry"}

        if access in {"no", "private"}:
            return True
        if vehicle in forbidden:
            return True
        if motor_vehicle in forbidden:
            return True
        if motorcar in forbidden:
            return True
        if motorcycle in {"no", "private", "agricultural", "forestry"}:
            return True

        highway = tag_string(data.get("highway"))
        if highway in {
            "footway",
            "path",
            "pedestrian",
            "steps",
            "cycleway",
            "bridleway",
            "corridor",
        }:
            return True

        return False


    def is_explicitly_forbidden_car(data):
        """汽車模式：依 car_road_suitability 的 D 級排除。"""
        return car_road_suitability(data)["grade"] == "D"


    def edge_factor_motor(data):

        highway = tag_string(
            data.get("highway")
        )

        access = tag_string(
            data.get("access")
        )

        motor_vehicle = tag_string(
            data.get("motor_vehicle")
        )

        motorcycle = tag_string(
            data.get("motorcycle")
        )

        surface = tag_string(
            data.get("surface")
        )


        factor = 1.0


        # --------------------------------------------------------
        # 明確 motorcycle / motor_vehicle designated
        # --------------------------------------------------------

        if motorcycle in {
            "designated",
            "yes"
        }:

            factor *= (
                MOTORCYCLE_DESIGNATED_FACTOR
            )

        elif motor_vehicle in {
            "designated",
            "yes"
        }:

            factor *= (
                MOTOR_VEHICLE_DESIGNATED_FACTOR
            )


        # --------------------------------------------------------
        # 道路類型
        # --------------------------------------------------------

        # V65.63.9：名稱明確為「快速路○段」的台66平面側車道，
        # 實測 OSM 可能標為 primary、tertiary 或 service；統一給輕微優先。
        # 注意：motorway/trunk/trunk_link 等受管制類型已在前面排除，不會由這裡漏放。
        _is_named_fastroad_side = (
            tag_string(data.get("ref")) in MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_REFS
            and _motor_fastroad_side_road_name(data)
            and highway not in {
                "motorway", "motorway_link", "trunk", "trunk_link",
            }
        )

        if _is_named_fastroad_side:
            factor *= MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_FACTOR

        if highway == "service":
            # 有明確快速路側車道身份時已套用側車道因子，不再疊加一般 service 成本。
            if not _is_named_fastroad_side:
                factor *= SERVICE_FACTOR

        elif highway == "living_street":
            factor *= LIVING_STREET_FACTOR

        elif highway == "track":
            factor *= TRACK_FACTOR


        # --------------------------------------------------------
        # surface
        # --------------------------------------------------------

        if surface in {
            "unpaved",
            "gravel",
            "dirt",
            "ground",
            "mud",
            "sand",
            "grass",
        }:

            factor *= UNPAVED_FACTOR


        # --------------------------------------------------------
        # destination
        # --------------------------------------------------------

        if access == "destination":
            factor *= DESTINATION_FACTOR


        return factor


    _perf_mark("osm_copy_water_snap_and_graph_ready", _perf_osm_t0, emit=True)

    # ============================================================
    # 12A. CAR 人工禁行／避開路段
    # ============================================================
    CAR_MANUAL_EDGE_CONTROL = {}
    CAR_MANUAL_DIAGNOSTICS = []
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
        CAR_MANUAL_EDGE_CONTROL, CAR_MANUAL_DIAGNOSTICS = _manual_control_edges(
            G, CAR_MANUAL_CONTROLS
        )
        if CAR_MANUAL_DIAGNOSTICS:
            total_matched = sum(int(x.get("匹配edge數", 0) or 0) for x in CAR_MANUAL_DIAGNOSTICS)
            print(
                f"汽車人工路段控制：{len(CAR_MANUAL_DIAGNOSTICS)} 筆規則 | "
                f"匹配 edge（規則加總）：{total_matched:,}"
            )
            for d in CAR_MANUAL_DIAGNOSTICS:
                print(
                    f"  規則 {d['規則編號']} | {d['處理方式']} | "
                    f"匹配={int(d['匹配edge數']):,} | {d['結果']}"
                )
        else:
            print("汽車人工路段控制：無設定，略過")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.3：建立水利／防汛管理道路的水體鄰近判斷幾何
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # v65.32：判定幾何仍完全維持 v65.28／v65.30：
    # water_union → EPSG:3826 → buffer 40m → EPSG:4326 → prepared。
    # 新增「衍生 geometry 快取」，因此第一次建立後，後續 Cell 4 執行
    # 不再重做昂貴的 40m buffer。
    _car_v64_water_prepared = None
    _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84 = None
    _car_v65_31_water_buffer_build_sec = 0.0
    _car_v65_31_water_prepare_sec = 0.0
    _car_v65_31_water_spatial_build_sec = 0.0
    if (
        VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR"
        and CAR_SPECIAL_MANAGEMENT_ROAD_ENABLED
        and water_union is not None
    ):
        _water_spatial_t0 = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            from shapely.prepared import prep as _shapely_prep

            _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84, _water_buffer_cache_hit = _load_cached_water_buffer(
                download_polygon,
                CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_BUFFER_M,
            )

            if not _water_buffer_cache_hit:
                _water_buffer_t0 = time.perf_counter()
                _water_gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(geometry=[water_union], crs="EPSG:4326")
                _water_buffer_geom = (
                    _water_gdf
                    .to_crs(DERIVED_WATER_BUFFER_SOURCE_CRS)
                    .geometry.iloc[0]
                    .buffer(float(CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_BUFFER_M))
                )
                _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84 = (
                    gpd.GeoDataFrame(geometry=[_water_buffer_geom], crs=DERIVED_WATER_BUFFER_SOURCE_CRS)
                    .to_crs(DERIVED_WATER_BUFFER_OUTPUT_CRS)
                    .geometry.iloc[0]
                )
                _car_v65_31_water_buffer_build_sec = time.perf_counter() - _water_buffer_t0
                _save_cached_water_buffer(
                    _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84,
                    download_polygon,
                    CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_BUFFER_M,
                )
            else:
                _car_v65_31_water_buffer_build_sec = 0.0

            _prepare_t0 = time.perf_counter()
            _car_v64_water_prepared = _shapely_prep(_car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84)
            _car_v65_31_water_prepare_sec = time.perf_counter() - _prepare_t0

            print(
                f"汽車防汛道路水體判斷：啟用，鄰近距離 "
                f"{CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_BUFFER_M:g} m | "
                f"既有向量化水體幾何判斷 | "
                f"衍生快取={'命中' if _water_buffer_cache_hit else '新建'}"
            )
        except Exception as _e:
            _car_v64_water_prepared = None
            _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84 = None
            _car_v65_31_water_buffer_build_sec = 0.0
            _car_v65_31_water_prepare_sec = 0.0
            try:
                _water_buffer_t0 = time.perf_counter()
                _water_gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(geometry=[water_union], crs="EPSG:4326")
                _water_buffer_geom = (
                    _water_gdf
                    .to_crs(DERIVED_WATER_BUFFER_SOURCE_CRS)
                    .geometry.iloc[0]
                    .buffer(float(CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_BUFFER_M))
                )
                _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84 = (
                    gpd.GeoDataFrame(geometry=[_water_buffer_geom], crs=DERIVED_WATER_BUFFER_SOURCE_CRS)
                    .to_crs(DERIVED_WATER_BUFFER_OUTPUT_CRS)
                    .geometry.iloc[0]
                )
                _car_v65_31_water_buffer_build_sec = time.perf_counter() - _water_buffer_t0
                _car_v64_water_prepared = _shapely_prep(_car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84)
                _save_cached_water_buffer(
                    _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84,
                    download_polygon,
                    CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_BUFFER_M,
                )
            except Exception:
                _car_v64_water_prepared = None
                _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84 = None
            print(
                "汽車防汛道路水體判斷：建立 cached/vectorized prepared geometry 失敗 → "
                "僅使用 OSM 標籤／名稱判斷 | "
                f"原因：{_e!r}"
            )
        finally:
            _car_v65_31_water_spatial_build_sec = time.perf_counter() - _water_spatial_t0
    elif VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and CAR_SPECIAL_MANAGEMENT_ROAD_ENABLED:
        print("汽車防汛道路水體判斷：沒有可用的水路／水體圖形資料，改用 OSM 標籤／道路名稱判斷")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.17：把原本獨立的「183k edge 水體／防汛預掃描」併入交通工具
    # edge processing。規則完全不變，只避免同一張 Graph 額外再掃一次。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    _v64_special_management_edge_count = 0
    _v64_special_water_adjacent_edge_count = 0
    _v64_candidate_rows = []

    # 本階段只保留必要計算；不在執行畫面輸出效能測試資訊。
    _perf_edge_t0 = time.perf_counter()

    # ============================================================
    # 13. 建立交通工具對應的 edge weight
    # ============================================================

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
        print("建立機車道路權重")
    else:
        print("建立汽車道路權重（含道路適用性分級）")
    print("=" * 70)

    removed_edges = 0
    weighted_edges = 0
    edges_to_remove = []
    car_removed_grade_counts = defaultdict(int)
    car_removed_length_m = defaultdict(float)
    car_removed_reason_counts = defaultdict(int)
    car_removed_motorcycle_only_count = 0
    car_removed_motorcycle_only_length_m = 0.0
    car_removed_v64_special_count = 0
    car_removed_v64_special_length_m = 0.0

    motor_removed_fastroad_count = 0
    motor_removed_fastroad_length_m = 0.0
    motor_removed_fastroad_reason_counts = defaultdict(int)
    motor_retained_trunk_count = 0
    motor_retained_trunk_length_m = 0.0
    motor_retained_trunk_name_counts = defaultdict(float)
    motor_retained_trunk_ref_counts = defaultdict(float)

    manual_forbidden_edges = 0
    manual_avoid_edges = 0
    manual_forbidden_length_m = 0.0
    manual_avoid_length_m = 0.0

    # V65.17：建立權重的同一個 edge pass 同步累計 CAR 道路適用性診斷，
    # 後面不再為了建立診斷表重新掃整張 G。
    _car_diag_total_edges_seen = 0
    _car_diag_grade_counts = defaultdict(int)
    _car_diag_grade_length = defaultdict(float)
    _car_diag_reason_counts = defaultdict(int)

    # v65.32：一次收集所有 service/track edge 幾何，
    # 直接在原 EPSG:4326 buffer 上使用 Shapely 2 vectorized intersects。
    # 判定幾何與 v65.28 完全相同，僅把逐 edge Python 呼叫改為批次 ufunc。
    _car_v65_31_water_adjacent_keys = set()
    _car_v65_31_water_collect_sec = 0.0
    _car_v65_31_water_vectorized_sec = 0.0
    _car_v65_31_water_lookup_sec = 0.0
    if (
        VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR"
        and CAR_SPECIAL_MANAGEMENT_ROAD_ENABLED
        and _car_v64_water_prepared is not None
        and _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84 is not None
    ):
        _collect_t0 = time.perf_counter()
        _water_candidate_geoms = []
        _water_candidate_keys = []
        for _wu, _wv, _wk, _wd in G.edges(keys=True, data=True):
            _highway_raw = tag_string(_wd.get("highway"))
            if _highway_raw not in CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_HIGHWAY_TYPES:
                continue
            _wgeom = _wd.get("geometry")
            if _wgeom is None:
                _wgeom = _edge_geometry_lonlat(G, _wu, _wv, _wd)
            if _wgeom is not None:
                _water_candidate_geoms.append(_wgeom)
                _water_candidate_keys.append((_wu, _wv, _wk))
        _car_v65_31_water_collect_sec = time.perf_counter() - _collect_t0

        if _water_candidate_geoms:
            _vector_t0 = time.perf_counter()
            _query_arr_wgs84 = np.asarray(_water_candidate_geoms, dtype=object)
            # 使用與原 v65.28 相同的 prepared buffer 幾何；Shapely 2 ufunc 會直接利用 prepared state。
            _hit_mask = np.asarray(
                shapely.intersects(_car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84, _query_arr_wgs84),
                dtype=bool,
            )
            _hit_positions = np.flatnonzero(_hit_mask)
            _car_v65_31_water_vectorized_sec = time.perf_counter() - _vector_t0

            _lookup_t0 = time.perf_counter()
            if _hit_positions.size:
                _car_v65_31_water_adjacent_keys = {
                    _water_candidate_keys[int(_idx)] for _idx in _hit_positions
                }
            _car_v65_31_water_lookup_sec = time.perf_counter() - _lookup_t0

    # V65.63.12：預先找出「台66命名平面側車道走廊」節點。
    # 只有同一命名側車道同時存在「進入」與「繼續出去」的方向，才視為
    # 可延續走廊；之後若從該節點轉入 tertiary 以下道路，才加 75m 離開成本。
    _motor_side_corridor_continuation_names = defaultdict(set)
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" and MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_EXIT_PENALTY_ENABLED:
        _side_in_names = defaultdict(set)
        _side_out_names = defaultdict(set)
        for _su, _sv, _sk, _sa in G.edges(keys=True, data=True):
            _shw = tag_string(_sa.get("highway"))
            _sref = tag_string(_sa.get("ref"))
            if (
                _sref in MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_ROAD_REFS
                and _motor_fastroad_side_road_name(_sa)
                and _shw not in {"motorway", "motorway_link", "trunk", "trunk_link"}
            ):
                _snm = _edge_name_text(_sa) or ""
                if _snm:
                    _side_out_names[int(_su)].add(_snm)
                    _side_in_names[int(_sv)].add(_snm)
        for _node in set(_side_in_names) | set(_side_out_names):
            _common = _side_in_names.get(_node, set()) & _side_out_names.get(_node, set())
            if _common:
                _motor_side_corridor_continuation_names[int(_node)] = set(_common)

        print(
            f"MOTOR 台66側車道走廊離開成本：啟用 | "
            f"固定等效成本={MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_EXIT_PENALTY_M:.0f}m | "
            f"可延續走廊節點={len(_motor_side_corridor_continuation_names):,}"
        )

    motor_side_exit_penalty_edge_count = 0
    motor_side_exit_penalty_length_m = 0.0
    motor_side_exit_penalty_by_highway = defaultdict(lambda: [0, 0.0])

    for u, v, k, data in G.edges(
        keys=True,
        data=True
    ):

        manual_rule_ids = CAR_MANUAL_EDGE_CONTROL.get((u, v, k), []) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else []
        manual_actions = {
            CAR_MANUAL_CONTROLS[rid - 1]["action"]
            for rid in manual_rule_ids
            if 0 < rid <= len(CAR_MANUAL_CONTROLS)
        }
        manual_forbidden = "禁止" in manual_actions
        manual_avoid = (not manual_forbidden) and ("避免" in manual_actions)

        data["car_manual_control"] = "禁止" if manual_forbidden else ("避免" if manual_avoid else "")
        data["car_manual_rule_ids"] = ";".join(map(str, manual_rule_ids)) if manual_rule_ids else ""
        data["car_manual_avoid_factor"] = CAR_MANUAL_AVOID_FACTOR if manual_avoid else 1.0

        _special_reason = ""
        suitability_check = None
        if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
            _st = time.perf_counter()
            _car_tags = _car_tag_bundle(data)
        else:
            _car_tags = None

        if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and CAR_SPECIAL_MANAGEMENT_ROAD_ENABLED:
            # V65.17：原本這段在主 edge processing 前獨立掃一次全圖；
            # 現在就在同一次 edge pass 完成，結果規則不變。
            data["_car_v64_water_adjacent"] = False
            data["_car_v64_special_management_reason"] = ""
            _highway = _car_tags.get("highway", "")

            if (
                _car_v64_water_prepared is not None
                and _highway in CAR_SPECIAL_WATER_ADJACENT_HIGHWAY_TYPES
            ):
                _st = time.perf_counter()
                try:
                    if _car_v65_31_water_buffer_wgs84 is not None:
                        _water_hit = (u, v, k) in _car_v65_31_water_adjacent_keys
                    else:
                        _geom = data.get("geometry")
                        if _geom is None:
                            _geom = _edge_geometry_lonlat(G, u, v, data)
                        _water_hit = bool(
                            _geom is not None
                            and _car_v64_water_prepared is not None
                            and _car_v64_water_prepared.intersects(_geom)
                        )
                    if _water_hit:
                        data["_car_v64_water_adjacent"] = True
                        _v64_special_water_adjacent_edge_count += 1
                except Exception:
                    pass
                _car_v65_31_water_lookup_sec += time.perf_counter() - _st

            _special_reason = _car_special_management_road_reason(data, _car_tags)
            if _special_reason:
                data["_car_v64_special_management_reason"] = _special_reason
                _v64_special_management_edge_count += 1

            # 只算一次 suitability；後面直接沿用，不再重跑。
            _st = time.perf_counter()
            suitability_check = car_road_suitability(data, special_reason=_special_reason, parsed_tags=_car_tags)

        if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
            _motor_fastroad_reason = _motor_fastroad_restriction_reason(data)
            forbidden = is_explicitly_forbidden_motor(data)
            if _motor_fastroad_reason:
                _removed_len = float(data.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
                motor_removed_fastroad_count += 1
                motor_removed_fastroad_length_m += _removed_len
                motor_removed_fastroad_reason_counts[_motor_fastroad_reason] += 1
                data["motor_restriction_reason"] = _motor_fastroad_reason
            else:
                data["motor_restriction_reason"] = ""
                _motor_highway = tag_string(data.get("highway"))
                if _motor_highway in MOTOR_CONDITIONAL_FASTROAD_HIGHWAY_TYPES:
                    motor_retained_trunk_count += 1
                    _retained_len = float(data.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
                    motor_retained_trunk_length_m += _retained_len
                    _retained_name = _edge_name_text(data) or "（未命名）"
                    motor_retained_trunk_name_counts[_retained_name] += _retained_len
                    _retained_ref = tag_string(data.get("ref")) or "（無ref）"
                    motor_retained_trunk_ref_counts[_retained_ref] += _retained_len
        else:
            if suitability_check is None:
                suitability_check = car_road_suitability(data, special_reason=_special_reason, parsed_tags=_car_tags)

            # 人工「禁止」優先於所有 OSM 分級。
            if manual_forbidden:
                forbidden = True
                manual_forbidden_edges += 1
                manual_forbidden_length_m += float(data.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
            else:
                forbidden = suitability_check["grade"] == "D"

            if forbidden:
                if manual_forbidden:
                    car_removed_grade_counts["人工禁止"] += 1
                    _removed_len = float(data.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
                    car_removed_length_m["人工禁止"] += _removed_len
                    car_removed_reason_counts["人工設定：禁止"] += 1
                else:
                    car_removed_grade_counts[suitability_check["grade"]] += 1
                    _removed_len = float(data.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
                    car_removed_length_m[suitability_check["grade"]] += _removed_len
                    car_removed_reason_counts[suitability_check["reason"]] += 1
                    if bool(suitability_check.get("explicit_motorcycle_only", False)):
                        car_removed_motorcycle_only_count += 1
                        car_removed_motorcycle_only_length_m += _removed_len
                    if bool(suitability_check.get("v64_special_management", False)):
                        car_removed_v64_special_count += 1
                        car_removed_v64_special_length_m += _removed_len

        if forbidden:
            edges_to_remove.append((u, v, k))
            continue

        length = data.get("length")
        if length is None:
            continue

        _st_weight = None
        if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
            factor = edge_factor_motor(data)
            _motor_side_exit_penalty_m = 0.0

            # V65.63.12：只對「仍可沿同名台66側車道繼續」的節點，
            # 且即將轉入 tertiary 以下道路的出口，加一次固定 75m 等效成本。
            # 主要道路（secondary / primary）仍不處罰；台66側車道本身也不處罰。
            if (
                MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_EXIT_PENALTY_ENABLED
                and int(u) in _motor_side_corridor_continuation_names
                and tag_string(data.get("highway")) in MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_EXIT_LOWER_CLASS_TYPES
            ):
                _motor_side_exit_penalty_m = float(MOTOR_FASTROAD_SIDE_EXIT_PENALTY_M)
                motor_side_exit_penalty_edge_count += 1
                motor_side_exit_penalty_length_m += float(length)
                _motor_side_exit_penalty_hw = tag_string(data.get("highway")) or "(未標示)"
                motor_side_exit_penalty_by_highway[_motor_side_exit_penalty_hw][0] += 1
                motor_side_exit_penalty_by_highway[_motor_side_exit_penalty_hw][1] += float(length)

            data["motor_side_corridor_exit_penalty_m"] = float(_motor_side_exit_penalty_m)
            data["route_weight"] = float(length) * float(factor) + float(_motor_side_exit_penalty_m)
            data["car_suitability_grade"] = "N/A"
            data["car_suitability_label"] = "不適用（機車模式）"
            data["car_suitability_reason"] = ""
            data["car_suitability_factor"] = 1.0
            data["car_explicit_motorcycle_only"] = False
            travel_speed = MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH
        else:
            # 汽車：道路長度 × 汽車道路適用性倍率。
            # 明確禁止（D）已在前面排除；C/B 級不直接砍掉，而是讓替代道路優先。
            suitability = suitability_check
            suitability_factor = float(suitability["factor"])
            if manual_avoid:
                suitability_factor *= CAR_MANUAL_AVOID_FACTOR
                manual_avoid_edges += 1
                manual_avoid_length_m += float(data.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
                manual_reason_suffix = f" + 人工設定：避免（×{CAR_MANUAL_AVOID_FACTOR:g}）"
            else:
                manual_reason_suffix = ""
            data["car_suitability_grade"] = suitability["grade"]
            data["car_suitability_label"] = suitability["label"]
            data["car_suitability_reason"] = suitability["reason"] + manual_reason_suffix
            data["car_suitability_factor"] = suitability_factor
            data["car_explicit_motorcycle_only"] = bool(suitability["explicit_motorcycle_only"])
            travel_speed = _car_road_speed_kmh(
                data,
                CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH,
                CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH,
                CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH,
            )

        data["route_distance"] = float(length)
        data["travel_speed_kmh"] = float(travel_speed)
        data["travel_time_min"] = float(length) / 1000.0 / float(travel_speed) * 60.0

        if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
            # V65.17：同一 pass 同步累計後段診斷需要的統計。
            _car_diag_total_edges_seen += 1
            _diag_grade = str(data.get("car_suitability_grade", "A"))
            _diag_length = float(data.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
            _diag_reason = str(data.get("car_suitability_reason", ""))
            _car_diag_grade_counts[_diag_grade] += 1
            _car_diag_grade_length[_diag_grade] += _diag_length
            _car_diag_reason_counts[_diag_reason] += 1

            # V49：汽車實際路線成本統一改為「旅行時間 × 適用性倍率」。
            # 以汽車平面道路速度為等效距離基準，讓高速／快速道路依各自設定速度
            # 在路線最佳化中自然具有時間優勢，而不是只有事後診斷才看到。
            # 單位：equivalent meter at CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH。
            data["route_weight"] = (
                float(data["travel_time_min"])
                * float(CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH)
                / 60.0
                * 1000.0
                * suitability_factor
            )
        weighted_edges += 1



    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and CAR_SPECIAL_MANAGEMENT_ROAD_ENABLED:
        print(
            f"水體／防汛道路處理：鄰水 service/track={_v64_special_water_adjacent_edge_count:,} edge | "
            f"實際排除={car_removed_v64_special_count:,} edge | "
            f"{car_removed_v64_special_length_m / 1000.0:.2f} km"
        )

    for u, v, k in edges_to_remove:

        if G.has_edge(
            u,
            v,
            k
        ):

            G.remove_edge(
                u,
                v,
                k
            )

            removed_edges += 1


    print(
        "排除道路：",
        removed_edges
    )

    print(
        "保留加權 edges：",
        weighted_edges
    )

    print(
        "目前節點數：",
        len(G.nodes)
    )

    print(
        "目前道路段數：",
        len(G.edges)
    )

    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
        print(
            f"人工 CAR 路段控制：禁止={manual_forbidden_edges:,} edge / "
            f"{manual_forbidden_length_m/1000:.2f} km；"
            f"避免={manual_avoid_edges:,} edge / {manual_avoid_length_m/1000:.2f} km"
        )
        _grade_counter = defaultdict(int)
        _motorcycle_only_counter = 0
        for _, _, _, _attrs in G.edges(keys=True, data=True):
            _grade_counter[str(_attrs.get("car_suitability_grade", "A"))] += 1
            if bool(_attrs.get("car_explicit_motorcycle_only", False)):
                _motorcycle_only_counter += 1
        print(
            "保留後汽車道路適用性："
            f"A={_grade_counter.get('A', 0):,} | "
            f"B={_grade_counter.get('B', 0):,} | "
            f"C={_grade_counter.get('C', 0):,}"
        )
        print(
            f"已排除汽車D級 道路段：{car_removed_grade_counts.get('D', 0):,}"
            f"（其中明確符合『汽車禁止＋機車明確允許』樣式："
            f"{car_removed_motorcycle_only_count:,}）"
        )
        print(
            f"疑似水利／防汛／堤防管理道路額外排除："
            f"{car_removed_v64_special_count:,} edge | "
            f"{car_removed_v64_special_length_m / 1000.0:.2f} km"
        )
        print(
            f"保留圖上標示疑似機車優先、但汽車未明確禁止 道路段：{_motorcycle_only_counter:,}"
        )



    # ============================================================
    # 14. 移除沒有道路的 isolated nodes
    # ============================================================

    isolated = [
        n
        for n in G.nodes
        if G.degree(n) == 0
    ]

    if isolated:
        G.remove_nodes_from(
            isolated
        )

    print(
        "移除沒有道路連接的節點：",
        len(isolated)
    )


    # ============================================================
    # 15. 找最大弱連通 component
    # ============================================================

    if not nx.is_empty(G):

        components = list(
            nx.weakly_connected_components(
                G
            )
        )

        if components:

            largest = max(
                components,
                key=len
            )

            print(
                "最大連通區塊節點數：",
                len(largest),
                "nodes"
            )

            # 不直接砍掉其他 components。
            # 地籍點可能散在不同區域。
            # snap 後會檢查每個點是否位於同 component。


    _perf_mark("edge_weighting_and_car_suitability", _perf_edge_t0, emit=True)

    # ============================================================
    # 16. V65.49：道路 edge-first snap＋道路末端誤吸附修正
    # ============================================================
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("將地籍點接到「最近道路段的實際位置」，避免只接道路端點造成超過 100 公尺的偏移")
    print("=" * 70)

    # 保留 v65.48 的 shared snap 作為「舊版基準」診斷，不再直接拿它做本模式路線 anchor。
    if shared_context is not None and "shared_nearest_nodes" in shared_context:
        old_nearest_nodes = np.asarray(
            shared_context["shared_nearest_nodes"], dtype=np.int64
        ).copy()
        old_snap_distances = np.asarray(
            shared_context["shared_snap_distances"], dtype=float
        ).copy()
        _shared_snap_ready = True
        print("沿用 BOTH 共用道路接點，只拿來與既有基準比較。")
    else:
        old_nearest_nodes, old_snap_distances = ox.distance.nearest_nodes(
            G,
            X=coords[:, 0],
            Y=coords[:, 1],
            return_dist=True,
        )
        old_nearest_nodes = np.asarray(old_nearest_nodes, dtype=np.int64)
        old_snap_distances = np.asarray(old_snap_distances, dtype=float)
        _shared_snap_ready = False
        print("沒有共用道路接點，先建立既有基準最近道路節點作為比較基準。")

    # 建立本模式 graph 的 node KDTree；注意此時 CAR 已完成禁行 edge 移除，
    # 所以 CAR 與 MOTOR 會各自依自己的實際道路圖重新判定道路接點。
    graph_nodes = list(G.nodes)
    graph_node_xy = np.array([
        lonlat_to_xy(float(G.nodes[n]["x"]), float(G.nodes[n]["y"]))
        for n in graph_nodes
    ])
    node_tree = cKDTree(graph_node_xy)

    def _edge_geometry_xy(u, v, attrs):
        geom = _edge_geometry_lonlat(G, int(u), int(v), attrs)
        if geom is None or geom.is_empty:
            return LineString([
                lonlat_to_xy(float(G.nodes[u]["x"]), float(G.nodes[u]["y"])),
                lonlat_to_xy(float(G.nodes[v]["x"]), float(G.nodes[v]["y"])),
            ])
        return LineString([
            lonlat_to_xy(float(x), float(y))
            for x, y in geom.coords
        ])

    def _edge_length_m(u, v, attrs):
        try:
            val = float(attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val > 0:
                return val
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            gxy = _edge_geometry_xy(u, v, attrs)
            return float(gxy.length)
        except Exception:
            return 0.0

    def _find_manual_motor_ingress_edge(lon, lat):
        """取得人工指定 MOTOR 進場座標附近的最近 OSM 道路段。"""
        try:
            raw_edge, raw_dist = ox.distance.nearest_edges(
                G, X=[float(lon)], Y=[float(lat)], return_dist=True
            )
            norm = _normalize_nearest_edge_ids(raw_edge, 1)
            d = float(np.asarray(raw_dist, dtype=float).reshape(-1)[0])
            if not np.isfinite(d) or d > float(SNAP_MOTOR_MANUAL_INGRESS_MAX_OSM_DISTANCE_M):
                return None
            eu, ev, ek = map(int, norm[0])
            attrs = G.get_edge_data(eu, ev, ek)
            if attrs is None:
                return None
            return {
                "edge": (eu, ev, ek, attrs),
                "osm_distance_m": d,
            }
        except Exception:
            return None

    def _add_manual_motor_exact_anchor(i, lon, lat, edge_info):
        """建立人工指定 MOTOR 精確進場節點，並以短連接線接回 OSM 道路投影點。

        人工指定座標與 OSM 最近道路若有數公尺誤差，不把兩者視為同一個位置；
        而是保留人工座標為真正的路線節點，再以實際偏移距離建立短連接線。
        這樣地圖幾何與路線距離都能如實反映人工指定的進場位置。
        """
        if not SNAP_MOTOR_MANUAL_INGRESS_EXACT_ANCHOR_ENABLED or edge_info is None:
            return None
        try:
            eu, ev, ek, eattrs = edge_info["edge"]
            osm_dist = float(edge_info.get("osm_distance_m", float("nan")))
            if (not np.isfinite(osm_dist)) or osm_dist > float(SNAP_MOTOR_MANUAL_INGRESS_MAX_OSM_DISTANCE_M):
                return None

            # 取得 OSM 道路上的最近投影位置。
            proj = _project_point_to_edge_m(
                int(eu), int(ev), eattrs, float(lon), float(lat)
            )
            projection_offset_m = float(proj.get("distance_m", float("inf")))
            projection_ll = proj.get("projection", (float("nan"), float("nan")))
            if (
                not np.isfinite(projection_offset_m)
                or projection_offset_m > float(SNAP_MOTOR_MANUAL_INGRESS_MAX_OSM_DISTANCE_M)
                or len(projection_ll) < 2
                or not np.isfinite(float(projection_ll[0]))
                or not np.isfinite(float(projection_ll[1]))
            ):
                return None

            # 先建立「道路投影節點」，再建立「人工指定精確節點」。
            projected_node = VIRTUAL_NODE_BASE - 2000000 - int(i)
            while projected_node in G:
                projected_node -= 1000000
            exact_node = VIRTUAL_NODE_BASE - 1000000 - int(i)
            while exact_node in G or exact_node == projected_node:
                exact_node -= 1000000

            proj_lon = float(projection_ll[0])
            proj_lat = float(projection_ll[1])
            G.add_node(
                int(projected_node),
                x=proj_lon,
                y=proj_lat,
                osmid=int(projected_node),
                virtual_snap_anchor=True,
                virtual_manual_ingress_projection_node=True,
                virtual_source_index=int(i),
                virtual_access_distance_m=float(projection_offset_m),
                virtual_source_edge=(int(eu), int(ev), int(ek)),
                virtual_source_fraction=float(proj.get("frac", 0.5)),
            )
            G.add_node(
                int(exact_node),
                x=float(lon),
                y=float(lat),
                osmid=int(exact_node),
                virtual_manual_ingress_anchor=True,
                virtual_source_index=int(i),
                manual_ingress_lon=float(lon),
                manual_ingress_lat=float(lat),
                manual_ingress_projection_offset_m=float(projection_offset_m),
            )

            split_edges = []

            def _split_edge_to_projected_node(_u, _v, _k, _attrs):
                _proj = _project_point_to_edge_m(
                    int(_u), int(_v), _attrs, float(lon), float(lat)
                )
                _off = float(_proj.get("distance_m", float("inf")))
                if not np.isfinite(_off) or _off > float(SNAP_MOTOR_MANUAL_INGRESS_CONNECT_RADIUS_M):
                    return None
                _frac = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(_proj.get("frac", 0.5))))
                _left = list(_proj.get("left", []))
                _right = list(_proj.get("right", []))
                def _fro_manual_polyline_len_m(_coords):
                    try:
                        _xy = [lonlat_to_xy(float(_p[0]), float(_p[1])) for _p in _coords]
                        return sum(math.hypot(float(b[0])-float(a[0]), float(b[1])-float(a[1]))
                                   for a, b in zip(_xy[:-1], _xy[1:]))
                    except Exception:
                        return float("inf")
                _fro_left_len_m = _fro_manual_polyline_len_m(_left)
                _fro_right_len_m = _fro_manual_polyline_len_m(_right)
                # Important: this check is before G.remove_edge(). Returning None preserves
                # the original road and lets the caller's established fallback/cleanup run.
                if (len(_left) < 2 or len(_right) < 2
                        or _fro_left_len_m <= 0.05
                        or _fro_right_len_m <= 0.05):
                    return None
                try:
                    G.remove_edge(int(_u), int(_v), int(_k))
                except Exception:
                    return None
                _a1 = _scale_edge_attrs(_attrs, _frac, _left)
                _a2 = _scale_edge_attrs(_attrs, 1.0 - _frac, _right)
                _a1["manual_ingress_split_edge"] = True
                _a2["manual_ingress_split_edge"] = True
                _a1["manual_ingress_source_edge"] = (int(_u), int(_v), int(_k))
                _a2["manual_ingress_source_edge"] = (int(_u), int(_v), int(_k))
                G.add_edge(int(_u), int(projected_node), **dict(_a1))
                G.add_edge(int(projected_node), int(_v), **dict(_a2))
                return {"edge": (int(_u), int(_v), int(_k)), "offset_m": _off, "frac": _frac}

            # 切分目前選中的最近道路；反向道路一併處理。
            forward_result = _split_edge_to_projected_node(int(eu), int(ev), int(ek), eattrs)
            if forward_result is None:
                if G.has_node(int(projected_node)):
                    G.remove_node(int(projected_node))
                if G.has_node(int(exact_node)):
                    G.remove_node(int(exact_node))
                return None
            split_edges.append(forward_result)

            if G.has_edge(int(ev), int(eu), int(ek)):
                _rev_attrs = G.get_edge_data(int(ev), int(eu), int(ek))
                if _rev_attrs is not None:
                    reverse_result = _split_edge_to_projected_node(
                        int(ev), int(eu), int(ek), dict(_rev_attrs)
                    )
                    if reverse_result is not None:
                        split_edges.append(reverse_result)

            # 人工指定節點與 OSM 投影節點之間建立極短雙向連接。
            connector_geom = LineString([
                (float(proj_lon), float(proj_lat)),
                (float(lon), float(lat)),
            ])
            connector_len = float(max(projection_offset_m, 0.0))
            base_speed = float(eattrs.get("travel_speed_kmh", MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH) or MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH)
            if not np.isfinite(base_speed) or base_speed <= 0:
                base_speed = float(MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH)
            connector_time = connector_len / 1000.0 / base_speed * 60.0
            connector_attrs = {
                "length": connector_len,
                "route_distance": connector_len,
                "travel_time_min": connector_time,
                "route_weight": connector_len,
                "travel_speed_kmh": base_speed,
                "geometry": connector_geom,
                "manual_ingress_connector": True,
                "manual_ingress_source_edge": (int(eu), int(ev), int(ek)),
            }
            G.add_edge(int(projected_node), int(exact_node), **dict(connector_attrs))
            G.add_edge(int(exact_node), int(projected_node), **dict(connector_attrs))

            return {
                "exact_node": int(exact_node),
                "projected_node": int(projected_node),
                "source_edge": (int(eu), int(ev), int(ek)),
                "projection_lon": float(lon),
                "projection_lat": float(lat),
                "projection_offset_m": float(projection_offset_m),
                "connector_length_m": float(connector_len),
                "osm_distance_m": float(osm_dist),
                "highway": str(eattrs.get("highway", "")),
                "name": str(eattrs.get("name", "")),
                "split_edges": split_edges,
                "travel_speed_kmh": float(base_speed),
            }
        except Exception:
            return None


    def _collect_nearby_road_diagnostics(px, py, origin_edge=None):
        """找出案件附近主要道路候選，供 MOTOR 主要道路進場判斷與結果表使用。"""
        if not SNAP_NEARBY_ROAD_DIAGNOSTIC_ENABLED:
            return {
                "nearby_major_distance_m": float("nan"),
                "nearby_major_edge": "",
                "nearby_major_name": "",
                "nearby_road_summary": "",
            }
        try:
            radius = float(SNAP_NEARBY_ROAD_DIAGNOSTIC_RADIUS_M)
            k = min(int(SNAP_NEARBY_ROAD_DIAGNOSTIC_K), len(graph_nodes))
            if k <= 0:
                return {
                    "nearby_major_distance_m": float("nan"),
                    "nearby_major_edge": "",
                    "nearby_major_name": "",
                    "nearby_road_summary": "",
                }
            dists, poss = node_tree.query([px, py], k=k)
            dists = np.atleast_1d(dists)
            poss = np.atleast_1d(poss)
            nearby_nodes = []
            for d, pos in zip(dists, poss):
                if not np.isfinite(float(d)) or float(d) > radius:
                    continue
                try:
                    nearby_nodes.append(int(graph_nodes[int(pos)]))
                except Exception:
                    continue

            rows = []
            seen = set()
            origin_pair = None
            if origin_edge is not None:
                try:
                    origin_pair = frozenset((int(origin_edge[0]), int(origin_edge[1])))
                except Exception:
                    origin_pair = None

            for n in nearby_nodes:
                # V65.64.13：附近道路診斷不能讀取前面案件建立的 virtual split edge；
                # 否則即使 KDTree 只含真實 OSM node，incident edge 仍可能把前案的投影切線
                # 誤認為本案附近的主要道路候選。
                for (u, v, kedge), attrs in _incident_edges_unique(n):
                    if bool(attrs.get("virtual_split_edge", False)):
                        continue
                    try:
                        if bool(G.nodes[int(u)].get("virtual_snap_anchor", False) or G.nodes[int(u)].get("virtual_manual_ingress_anchor", False)):
                            continue
                        if bool(G.nodes[int(v)].get("virtual_snap_anchor", False) or G.nodes[int(v)].get("virtual_manual_ingress_anchor", False)):
                            continue
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                    pair_key = (frozenset((int(u), int(v))), int(kedge))
                    if pair_key in seen:
                        continue
                    seen.add(pair_key)
                    try:
                        geom = _edge_geometry_xy(int(u), int(v), attrs)
                        dist_m = float(Point(px, py).distance(geom))
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                    if not np.isfinite(dist_m) or dist_m > radius:
                        continue
                    vals = _edge_highway_values(attrs)
                    if vals & SNAP_MAIN_ROAD_INGRESS_HIGHWAY_TYPES:
                        road_group = "主要道路"
                    elif vals & SNAP_BRANCH_LOW_CLASS_TYPES:
                        road_group = "低階道路"
                    else:
                        road_group = "其他道路"
                    name = str(attrs.get("name", "") or "")
                    hw = "/".join(sorted(vals)) if vals else "-"
                    direct = "原最近道路相同路段" if origin_pair is not None and frozenset((int(u), int(v))) == origin_pair else ""
                    rows.append({
                        "dist_m": dist_m,
                        "u": int(u), "v": int(v), "k": int(kedge),
                        "highway": hw, "name": name,
                        "group": road_group,
                        "direct": direct,
                    })

            rows.sort(key=lambda r: (float(r["dist_m"]), r["group"], r["highway"], r["u"], r["v"], r["k"]))
            major_rows = [r for r in rows if r["group"] == "主要道路"]
            top_major = major_rows[0] if major_rows else None

            summary_parts = []
            for idx, r in enumerate(rows[:int(SNAP_NEARBY_ROAD_DIAGNOSTIC_MAX_ROWS)], start=1):
                nm = r["name"] if r["name"] else "未命名"
                summary_parts.append(
                    f"{idx}:{r['group']}:{r['highway']}:{nm}:{r['dist_m']:.1f}m"
                )

            return {
                "nearby_major_distance_m": float(top_major["dist_m"]) if top_major else float("nan"),
                "nearby_major_edge": (f"({top_major['u']},{top_major['v']},{top_major['k']})" if top_major else ""),
                "nearby_major_edge_tuple": ((int(top_major["u"]), int(top_major["v"]), int(top_major["k"])) if top_major else None),
                "nearby_major_name": (str(top_major["name"]) if top_major else ""),
                "nearby_road_summary": "；".join(summary_parts),
            }
        except Exception:
            return {
                "nearby_major_distance_m": float("nan"),
                "nearby_major_edge": "",
                "nearby_major_name": "",
                "nearby_road_summary": "",
            }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # 建立 dead-end branch edge set。
    # V65.50 加強：
    #   A. low-class 不只 service/track/path，也納入 residential/unclassified/living_street。
    #   B. 葉端→degree 2→junction 的短支路照舊標記。
    #   C. 若 OSM 拆成「兩端都是葉節點」的孤立短低階路段，也視為 dead-end candidate。
    #      這是為了涵蓋現場看得到道路、但 OSM 拓樸沒有真正接到另一條路的情況。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    dead_end_edges = set()
    if SNAP_DEAD_END_AVOID_ENABLED:
        ug = nx.Graph()
        for u, v, _k, _attrs in G.edges(keys=True, data=True):
            if int(u) == int(v):
                continue
            ug.add_edge(int(u), int(v))

        ug_degree = dict(ug.degree())

        def _pair_is_low_class(a_list):
            for _a in a_list:
                _hw = _a.get("highway")
                if isinstance(_hw, (list, tuple, set)):
                    _hw_values = {str(x).strip().lower() for x in _hw}
                elif _hw is None:
                    _hw_values = set()
                else:
                    _hw_values = {str(_hw).strip().lower()}
                if _hw_values & SNAP_LOW_CLASS_HIGHWAY_TYPES:
                    return True
            return False

        def _pair_is_major_highway(a_list):
            for _a in a_list:
                _hw = _a.get("highway")
                if isinstance(_hw, (list, tuple, set)):
                    _hw_values = {str(x).strip().lower() for x in _hw}
                elif _hw is None:
                    _hw_values = set()
                else:
                    _hw_values = {str(_hw).strip().lower()}
                if _hw_values & SNAP_DEAD_END_EXCLUDE_MAJOR_HIGHWAY_TYPES:
                    return True
            return False

        # 一般 leaf→junction chain。
        leaf_nodes = [n for n, d in ug_degree.items() if int(d) == 1]
        for leaf in leaf_nodes:
            prev = None
            cur = int(leaf)
            chain_edges = []
            chain_len = 0.0
            chain_has_low_class = False
            chain_has_major = False
            visited_nodes = {cur}

            while True:
                cur_degree = int(ug_degree.get(cur, 0))
                nbrs = [int(n) for n in ug.neighbors(cur) if int(n) != prev]
                if not nbrs:
                    break
                if cur_degree >= 3:
                    if (
                        chain_edges
                        and chain_len <= float(SNAP_DEAD_END_MAX_CHAIN_M)
                        and (
                            chain_has_low_class
                            or (SNAP_DEAD_END_ANY_CLASS_ENABLED and not chain_has_major)
                        )
                    ):
                        dead_end_edges.update(chain_edges)
                    break
                nxt = nbrs[0]
                if nxt in visited_nodes:
                    break

                pair_attrs = []
                if G.has_edge(cur, nxt):
                    pair_attrs.extend(list(G.get_edge_data(cur, nxt).values()))
                if G.has_edge(nxt, cur):
                    pair_attrs.extend(list(G.get_edge_data(nxt, cur).values()))
                step_len = min((_edge_length_m(cur, nxt, a) for a in pair_attrs), default=0.0)
                if _pair_is_low_class(pair_attrs):
                    chain_has_low_class = True
                if _pair_is_major_highway(pair_attrs):
                    chain_has_major = True
                chain_len += float(step_len)
                chain_edges.append(frozenset((cur, nxt)))
                visited_nodes.add(nxt)
                prev, cur = cur, nxt

        # OSM 拓樸未接上的短低階 component：兩端都是 degree=1。
        # 不直接把它們全部刪除，只標成 dead-end candidate，後續仍需附近有更合理道路才替換。
        for comp in nx.connected_components(ug):
            comp = set(comp)
            if len(comp) < 2:
                continue
            comp_degrees = [int(ug_degree.get(n, 0)) for n in comp]
            if not comp_degrees or max(comp_degrees) > 2:
                continue
            comp_edges = []
            comp_len = 0.0
            comp_low = False
            comp_major = False
            for u, v in ug.subgraph(comp).edges():
                pair_attrs = []
                if G.has_edge(u, v):
                    pair_attrs.extend(list(G.get_edge_data(u, v).values()))
                if G.has_edge(v, u):
                    pair_attrs.extend(list(G.get_edge_data(v, u).values()))
                comp_len += min((_edge_length_m(u, v, a) for a in pair_attrs), default=0.0)
                comp_low = comp_low or _pair_is_low_class(pair_attrs)
                comp_major = comp_major or _pair_is_major_highway(pair_attrs)
                comp_edges.append(frozenset((int(u), int(v))))
            if (
                comp_len <= float(SNAP_DEAD_END_MAX_CHAIN_M)
                and (comp_low or (SNAP_DEAD_END_ANY_CLASS_ENABLED and not comp_major))
            ):
                dead_end_edges.update(comp_edges)

    def _edge_is_dead_end(u, v, k):
        return frozenset((int(u), int(v))) in dead_end_edges

    def _normalize_road_text(value):
        """道路名稱正規化：全形英數轉半形、去除空白。"""
        if value is None:
            return ""
        try:
            if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
                return ";".join(_normalize_road_text(x) for x in value)
            s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", str(value)).strip()
            return re.sub(r"\s+", "", s)
        except Exception:
            return str(value).strip()

    def _parse_address_access_road(address):
        """解析門牌地址最後一段道路：路/街 + 段 + 巷 + 弄 + 衖。"""
        s = _normalize_road_text(address)
        if not s:
            return ""
        road_matches = list(re.finditer(r"[路街]", s))
        if not road_matches:
            return ""
        road_end = road_matches[-1].end()
        before_road = s[:road_end]
        boundaries = list(re.finditer(r"(?:區|鎮|鄉|市|里|村|鄰)", before_road))
        start = boundaries[-1].end() if boundaries else 0
        road = s[start:road_end]
        tail = s[road_end:]
        m = re.match(r"([一二三四五六七八九十百千0-9]+段)", tail)
        if m:
            road += m.group(1)
            tail = tail[m.end():]
        for unit in ("巷", "弄", "衖"):
            m = re.match(rf"([0-9]+{unit})", tail)
            if not m:
                break
            road += m.group(1)
            tail = tail[m.end():]
        return road

    def _find_address_road_edge(px, py, address_lon, address_lat, road_name):
        """尋找 OSM name 與地址最後一段道路完全一致的 edge。"""
        if not SNAP_ADDRESS_ROAD_PRIORITY_ENABLED:
            return None
        road_norm = _normalize_road_text(road_name)
        if not road_norm:
            return None
        try:
            if np.isfinite(float(address_lon)) and np.isfinite(float(address_lat)):
                qx, qy = lonlat_to_xy(float(address_lon), float(address_lat))
            else:
                qx, qy = float(px), float(py)
            k = min(int(SNAP_ADDRESS_ROAD_NODE_K), len(graph_nodes))
            if k <= 0:
                return None
            dists, poss = node_tree.query([qx, qy], k=k)
            dists = np.atleast_1d(dists)
            poss = np.atleast_1d(poss)
            nearby_nodes = [
                graph_nodes[int(pos)]
                for d, pos in zip(dists, poss)
                if np.isfinite(float(d)) and float(d) <= float(SNAP_ADDRESS_ROAD_SEARCH_RADIUS_M)
            ]
            candidates = []
            seen = set()
            for (eu, ev, ek), attrs in _incident_edge_candidates(nearby_nodes):
                key = (int(eu), int(ev), int(ek))
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                if not _is_real_osm_edge(eu, ev, attrs):
                    continue
                if _normalize_road_text(attrs.get("name", "")) != road_norm:
                    continue
                try:
                    edge_xy = _edge_geometry_xy(int(eu), int(ev), attrs)
                    d_edge = float(Point(qx, qy).distance(edge_xy))
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if np.isfinite(d_edge) and d_edge <= float(SNAP_ADDRESS_ROAD_MAX_EDGE_DISTANCE_M):
                    candidates.append((d_edge, int(eu), int(ev), int(ek), attrs))
            if not candidates:
                return None
            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1], x[2], x[3]))
            best = candidates[0]
            return {
                "edge": (best[1], best[2], best[3], best[4]),
                "distance_m": float(best[0]),
                "candidate_count": int(len(candidates)),
                "road_name": road_norm,
            }
        except Exception:
            return None

    # V65.61：在地址解析函式已建立後，逐筆解析「地址最後一段道路」。
    for _addr_i, _row in work_df.iterrows():
        _addr = _row.get("完整查詢地址", "")
        if not str(_addr or "").strip():
            _addr = _row.get("地址", "")
        if not str(_addr or "").strip():
            _addr = _row.get("命中地址", "")
        address_road_names[int(_addr_i)] = _parse_address_access_road(_addr)

    def _incident_edge_candidates(node_ids):
        seen = set()
        for nid in node_ids:
            nid = int(nid)
            try:
                for u, v, k, attrs in G.out_edges(nid, keys=True, data=True):
                    key = (int(u), int(v), int(k))
                    if key not in seen:
                        seen.add(key)
                        yield key, attrs
                for u, v, k, attrs in G.in_edges(nid, keys=True, data=True):
                    key = (int(u), int(v), int(k))
                    if key not in seen:
                        seen.add(key)
                        yield key, attrs
            except Exception:
                continue

    # OSMnx nearest_edges 是向量化最近 edge 查詢。
    # OSMnx 2.1.x 對多點查詢可能回傳 shape=(N,) 的 object array，
    # 每個元素本身是 (u, v, k) tuple；不能硬套成 (N, 3) 數值矩陣。
    # v65.49.1 對常見回傳形式全部正規化成 (N, 3) int64。
    def _normalize_nearest_edge_ids(raw, n_expected):
        if raw is None:
            raise ValueError("nearest_edges 回傳 None")

        # return_dist=False 時 raw 直接就是 edge IDs。
        arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=object)

        # 最常見：N 筆 object tuple -> shape=(N,)，每元素 (u,v,k)。
        if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size == n_expected:
            rows = []
            for item in arr.tolist():
                if isinstance(item, np.ndarray):
                    vals = item.reshape(-1).tolist()
                elif isinstance(item, (tuple, list)):
                    vals = list(item)
                else:
                    vals = []
                if len(vals) < 3:
                    raise ValueError(f"nearest_edges 單筆 edge ID 無法解析：{item!r}")
                rows.append((int(vals[0]), int(vals[1]), int(vals[2])))
            return np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64)

        # 少數情況：一般數值／object 二維矩陣 N×3。
        if arr.ndim == 2 and arr.shape == (n_expected, 3):
            return arr.astype(np.int64, copy=False)

        # 相反方向 3×N。
        if arr.ndim == 2 and arr.shape == (3, n_expected):
            return arr.T.astype(np.int64, copy=False)

        # 單筆也可能是 (u,v,k)。
        if n_expected == 1 and arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size == 3 and not any(
            isinstance(x, (tuple, list, np.ndarray)) for x in arr.tolist()
        ):
            return arr.reshape(1, 3).astype(np.int64, copy=False)

        raise ValueError(f"nearest_edges 回傳形狀／格式異常：shape={arr.shape}, dtype={arr.dtype}")

    nearest_edge_ids = None
    nearest_edge_distances = None
    address_nearest_edge_ids = None
    address_nearest_edge_distances = None
    if SNAP_EDGE_FIRST_ENABLED and len(coords) > 0:
        try:
            raw_edges, raw_edge_dist = ox.distance.nearest_edges(
                G,
                X=coords[:, 0],
                Y=coords[:, 1],
                return_dist=True,
            )
            nearest_edge_ids = _normalize_nearest_edge_ids(raw_edges, len(coords))
            raw_edge_distances = np.asarray(raw_edge_dist, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
            if raw_edge_distances.size != len(coords):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"nearest_edges 距離筆數異常：{raw_edge_distances.size} != {len(coords)}"
                )

            # 正式診斷一律以公尺化 edge geometry 計算距離，避免 CRS／OSMnx 版本造成單位誤讀。
            nearest_edge_distances = np.full(len(coords), np.nan, dtype=float)
            for _i, _eid in enumerate(nearest_edge_ids):
                try:
                    _eu, _ev, _ek = map(int, _eid)
                    _attrs = G.get_edge_data(_eu, _ev, _ek)
                    if _attrs is None:
                        continue
                    _edge_xy = _edge_geometry_xy(_eu, _ev, _attrs)
                    _px, _py = lonlat_to_xy(float(coords[_i, 0]), float(coords[_i, 1]))
                    nearest_edge_distances[_i] = float(Point(_px, _py).distance(_edge_xy))
                except Exception:
                    continue

            if not np.isfinite(nearest_edge_distances).all():
                bad = int(np.sum(~np.isfinite(nearest_edge_distances)))
                raise ValueError(f"nearest edge 公尺距離計算失敗：{bad} 筆")
            print(
                f"主要定位最近道路段：{len(nearest_edge_ids)} 筆 | "
                f"平均道路距離={float(np.mean(nearest_edge_distances)):.1f} m | "
                f"最大={float(np.max(nearest_edge_distances)):.1f} m"
            )

            # 地址座標若存在，另外查一次最近道路段；只在後續進場判斷需要時使用。
            _addr_ok_mask = np.isfinite(address_coords[:, 0]) & np.isfinite(address_coords[:, 1])
            if SNAP_ADDRESS_MIN_SEPARATION_M > 0:
                _coord_delta_m = np.full(len(coords), np.inf, dtype=float)
                _same_mask = _addr_ok_mask & np.isfinite(coords[:, 0]) & np.isfinite(coords[:, 1])
                for _si in np.flatnonzero(_same_mask):
                    _mx, _my = lonlat_to_xy(float(coords[_si, 0]), float(coords[_si, 1]))
                    _ax, _ay = lonlat_to_xy(float(address_coords[_si, 0]), float(address_coords[_si, 1]))
                    _coord_delta_m[_si] = math.hypot(_ax - _mx, _ay - _my)
                _addr_ok_mask &= _coord_delta_m >= float(SNAP_ADDRESS_MIN_SEPARATION_M)
            if SNAP_ADDRESS_COORD_ENABLED and bool(_addr_ok_mask.any()):
                _addr_positions = np.flatnonzero(_addr_ok_mask)
                _raw_addr_edges, _raw_addr_dist = ox.distance.nearest_edges(
                    G,
                    X=address_coords[_addr_positions, 0],
                    Y=address_coords[_addr_positions, 1],
                    return_dist=True,
                )
                _addr_norm = _normalize_nearest_edge_ids(_raw_addr_edges, len(_addr_positions))
                address_nearest_edge_ids = np.full((len(coords), 3), -1, dtype=np.int64)
                address_nearest_edge_distances = np.full(len(coords), np.nan, dtype=float)
                address_nearest_edge_ids[_addr_positions] = _addr_norm
                for _j, _i in enumerate(_addr_positions):
                    try:
                        _eu, _ev, _ek = map(int, _addr_norm[_j])
                        _attrs = G.get_edge_data(_eu, _ev, _ek)
                        if _attrs is None:
                            continue
                        _edge_xy = _edge_geometry_xy(_eu, _ev, _attrs)
                        _apx, _apy = lonlat_to_xy(float(address_coords[_i, 0]), float(address_coords[_i, 1]))
                        address_nearest_edge_distances[_i] = float(Point(_apx, _apy).distance(_edge_xy))
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                _addr_valid_dist = address_nearest_edge_distances[np.isfinite(address_nearest_edge_distances)]
                if len(_addr_valid_dist):
                    print(
                        f"地址定位最近道路段：{len(_addr_valid_dist)} 筆 | "
                        f"平均道路距離={float(np.mean(_addr_valid_dist)):.1f} m | "
                        f"最大={float(np.max(_addr_valid_dist)):.1f} m"
                    )
        except Exception as _edge_snap_exc:
            print("⚠️ 最近道路段查詢失敗，改回原有最近道路節點：", repr(_edge_snap_exc))
            nearest_edge_ids = None
            nearest_edge_distances = None

    # V65.64.11：建立「原始最近道路名稱／群組」索引，供 MOTOR 進場道路保護使用。
    nearest_edge_names = [""] * N
    nearest_edge_low_flags = np.zeros(N, dtype=bool)
    nearest_edge_cluster_member = np.zeros(N, dtype=bool)
    if nearest_edge_ids is not None and len(nearest_edge_ids) == N:
        for _i, _eid in enumerate(nearest_edge_ids):
            try:
                _eu, _ev, _ek = map(int, _eid)
                _attrs = G.get_edge_data(_eu, _ev, _ek)
                if _attrs is None:
                    continue
                _name = _normalize_road_text(_attrs.get("name", "")) if "_normalize_road_text" in locals() else str(_attrs.get("name", "") or "").strip()
                nearest_edge_names[_i] = _name
                _hw = _attrs.get("highway")
                if isinstance(_hw, (list, tuple, set)):
                    _hws = {str(x).strip().lower() for x in _hw}
                else:
                    _hws = {str(_hw).strip().lower()} if _hw is not None else set()
                nearest_edge_low_flags[_i] = bool(_hws & SNAP_BRANCH_LOW_CLASS_TYPES)
            except Exception:
                continue

        if SNAP_MOTOR_NAMED_LOW_ROAD_CLUSTER_PROTECT_ENABLED:
            _point_xy = np.asarray([
                lonlat_to_xy(float(coords[_i, 0]), float(coords[_i, 1]))
                for _i in range(N)
            ], dtype=float)
            for _i in range(N):
                _name_i = nearest_edge_names[_i]
                if not _name_i or not nearest_edge_low_flags[_i]:
                    continue
                _d_i = float(nearest_edge_distances[_i]) if np.isfinite(nearest_edge_distances[_i]) else float("inf")
                if _d_i > float(SNAP_MOTOR_NAMED_LOW_ROAD_CLUSTER_EDGE_MAX_M):
                    continue
                for _j in range(N):
                    if _j == _i or nearest_edge_names[_j] != _name_i or not nearest_edge_low_flags[_j]:
                        continue
                    _d_j = float(nearest_edge_distances[_j]) if np.isfinite(nearest_edge_distances[_j]) else float("inf")
                    if _d_j > float(SNAP_MOTOR_NAMED_LOW_ROAD_CLUSTER_EDGE_MAX_M):
                        continue
                    _pd = math.hypot(float(_point_xy[_i,0]-_point_xy[_j,0]), float(_point_xy[_i,1]-_point_xy[_j,1]))
                    if _pd <= float(SNAP_MOTOR_NAMED_LOW_ROAD_CLUSTER_POINT_MAX_M):
                        nearest_edge_cluster_member[_i] = True
                        break

    nearest_nodes = old_nearest_nodes.copy()
    snap_distances = old_snap_distances.copy()
    nearest_edge_distances = np.full(N, np.nan, dtype=float)
    vehicle_ingress_distances = np.full(N, np.nan, dtype=float)
    vehicle_ingress_road_distances = np.full(N, np.nan, dtype=float)
    snap_diagnostic_records = []
    snap_changed_count = 0
    snap_dead_end_replaced_count = 0
    snap_branch_replaced_count = 0
    motor_geometric_main_road_count = 0
    motor_manual_ingress_count = 0
    address_coord_used_count = 0
    address_road_priority_count = 0
    address_road_priority_miss_count = 0

    def _node_distance_to_point_m(node, px, py):
        nd = G.nodes[int(node)]
        nx_, ny_ = lonlat_to_xy(float(nd["x"]), float(nd["y"]))
        return math.hypot(float(nx_) - float(px), float(ny_) - float(py))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.50：道路投影 virtual anchor
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    VIRTUAL_NODE_BASE = -9000000000000
    virtual_anchor_records = []

    def _project_point_to_edge_m(u, v, attrs, target_lon, target_lat):
