                    nearest_nodes[i] = best_node
                    snap_distances[i] = distance_m
                    adjusted_count += 1

                    print(
                        f"  水路調整："
                        f"{work_df.iloc[i]['名稱']} | "
                        f"snap={distance_m:.1f} m"
                    )

        print(
            "因水路輔助檢查而調整道路節點：",
            adjusted_count,
            "筆"
        )


    print(
        "平均 snap：",
        float(np.mean(snap_distances)),
        "m"
    )

    print(
        "最大 snap：",
        float(np.max(snap_distances)),
        "m"
    )

    print(
        "超過 500m：",
        int(np.sum(snap_distances > 500))
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # 相同 OSM snap node
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    snap_groups = defaultdict(list)

    for i, node in enumerate(nearest_nodes):
        snap_groups[int(node)].append(i)

    duplicate_snap_groups = [
        (node, indices)
        for node, indices in snap_groups.items()
        if len(indices) > 1
    ]

    print(
        "相同 OSM snap node 群組：",
        len(duplicate_snap_groups)
    )

    # V65.64.13：檢查最終進場節點是否被錯誤借用給其他案件，
    # 以及每個 virtual anchor 是否只有自己的案件能把它當作 nearest_nodes。
    _virtual_reuse_rows = []
    _virtual_owner_errors = []
    _virtual_node_owner_map = {}
    for _vi in range(len(nearest_nodes)):
        try:
            _vnode = int(nearest_nodes[_vi])
            _va = G.nodes[_vnode]
            if bool(_va.get("virtual_snap_anchor", False) or _va.get("virtual_manual_ingress_anchor", False)):
                _src_i = int(_va.get("virtual_source_index", -999999999))
                _virtual_node_owner_map[_vnode] = _src_i
                if _src_i != int(_vi):
                    _virtual_reuse_rows.append((_vi, _vnode, _src_i))
        except Exception:
            continue

    # 每筆 virtual anchor record 也必須能回到唯一 owner。
    for _vi_rec in virtual_anchor_records:
        try:
            _vnode = int(_vi_rec["virtual_node"])
            _src_i = int(_vi_rec["點索引"])
            if _vnode not in G:
                _virtual_owner_errors.append((_src_i, _vnode, "node_not_in_graph"))
                continue
            _graph_owner = int(G.nodes[_vnode].get("virtual_source_index", -999999999))
            if _graph_owner != _src_i:
                _virtual_owner_errors.append((_src_i, _vnode, _graph_owner))
        except Exception as _e:
            _virtual_owner_errors.append(("?", "?", repr(_e)))

    if _virtual_reuse_rows or _virtual_owner_errors:
        print("❌ V65.64.23 virtual anchor reuse：", len(_virtual_reuse_rows), "筆")
        print("  reuse 範例：", _virtual_reuse_rows[:10])
        print("  owner 錯誤：", _virtual_owner_errors[:10])
        raise RuntimeError(
            "V65.64.23 失敗：virtual anchor ownership / reuse 驗證未通過；"
            "停止後續路線最佳化，避免輸出錯誤路線。"
        )
    else:
        print("V65.64.23 virtual anchor reuse：0 筆（通過）")
        print(f"V65.64.23 virtual anchor owner 驗證：{len(virtual_anchor_records)} 筆（通過）")

    if nearest_edge_cluster_member.size:
        print(
            "V65.64.23 同一路名鄰近低階道路保護案件：",
            int(np.sum(nearest_edge_cluster_member)),
            "筆"
        )

    if duplicate_snap_groups:

        duplicate_count = sum(
            len(indices) - 1
            for _, indices in duplicate_snap_groups
        )

        print(
            "因此可能產生 0 m 地籍點間距離：",
            duplicate_count,
            "筆"
        )


    # ============================================================

    snap_warnings = []

    for i, d in enumerate(snap_distances):
        if d > 500:
            snap_warnings.append({
                "index": i,
                "name": work_df.iloc[i]["名稱"],
                "snap_m": float(d),
            })

    # 18. shortest path helper
    # ============================================================

    def get_shortest_path(
        a_idx,
        b_idx
    ):

        a_node = int(
            nearest_nodes[
                a_idx
            ]
        )

        b_node = int(
            nearest_nodes[
                b_idx
            ]
        )

        path = nx.shortest_path(
            G,
            a_node,
            b_node,
            weight="route_weight"
        )

        return path


    def path_distance(
        path
    ):

        # ------------------------------------------------------------
        # 重要：
        # 不同地籍點可能 snap 到同一個 OSM node。
        #
        # 此時 NetworkX shortest_path 會合法地回傳：
        #     [node]
        #
        # 這代表兩個點在目前 OSM snap 粒度下距離為 0，
        # 不是「不可達」。
        # 原版把 len(path) < 2 一律判成 inf，
        # 因而造成第三階段整個 batch=inf。
        # ------------------------------------------------------------

        if path is None or len(path) == 0:
            return float("inf")

        if len(path) == 1:
            return 0.0

        total = 0.0

        for u, v in zip(
            path[:-1],
            path[1:]
        ):

            edge_data = G.get_edge_data(
                u,
                v
            )

            if not edge_data:
                return float("inf")

            # 多重 edge 中取目前交通工具 route_weight 最小者
            best = min(
                edge_data.values(),
                key=lambda d:
                    float(
                        d.get(
                            "route_weight",
                            float("inf")
                        )
                    )
            )

            total += float(
                best.get(
                    "route_distance",
                    best.get(
                        "length",
                        0
                    )
                )
            )

        return total


    def path_cost(
        path
    ):

        if path is None or len(path) < 2:
            return float("inf")

        total = 0.0

        for u, v in zip(
            path[:-1],
            path[1:]
        ):

            edge_data = G.get_edge_data(
                u,
                v
            )

            if not edge_data:
                return float("inf")

            best = min(
                edge_data.values(),
                key=lambda d:
                    float(
                        d.get(
                            "route_weight",
                            float("inf")
                        )
                    )
            )

            total += float(
                best.get(
                    "route_weight",
                    float(
                        best.get(
                            "length",
                            0
                        )
                    )
                )
            )

        return total


    # ============================================================
    # 19. 建立完整 N × N 路徑成本矩陣
    #
    # V52：移除 NetworkX single_source_dijkstra × N。
    # 改成 scipy CSR + 單次多來源 Dijkstra；106 點在目前資料量
    # 下可大幅降低 Python 層迴圈與 NetworkX overhead。
    # ============================================================

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"建立本機 OSM 道路成本矩陣（{VEHICLE_LABEL}）")
    print("=" * 70)

    GRAPH_NODE_LIST = [int(n) for n in G.nodes()]
    GRAPH_NODE_POS = {node: pos for pos, node in enumerate(GRAPH_NODE_LIST)}
    TARGET_POS = np.asarray([GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(n)] for n in nearest_nodes], dtype=np.int64)
    SOURCE_POS = TARGET_POS.copy()
    GRAPH_SIZE = len(GRAPH_NODE_LIST)

    def _build_sparse_weight_matrix(graph, attr_name):
        """將有向 MultiDiGraph 轉成每對(u,v)取最小權重的 CSR 矩陣。"""
        best = {}
        if graph.is_multigraph():
            for u, v, _k, attrs in graph.edges(keys=True, data=True):
                try:
                    w = float(attrs.get(attr_name, float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    w = float("inf")
                if not np.isfinite(w):
                    continue
                key = (GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(u)], GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(v)])
                old = best.get(key)
                if old is None or w < old:
                    best[key] = w
        else:
            for u, v, attrs in graph.edges(data=True):
                try:
                    w = float(attrs.get(attr_name, float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    w = float("inf")
                if not np.isfinite(w):
                    continue
                key = (GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(u)], GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(v)])
                old = best.get(key)
                if old is None or w < old:
                    best[key] = w

        if not best:
            raise RuntimeError(f"OSM graph 沒有可用的 {attr_name} 權重 edge。")

        rows = np.fromiter((k[0] for k in best.keys()), dtype=np.int64, count=len(best))
        cols = np.fromiter((k[1] for k in best.keys()), dtype=np.int64, count=len(best))
        data = np.fromiter(best.values(), dtype=np.float64, count=len(best))
        return csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(GRAPH_SIZE, GRAPH_SIZE), dtype=np.float64)

    def _build_route_time_csrs_and_length_signature(graph):
        """V65.17：一次 edge pass 同時建立 route/time CSR 所需最小權重，並建立 length signature。
        規則與舊版 _build_sparse_weight_matrix + _graph_length_signature 完全一致；
        目的只是避免 ROUTE、TIME、signature 各自重新掃整張 Graph。
        """
        best_route = {}
        best_time = {}
        h = hashlib.blake2b(digest_size=16)
        length_edge_count = 0

        if graph.is_multigraph():
            edge_iter = graph.edges(keys=True, data=True)
            for u, v, k, attrs in edge_iter:
                pi = GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(u)]
                pj = GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(v)]
                key = (pi, pj)

                try:
                    length = float(attrs.get("length", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    length = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(length):
                    h.update(f"{int(u)}|{int(v)}|{repr(k)}|{length:.6f}\n".encode("utf-8"))
                    length_edge_count += 1

                try:
                    route_w = float(attrs.get("route_weight", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    route_w = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(route_w):
                    old = best_route.get(key)
                    if old is None or route_w < old:
                        best_route[key] = route_w

                try:
                    time_w = float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    time_w = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(time_w):
                    old = best_time.get(key)
                    if old is None or time_w < old:
                        best_time[key] = time_w
        else:
            edge_iter = graph.edges(data=True)
            for u, v, attrs in edge_iter:
                pi = GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(u)]
                pj = GRAPH_NODE_POS[int(v)]
                key = (pi, pj)

                try:
                    length = float(attrs.get("length", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    length = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(length):
                    h.update(f"{int(u)}|{int(v)}|{repr(0)}|{length:.6f}\n".encode("utf-8"))
                    length_edge_count += 1

                try:
                    route_w = float(attrs.get("route_weight", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    route_w = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(route_w):
                    old = best_route.get(key)
                    if old is None or route_w < old:
                        best_route[key] = route_w

                try:
                    time_w = float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    time_w = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(time_w):
                    old = best_time.get(key)
                    if old is None or time_w < old:
                        best_time[key] = time_w

        if not best_route:
            raise RuntimeError("OSM graph 沒有可用的 route_weight 權重 edge。")
        if not best_time:
            raise RuntimeError("OSM graph 沒有可用的 travel_time_min 權重 edge。")

        route_rows = np.fromiter((k[0] for k in best_route.keys()), dtype=np.int64, count=len(best_route))
        route_cols = np.fromiter((k[1] for k in best_route.keys()), dtype=np.int64, count=len(best_route))
        route_data = np.fromiter(best_route.values(), dtype=np.float64, count=len(best_route))
        time_rows = np.fromiter((k[0] for k in best_time.keys()), dtype=np.int64, count=len(best_time))
        time_cols = np.fromiter((k[1] for k in best_time.keys()), dtype=np.int64, count=len(best_time))
        time_data = np.fromiter(best_time.values(), dtype=np.float64, count=len(best_time))

        route_csr = csr_matrix(
            (route_data, (route_rows, route_cols)),
            shape=(GRAPH_SIZE, GRAPH_SIZE),
            dtype=np.float64,
        )
        time_csr = csr_matrix(
            (time_data, (time_rows, time_cols)),
            shape=(GRAPH_SIZE, GRAPH_SIZE),
            dtype=np.float64,
        )
        return route_csr, time_csr, (length_edge_count, h.hexdigest())

    _perf_csr_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    print("建立路徑／旅行時間矩陣（每條道路只計算一次）...")
    ROUTE_GRAPH_CSR, TIME_GRAPH_CSR, _graph_length_sig_v6517 = _build_route_time_csrs_and_length_signature(G)
    print(
        f"  CSR 節點={GRAPH_SIZE:,} | directed 道路段={ROUTE_GRAPH_CSR.nnz:,}"
    )

    _v52_route_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    print("計算各查核點之間的最短道路路線...")
    ROUTE_DIST_ALL, ROUTE_PRED_ALL = scipy_csgraph_dijkstra(
        ROUTE_GRAPH_CSR,
        directed=True,
        indices=SOURCE_POS,
        return_predecessors=True,
    )
    ROUTE_D = np.asarray(ROUTE_DIST_ALL[:, TARGET_POS], dtype=float)
    ROUTE_PREDECESSOR = np.asarray(ROUTE_PRED_ALL, dtype=np.int32)
    ROUTE_PREDECESSOR[ROUTE_PREDECESSOR < 0] = -1
    del ROUTE_DIST_ALL, ROUTE_PRED_ALL, ROUTE_GRAPH_CSR
    print("最短道路路線計算完成。")
    _perf_mark("route_time_csr_and_route_dijkstra", _perf_csr_t0, emit=True)

    ROUTE_T = None
    ROUTE_PATHS = {}
    matrix_errors = []

    # ============================================================
    # 20. Matrix 可達率
    # ============================================================

    finite_count = int(
        np.isfinite(
            ROUTE_D
        ).sum()
    )

    total_count = N * N

    non_diag_total = (
        N * N - N
    )

    non_diag_finite = int(
        np.isfinite(
            ROUTE_D
        ).sum()
        - N
    )

    non_diag_ratio = (
        non_diag_finite
        /
        non_diag_total
        if non_diag_total
        else 0.0
    )

    print(
        f"可達元素："
        f"{finite_count}/{total_count}"
    )

    print(
        f"可達率："
        f"{finite_count / total_count * 100:.2f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"非對角可達率："
        f"{non_diag_ratio * 100:.2f}%"
    )

    # 不再用「所有點對所有點」的 80% 當硬性失敗條件。
    # drive_service 是有向車道路網，單行道會自然造成 A→B 可達但 B→A 不可達。
    # 真正需要確認的是：固定起點能否到達全部地籍點，以及後續最佳化
    # 是否能為每一個下一站找到有限道路距離。
    print()
    print("檢查固定起點的實際可達性")
    start_reachable = np.isfinite(ROUTE_D[start_idx])
    start_reachable[start_idx] = True
    start_reachable_count = int(start_reachable.sum())
    start_reachable_ratio = start_reachable_count / N if N else 0.0

    print(
        f"固定起點可到達：{start_reachable_count}/{N}"
        f" ({start_reachable_ratio * 100:.2f}%)"
    )

    if start_reachable_count < N:
        unreachable_from_start = [
            int(i)
            for i in range(N)
            if not start_reachable[i]
        ]

        print()
        print("固定起點目前無法直接依有向道路到達的地籍點：")
        for idx in unreachable_from_start[:30]:
            print(
                f"  - {idx + 1}: "
                f"{work_df.iloc[idx]['名稱']} | "
                f"snap={snap_distances[idx]:.1f} m"
            )

        if len(unreachable_from_start) > 30:
            print(
                f"  ... 其餘 {len(unreachable_from_start) - 30} 筆省略"
            )

        raise RuntimeError(
            "固定起點無法依目前 OSM 有向道路網到達全部地籍點。"
            "這不是單純的全矩陣可達率問題；通常與單行道、道路分區、"
            "OSM 缺路或部分道路 access 標記有關。"
            "請優先檢查上面列出的不可達地籍點。"
        )

    if non_diag_ratio < 0.80:
        print()
        print(
            "注意：全矩陣非對角可達率低於 80%，"
            "但固定起點可以到達全部地籍點，因此不停止。"
        )


    # ============================================================
    # 21. 以實際道路距離為主
    # ============================================================

    def route_pair_distance(
        a,
        b
    ):

        d = ROUTE_D[
            a,
            b
        ]

        if np.isfinite(d):
            return float(d)

        raise RuntimeError(
            f"OSM 路網不可達：{a} -> {b}"
        )


    # ============================================================
    # 22. Crossing
    # ============================================================

    def orientation(
        a,
        b,
        c
    ):

        v = (
            (b[0] - a[0])
            *
            (c[1] - a[1])
            -
            (b[1] - a[1])
            *
            (c[0] - a[0])
        )

        if abs(v) < 1e-9:
            return 0

        return (
            1
            if v > 0
            else 2
        )


    def on_segment(
        a,
        b,
        c
    ):

        return (
            min(a[0], b[0]) - 1e-9
            <= c[0]
            <= max(a[0], b[0]) + 1e-9
            and
            min(a[1], b[1]) - 1e-9
            <= c[1]
            <= max(a[1], b[1]) + 1e-9
        )


    def segments_cross(
        p1,
        p2,
        p3,
        p4
    ):

        if (
            np.allclose(p1, p3)
            or np.allclose(p1, p4)
            or np.allclose(p2, p3)
            or np.allclose(p2, p4)
        ):
            return False

        o1 = orientation(
            p1,
            p2,
            p3
        )

        o2 = orientation(
            p1,
            p2,
            p4
        )

        o3 = orientation(
            p3,
            p4,
            p1
        )

        o4 = orientation(
            p3,
            p4,
            p2
        )

        if (
            o1 != o2
            and
            o3 != o4
        ):
            return True

        if (
            o1 == 0
            and
            on_segment(
                p1,
                p2,
                p3
            )
        ):
            return True

        if (
            o2 == 0
            and
            on_segment(
                p1,
                p2,
                p4
            )
        ):
            return True

        if (
            o3 == 0
            and
            on_segment(
                p3,
                p4,
                p1
            )
        ):
            return True

        if (
            o4 == 0
            and
            on_segment(
                p3,
                p4,
                p2
            )
        ):
            return True

        return False


    def count_crossings(
        order
    ):
        """
        V65.48：向量化 Crossing 計算。

        演算法不變：
        - 相同的非相鄰 segment 配對
        - 相同的 shared-endpoint 排除規則
        - 相同的 orientation / on_segment / collinear 判定
        - 相同的 segments_cross 幾何條件

        差別只有把原本 Python 雙層迴圈改成 NumPy 批次運算。
        """
        n = len(order) - 1

        if n < 3:
            return 0

        idx = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64)

        p1 = XY[idx[:-1]]
        p2 = XY[idx[1:]]
        p3 = p1
        p4 = p2

        # 建立所有 segment pair 的矩陣。
        # 只取 j >= i + 2，與原本雙層迴圈完全相同。
        ii, jj = np.triu_indices(n, k=2)

        if len(ii) == 0:
            return 0

        # 原始程式的兩個 shared-endpoint 排除條件。
        valid = (
            (idx[ii + 1] != idx[jj])
            &
            (idx[ii] != idx[jj + 1])
        )

        # segments_cross() 原本另外使用 np.allclose() 排除四種
        # 「端點座標相同」情況；這裡也完整保留。
        # 正常資料下不同地籍點通常不會有相同座標，但為保持結果完全一致，
        # 不只比對 index，也比對實際 XY。
        _p1 = p1[ii]
        _p2 = p2[ii]
        _p3 = p1[jj]
        _p4 = p2[jj]

        _same_p1_p3 = np.all(
            np.isclose(_p1, _p3),
            axis=1
        )
        _same_p1_p4 = np.all(
            np.isclose(_p1, _p4),
            axis=1
        )
        _same_p2_p3 = np.all(
            np.isclose(_p2, _p3),
            axis=1
        )
        _same_p2_p4 = np.all(
            np.isclose(_p2, _p4),
            axis=1
        )

        valid &= ~(
            _same_p1_p3
            |
            _same_p1_p4
            |
            _same_p2_p3
            |
            _same_p2_p4
        )

        if not np.any(valid):
            return 0

        ii = ii[valid]
        jj = jj[valid]

        a = p1[ii]
        b = p2[ii]
        c = p3[jj]
        d = p4[jj]

        # 與 orientation() 完全相同的叉積公式。
        o1v = (
            (b[:, 0] - a[:, 0]) * (c[:, 1] - a[:, 1])
            -
            (b[:, 1] - a[:, 1]) * (c[:, 0] - a[:, 0])
        )
        o2v = (
            (b[:, 0] - a[:, 0]) * (d[:, 1] - a[:, 1])
            -
            (b[:, 1] - a[:, 1]) * (d[:, 0] - a[:, 0])
        )
        o3v = (
            (d[:, 0] - c[:, 0]) * (a[:, 1] - c[:, 1])
            -
            (d[:, 1] - c[:, 1]) * (a[:, 0] - c[:, 0])
        )
        o4v = (
            (d[:, 0] - c[:, 0]) * (b[:, 1] - c[:, 1])
            -
            (d[:, 1] - c[:, 1]) * (b[:, 0] - c[:, 0])
        )

        o1 = np.where(
            np.abs(o1v) < 1e-9,
            0,
            np.where(o1v > 0, 1, 2)
        )
        o2 = np.where(
            np.abs(o2v) < 1e-9,
            0,
            np.where(o2v > 0, 1, 2)
        )
        o3 = np.where(
            np.abs(o3v) < 1e-9,
            0,
            np.where(o3v > 0, 1, 2)
        )
        o4 = np.where(
            np.abs(o4v) < 1e-9,
            0,
            np.where(o4v > 0, 1, 2)
        )

        cross = (
            (o1 != o2)
            &
            (o3 != o4)
        )

        # 原本的 on_segment() 四個共線補充條件。
        def _on_segment_vec(a_, b_, c_):
            return (
                (np.minimum(a_[:, 0], b_[:, 0]) - 1e-9 <= c_[:, 0])
                &
                (c_[:, 0] <= np.maximum(a_[:, 0], b_[:, 0]) + 1e-9)
                &
                (np.minimum(a_[:, 1], b_[:, 1]) - 1e-9 <= c_[:, 1])
                &
                (c_[:, 1] <= np.maximum(a_[:, 1], b_[:, 1]) + 1e-9)
            )

        cross |= (
            (o1 == 0)
            & _on_segment_vec(a, b, c)
        )
        cross |= (
            (o2 == 0)
            & _on_segment_vec(a, b, d)
        )
        cross |= (
            (o3 == 0)
            & _on_segment_vec(c, d, a)
        )
        cross |= (
            (o4 == 0)
            & _on_segment_vec(c, d, b)
        )

        return int(np.count_nonzero(cross))



    # ============================================================
    # 23. U-turn
    # ============================================================

    def vector_angle(
        v1,
        v2
    ):

        n1 = np.linalg.norm(
            v1
        )

        n2 = np.linalg.norm(
            v2
        )

        if (
            n1 < 1e-9
            or
            n2 < 1e-9
        ):
            return 0.0

        c = (
            np.dot(
                v1,
                v2
            )
            /
            (
                n1 * n2
            )
        )

        c = np.clip(
            c,
            -1,
            1
        )

        return math.degrees(
            math.acos(c)
        )


    def count_uturns(
        order
    ):
        """
        V65.48：向量化 U-turn 計算。

        演算法不變：
        - 前後兩段都必須 >= 250 m
        - 使用同一個向量角度公式
        - 同一個 > 140° 門檻

        差別只有把逐點 Python 迴圈改成 NumPy 批次運算。
        """
        if len(order) < 3:
            return 0

        idx = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64)

        v1 = XY[idx[1:-1]] - XY[idx[:-2]]
        v2 = XY[idx[2:]] - XY[idx[1:-1]]

        l1 = np.linalg.norm(v1, axis=1)
        l2 = np.linalg.norm(v2, axis=1)

        valid = (
            (l1 >= 250)
            &
            (l2 >= 250)
        )

        if not np.any(valid):
            return 0

        v1v = v1[valid]
        v2v = v2[valid]
        l1v = l1[valid]
        l2v = l2[valid]

        c = np.sum(v1v * v2v, axis=1) / (l1v * l2v)
        c = np.clip(c, -1, 1)

        angle = np.degrees(np.arccos(c))

        return int(np.count_nonzero(angle > 140.0))



    # ============================================================
    # 24. Order cost
    # ============================================================

    def route_distance(a, b=None):
        """相容兩種呼叫：route_distance(order) 或 route_distance(a, b)。"""
        if b is not None:
            return route_pair_distance(int(a), int(b))

        order = list(a)
        if len(order) < 2:
            return 0.0

        total = 0.0
        for x, y in zip(order[:-1], order[1:]):
            d = route_pair_distance(x, y)
            if not np.isfinite(d):
                return float("inf")
            total += d
        return float(total)


    def batch_spread(
        order
    ):

        if len(order) < 2:
            return 0.0

        return max(
            EUCLIDEAN_M[
                a,
                b
            ]
            for p, a in enumerate(order)
            for b in order[p + 1:]
        )


    def centroid_spread(
        order
    ):

        if len(order) <= 1:
            return 0.0

        center = XY[
            order
        ].mean(
            axis=0
        )

        d = np.linalg.norm(
            XY[order]
            - center,
            axis=1
        )

        return float(
            d.mean()
        )


    def order_score(
        order
    ):

        score = (
            route_distance(
                order
            )
        )

        crossings = (
            count_crossings(
                order
            )
        )

        uturns = (
            count_uturns(
                order
            )
        )

        score += (
            crossings
            *
            _ROUTE_CROSSING_PENALTY_M
        )

        score += (
            uturns
            *
            _ROUTE_UTURN_PENALTY_M
        )

        score += (
            centroid_spread(order)
            *
            CLUSTER_SPREAD_PENALTY
        )

        spread = (
            batch_spread(
                order
            )
        )

        if spread > MAX_BATCH_SPREAD_M:

            score += (
                spread
                -
                MAX_BATCH_SPREAD_M
            ) * LONG_BATCH_SPREAD_PENALTY

        return score


    # ============================================================
    # 25. Nearest Neighbor
    # ============================================================

    def nearest_neighbor(
        members,
        start
    ):

        remaining = set(
            members
        )

        if start not in remaining:
            raise ValueError(
                "start 不在 members"
            )

        order = [
            start
        ]

        remaining.remove(
            start
        )

        current = start

        while remaining:

            reachable = []
            for x in remaining:
                try:
                    d = float(route_distance(current, x))
                except Exception:
                    d = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(d):
                    reachable.append(x)

            if not reachable:
                unreachable = sorted(
                    list(remaining)
                )
                names = [
                    str(work_df.iloc[i]["名稱"])
                    for i in unreachable[:10]
                ]
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"目前點 {current + 1} ({work_df.iloc[current]['名稱']}) "
                    f"沒有任何可達的下一地籍點。"
                    f"剩餘 {len(unreachable)} 點，例如：{names}"
                )

            nxt = min(
                reachable,
                key=lambda x:
                    route_distance(
                        current,
                        x
                    )
            )

            order.append(
                nxt
            )

            remaining.remove(
                nxt
            )

            current = nxt

        return order


    # ============================================================
    # 26. 2-opt
    # ============================================================

    def _validate_route_order(order, require_start=True, name="route"):
        """驗證路線索引型態、唯一性及固定起點位置，避免 2-opt/最佳化破壞閉合路線結構。"""
        if order is None:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} 不得為 None。")
        try:
            seq = [int(x) for x in list(order)]
        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} 含有無法轉成整數的節點索引。") from exc

        if not seq:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} 不得為空路線。")
        if any(x < 0 or x >= N for x in seq):
            bad = [x for x in seq if x < 0 or x >= N][:10]
            raise ValueError(f"{name} 含有超出 0~{N-1} 範圍的節點索引：{bad}")
        if len(seq) != len(set(seq)):
            dup = sorted({x for x in seq if seq.count(x) > 1})[:10]
            raise ValueError(f"{name} 含有重複節點索引：{dup}")
        if require_start and seq[0] != int(start_idx):
            raise ValueError(
                f"{name} 固定起點錯誤：第一個索引應為 {int(start_idx)}，實際為 {seq[0]}。"
            )
        if require_start and int(start_idx) in seq[1:]:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} 在起點以外的位置再次出現固定起點。")
        return seq


    def two_opt(
        order
    ):
        """安全版 2-opt：只翻轉查核點區段，不允許固定起點被移入中間。"""
        best = _validate_route_order(order, require_start=True, name="2-opt route")
        if len(best) < 4:
            return best

        best_score = float(order_score(best))
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            return best

        improved = True
        iteration = 0

        # route 格式為 [固定起點, 查核點..., 最後查核點]
        # 不含重複的回程起點，因此合法翻轉區間為 1 ~ len(best)-1。
        # i/j 永不碰 position 0，且 j 不會超出最後一個實際節點。
        while improved and iteration < TWO_OPT_MAX_ITERATIONS:
            improved = False
            iteration += 1

            n = len(best)
            for i in range(1, n - 2):
                for j in range(i + 1, n - 1):
                    candidate = (
                        best[:i]
                        + best[i:j + 1][::-1]
                        + best[j + 1:]
                    )

                    # 邊界安全／唯一性檢查；正常情況永遠成立，
                    # 但可避免未來修改切片邏輯時悄悄破壞起點。
                    if (
                        not candidate
                        or candidate[0] != int(start_idx)
                        or len(candidate) != n
                        or len(set(candidate)) != n
                        or int(start_idx) in candidate[1:]
                    ):
                        continue

                    score = float(order_score(candidate))
                    if not np.isfinite(score):
                        continue

                    if score < best_score - 1e-6:
                        best = candidate
                        best_score = score
                        improved = True
                        break

                if improved:
                    break

        return best


    # ============================================================
    # 27. 全部點位一起建立「全域初始路線」
    #
    # v9：不再把「每 10 點」當成路線規劃的核心限制。
    # 所有有效點位一次放進同一條 global route，最後才依每日工時切割。
    # ============================================================

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    # V65.48：全域路線最佳化純計時起點；包含全域 seed 建立＋Relocate/Swap＋最終確認。
    _perf_global_route_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    print("第一階段：全部點位一起建立全域初始路線")
    print("=" * 70)

    ALL_MEMBERS = list(range(N))

    # 固定起點一定在第一位置；其餘 105 點由同一條全域路線一起計算。
    global_seed = nearest_neighbor(
        ALL_MEMBERS,
        start_idx
    )

    print(
        f"全域初始路線：{len(global_seed)} 點 | "
        f"起點={work_df.iloc[start_idx]['名稱']}"
    )

    # 保留一個簡單的全域路線診斷，供後續 v9 最佳化使用。
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
        print(f"初始全域汽車時間等效成本：{route_distance(global_seed)/1000:.2f} 公里等效值")
    else:
        print(f"初始全域道路成本：{route_distance(global_seed)/1000:.2f} km")

    # v9 不再執行「空間分群→批次邊界交換」。
    # 這些概念由後面的全域排序與每日工時切割取代。

    # ============================================================
    # 28-29. v9 不使用固定 10 點分群
    # ============================================================

    batches = [global_seed]

    # ============================================================
    # 30. 全部點位一起做「合理折返」全域路線最佳化
    #
    # 核心：
    #   1. 106 點全部一起排序，不受原本批次邊界限制。
    #   2. 以道路距離為主目標。
    #   3. 小幅回頭不處罰；明顯、大幅折返才提高成本。
    #   4. 避免突然跳到很遠的區域，但不禁止必要跨區。
    #   5. Relocate + Swap 只在局部位置窗內搜尋，避免 v6 那種暴力 2-opt。
    #   6. 最後依「每日 5 小時絕對上限」切成工作日。
    # ============================================================

    if ENABLE_GLOBAL_ROUTE_RESEQUENCING:

        print()
        print("=" * 70)
        print("第二階段：全部點位一起做全域道路路線最佳化")
        print("=" * 70)

        global_order = list(global_seed)

        GLOBAL_LOCAL_WINDOW = 24
        GLOBAL_RELOCATE_PASSES = 10
        GLOBAL_SWAP_PASSES = 6
        GLOBAL_MAX_ACCEPT_DISTANCE_INCREASE_M = 500.0
        GLOBAL_MIN_OBJECTIVE_IMPROVEMENT_M = 10.0

        # --------------------------------------------------------
        # 不把「跨日重複道路」當成問題。
        # 真正要避免的是同一天／同一路序中，已經繞出去又在
        # 很短的空間範圍內繞回原區域，而且中間沒有足夠的新點
        # 可以解釋這段迴圈。
        #
        # 這裡使用 OSM 路網矩陣（ROUTE_D）判斷「實際道路距離」
        # 而非單純以地籍點直線向量判斷，且採低權重 soft penalty，
        # 避免把必要的巷道／農路回頭誤判成錯誤。
        LOOPBACK_WINDOW = 8
        LOOPBACK_MIN_EXCESS_M = 1200.0
        LOOPBACK_DIRECT_MAX_M = 3000.0
        LOOPBACK_RATIO = 1.75
        LOOPBACK_PENALTY_PER_M = 0.08

        LONG_JUMP_THRESHOLD_M = 6000.0
        LONG_JUMP_PENALTY_PER_M = 0.12

        # ============================================================
        # V65.64.14：Virtual anchor 路線順序硬性限制
        #
        # 不再只於全域最佳化完成後「事後搬點」。
        # 先建立 directed point-pair → 中途 virtual anchor 案件快取，
        # 再讓 route_objective_v9 直接拒絕任何 forward-crossing 候選。
        # 這樣 Relocate / Swap 的每一個候選都必須維持物理通過順序正確。
        # ============================================================
        _VA_PAIR_INTERMEDIATE_CACHE = {}

        _VA_NODE_TO_POINT = {}
        if (VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR"
                and VIRTUAL_ANCHOR_ROUTE_ORDER_CONSTRAINT_ENABLED
                and virtual_anchor_records):
            for _rec in virtual_anchor_records:
                try:
                    _pi = int(_rec.get("點索引"))
                    _vn = int(_rec.get("virtual_node"))
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if _vn in G and 0 <= _pi < N and _pi != int(start_idx):
                    _VA_NODE_TO_POINT[int(_vn)] = int(_pi)

        def _va_pair_intermediate_points(a, b):
            """V65.64.23：直接用 ROUTE_PREDECESSOR 回溯既定最短路徑。

            不再呼叫 NetworkX shortest_path，因此 Virtual anchor 候選檢查
            不會反覆建立大型 Python node path。只回傳中途案件索引 tuple。
            路徑採用與前面 ROUTE_D / route_weight 完全相同的 predecessor，
            因此檢查對象就是 Cell 4 實際選定的 shortest-path tree。
            """
            if not _VA_NODE_TO_POINT or int(a) == int(b):
                return ()
            key = (int(a), int(b))
            cached = _VA_PAIR_INTERMEDIATE_CACHE.get(key)
            if cached is not None:
                return cached

            try:
                source_node = int(nearest_nodes[int(a)])
                target_node = int(nearest_nodes[int(b)])
                source_pos = int(GRAPH_NODE_POS[source_node])
                target_pos = int(GRAPH_NODE_POS[target_node])
                pred_row = ROUTE_PREDECESSOR[int(a)]
            except Exception:
                out = ()
                _VA_PAIR_INTERMEDIATE_CACHE[key] = out
                return out

            if source_pos == target_pos:
                out = ()
                _VA_PAIR_INTERMEDIATE_CACHE[key] = out
                return out

            seen_points = set()
            vals_rev = []
            cur = int(target_pos)
            steps = 0
            max_steps = int(GRAPH_SIZE) + 5

            try:
                while cur != source_pos and steps < max_steps:
                    node_id = int(GRAPH_NODE_LIST[cur])
                    pi = _VA_NODE_TO_POINT.get(node_id)
                    # 排除 B 自身；只看 A→B 中間節點。
                    if pi is not None and int(pi) != int(a) and int(pi) != int(b):
                        if int(pi) not in seen_points:
                            seen_points.add(int(pi))
                            vals_rev.append(int(pi))
                    prev = int(pred_row[cur])
                    if prev < 0:
                        raise RuntimeError("ROUTE_PREDECESSOR path broken")
                    cur = prev
                    steps += 1
                if cur != source_pos:
                    raise RuntimeError("ROUTE_PREDECESSOR path did not reach source")
            except Exception:
                out = ()
            else:
                # 回溯時是 B→A，因此要反轉回實際 A→B 的通過順序。
                out = tuple(reversed(vals_rev))

            _VA_PAIR_INTERMEDIATE_CACHE[key] = out
            return out

        def _va_forward_crossing_count(order):
            """目前順序下，A→B 邊是否提前穿過排在 B 後面的 virtual anchor。"""
            if not _VA_NODE_TO_POINT:
                return 0
            seq = [int(x) for x in order]
            pos = {int(x): i for i, x in enumerate(seq)}
            count = 0
            for edge_pos, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(seq[:-1], seq[1:])):
                for _pi in _va_pair_intermediate_points(a, b):
                    cp = pos.get(int(_pi), -1)
                    if cp > edge_pos + 1:
                        count += 1
            return int(count)

        def _va_repair_order_before_global_optimization(order):
            """全域最佳化前先消除既有 forward-crossing，建立可行初始解。"""
            seq = [int(x) for x in order]
            if not _VA_NODE_TO_POINT or len(seq) <= 2:
                return seq, 0, []

            moves = []
            max_passes = max(4, min(len(seq) * 2, 256))
            for pass_no in range(1, max_passes + 1):
                pos = {int(x): i for i, x in enumerate(seq)}
                changed = False
                for edge_pos, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(seq[:-1], seq[1:])):
                    encountered = []
                    for _pi in _va_pair_intermediate_points(a, b):
                        cp = pos.get(int(_pi), -1)
                        if cp > edge_pos + 1:
                            encountered.append((int(_pi), int(cp)))
                    if not encountered:
                        continue

                    moving = []
                    moving_set = set()
                    for _pi, _old_pos in encountered:
                        if _pi not in moving_set:
                            moving.append(_pi)
                            moving_set.add(_pi)
                    if not moving:
                        continue

                    old_seq = list(seq)
                    seq = [x for x in seq if x not in moving_set]
                    insert_pos = edge_pos + 1
                    for _pi in moving:
                        seq.insert(insert_pos, int(_pi))
                        insert_pos += 1

                    for _pi, _old_pos in encountered:
                        moves.append({
                            "pass": int(pass_no),
                            "from_edge_pos": int(edge_pos),
                            "from_point": int(a),
                            "to_point": int(b),
                            "moved_point": int(_pi),
                            "old_position": int(_old_pos),
                            "new_position": int(seq.index(int(_pi))),
                        })
                    changed = True
                    break

                if not changed:
                    break
            return seq, len(moves), moves

        # 全域 seed 在進入 Relocate / Swap 前先成為「物理通過順序可行解」。
        if (VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR"
                and VIRTUAL_ANCHOR_ROUTE_ORDER_CONSTRAINT_ENABLED
                and _VA_NODE_TO_POINT):
            _global_pre_va_order = list(global_order)
            global_order, _pre_va_moves_count, _pre_va_moves = _va_repair_order_before_global_optimization(global_order)
            print(f"V65.64.23 全域最佳化前 Virtual anchor 順序可行化：移動 {_pre_va_moves_count} 次")
            for _rec in _pre_va_moves[:20]:
                print(
                    f"  {work_df.iloc[_rec['from_point']]['名稱']} → {work_df.iloc[_rec['to_point']]['名稱']}"
                    f"：提前 {work_df.iloc[_rec['moved_point']]['名稱']}"
                )

        def fast_route_distance_v9(order):
            if len(order) < 2:
                return 0.0
            a = np.asarray(order[:-1], dtype=int)
            b = np.asarray(order[1:], dtype=int)
            vals = ROUTE_D[a, b]
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
                return float("inf")
            return float(vals.sum())

        _V65_25_LOOPBACK_PAIR_CACHE = {}

        def _loopback_pair_index_cache_v65_25(n):
            """V65.25：預先建立 loopback 判定的 (i,j) 索引，保留原掃描順序。"""
            n = int(n)
            cached = _V65_25_LOOPBACK_PAIR_CACHE.get(n)
            if cached is not None:
                return cached
            i_list = []
            j_list = []
            for j in range(3, n):
                i0 = max(0, j - LOOPBACK_WINDOW)
                for i in range(i0, j - 2):
                    i_list.append(i)
                    j_list.append(j)
            out = (
                np.asarray(i_list, dtype=np.int32),
                np.asarray(j_list, dtype=np.int32),
            )
            _V65_25_LOOPBACK_PAIR_CACHE[n] = out
            return out

        def wasteful_loopback_cost_v10(order):
            """以 OSM 路網距離抓「無意義的繞回」；V65.25 向量化判定。

            數學條件、掃描範圍與 penalty 完全沿用原版；僅把原本
            j/i 雙層 Python 迴圈改成固定 pair-index 的 NumPy 批次計算。
            """
            n = len(order)
            if n < 4:
                return 0.0

            order_np = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int32)
            edge_dist = ROUTE_D[order_np[:-1], order_np[1:]]
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(edge_dist)):
                return float("inf")

            prefix = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
            prefix[0] = 0.0
            prefix[1:] = np.cumsum(edge_dist, dtype=np.float64)

            pair_i, pair_j = _loopback_pair_index_cache_v65_25(n)
            if pair_i.size == 0:
                return 0.0

            direct = ROUTE_D[order_np[pair_i], order_np[pair_j]]
            along = prefix[pair_j] - prefix[pair_i]
            excess = along - direct
            mask = (
                np.isfinite(direct)
                & (direct > 0.0)
                & (direct <= LOOPBACK_DIRECT_MAX_M)
                & (excess >= LOOPBACK_MIN_EXCESS_M)
                & (along >= direct * LOOPBACK_RATIO)
            )
            if not np.any(mask):
                return 0.0
            return float(
                np.sum(excess[mask], dtype=np.float64)
                * LOOPBACK_PENALTY_PER_M
            )

        def long_jump_cost_v9(order):
            if len(order) < 2:
                return 0.0
            order_np = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int32)
            edge_euclid = EUCLIDEAN_M[order_np[:-1], order_np[1:]]
            mask = edge_euclid > LONG_JUMP_THRESHOLD_M
            if not np.any(mask):
                return 0.0
            return float(
                np.sum(edge_euclid[mask] - LONG_JUMP_THRESHOLD_M, dtype=np.float64)
                * LONG_JUMP_PENALTY_PER_M
            )

        def route_objective_v9(order):
            distance = fast_route_distance_v9(order)
            if not np.isfinite(distance):
                return float("inf")
            if (VIRTUAL_ANCHOR_ROUTE_ORDER_CONSTRAINT_ENABLED
                    and VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR"
                    and _VA_NODE_TO_POINT
                    and _va_forward_crossing_count(order) > 0):
                return float("inf")
            return (
                distance
                + wasteful_loopback_cost_v10(order)
                + long_jump_cost_v9(order)
            )

        def make_relocate_candidate_v9(order, src_pos, dst_pos):
            n = len(order)
            if src_pos <= 0 or src_pos >= n:
                return None
            if dst_pos <= 0 or dst_pos >= n:
                return None
            if src_pos == dst_pos or src_pos == dst_pos - 1:
                return None
            candidate = list(order)
            x = candidate.pop(src_pos)
            insert_at = dst_pos
            if dst_pos > src_pos:
                insert_at -= 1
            insert_at = max(1, min(insert_at, len(candidate)))
            candidate.insert(insert_at, x)
            return candidate if candidate[0] == start_idx else None

        def _route_edge_v9(order, edge_pos):
            a = int(order[edge_pos])
            b = int(order[edge_pos + 1]) if edge_pos + 1 < len(order) else int(start_idx)
            return float(ROUTE_D[a, b])

        def make_swap_candidate_v9(order, pos_a, pos_b):
            """V52：建立 swap 候選路線，固定起點位置 0 不得移動。"""
            n = len(order)
            if pos_a <= 0 or pos_b <= 0 or pos_a >= n or pos_b >= n:
                return None
            if pos_a == pos_b:
                return None
            if pos_a > pos_b:
                pos_a, pos_b = pos_b, pos_a
            candidate = list(order)
            candidate[pos_a], candidate[pos_b] = candidate[pos_b], candidate[pos_a]
            return candidate if candidate[0] == start_idx else None

        def relocate_distance_delta_v9(order, src_pos, dst_pos):
            """V52：relocate 的旅行成本增量，避免每候選重算整條路線。"""
            n = len(order)
            if src_pos <= 0 or src_pos >= n or dst_pos <= 0 or dst_pos >= n:
                return float("inf")
            if src_pos == dst_pos:
                return 0.0
            x = int(order[src_pos])
            prev = int(order[src_pos - 1]) if src_pos - 1 >= 0 else int(start_idx)
            nxt = int(order[src_pos + 1]) if src_pos + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
            remove_delta = (
                float(ROUTE_D[prev, nxt])
                - float(ROUTE_D[prev, x])
                - float(ROUTE_D[x, nxt])
            )
            if not np.isfinite(remove_delta):
                return float("inf")

            base = order[:src_pos] + order[src_pos + 1:]
            a = int(base[dst_pos - 1])
            b = int(base[dst_pos]) if dst_pos < len(base) else int(start_idx)
            insert_delta = (
                float(ROUTE_D[a, x])
                + float(ROUTE_D[x, b])
                - float(ROUTE_D[a, b])
            )
            if not np.isfinite(insert_delta):
                return float("inf")
            return float(remove_delta + insert_delta)

        def swap_distance_delta_v9(order, pos_a, pos_b):
            """V52：swap 的旅行成本增量，只重算受影響的 4 個 edge。"""
            n = len(order)
            if pos_a <= 0 or pos_b <= 0 or pos_a >= n or pos_b >= n or pos_a == pos_b:
                return float("inf")
            affected = sorted(set([pos_a - 1, pos_a, pos_b - 1, pos_b]))
            old_cost = 0.0
            new_cost = 0.0
            for edge_pos in affected:
                a_old = int(order[edge_pos])
                b_old = int(order[edge_pos + 1]) if edge_pos + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
                if not np.isfinite(ROUTE_D[a_old, b_old]):
                    return float("inf")
                old_cost += float(ROUTE_D[a_old, b_old])

                def node_after_swap(p):
                    if p == pos_a:
                        return int(order[pos_b])
                    if p == pos_b:
                        return int(order[pos_a])
                    return int(order[p])

                a_new = node_after_swap(edge_pos)
                b_new = node_after_swap(edge_pos + 1) if edge_pos + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
                if not np.isfinite(ROUTE_D[a_new, b_new]):
                    return float("inf")
                new_cost += float(ROUTE_D[a_new, b_new])
            return float(new_cost - old_cost)

        current_distance = fast_route_distance_v9(global_order)
        current_objective = route_objective_v9(global_order)

        if not np.isfinite(current_distance):
            raise RuntimeError("全域初始路線含有不可達道路連線。")

        if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
            print(f"全域最佳化前汽車時間等效成本：{current_distance/1000:.2f} 公里等效值")
        else:
            print(f"全域最佳化前道路成本：{current_distance/1000:.2f} km")
        print(f"最佳化視窗：前後各 {GLOBAL_LOCAL_WINDOW} 個位置")
        print(
            f"無意義繞回判定：近 {LOOPBACK_WINDOW} 站、直接道路距離 ≤ "
            f"{LOOPBACK_DIRECT_MAX_M:.0f} m、路徑繞行至少 "
            f"{LOOPBACK_MIN_EXCESS_M:.0f} m"
        )

        total_improvements = 0

        def _batch_relocate_distance_deltas_v65_24(order, src_pos, dst_positions):
            """V65.24：一次計算同一 source 的所有 relocate ROUTE_D 增量。

            數學公式與 relocate_distance_delta_v9 完全相同；只把固定的
            scalar 矩陣查值改成 NumPy 批次索引。回傳順序與 dst_positions 完全一致。
            """
            n_local = len(order)
            src_pos = int(src_pos)
            dst_arr = np.asarray(dst_positions, dtype=np.int32)
            if src_pos <= 0 or src_pos >= n_local or dst_arr.size == 0:
                return np.full(dst_arr.shape, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)

            x = int(order[src_pos])
            prev = int(order[src_pos - 1])
            nxt = int(order[src_pos + 1]) if src_pos + 1 < n_local else int(start_idx)
            remove_delta = (
                float(ROUTE_D[prev, nxt])
                - float(ROUTE_D[prev, x])
                - float(ROUTE_D[x, nxt])
            )
            if not np.isfinite(remove_delta):
                return np.full(dst_arr.shape, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)

            base = np.asarray(order[:src_pos] + order[src_pos + 1:], dtype=np.int32)
            valid = (dst_arr > 0) & (dst_arr < n_local) & (dst_arr != src_pos) & (dst_arr != src_pos + 1)
            out_delta = np.full(dst_arr.shape, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.any(valid):
                return out_delta

            d = dst_arr[valid]
            left = base[d - 1]
            safe_right_idx = np.minimum(d, len(base) - 1)
            right = base[safe_right_idx].copy()
            right[d >= len(base)] = int(start_idx)
            insert_delta = ROUTE_D[left, x] + ROUTE_D[x, right] - ROUTE_D[left, right]
            deltas = float(remove_delta) + insert_delta
            finite = np.isfinite(deltas)
            temp = np.full(d.shape, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
            temp[finite] = deltas[finite]
            out_delta[valid] = temp
            return out_delta

        for pass_no in range(1, GLOBAL_RELOCATE_PASSES + 1):
            t0 = time.time()
            best_score = current_objective
            best_distance = current_distance
            best_order = None
            n = len(global_order)

            for src_pos in range(1, n):
                left = max(1, src_pos - GLOBAL_LOCAL_WINDOW)
                right = min(n - 1, src_pos + GLOBAL_LOCAL_WINDOW)
                dst_positions = np.arange(left, right + 1, dtype=np.int32)
                deltas = _batch_relocate_distance_deltas_v65_24(global_order, src_pos, dst_positions)

                # 保留原本 dst 由小到大的候選掃描順序；只把 ROUTE_D 增量查值批次化。
                for k, dst_pos_np in enumerate(dst_positions):
                    delta = float(deltas[k])
                    if not np.isfinite(delta):
                        continue
                    candidate_distance = current_distance + delta
                    if candidate_distance > current_distance + GLOBAL_MAX_ACCEPT_DISTANCE_INCREASE_M:
                        continue
                    # loopback / long-jump penalty 非負；若純距離下界都無法改善目前目標，
                    # 不再建立候選並計算昂貴的完整 route_objective。
                    if candidate_distance >= current_objective - GLOBAL_MIN_OBJECTIVE_IMPROVEMENT_M:
                        continue
                    dst_pos = int(dst_pos_np)
                    candidate = make_relocate_candidate_v9(global_order, src_pos, dst_pos)
                    if candidate is None:
                        continue
                    score = route_objective_v9(candidate)
                    if score < best_score - GLOBAL_MIN_OBJECTIVE_IMPROVEMENT_M:
                        best_score = score
                        best_distance = candidate_distance
                        best_order = candidate

            if best_order is None:
                print(f"調整路線順序第 {pass_no}: 無進一步改善")
                break

            current_distance = best_distance
            current_objective = best_score
            global_order = best_order
            total_improvements += 1
            print(
                f"調整路線順序第 {pass_no}: "
                f"成本 {current_distance/1000:.2f} 公里等效值 | "
                f"耗時 {time.time()-t0:.1f} 秒"
            )

        for pass_no in range(1, GLOBAL_SWAP_PASSES + 1):
            t0 = time.time()
            best_score = current_objective
            best_distance = current_distance
            best_order = None
            n = len(global_order)

            for pos_a in range(1, n - 1):
                right = min(n - 1, pos_a + GLOBAL_LOCAL_WINDOW)
                for pos_b in range(pos_a + 1, right + 1):
                    delta = swap_distance_delta_v9(global_order, pos_a, pos_b)
                    if not np.isfinite(delta):
                        continue
                    candidate_distance = current_distance + delta
                    if candidate_distance > current_distance + GLOBAL_MAX_ACCEPT_DISTANCE_INCREASE_M:
                        continue
                    if candidate_distance >= current_objective - GLOBAL_MIN_OBJECTIVE_IMPROVEMENT_M:
                        continue
                    candidate = make_swap_candidate_v9(global_order, pos_a, pos_b)
                    if candidate is None:
                        continue
                    score = route_objective_v9(candidate)
                    if score < best_score - GLOBAL_MIN_OBJECTIVE_IMPROVEMENT_M:
                        best_score = score
                        best_distance = candidate_distance
                        best_order = candidate

            if best_order is None:
                print(f"交換路線順序第 {pass_no}: 無進一步改善")
                break

            current_distance = best_distance
            current_objective = best_score
            global_order = best_order
            total_improvements += 1
            print(
                f"交換路線順序第 {pass_no}: "
                f"成本 {current_distance/1000:.2f} 公里等效值 | "
                f"耗時 {time.time()-t0:.1f} 秒"
            )

        final_global_distance = fast_route_distance_v9(global_order)
        final_global_objective = route_objective_v9(global_order)

        print()
        print(f"全域最佳化完成：改善 {total_improvements} 次")
        if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
            print(f"全域汽車時間等效成本：{final_global_distance/1000:.2f} 公里等效值")
        else:
            print(f"全域道路成本：{final_global_distance/1000:.2f} km")
        print(f"全域目標函數：{final_global_objective/1000:.2f} 公里等效值")
        print(f"全域 Crossing：{count_crossings(global_order)}")
        print(f"全域 U-turn：{count_uturns(global_order)}")
        if (VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR"
                and VIRTUAL_ANCHOR_ROUTE_ORDER_CONSTRAINT_ENABLED
                and _VA_NODE_TO_POINT):
            print(f"V65.64.23 全域最佳化內建 Virtual anchor forward-crossing：{_va_forward_crossing_count(global_order)} 段")

    else:
        global_order = list(global_seed)
        final_global_distance = route_distance(global_order)
        final_global_objective = final_global_distance


    _perf_mark("global_route_optimization", _perf_global_route_t0, emit=True)

    # ============================================================
    # 31. 建立「實際道路長度」矩陣，供每日工時切分
    #
    # v43 加速：不再對 106 個來源逐一呼叫 NetworkX Dijkstra。
    # 先把 MultiDiGraph 壓成 scipy CSR 稀疏矩陣，再一次執行
    # scipy.sparse.csgraph.dijkstra(indices=全部地籍點)，
    # 距離結果完全對應相同 edge length 權重，但速度通常會快很多。
    # ============================================================

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("第三階段前置：建立實際道路距離矩陣（供每日工時計算）")
    print("=" * 70)

    # V52：GRAPH_NODE_LIST / GRAPH_NODE_POS / TARGET_POS / SOURCE_POS /
    # _build_sparse_weight_matrix 已在第 19 階段建立，這裡直接沿用。

    # V65.17：length signature 已在 ROUTE/TIME CSR 的同一個 edge pass 中建立。
    road_signature = _graph_length_sig_v6517
    _perf_road_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    print("建立 scipy CSR 實際道路長度矩陣...")
    road_cache = shared_context.get("road_matrix_cache") if shared_context is not None else None
    road_cache_lock = shared_context.get("road_matrix_cache_lock") if shared_context is not None else None

    cached_road = None
    if road_cache is not None:
        with road_cache_lock:
            cached_road = road_cache.get(road_signature)

    if cached_road is not None:
        ROAD_D = cached_road
        print("  V56：沿用 BOTH 共用 ROAD_D（相同保留道路拓樸）。")
    else:
        LENGTH_GRAPH_CSR = _build_sparse_weight_matrix(G, "length")
        print(
            f"  CSR 節點={GRAPH_SIZE:,} | directed 道路段={LENGTH_GRAPH_CSR.nnz:,}"
        )
        print("計算所有查核點之間的道路距離...")
        ROAD_DIST_ALL = scipy_csgraph_dijkstra(
            LENGTH_GRAPH_CSR,
            directed=True,
            indices=SOURCE_POS,
            return_predecessors=False,
        )
        ROAD_D = np.asarray(ROAD_DIST_ALL[:, TARGET_POS], dtype=float)
        del ROAD_DIST_ALL, LENGTH_GRAPH_CSR
        if road_cache is not None:
            with road_cache_lock:
                road_cache.setdefault(road_signature, ROAD_D)

    road_finite = np.isfinite(ROAD_D)
    print(
        f"實際道路矩陣非對角可達率："
        f"{(road_finite.sum()-N)/(N*N-N)*100:.2f}%"
    )
    _perf_mark("road_matrix_dijkstra", _perf_road_t0, emit=True)

    _perf_time_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    print()
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
        print(f"建立機車旅行時間矩陣（平均 {MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH:.1f} km/h）")
    else:
        print(
            f"建立汽車旅行時間矩陣（平面 {CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH:.1f} km/h | "
            f"高速 {CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH:.1f} km/h | "
            f"快速 {CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH:.1f} km/h）"
        )

    # v43：旅行時間也改用 scipy Dijkstra。
    # V65.17：TIME_GRAPH_CSR 已與 ROUTE_GRAPH_CSR 在前一個單次 edge pass 中建立。
    # return_predecessors=True 直接留下最短旅行時間樹，
    # 第 5 階段診斷可直接回溯，不再重新 shortest_path。
    print(
        f"  TIME CSR 節點={GRAPH_SIZE:,} | directed 道路段={TIME_GRAPH_CSR.nnz:,}"
    )
    print("計算所有查核點之間的行車時間...")
    TIME_DIST_ALL, TIME_PRED_ALL = scipy_csgraph_dijkstra(
        TIME_GRAPH_CSR,
        directed=True,
        indices=SOURCE_POS,
        return_predecessors=True,
    )
    TIME_D = np.asarray(TIME_DIST_ALL[:, TARGET_POS], dtype=float)
    # scipy 以 -9999 表示無 predecessor；統一成原程式使用的 -1。
    TIME_PREDECESSOR = np.asarray(TIME_PRED_ALL, dtype=np.int32)
    TIME_PREDECESSOR[TIME_PREDECESSOR < 0] = -1
    del TIME_DIST_ALL, TIME_PRED_ALL, TIME_GRAPH_CSR

    time_finite = np.isfinite(TIME_D)
    _perf_mark("time_matrix_dijkstra", _perf_time_t0, emit=True)

    # ============================================================
    # V65.9：外勤路線方向固定「由近到遠」
    #
    # 既有 global optimization / daily optimization 仍負責找合理的閉合路線。
    # 但固定起點的閉合路線本質上有「正走／反走」兩個方向；若兩者皆可行，
    # 外勤實務採「第一個查核點離固定起點較近」的方向。
    # 此處只交換整條序列方向，不重算／改寫 OSM edge，也不改變查核點集合。
    # ============================================================
    def orient_order_near_to_far(order, label='route'):
        order = [int(x) for x in list(order)]
        if not PREFER_NEAR_TO_FAR_ORDER or len(order) <= 2:
            return order
        if order[0] != int(start_idx):
            raise ValueError(f"{label} 固定起點錯誤：第一個索引應為 {start_idx}。")

        forward = list(order)
        reverse = [int(start_idx)] + list(reversed(forward[1:]))

        f_first = int(forward[1])
        r_first = int(reverse[1])
        f_dist = float(ROAD_D[int(start_idx), f_first])
        r_dist = float(ROAD_D[int(start_idx), r_first])

        if np.isfinite(r_dist) and (not np.isfinite(f_dist) or r_dist < f_dist - 1e-6):
            print(
                f"{label}：方向由遠→近改為近→遠 | "
                f"原首站={work_df.iloc[f_first]['名稱']} ({f_dist/1000:.2f} km) → "
                f"新首站={work_df.iloc[r_first]['名稱']} ({r_dist/1000:.2f} km)"
            )
            return reverse

        print(
            f"{label}：維持目前方向 | "
            f"首站={work_df.iloc[f_first]['名稱']} ({f_dist/1000:.2f} km)"
        )
        return forward

    # TIME_D 已建立，因此此處亦可同時計算方向調整前後的第一段時間差，供診斷。
    global_order_before_v65_9 = list(global_order)
    global_order = orient_order_near_to_far(global_order, label='全域路線')
    final_global_distance = fast_route_distance_v9(global_order) if 'fast_route_distance_v9' in locals() else route_distance(global_order)
    final_global_objective = route_objective_v9(global_order) if 'route_objective_v9' in locals() else final_global_distance

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.64.13：Virtual anchor「實際通過順序」修正
    #
    # V65.64.12 已解決「A 案件的 virtual anchor 被 B 案件直接拿來
    # 當自己的 ingress node」；但診斷仍發現另一種更深層的情況：
    #   A -> B 的實際最短道路路徑途中，會先穿過 C 案件的 virtual anchor。
    # 若 C 目前排在 B 後面，Cell 4 雖然沒有 ownership 錯誤，實際物理行進
    # 卻已經先經過 C，造成「排定順序」與「實際行進順序」不一致。
    #
    # 本版只處理這個問題，不納入每日工時／每日點數。
    # 規則：
    #   1) 僅針對目前路線中「尚未到達」的其他案件 virtual anchor。
    #   2) 以目前 G + route_weight 的實際最短路徑判斷是否穿越。
    #   3) 若 A -> B 中間先遇到 C，將 C 從原位置移到 A 後面，依實際 path
    #      的先後順序插入，直到不再發現這種 forward-crossing。
    #   4) 已經先前走過的 anchor 若只是再次經過，視為道路回程／重複路段，
    #      不為了消除 revisit 而任意洗牌。
    #
    # 這不是「禁止通過其他案件」，而是讓既然會在實際道路上先經過的案件，
    #      真正先出現在查核順序中。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def repair_virtual_anchor_traversal_order(order, label="路線"):
        if VEHICLE_MODE != "MOTOR" or not virtual_anchor_records:
            return list(order), 0, []

        anchor_point_to_node = {}
        for rec in virtual_anchor_records:
            try:
                pi = int(rec.get("點索引"))
                vn = int(rec.get("virtual_node"))
            except Exception:
                continue
            if vn in G and 0 <= pi < N and pi != int(start_idx):
                anchor_point_to_node[pi] = vn

        if not anchor_point_to_node:
            return list(order), 0, []

        repaired = [int(x) for x in order]
        moves = []
        max_passes = max(4, min(len(repaired) * 2, 256))

        for pass_no in range(1, max_passes + 1):
            changed = False
            pos_map = {int(x): i for i, x in enumerate(repaired)}

            for edge_pos in range(len(repaired) - 1):
                a = int(repaired[edge_pos])
                b = int(repaired[edge_pos + 1])
                if a == b:
                    continue

                # V65.64.23：只取已快取的「途中 virtual anchor 案件」，
                # 不再於 repair 迴圈中反覆建立完整 shortest_path。
                try:
                    intermediate_points = _va_pair_intermediate_points(a, b)
                except Exception:
                    intermediate_points = ()
                if not intermediate_points:
                    continue

                encountered = []
                seen_points = set()
                for pi in intermediate_points:
                    pi = int(pi)
                    if pi in seen_points:
                        continue
                    cur_pos = pos_map.get(pi, -1)
                    if cur_pos > edge_pos + 1 and pi in anchor_point_to_node:
                        encountered.append((pi, int(cur_pos), int(anchor_point_to_node[pi])))
                        seen_points.add(pi)

                if not encountered:
                    continue

                moving = [pi for pi, _, _ in encountered]
                moving_set = set(moving)
                repaired = [x for x in repaired if x not in moving_set]

                insert_pos = edge_pos + 1
                for pi in moving:
                    repaired.insert(insert_pos, int(pi))
                    insert_pos += 1

                for pi, old_pos, vn in encountered:
                    moves.append({
                        "label": str(label),
                        "pass": int(pass_no),
                        "from_edge_pos": int(edge_pos),
                        "from_point": int(a),
                        "to_point": int(b),
                        "moved_point": int(pi),
                        "virtual_node": int(vn),
                        "old_position": int(old_pos),
                        "new_position": int(insert_pos - len(moving) + moving.index(pi)),
                    })

                changed = True
                break

            if not changed:
                break

        return repaired, len(moves), moves

    def count_forward_virtual_anchor_crossings(order):
        """V65.64.23：以 directed pair cache 檢查 forward-crossing。

        舊版 v65.64.15 在每一個全點一次 relocate / swap 候選中重新呼叫
        get_shortest_path()，造成大量暫時的 shortest-path 節點串列與記憶體壓力。
        本版只查 _VA_PAIR_INTERMEDIATE_CACHE；cache miss 才建立一次 shortest path，
        且 cache 只保存中途 virtual anchor 案件索引，不保存完整 OSM path。
        """
        if VEHICLE_MODE != "MOTOR" or not virtual_anchor_records:
            return 0, []
        if not _VA_NODE_TO_POINT:
            return 0, []

        pos_map = {int(x): i for i, x in enumerate(order)}
        seq = [int(x) for x in order]
        hits = []

        for edge_pos, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(seq[:-1], seq[1:])):
            try:
                intermediate_points = _va_pair_intermediate_points(int(a), int(b))
            except Exception:
                intermediate_points = ()
            if not intermediate_points:
                continue

            seen = set()
            for pi in intermediate_points:
                pi = int(pi)
                if pi in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(pi)
                cp = pos_map.get(pi, -1)
                if cp > edge_pos + 1:
                    hits.append({
                        "edge_pos": int(edge_pos),
                        "from_point": int(a),
                        "to_point": int(b),
                        "intermediate_point": int(pi),
                        "intermediate_position": int(cp),
                    })
        return len(hits), hits

    _global_before_virtual_repair = list(global_order)
    global_order, _global_va_move_count, _global_va_moves = repair_virtual_anchor_traversal_order(
        global_order, label="全域路線"
    )
    if _global_va_move_count:
        final_global_distance = fast_route_distance_v9(global_order) if 'fast_route_distance_v9' in locals() else route_distance(global_order)
        final_global_objective = route_objective_v9(global_order) if 'route_objective_v9' in locals() else final_global_distance
        print(
            f"V65.64.23 Virtual anchor 實際通過順序驗證：移動 {_global_va_move_count} 次"
        )
        for rec in _global_va_moves[:20]:
            print(
                f"  {work_df.iloc[rec['from_point']]['名稱']} → {work_df.iloc[rec['to_point']]['名稱']}"
                f"：提前 {work_df.iloc[rec['moved_point']]['名稱']}"
            )
        if len(_global_va_moves) > 20:
            print(f"  ... 其餘 {_global_va_moves.__len__() - 20} 筆略過。")
    else:
        print("V65.64.23 Virtual anchor 實際通過順序修正：0 次")

    _global_va_remaining_count, _global_va_remaining_hits = count_forward_virtual_anchor_crossings(global_order)
    if _global_va_remaining_count:
        print(f"⚠️ V65.64.23 全域路線修正後仍有 {_global_va_remaining_count} 段 forward-crossing")
    else:
        print("✅ V65.64.23 全域路線修正後 Virtual anchor forward-crossing：0 段")

    # V65.9.3：補回每日分界所需的全域查核順序。
    # global_order 的第 0 筆固定為起點，後續才是查核點；
    # 每日 DP 必須以這個已完成「近到遠」定向的順序作為唯一母序列。
    visit_order = [int(x) for x in global_order[1:]]
    if len(visit_order) != N - 1:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"全域查核順序建立失敗：visit_order={len(visit_order)}、預期={N - 1}。"
        )

    print(
        f"實際旅行時間矩陣非對角可達率："
        f"{(time_finite.sum()-N)/(N*N-N)*100:.2f}%"
    )


    def drive_minutes_from_meters(distance_m):
        # 僅保留給不需要依道路類型分速的舊呼叫；實際每日工時一律使用 TIME_D。
        if not np.isfinite(distance_m):
            return float("inf")
        speed = MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH
        return float(distance_m / 1000.0 / speed * 60.0)


    def pair_drive_minutes(a, b):
        d = float(TIME_D[int(a), int(b)])
        return d if np.isfinite(d) else float("inf")


    def closed_day_drive_minutes_from_order(order):
        if not order or order[0] != start_idx:
            return float("inf")
        if len(order) == 1:
            return 0.0
        total = 0.0
        for a, b in zip(order[:-1], order[1:]):
            d = pair_drive_minutes(a, b)
            if not np.isfinite(d):
                return float("inf")
            total += d
        back = pair_drive_minutes(order[-1], start_idx)
        if not np.isfinite(back):
            return float("inf")
        return float(total + back)


    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # 第五階段使用：計算兩個地籍點之間的「真正 OSM 道路路徑」
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # 注意：前面的 ROAD_D 只儲存距離；第五階段還需要實際道路 geometry。
    # v12 呼叫了 route_pair()，但漏了函式定義，導致 NameError。
    # 這裡直接從目前 OSM MultiDiGraph 以 length 最短路徑取得 geometry。

    def route_pair(a, b, weight_attr="route_weight"):
        """
        回傳地籍點 a -> b 的本機 OSM 最低指定權重路徑。

        weight_attr：
          - route_weight：每日方案與既有路線，維持原本交通工具／汽車道路適用性成本。
          - travel_time_min：V50 全點一次跑完方案，直接以旅行時間計算獨立路線。

        回傳：
          distance  : 公尺
          path      : OSM node 序列
          geometry  : [(lon, lat), ...]，內部統一使用 GeoJSON 順序；Folium 繪圖時再轉為 [lat, lon]

        注意：geometry 統一為 (lon, lat)，避免 RoadIndex、GeoJSON、Folium 之間座標順序不一致。
        """
        a = int(a)
        b = int(b)

        # 同一 snap node：道路距離與 geometry 都是 0。
        if int(nearest_nodes[a]) == int(nearest_nodes[b]):
            return {
                "distance": 0.0,
                "path": [int(nearest_nodes[a])],
                "geometry": [(float(coords[a, 0]), float(coords[a, 1]))],
            }

        source = int(nearest_nodes[a])
        target = int(nearest_nodes[b])

        # V53：不要在第五階段重新呼叫 NetworkX shortest_path。
        # 前面已用 SciPy Dijkstra 計算全部地籍點來源，因此直接回溯
        # 對應 predecessor row，即可取得相同權重下的 OSM node path。
        predecessor = ROUTE_PREDECESSOR if weight_attr == "route_weight" else TIME_PREDECESSOR
        try:
            source_row = int(a)
            source_pos = int(TARGET_POS[source_row])
            target_pos = int(GRAPH_NODE_POS[target])
            pred_row = predecessor[source_row]
            if source_pos == target_pos:
                path = [source]
            else:
                path_pos_rev = [target_pos]
                cur = target_pos
                max_steps = GRAPH_SIZE + 5
                steps = 0
                while cur != source_pos and steps < max_steps:
                    prev = int(pred_row[cur])
                    if prev < 0:
                        raise nx.NetworkXNoPath
                    path_pos_rev.append(prev)
                    cur = prev
                    steps += 1
                if cur != source_pos:
                    raise nx.NetworkXNoPath
                path = [int(GRAPH_NODE_LIST[pos]) for pos in reversed(path_pos_rev)]
        except (IndexError, KeyError, ValueError):
            return {
                "distance": float("inf"),
                "path": [],
                "geometry": [],
            }
        except nx.NetworkXNoPath:
            return {
                "distance": float("inf"),
                "path": [],
                "geometry": [],
            }

        if not path:
            return {
                "distance": float("inf"),
                "path": [],
                "geometry": [],
            }

        # 單點路徑。
        if len(path) == 1:
            node = path[0]
            nd = G.nodes[node]
            lon = float(nd.get("x", coords[a, 0]))
            lat = float(nd.get("y", coords[a, 1]))
            return {
                "distance": 0.0,
                "path": path,
                "geometry": [(lon, lat)],
            }

        geometry_coords = []
        total_distance = 0.0
        total_travel_time = 0.0

        for u, v in zip(path[:-1], path[1:]):
            data = G.get_edge_data(u, v)
            if data is None:
                continue

            # MultiDiGraph：與 shortest_path() 同樣依 route_weight 選平行 edge；
            # 輸出距離仍採該 edge 的實際 length。
            best_edge = None
            best_weight = float("inf")

            for _, attrs in data.items():
                try:
                    route_w = float(attrs.get(weight_attr, attrs.get("length", float("inf"))))
                except Exception:
                    route_w = float("inf")
                if route_w < best_weight:
                    best_weight = route_w
                    best_edge = attrs

            if best_edge is None:
                continue

            # 使用實際 edge length 累計幾何路徑距離。
            # V45 原版這裡誤用了未定義的 best_len，會造成 NameError。
            try:
                best_len = float(best_edge.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
            except Exception:
                best_len = 0.0
            total_distance += best_len
            try:
                total_travel_time += float(best_edge.get("travel_time_min", 0.0) or 0.0)
            except Exception:
                total_travel_time += 0.0

            edge_geom = best_edge.get("geometry")

            if edge_geom is not None:
                # Shapely LineString.coords 與本程式內部 geometry 一律使用 (lon, lat)。
                edge_geometry_coords = [
                    (float(lon), float(lat))
                    for lon, lat in edge_geom.coords
                ]
            else:
                udata = G.nodes[u]
                vdata = G.nodes[v]
                edge_geometry_coords = [
                    (float(udata["x"]), float(udata["y"])),
                    (float(vdata["x"]), float(vdata["y"])),
                ]

            if not geometry_coords:
                geometry_coords.extend(edge_geometry_coords)
            else:
                if geometry_coords[-1] == edge_geometry_coords[0]:
                    geometry_coords.extend(edge_geometry_coords[1:])
                else:
                    geometry_coords.extend(edge_geometry_coords)

        # 若 edge geometry 不完整，至少用 node 座標補上。
        if not geometry_coords:
            geometry_coords = [
                (float(G.nodes[n]["x"]), float(G.nodes[n]["y"]))
                for n in path
            ]

        # V49：第五階段輸出距離改採「實際 route_weight 最短路徑所選 edge」的
        # length 總和。舊版雖然 geometry 使用 route_weight 路徑，但 distance 又
        # 被 ROAD_D[a,b] 覆蓋成「純道路長度最短路徑」，因此 CAR 會出現摘要總距離
        # 與實際速度分類里程不一致。現在兩者以同一條最終 geometry 路徑為準。
        road_distance = float(total_distance)
        if not np.isfinite(road_distance):
            road_distance = float(ROAD_D[a, b])

        return {
            "distance": road_distance,
            "path": path,
            "geometry": geometry_coords,
            "travel_time_min": float(total_travel_time),
        }


    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # 31-0. V50：所有點位一次跑完（不受每日時間上限）
    #
    # 目的：提供一條與「分日工作」完全獨立計算的單趟閉合路線：
    #   固定起點 → 105 個查核點各一次 → 固定起點
    #
    # 重要：不沿用後面的 daily batches，也不把每日 5 小時納入目標。
    # 以 travel_time_min 作為主要路線成本，先用 nearest-insertion 建立
    # 獨立初始解，再以 relocate + swap 最小化完整閉合旅行時間。
    # 這樣即使最後順序與分日方案接近，也確實是另一套獨立計算。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------

    def unrestricted_roundtrip_time(order):
        """固定起點閉合旅行時間（分鐘）：start → visits → start。"""
        if not order or int(order[0]) != int(start_idx):
            return float("inf")
        if len(order) == 1:
            return 0.0
        total = 0.0
        for a, b in zip(order[:-1], order[1:]):
            t = float(TIME_D[int(a), int(b)])
            if not np.isfinite(t):
                return float("inf")
            total += t
        back = float(TIME_D[int(order[-1]), int(start_idx)])
        if not np.isfinite(back):
            return float("inf")
        return float(total + back)

    def unrestricted_roundtrip_distance(order):
        """固定起點閉合道路距離（純道路 length 矩陣）。"""
        if not order or int(order[0]) != int(start_idx):
            return float("inf")
        if len(order) == 1:
            return 0.0
        total = 0.0
        for a, b in zip(order[:-1], order[1:]):
            d = float(ROAD_D[int(a), int(b)])
            if not np.isfinite(d):
                return float("inf")
            total += d
        back = float(ROAD_D[int(order[-1]), int(start_idx)])
        if not np.isfinite(back):
            return float("inf")
        return float(total + back)

    def build_nearest_insertion_seed(start, points):
        """V65.23：nearest-insertion seed 的數學定義不變，改用 NumPy 批次評估。
