        保留 v65.22 原本的平手順序：remaining 點依既有順序優先，
        同一點的 insertion position 依由小到大優先。
        """
        remaining = [int(x) for x in points if int(x) != int(start)]
        if not remaining:
            return [int(start)]

        nearest = min(
            remaining,
            key=lambda x: float(TIME_D[int(start), int(x)])
            if np.isfinite(TIME_D[int(start), int(x)]) else float("inf"),
        )
        route = [int(start), int(nearest)]
        remaining.remove(int(nearest))

        while remaining:
            rem = np.asarray(remaining, dtype=np.int32)
            # insertion positions：1..len(route)，最後一格代表插在 route 尾端、回到 start 前。
            left_nodes = np.asarray(route, dtype=np.int32)
            # pos=1 -> route[0] 左邊，pos=len(route) -> route[-1] 右邊接回 start。
            right_nodes = np.asarray(route[1:] + [int(start)], dtype=np.int32)
            # route 的每一個節點都可作為 insertion 的左端。
            # 以上 left_nodes 正好為 route[0:len(route)]。
            base_lr = TIME_D[left_nodes, right_nodes]
            tx_left = TIME_D[left_nodes[None, :], rem[:, None]]
            tx_right = TIME_D[rem[:, None], right_nodes[None, :]]
            delta = tx_left + tx_right - base_lr[None, :]
            finite = np.isfinite(delta)
            if not np.any(finite):
                raise RuntimeError("V50 全點一次跑完路線：無法插入剩餘點位。")
            work = np.where(finite, delta, np.inf)
            flat_idx = int(np.argmin(work))
            rem_idx, pos_idx = np.unravel_index(flat_idx, work.shape)
            best_x = int(rem[rem_idx])
            best_pos = int(pos_idx) + 1
            route.insert(best_pos, best_x)
            remaining.remove(best_x)
        return route

    def _ai_edge_time(order, edge_pos):
        a = int(order[edge_pos])
        b = int(order[edge_pos + 1]) if edge_pos + 1 < len(order) else int(start_idx)
        return float(TIME_D[a, b])

    # V65.64.23：一般案件 physical forward-crossing 硬性限制。
    # 與 Virtual anchor 相同，僅保存「路段 A→B 實際 TIME path 中間經過的案件索引」，
    # 不保存完整 OSM path，因此候選檢查不會複製大型 NetworkX path 物件。
    _ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER = {"relocate": 0, "swap": 0}
    _ALL_IN_ONE_GENERAL_PAIR_CACHE = {}

    _ALL_IN_ONE_NODE_TO_POINTS = {}
    for _pi, _n in enumerate(nearest_nodes):
        try:
            _nid = int(_n)
        except Exception:
            continue
        _ALL_IN_ONE_NODE_TO_POINTS.setdefault(_nid, []).append(int(_pi))

    def _all_in_one_reconstruct_time_path(a, b):
        a = int(a); b = int(b)
        try:
            source = int(nearest_nodes[a])
            target = int(nearest_nodes[b])
            source_pos = int(TARGET_POS[a])
            target_pos = int(GRAPH_NODE_POS[target])
            pred_row = TIME_PREDECESSOR[a]
        except Exception:
            return []
        if source_pos == target_pos:
            return [source]
        cur = target_pos
        rev = [cur]
        steps = 0
        max_steps = int(GRAPH_SIZE) + 5
        while cur != source_pos and steps < max_steps:
            prev = int(pred_row[cur])
            if prev < 0:
                return []
            rev.append(prev)
            cur = prev
            steps += 1
        if cur != source_pos:
            return []
        return [int(GRAPH_NODE_LIST[pos]) for pos in reversed(rev)]

    def _all_in_one_general_intermediate_points(a, b):
        key = (int(a), int(b))
        cached = _ALL_IN_ONE_GENERAL_PAIR_CACHE.get(key)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        path = _all_in_one_reconstruct_time_path(a, b)
        if not path:
            _ALL_IN_ONE_GENERAL_PAIR_CACHE[key] = ()
            return ()
        seen = set()
        mids = []
        for _node in path[1:-1]:
            for _pi in _ALL_IN_ONE_NODE_TO_POINTS.get(int(_node), []):
                _pi = int(_pi)
                if _pi in (int(a), int(b)) or _pi in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(_pi)
                mids.append(_pi)
        out = tuple(mids)
        _ALL_IN_ONE_GENERAL_PAIR_CACHE[key] = out
        return out

    def _all_in_one_general_forward_crossings(order):
        seq = [int(x) for x in order]
        pos = {x: i for i, x in enumerate(seq)}
        hits = []
        for epos, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(seq[:-1], seq[1:]), start=1):
            for pi in _all_in_one_general_intermediate_points(a, b):
                cp = pos.get(int(pi), -1)
                if cp > epos:
                    hits.append((int(epos), int(a), int(b), int(pi), int(cp)))
        return hits

    def _all_in_one_general_order_feasible(order):
        return len(_all_in_one_general_forward_crossings(order)) == 0

    def _all_in_one_repair_general_physical_order(order, max_passes=256):
        repaired = [int(x) for x in order]
        moves = []
        for pass_no in range(1, int(max_passes) + 1):
            pos = {int(x): i for i, x in enumerate(repaired)}
            changed = False
            for epos, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(repaired[:-1], repaired[1:]), start=1):
                mids = _all_in_one_general_intermediate_points(a, b)
                encountered = []
                seen = set()
                for pi in mids:
                    pi = int(pi)
                    ppos = pos.get(pi, -1)
                    if pi in (int(a), int(b)) or pi in seen:
                        continue
                    if ppos > epos:
                        encountered.append((pi, ppos))
                        seen.add(pi)
                if not encountered:
                    continue
                moving = [pi for pi, _ in encountered]
                moving_set = set(moving)
                repaired = [x for x in repaired if x not in moving_set]
                insert_pos = epos
                for pi in moving:
                    repaired.insert(insert_pos, pi)
                    insert_pos += 1
                for order_in_move, (pi, old_pos) in enumerate(encountered, start=1):
                    moves.append({
                        "pass": int(pass_no),
                        "段次": int(epos),
                        "起點索引": int(a),
                        "起點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[a]["名稱"]),
                        "終點索引": int(b),
                        "終點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[b]["名稱"]),
                        "提前案件索引": int(pi),
                        "提前案件名稱": str(work_df.iloc[pi]["名稱"]),
                        "原排定序號": int(old_pos),
                        "新排定序號": int(epos + order_in_move),
                    })
                changed = True
                break
            if not changed:
                break
        return repaired, moves

    def _all_in_one_combined_order_feasible(order):
        if not _all_in_one_va_order_feasible(order):
            return False
        return _all_in_one_general_order_feasible(order)

    _ALL_IN_ONE_VA_REJECT_COUNTER = {"relocate": 0, "swap": 0}

    def _all_in_one_va_order_feasible(order):
        """V65.64.23：全點一次候選的完整 Virtual anchor 順序硬性檢查。"""
        if (VEHICLE_MODE != "MOTOR"
                or not VIRTUAL_ANCHOR_ROUTE_ORDER_CONSTRAINT_ENABLED
                or not virtual_anchor_records):
            return True
        count, _ = count_forward_virtual_anchor_crossings(order)
        return int(count) == 0

    def unrestricted_relocate(order, max_passes=12, respect_virtual_anchor=True, respect_physical_order=True):
        """V65.64.23：全點一次 relocate，納入 Virtual anchor 硬性順序限制。

        原本的 TIME_D 成本與候選掃描邏輯保留；若候選造成 Virtual anchor
        forward-crossing，就跳過該候選並繼續尋找下一個可接受候選。
        """
        current = list(order)
        current_score = unrestricted_roundtrip_time(current)
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            raise RuntimeError("V50 全點一次跑完初始路線不可達。")
        n = len(current)
        for _ in range(max_passes):
            best_delta = 0.0
            best_src = None
            best_dst = None
            for src in range(1, n):
                x = int(current[src])
                prev = int(current[src - 1])
                nxt = int(current[src + 1]) if src + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
                remove_delta = (
                    float(TIME_D[prev, nxt])
                    - float(TIME_D[prev, x])
                    - float(TIME_D[x, nxt])
                )
                if not np.isfinite(remove_delta):
                    continue

                base = current[:src] + current[src + 1:]
                dst = np.arange(1, len(base) + 1, dtype=np.int32)
                valid = (dst != src) & (dst != src + 1)
                if not np.any(valid):
                    continue
                dst_v = dst[valid]
                left = np.asarray([base[int(d) - 1] for d in dst_v], dtype=np.int32)
                right = np.asarray(
                    [base[int(d)] if int(d) < len(base) else int(start_idx) for d in dst_v],
                    dtype=np.int32,
                )
                insert_delta = TIME_D[left, x] + TIME_D[x, right] - TIME_D[left, right]
                deltas = remove_delta + insert_delta
                finite = np.isfinite(deltas)
                if not np.any(finite):
                    continue
                local = np.where(finite, deltas, np.inf)

                # 依原始候選順序的成本升冪找可接受候選。
                candidate_order = np.argsort(local, kind="stable")
                for k_np in candidate_order:
                    k = int(k_np)
                    delta = float(local[k])
                    if not np.isfinite(delta):
                        break
                    if delta >= best_delta - 1e-9:
                        break
                    dst_pos = int(dst_v[k])
                    candidate = current[:src] + current[src + 1:]
                    candidate.insert(dst_pos, x)

                    if respect_virtual_anchor and not _all_in_one_va_order_feasible(candidate):
                        _ALL_IN_ONE_VA_REJECT_COUNTER["relocate"] += 1
                        continue
                    if respect_physical_order and not _all_in_one_general_order_feasible(candidate):
                        _ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER["relocate"] += 1
                        continue

                    best_delta = delta
                    best_src = src
                    best_dst = dst_pos
                    break

            if best_src is None:
                break
            x = current.pop(int(best_src))
            current.insert(int(best_dst), x)
            current_score += best_delta
        return current

    def unrestricted_swap(order, max_passes=8, respect_virtual_anchor=True, respect_physical_order=True):
        """V65.64.23：全點一次 swap，納入 Virtual anchor 硬性順序限制。"""
        current = list(order)
        current_score = unrestricted_roundtrip_time(current)
        n = len(current)
        for _ in range(max_passes):
            best_delta = 0.0
            best_i = None
            best_j = None
            for i in range(1, n - 1):
                for j in range(i + 1, n):
                    affected = sorted(set([i - 1, i, j - 1, j]))
                    old_cost = 0.0
                    new_cost = 0.0

                    def node_after_swap(p):
                        if p == i:
                            return int(current[j])
                        if p == j:
                            return int(current[i])
                        return int(current[p])

                    valid = True
                    for ep in affected:
                        a_old = int(current[ep])
                        b_old = int(current[ep + 1]) if ep + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
                        old_t = float(TIME_D[a_old, b_old])
                        a_new = node_after_swap(ep)
                        b_new = node_after_swap(ep + 1) if ep + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
                        new_t = float(TIME_D[a_new, b_new])
                        if not (np.isfinite(old_t) and np.isfinite(new_t)):
                            valid = False
                            break
                        old_cost += old_t
                        new_cost += new_t
                    if not valid:
                        continue
                    delta = new_cost - old_cost
                    if delta >= best_delta - 1e-9:
                        continue

                    candidate = list(current)
                    candidate[i], candidate[j] = candidate[j], candidate[i]
                    if respect_virtual_anchor and not _all_in_one_va_order_feasible(candidate):
                        _ALL_IN_ONE_VA_REJECT_COUNTER["swap"] += 1
                        continue
                    if respect_physical_order and not _all_in_one_general_order_feasible(candidate):
                        _ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER["swap"] += 1
                        continue

                    best_delta = float(delta)
                    best_i = i
                    best_j = j

            if best_i is None:
                break
            current[best_i], current[best_j] = current[best_j], current[best_i]
            current_score += best_delta
        return current

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    # V65.48：全點一次方案純計時起點；包含 seed＋relocate＋swap＋方向整理。
    _perf_all_in_one_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    print("附加方案：所有點位一次跑完（不受每日時間限制；單日最終方案將於實際 OSM 路線比較後決定）")
    print("=" * 70)
    print("v65.64.23：全點一次方案正式納入 Virtual anchor＋一般案件物理通過順序硬性限制。")
    all_in_one_order = build_nearest_insertion_seed(start_idx, ALL_MEMBERS)

    # v65.64.23：seed 同時先滿足 Virtual anchor 與一般案件 physical order。
    all_in_one_order, _all_in_one_seed_va_moves, _all_in_one_seed_va_records = repair_virtual_anchor_traversal_order(
        all_in_one_order, label='全點一次 seed'
    )
    all_in_one_order, _all_in_one_seed_general_moves = _all_in_one_repair_general_physical_order(all_in_one_order)
    if _all_in_one_seed_va_moves:
        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次 seed Virtual anchor 可行化：移動 {_all_in_one_seed_va_moves} 次")
    else:
        print("V65.64.23 全點一次 seed Virtual anchor 可行化：0 次")

    seed_time = unrestricted_roundtrip_time(all_in_one_order)
    _ALL_IN_ONE_VA_REJECT_COUNTER["relocate"] = 0
    _ALL_IN_ONE_VA_REJECT_COUNTER["swap"] = 0
    _ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER["relocate"] = 0
    _ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER["swap"] = 0
    all_in_one_order = unrestricted_relocate(
        all_in_one_order, max_passes=12, respect_virtual_anchor=True, respect_physical_order=True
    )
    all_in_one_order = unrestricted_swap(
        all_in_one_order, max_passes=8, respect_virtual_anchor=True, respect_physical_order=True
    )
    print(
        "V65.64.23 全點一次 Virtual anchor 候選硬限制："
        f"relocate 拒絕={_ALL_IN_ONE_VA_REJECT_COUNTER['relocate']}、"
        f"swap 拒絕={_ALL_IN_ONE_VA_REJECT_COUNTER['swap']}"
    )
    print(
        "V65.64.23 全點一次一般案件 physical 候選硬限制："
        f"relocate 拒絕={_ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER['relocate']}、"
        f"swap 拒絕={_ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER['swap']}"
    )
    print(f"V65.64.23 Virtual anchor pair cache：{len(_VA_PAIR_INTERMEDIATE_CACHE)} 組（僅保存中途案件索引，不保存完整 OSM path）")

    # 最後固定由近到遠；方向反轉可能重新產生 crossing，因此再做一次最終修正。
    all_in_one_order = orient_order_near_to_far(all_in_one_order, label='一次完成路線')

    _all_in_one_before_virtual_repair = list(all_in_one_order)
    all_in_one_order, _all_in_one_va_move_count, _all_in_one_va_moves = repair_virtual_anchor_traversal_order(
        all_in_one_order, label='全點一次路線'
    )
    if _all_in_one_va_move_count:
        print(
            f"V65.64.23 全點一次路線 Virtual anchor 最終驗證：移動 {_all_in_one_va_move_count} 次"
        )
        for rec in _all_in_one_va_moves[:20]:
            print(
                f"  {work_df.iloc[rec['from_point']]['名稱']} → {work_df.iloc[rec['to_point']]['名稱']}"
                f"：提前 {work_df.iloc[rec['moved_point']]['名稱']}"
            )
        if len(_all_in_one_va_moves) > 20:
            print(f"  ... 其餘 {len(_all_in_one_va_moves) - 20} 筆略過。")
    else:
        print("V65.64.23 全點一次路線 Virtual anchor 最終驗證：0 次")

    _all_in_one_va_remaining_count, _all_in_one_va_remaining_hits = count_forward_virtual_anchor_crossings(all_in_one_order)
    if _all_in_one_va_remaining_count:
        print(f"⚠️ V65.64.23 全點一次路線最終仍有 {_all_in_one_va_remaining_count} 段 forward-crossing")
    else:
        print("✅ V65.64.23 全點一次路線 Virtual anchor forward-crossing：0 段")

    all_in_one_time_min = unrestricted_roundtrip_time(all_in_one_order)
    all_in_one_distance_m = unrestricted_roundtrip_distance(all_in_one_order)
    all_in_one_service_min = (N - 1) * POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES
    all_in_one_total_min = all_in_one_time_min + all_in_one_service_min
    print(
        f"全點一次跑完：{N - 1} 個查核點 | 起點＋查核點共 {N} 點 | 無每日工時上限"
    )
    print(f"獨立初始閉合旅行時間：{seed_time/60:.2f} hr")
    print(f"最佳化後閉合旅行時間：{all_in_one_time_min/60:.2f} hr")
    if len(all_in_one_order) > 1:
        _first_idx = int(all_in_one_order[1])
        _first_leg_time = float(TIME_D[int(start_idx), _first_idx])
        _first_leg_dist = float(ROAD_D[int(start_idx), _first_idx])
        print(
            f"全點一次跑完首站：{work_df.iloc[_first_idx]['名稱']} | "
            f"起點→首站：{_first_leg_dist/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"約 {_first_leg_time:.1f} min"
        )
    print(f"點位處理時間：{all_in_one_service_min/60:.2f} hr")
    print(f"預估總外勤時間：{all_in_one_total_min/60:.2f} hr")

    _perf_mark("all_in_one_optimization", _perf_all_in_one_t0, emit=True)


    # ============================================================
    # V65.64.23：純全點一次隔離測試。
    # 先完成全點一次的「結果路線」，確認一般案件與 Virtual anchor 都為 0 crossing，
    # 然後直接輸出並 return；這一輪不進每日工時／每日點數／每日分界。
    # ============================================================
    if V65_64_20_VA_ONLY_TEST and VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
        print()
        print("=" * 70)
        print("V65.64.23：進入 Virtual anchor＋一般案件／全點一次純路線隔離測試")
        print("本階段完成後直接結束；不執行每日工時、每日點數、每日分界、跨日交換與第五階段")
        print("=" * 70)

        # orient_order_near_to_far 可能改變方向，因此再做一次 combined repair。
        all_in_one_order, _va23_general_moves = _all_in_one_repair_general_physical_order(all_in_one_order)
        all_in_one_order, _va23_va_moves_count, _va23_va_moves = repair_virtual_anchor_traversal_order(
            all_in_one_order, label='V65.64.23 全點一次最終'
        )
        # 兩種 repair 交替一次，避免其中一種修正造成另一種 crossing。
        for _round in range(4):
            before = list(all_in_one_order)
            all_in_one_order, _ = _all_in_one_repair_general_physical_order(all_in_one_order)
            all_in_one_order, _, _ = repair_virtual_anchor_traversal_order(all_in_one_order, label=f'V65.64.23 final pass {_round+1}')
            if all_in_one_order == before:
                break

        _va23_general_hits = _all_in_one_general_forward_crossings(all_in_one_order)
        _va23_va_hits_count, _va23_va_hits = count_forward_virtual_anchor_crossings(all_in_one_order)

        # v65.64.23：不呼叫後段才定義的 route_pair_v65_8()，直接使用目前
        # G + TIME_PREDECESSOR 重建 route geometry；避免再次出現 UnboundLocalError，
        # 也避免建立大型額外 path cache。
        def _va23_edge_attrs(u, v):
            data = G.get_edge_data(int(u), int(v))
            if not data:
                return None
            best = None
            best_w = float("inf")
            items = data.values() if G.is_multigraph() else [data]
            for attrs in items:
                try:
                    w = float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    w = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(w) and w < best_w:
                    best_w = w
                    best = attrs
            return best

        def _va23_path_geometry(path):
            geom = []
            dist = 0.0
            tmin = 0.0
            for u, v in zip(path[:-1], path[1:]):
                attrs = _va23_edge_attrs(u, v)
                if attrs is None:
                    continue
                dist += float(attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
                tmin += float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", 0.0) or 0.0)
                eg = attrs.get("geometry")
                if eg is not None:
                    pts = [(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in eg.coords]
                else:
                    un = G.nodes[int(u)]; vn = G.nodes[int(v)]
                    pts = [
                        (float(un.get("x", 0.0)), float(un.get("y", 0.0))),
                        (float(vn.get("x", 0.0)), float(vn.get("y", 0.0))),
                    ]
                if not geom:
                    geom.extend(pts)
                elif geom[-1] == pts[0]:
                    geom.extend(pts[1:])
                else:
                    geom.extend(pts)
            return geom, dist, tmin

        _va23_total_dist = 0.0
        _va23_total_time = 0.0
        _va23_main_geom = []
        for _segno, (_a, _b) in enumerate(zip(all_in_one_order[:-1], all_in_one_order[1:]), start=1):
            path = _all_in_one_reconstruct_time_path(_a, _b)
            if not path:
                raise RuntimeError(f"V65.64.23 實際 OSM 路徑不可達：{work_df.iloc[_a]['名稱']} → {work_df.iloc[_b]['名稱']}")
            g, dist, tmin = _va23_path_geometry(path)
            _va23_total_dist += dist
            _va23_total_time += tmin
            if g:
                if not _va23_main_geom:
                    _va23_main_geom.extend(g)
                elif _va23_main_geom[-1] == g[0]:
                    _va23_main_geom.extend(g[1:])
                else:
                    _va23_main_geom.extend(g)

        _va23_back_path = _all_in_one_reconstruct_time_path(all_in_one_order[-1], int(start_idx))
        if not _va23_back_path:
            raise RuntimeError("V65.64.23 最後一點回固定起點不可達。")
        _, _va23_back_dist, _va23_back_time = _va23_path_geometry(_va23_back_path)
        _va23_total_dist += _va23_back_dist
        _va23_total_time += _va23_back_time

        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次一般案件 physical forward-crossing：{len(_va23_general_hits)} 段")
        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次 Virtual anchor forward-crossing：{int(_va23_va_hits_count)} 段")
        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次實際 OSM 路線距離：{_va23_total_dist / 1000.0:.2f} km")
        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次實際 OSM 駕駛時間：{_va23_total_time / 60.0:.2f} hr")

        if _va23_general_hits:
            for epos, a, b, pi, cp in _va23_general_hits[:30]:
                print(f"  一般案件第{epos}段：{work_df.iloc[a]['名稱']} → {work_df.iloc[b]['名稱']}，途中先遇到 {work_df.iloc[pi]['名稱']} (排定序號={cp})")
            raise RuntimeError("V65.64.23：全點一次路線仍存在一般案件 physical forward-crossing。")
        if _va23_va_hits_count:
            raise RuntimeError("V65.64.23：全點一次路線仍存在 Virtual anchor forward-crossing。")

        _va23_rows = []
        for seq, idx in enumerate(all_in_one_order):
            r = work_df.iloc[int(idx)]
            _va23_rows.append({
                "全點一次跑完序號": int(seq),
                "角色": "固定起點" if int(seq) == 0 else "查核點",
                "點索引": int(idx),
                "原始_df_index": int(r["_原始_df_index"]),
                "原始序號": int(r["_原始_df_index"]) + 1,
                "Excel列": int(r["_原始_df_index"]) + 2,
                "名稱": str(r["名稱"]),
                "縣市": r["縣市"] if "縣市" in r.index else "",
                "鄉鎮市區": r["鄉鎮市區"] if "鄉鎮市區" in r.index else "",
                "地段": r["地段"] if "地段" in r.index else "",
                "地號": r["地號"] if "地號" in r.index else "",
                "經度": float(coords[int(idx), 0]),
                "緯度": float(coords[int(idx), 1]),
            })
        _va23_prefix = "/content/MOTOR_VA_ONLY_v65.64.23"
        pd.DataFrame(_va23_rows).to_excel(_va23_prefix + "_route_order.xlsx", index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(_va23_rows).to_csv(_va23_prefix + "_route_order.csv", index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
        pd.DataFrame(_va23_general_hits).to_excel(_va23_prefix + "_general_forward_crossing.xlsx", index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(_all_in_one_seed_general_moves if '_all_in_one_seed_general_moves' in globals() else []).to_excel(_va23_prefix + "_seed_physical_moves.xlsx", index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(_ALL_IN_ONE_GENERAL_PAIR_CACHE and [
            {"起點索引": k[0], "終點索引": k[1], "途中案件索引": ";".join(map(str, v))}
            for k, v in _ALL_IN_ONE_GENERAL_PAIR_CACHE.items()
        ] or []).to_excel(_va23_prefix + "_pair_cache.xlsx", index=False)
        pd.DataFrame([
            {"項目": "測試版本", "數值": "v65.64.23 TEST"},
            {"項目": "Virtual anchor forward-crossing", "數值": int(_va23_va_hits_count)},
            {"項目": "一般案件 physical forward-crossing", "數值": int(len(_va23_general_hits))},
            {"項目": "一般案件候選硬限制 relocate 拒絕", "數值": int(_ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER["relocate"])},
            {"項目": "一般案件候選硬限制 swap 拒絕", "數值": int(_ALL_IN_ONE_PHYSICAL_REJECT_COUNTER["swap"])},
            {"項目": "全點一次實際OSM道路距離_km", "數值": float(_va23_total_dist) / 1000.0},
            {"項目": "全點一次實際OSM駕駛時間_hr", "數值": float(_va23_total_time) / 60.0},
        ]).to_excel(_va23_prefix + "_summary.xlsx", index=False)

        print("✅ V65.64.23：Virtual anchor＋一般案件實際物理通過順序測試完成")
        print("✅ 不進入每日工時／每日點數／每日分界／跨日交換／每日第五階段")
        return {
            "VEHICLE_MODE": "MOTOR",
            "VEHICLE_LABEL": "機車",
            "work_df": work_df,
            "all_in_one_order": all_in_one_order,
            "all_in_one_actual_distance": float(_va23_total_dist),
            "all_in_one_actual_drive_min": float(_va23_total_time),
            "virtual_anchor_forward_crossing": int(_va23_va_hits_count),
            "general_point_forward_crossing": int(len(_va23_general_hits)),
            "test_only": True,
        }

    # ============================================================

    # 31-1. 每日工時＋固定起點「共同最佳化」
    # V65.9.3：每日分界邏輯修正版＋加速＋visit_order 修正
    #
    # 核心原則：
    #   1) 全域 visit_order 維持既有「由近到遠」方向，不再做跨日 point transfer / swap。
    #   2) 每日分界改由「連續區段 DP」一次共同決定，最佳化的是「哪裡切日」，
    #      而不是重新洗牌「哪個點屬於哪一天」。
    #   3) 預先快取所有連續區段的時間／距離／可行性，DP 不再反覆呼叫
    #      segment_day_stats_fast()。
    #   4) 每日路線最佳化後若超過每日硬上限，直接退回該日 DP 原始順序，
    #      不允許把可行分界變成 inf。
    #   5) 每日最佳化只執行一次；取消 v34 的跨日搬移／交換 4-pass，
    #      大幅降低候選路線重算量。
    #   6) 保留 OSM 路網、TIME_D、ROAD_D、CAR 道路適用性、水體避讓及既有
    #      optimize_closed_day() 核心，不修改第五階段實際道路 geometry。
    #
    # 這一版的「跨日共同最佳化」定義為：
    #   全部工作日的切點同時進入同一個 DP 目標函數，
    #   並以分界連續性／反向重跑風險作為次要成本。
    #   不再用 transfer / swap 改變全域近→遠查核順序。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------


    # =====================================================================
    # V65.64.23：MOTOR／全點一次純路線隔離測試
    #
    # 本版在 v65.64.21 的 Virtual anchor 驗證基礎上，進一步處理：
    #   「A → B 的實際 TIME 最短道路 path 中，提前經過排在 B 後面的
    #    一般查核點 C」的 physical forward-crossing。
    #
    # 這一版採「事後物理順序可行化」而不是把所有一般案件的 crossing
    # 大量塞進 relocate/swap 候選函數，避免再次造成 RAM 壓力。
    # 修正方式與 Virtual anchor 相同：
    #   A → B path 先遇到 C，而且 C 原本排在 B 後面
    #   → 把 C 移到 A 後面，依實際 path 先後順序插入
    #   → 反覆直到沒有 forward-crossing。
    #
    # 本階段完成後直接結束，不執行每日工時／每日點數／每日分界／跨日交換／第五階段。
    # =====================================================================
    if V65_64_20_VA_ONLY_TEST and VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
        print()
        print("=" * 70)
        print("V65.64.23：進入 Virtual anchor／全點一次純路線隔離測試")
        print("本階段完成後直接結束；不執行每日工時、每日點數、每日分界、跨日交換與第五階段")
        print("=" * 70)

        _va22_order = [int(x) for x in all_in_one_order]

        # 以 Virtual anchor 記錄建立 virtual node → point 對應。
        _va22_va_node_to_point = {}
        for _rec in virtual_anchor_records:
            try:
                _pi = int(_rec.get("點索引"))
                _vn = int(_rec.get("virtual_node"))
            except Exception:
                continue
            if _vn in G and 0 <= _pi < N and _pi != int(start_idx):
                _va22_va_node_to_point[int(_vn)] = int(_pi)

        # 全點一次方案以 TIME_D 為最佳化基礎，因此 physical traversal 一律使用 TIME_PREDECESSOR。
        def _va22_reconstruct_time_path(a, b):
            a = int(a); b = int(b)
            source = int(nearest_nodes[a])
            target = int(nearest_nodes[b])
            try:
                source_pos = int(TARGET_POS[a])
                target_pos = int(GRAPH_NODE_POS[target])
                pred_row = TIME_PREDECESSOR[a]
            except Exception:
                return []

            if source_pos == target_pos:
                return [source]

            cur = target_pos
            rev = [cur]
            steps = 0
            max_steps = int(GRAPH_SIZE) + 5
            while cur != source_pos and steps < max_steps:
                prev = int(pred_row[cur])
                if prev < 0:
                    return []
                rev.append(prev)
                cur = prev
                steps += 1
            if cur != source_pos:
                return []
            return [int(GRAPH_NODE_LIST[p]) for p in reversed(rev)]

        # 所有查核點 ingress node → point 索引；virtual / real 都納入。
        _va22_node_to_points = {}
        for _pi, _n in enumerate(nearest_nodes):
            try:
                _nid = int(_n)
            except Exception:
                continue
            _va22_node_to_points.setdefault(_nid, []).append(int(_pi))

        # 只保存「中間案件索引」，不保存完整 path，避免 RAM 壓力。
        _va22_general_pair_cache = {}
        _va22_va_pair_cache = {}

        def _va22_pair_intermediate_points(a, b):
            key = (int(a), int(b))
            cached = _va22_general_pair_cache.get(key)
            if cached is not None:
                return cached
            path = _va22_reconstruct_time_path(a, b)
            if not path:
                _va22_general_pair_cache[key] = ()
                return ()
            seen = set()
            mids = []
            for _node in path[1:-1]:
                for _pi in _va22_node_to_points.get(int(_node), []):
                    if int(_pi) in (int(a), int(b)) or int(_pi) in seen:
                        continue
                    seen.add(int(_pi))
                    mids.append(int(_pi))
            out = tuple(mids)
            _va22_general_pair_cache[key] = out
            return out

        def _va22_virtual_pair_intermediate_points(a, b):
            key = (int(a), int(b))
            cached = _va22_va_pair_cache.get(key)
            if cached is not None:
                return cached
            path = _va22_reconstruct_time_path(a, b)
            if not path:
                _va22_va_pair_cache[key] = ()
                return ()
            seen = set()
            mids = []
            for _node in path[1:-1]:
                _pi = _va22_va_node_to_point.get(int(_node))
                if _pi is None or int(_pi) in (int(a), int(b)) or int(_pi) in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(int(_pi))
                mids.append(int(_pi))
            out = tuple(mids)
            _va22_va_pair_cache[key] = out
            return out

        def _va22_forward_crossings(order):
            seq = [int(x) for x in order]
            pos = {x: i for i, x in enumerate(seq)}
            hits = []
            for epos, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(seq[:-1], seq[1:]), start=1):
                for pi in _va22_pair_intermediate_points(a, b):
                    cp = pos.get(int(pi), -1)
                    if cp > epos:
                        hits.append((int(epos), int(a), int(b), int(pi), int(cp)))
            return hits

        def _va22_virtual_forward_crossings(order):
            seq = [int(x) for x in order]
            pos = {x: i for i, x in enumerate(seq)}
            hits = []
            for epos, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(seq[:-1], seq[1:]), start=1):
                for pi in _va22_virtual_pair_intermediate_points(a, b):
                    cp = pos.get(int(pi), -1)
                    if cp > epos:
                        hits.append((int(epos), int(a), int(b), int(pi), int(cp)))
            return hits

        def _va22_repair_physical_order(order, max_passes=256):
            repaired = [int(x) for x in order]
            moves = []
            for pass_no in range(1, int(max_passes) + 1):
                pos = {int(x): i for i, x in enumerate(repaired)}
                changed = False
                for epos, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(repaired[:-1], repaired[1:]), start=1):
                    mids = _va22_pair_intermediate_points(a, b)
                    encountered = []
                    seen = set()
                    for pi in mids:
                        pi = int(pi)
                        ppos = pos.get(pi, -1)
                        if pi in (int(a), int(b)) or pi in seen:
                            continue
                        if ppos > epos:
                            encountered.append((pi, ppos))
                            seen.add(pi)
                    if not encountered:
                        continue

                    moving = [pi for pi, _ in encountered]
                    moving_set = set(moving)
                    repaired = [x for x in repaired if x not in moving_set]
                    insert_pos = epos
                    for pi in moving:
                        repaired.insert(insert_pos, pi)
                        insert_pos += 1
                    for order_in_move, (pi, old_pos) in enumerate(encountered, start=1):
                        moves.append({
                            "pass": int(pass_no),
                            "段次": int(epos),
                            "起點索引": int(a),
                            "起點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[a]["名稱"]),
                            "終點索引": int(b),
                            "終點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[b]["名稱"]),
                            "提前案件索引": int(pi),
                            "提前案件名稱": str(work_df.iloc[pi]["名稱"]),
                            "原排定序號": int(old_pos),
                            "新排定序號": int(epos + order_in_move),
                        })
                    changed = True
                    break
                if not changed:
                    break
            return repaired, moves

        # 先確認 v65.64.20／v65.64.21 已解決的 Virtual anchor 條件仍為 0。
        _va22_va_before = _va22_virtual_forward_crossings(_va22_order)
        if _va22_va_before:
            print(f"⚠️ V65.64.23 起始路線仍有 Virtual anchor forward-crossing：{len(_va22_va_before)} 段")
        else:
            print("V65.64.23 起始路線 Virtual anchor forward-crossing：0 段")

        # 核心：修正所有一般查核點 physical forward-crossing。
        _va22_order, _va22_physical_moves = _va22_repair_physical_order(_va22_order)
        print(f"V65.64.23 一般案件 actual-TIME 物理通過順序修正：移動 {len(_va22_physical_moves)} 次")
        for _rec in _va22_physical_moves[:30]:
            print(
                f"  第{_rec['段次']}段：{_rec['起點名稱']} → {_rec['終點名稱']}："
                f"提前 {_rec['提前案件名稱']}"
            )
        if len(_va22_physical_moves) > 30:
            print(f"  ... 其餘 {len(_va22_physical_moves) - 30} 次略過。")

        _va22_general_hits = _va22_forward_crossings(_va22_order)
        _va22_va_hits = _va22_virtual_forward_crossings(_va22_order)

        # 實際 TIME path geometry；使用同一 predecessor，確保順序檢查與全點一次路線一致。
        def _va22_edge_attrs(u, v):
            data = G.get_edge_data(int(u), int(v))
            if not data:
                return None
            best = None
            best_w = float("inf")
            items = data.values() if G.is_multigraph() else [data]
            for attrs in items:
                try:
                    w = float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    w = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(w) and w < best_w:
                    best_w = w
                    best = attrs
            return best

        def _va22_path_geometry(path):
            geom = []
            dist = 0.0
            tmin = 0.0
            for u, v in zip(path[:-1], path[1:]):
                attrs = _va22_edge_attrs(u, v)
                if attrs is None:
                    continue
                dist += float(attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
                tmin += float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", 0.0) or 0.0)
                eg = attrs.get("geometry")
                if eg is not None:
                    pts = [(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in eg.coords]
                else:
                    un = G.nodes[int(u)]; vn = G.nodes[int(v)]
                    pts = [
                        (float(un.get("x", 0.0)), float(un.get("y", 0.0))),
                        (float(vn.get("x", 0.0)), float(vn.get("y", 0.0))),
                    ]
                if not geom:
                    geom.extend(pts)
                elif geom[-1] == pts[0]:
                    geom.extend(pts[1:])
                else:
                    geom.extend(pts)
            return geom, dist, tmin

        _va22_main_geom = []
        _va22_return_geom = []
        _va22_total_dist = 0.0
        _va22_total_time = 0.0
        _va22_pair_records = []

        for _segno, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(_va22_order[:-1], _va22_order[1:]), start=1):
            path = _va22_reconstruct_time_path(a, b)
            if not path:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"V65.64.23 實際 OSM 路徑不可達：{work_df.iloc[a]['名稱']} → {work_df.iloc[b]['名稱']}"
                )
            geom, dist, tmin = _va22_path_geometry(path)
            _va22_total_dist += dist
            _va22_total_time += tmin
            if geom:
                if not _va22_main_geom:
                    _va22_main_geom.extend(geom)
                elif _va22_main_geom[-1] == geom[0]:
                    _va22_main_geom.extend(geom[1:])
                else:
                    _va22_main_geom.extend(geom)

            _general_mids = _va22_pair_intermediate_points(a, b)
            _virtual_mids = _va22_virtual_pair_intermediate_points(a, b)
            _later_general = []
            _order_pos = {int(v): int(i) for i, v in enumerate(_va22_order)}
            for pi in _general_mids:
                if _order_pos.get(int(pi), -1) > int(_segno):
                    _later_general.append(int(pi))

            _va22_pair_records.append({
                "段次": int(_segno),
                "起點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[a]["名稱"]),
                "終點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[b]["名稱"]),
                "途中VirtualAnchor案件數": len(_virtual_mids),
                "途中VirtualAnchor案件": "；".join(str(work_df.iloc[int(pi)]["名稱"]) for pi in _virtual_mids),
                "途中後續一般案件數": len(_later_general),
                "途中後續一般案件": "；".join(str(work_df.iloc[int(pi)]["名稱"]) for pi in _later_general),
            })

        _last = int(_va22_order[-1])
        _rpath = _va22_reconstruct_time_path(_last, int(start_idx))
        if not _rpath:
            raise RuntimeError("V65.64.23 最後一點回固定起點不可達。")
        _va22_return_geom, _rdist, _rtime = _va22_path_geometry(_rpath)
        _va22_total_dist += _rdist
        _va22_total_time += _rtime

        print(f"V65.64.23 actual-TIME Virtual anchor pair cache：{len(_va22_va_pair_cache)} 組")
        print(f"V65.64.23 actual-TIME 一般案件 pair cache：{len(_va22_general_pair_cache)} 組")
        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次實際 OSM 路線距離：{_va22_total_dist / 1000:.2f} km")
        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次實際 OSM 駕駛時間：{_va22_total_time / 60:.2f} hr")
        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次 Virtual anchor forward-crossing：{len(_va22_va_hits)} 段")
        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次一般案件 forward-crossing：{len(_va22_general_hits)} 段")

        if _va22_va_hits:
            for epos, a, b, pi, cp in _va22_va_hits[:30]:
                print(
                    f"  VA 第{epos}段：{work_df.iloc[a]['名稱']} → {work_df.iloc[b]['名稱']}，"
                    f"途中先遇到 {work_df.iloc[pi]['名稱']} (排定序號={cp})"
                )
            raise RuntimeError("V65.64.23：actual-TIME route 仍存在 Virtual anchor forward-crossing。")

        if _va22_general_hits:
            for epos, a, b, pi, cp in _va22_general_hits[:30]:
                print(
                    f"  一般案件第{epos}段：{work_df.iloc[a]['名稱']} → {work_df.iloc[b]['名稱']}，"
                    f"途中先遇到 {work_df.iloc[pi]['名稱']} (排定序號={cp})"
                )
            raise RuntimeError("V65.64.23：actual-TIME route 仍存在一般案件 forward-crossing。")

        # 輸出；不覆蓋正常 Cell4 handoff。
        _va22_order_rows = []
        for seq, idx in enumerate(_va22_order):
            row = work_df.iloc[int(idx)]
            _va22_order_rows.append({
                "全點一次跑完序號": int(seq),
                "角色": "固定起點" if int(seq) == 0 else "查核點",
                "點索引": int(idx),
                "原始_df_index": int(row["_原始_df_index"]),
                "原始序號": int(row["_原始_df_index"]) + 1,
                "Excel列": int(row["_原始_df_index"]) + 2,
                "名稱": str(row["名稱"]),
                "縣市": row["縣市"] if "縣市" in row.index else "",
                "鄉鎮市區": row["鄉鎮市區"] if "鄉鎮市區" in row.index else "",
                "地段": row["地段"] if "地段" in row.index else "",
                "地號": row["地號"] if "地號" in row.index else "",
                "經度": float(coords[int(idx), 0]),
                "緯度": float(coords[int(idx), 1]),
            })

        _va22_geojson = {"type": "FeatureCollection", "features": []}
        if len(_va22_main_geom) >= 2:
            _va22_geojson["features"].append({
                "type": "Feature",
                "geometry": {"type": "LineString", "coordinates": _va22_main_geom},
                "properties": {"type": "v65.64.22 MOTOR 全點一次主路線", "距離_m": float(_va22_total_dist - _rdist)},
            })
        if len(_va22_return_geom) >= 2:
            _va22_geojson["features"].append({
                "type": "Feature",
                "geometry": {"type": "LineString", "coordinates": _va22_return_geom},
                "properties": {"type": "v65.64.22 MOTOR 回固定起點", "距離_m": float(_rdist)},
            })
        for rec in _va22_order_rows:
            _va22_geojson["features"].append({
                "type": "Feature",
                "geometry": {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [float(rec["經度"]), float(rec["緯度"])]},
                "properties": {"全點一次跑完序號": rec["全點一次跑完序號"], "角色": rec["角色"], "名稱": rec["名稱"], "地段": rec["地段"], "地號": rec["地號"]},
            })

        _va22_prefix = "/content/MOTOR_VA_ONLY_v65.64.22"
        pd.DataFrame(_va22_order_rows).to_excel(_va22_prefix + "_route_order.xlsx", index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(_va22_order_rows).to_csv(_va22_prefix + "_route_order.csv", index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
        pd.DataFrame(_va22_pair_records).to_excel(_va22_prefix + "_pair_diagnostic.xlsx", index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(_va22_physical_moves).to_excel(_va22_prefix + "_physical_order_moves.xlsx", index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(_va22_general_hits).to_excel(_va22_prefix + "_general_forward_crossing.xlsx", index=False)
        with open(_va22_prefix + "_route.geojson", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(_va22_geojson, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        pd.DataFrame([
            {"項目": "測試版本", "數值": "v65.64.22 TEST"},
            {"項目": "Virtual anchor forward-crossing", "數值": int(len(_va22_va_hits))},
            {"項目": "一般案件 forward-crossing", "數值": int(len(_va22_general_hits))},
            {"項目": "一般案件物理順序修正移動次數", "數值": int(len(_va22_physical_moves))},
            {"項目": "Virtual anchor pair cache組數", "數值": int(len(_va22_va_pair_cache))},
            {"項目": "一般案件 pair cache組數", "數值": int(len(_va22_general_pair_cache))},
            {"項目": "全點一次實際OSM道路距離_km", "數值": float(_va22_total_dist) / 1000.0},
            {"項目": "全點一次實際OSM駕駛時間_hr", "數值": float(_va22_total_time) / 60.0},
        ]).to_excel(_va22_prefix + "_summary.xlsx", index=False)

        _va22_payload = {
            "VEHICLE_MODE": "MOTOR",
            "VEHICLE_LABEL": "機車",
            "work_df": work_df,
            "coords": np.asarray(coords),
            "snap_distances": np.asarray(snap_distances),
            "vehicle_ingress_nodes": np.asarray(nearest_nodes, dtype=np.int64),
            "start_name": start_name,
            "start_lat": float(start_lat),
            "start_lon": float(start_lon),
            "all_in_one_order": _va22_order,
            "all_in_one_geometry": _va22_main_geom,
            "all_in_one_return_geometry": _va22_return_geom,
            "all_in_one_actual_distance": float(_va22_total_dist),
            "all_in_one_actual_drive_min": float(_va22_total_time),
            "virtual_anchor_records": virtual_anchor_records,
            "virtual_anchor_forward_crossing": int(len(_va22_va_hits)),
            "general_point_forward_crossing": int(len(_va22_general_hits)),
            "physical_order_moves": _va22_physical_moves,
            "physical_pair_cache_size": int(len(_va22_general_pair_cache)),
            "virtual_anchor_pair_cache_size": int(len(_va22_va_pair_cache)),
            "test_only": True,
        }
        with open(_va22_prefix + "_map_data.pkl", "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(_va22_payload, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)

        print("✅ V65.64.23：Virtual anchor＋一般案件實際物理通過順序測試完成")
        print("✅ 不進入每日工時／每日點數／每日分界／跨日交換／每日第五階段")
        return _va22_payload

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("第四階段：每日工時＋固定起點往返共同最佳化")
    print("=" * 70)
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
        print(
            f"每日出門時間上限：{DAILY_MAX_HOURS:.1f} 小時 | "
            f"每點處理：{POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES:.0f} 分鐘 | "
            f"機車平均行駛速度：{MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH:.1f} km/h"
        )
    else:
        print(
            f"每日出門時間上限：{DAILY_MAX_HOURS:.1f} 小時 | "
            f"每點處理：{POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES:.0f} 分鐘 | "
            f"汽車平面道路：{CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH:.1f} km/h | "
            f"高速公路：{CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH:.1f} km/h | "
            f"快速公路：{CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH:.1f} km/h"
        )

    max_day_min = DAILY_MAX_HOURS * 60.0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.9.2-1：單點可行性檢查
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    impossible_single_points = []
    for p in visit_order:
        p = int(p)
        out_t = float(TIME_D[start_idx, p])
        back_t = float(TIME_D[p, start_idx])
        one_day_min = out_t + float(POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES) + back_t
        if not (np.isfinite(out_t) and np.isfinite(back_t)) or one_day_min > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            impossible_single_points.append({
                "idx": p,
                "name": str(work_df.iloc[p]["名稱"]),
                "out_min": out_t,
                "back_min": back_t,
                "total_min": one_day_min,
            })

    if impossible_single_points:
        preview = impossible_single_points[:10]
        detail = "; ".join(
            f"{x['idx'] + 1}({x['name']})={x['total_min']:.1f} min"
            for x in preview
        )
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"有 {len(impossible_single_points)} 個單一查核點連同固定起點往返即超過每日 "
            f"{DAILY_MAX_HOURS:.1f} 小時上限，無法合法排入任何工作日。示例：{detail}"
        )

    M = len(visit_order)

    edge_prefix = np.zeros(max(M, 1), dtype=float)
    time_edge_prefix = np.zeros(max(M, 1), dtype=float)
    if M >= 2:
        for k in range(M - 1):
            d = float(ROAD_D[visit_order[k], visit_order[k + 1]])
            t = float(TIME_D[visit_order[k], visit_order[k + 1]])
            if not np.isfinite(d):
                d = np.inf
            if not np.isfinite(t):
                t = np.inf
            edge_prefix[k + 1] = edge_prefix[k] + d
            time_edge_prefix[k + 1] = time_edge_prefix[k] + t


    def daily_service_minutes(day_no, visit_count):
        """每日服務時間統一計算；只有第 1 天可選擇計入固定起點服務時間。"""
        visits = max(0, int(visit_count))
        service = float(visits * POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES)
        if int(day_no) == 1 and COUNT_FIXED_START_AS_WORK_POINT:
            service += float(POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES)
        return service


    def segment_day_stats_fast(i, j, day_no=None):
        """先以 global_order 連續區段估算：起點→查核點→起點。
        day_no=1 時才計固定起點服務時間；後續工作日不重複計算。
        """
        if i < 0 or j > M or i >= j:
            return {
                "distance_m": 0.0,
                "drive_min": 0.0,
                "service_min": 0.0,
                "total_min": 0.0,
                "return_distance_m": 0.0,
                "outbound_distance_m": 0.0,
                "point_count": 0,
            }

        first_idx = visit_order[i]
        last_idx = visit_order[j - 1]
        first_leg = float(ROAD_D[start_idx, first_idx])
        return_leg = float(ROAD_D[last_idx, start_idx])
        first_time = float(TIME_D[start_idx, first_idx])
        return_time = float(TIME_D[last_idx, start_idx])

        if (
            not np.isfinite(first_leg)
            or not np.isfinite(return_leg)
            or not np.isfinite(first_time)
            or not np.isfinite(return_time)
        ):
            return {
                "distance_m": np.inf,
                "drive_min": np.inf,
                "service_min": daily_service_minutes(day_no or 1, j - i),
                "total_min": np.inf,
                "return_distance_m": return_leg,
                "outbound_distance_m": np.inf,
                "point_count": j - i,
            }

        internal = 0.0
        if j - i >= 2:
            internal = float(edge_prefix[j - 1] - edge_prefix[i])

        outbound = first_leg + internal
        total_distance = outbound + return_leg
        internal_time = 0.0
        if j - i >= 2:
            internal_time = float(time_edge_prefix[j - 1] - time_edge_prefix[i])
        drive_min = first_time + internal_time + return_time
        service_min = daily_service_minutes(day_no or (1 if i == 0 else 2), j - i)
        total_min = drive_min + service_min

        return {
            "distance_m": total_distance,
            "drive_min": drive_min,
            "service_min": service_min,
            "total_min": total_min,
            "return_distance_m": return_leg,
            "outbound_distance_m": outbound,
            "point_count": j - i,
        }


    def closed_day_distance(order):
        """固定起點閉合路線距離：start → visits → start。"""
        if not order:
            return 0.0
        if order[0] != start_idx:
            return float("inf")
        if len(order) == 1:
            return 0.0

        total = 0.0
        for a, b in zip(order[:-1], order[1:]):
            d = float(ROAD_D[int(a), int(b)])
            if not np.isfinite(d):
                return float("inf")
            total += d

        back = float(ROAD_D[int(order[-1]), int(start_idx)])
        if not np.isfinite(back):
            return float("inf")
        return total + back


    def closed_day_drive_minutes(order):
        return closed_day_drive_minutes_from_order(order)


    def closed_day_total_minutes(order, day_no):
        visits = max(0, len(order) - 1)
        service_min = daily_service_minutes(day_no, visits)
        return closed_day_drive_minutes(order) + service_min


    # v49：所有「每日工作日配置」的主要最佳化目標改用實際旅行時間。
    # 每個查核點的服務時間總量固定，因此在工作日數固定後，
    # 最小化全體每日總工時等價於最小化全體每日駕駛時間。
    # 距離型 loop / crossing / spread 僅作次要約束，換算為小幅分鐘懲罰。
    OPTIMIZATION_PENALTY_SPEED_KMH = (
        MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH
    )

    def distance_penalty_to_minutes(distance_m):
        if not np.isfinite(distance_m):
            return float("inf")
        return float(distance_m / 1000.0 / OPTIMIZATION_PENALTY_SPEED_KMH * 60.0)


    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # v30：固定起點的每日路線也抓「無意義繞回」。
    # ROAD_D 已建立完成，因此這裡直接使用實際 OSM 道路距離。
    # 不處罰跨日重複，也不處罰正常的巷道／農路必要回頭。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    DAY_LOOPBACK_WINDOW = 7
    DAY_LOOPBACK_MIN_EXCESS_M = 1000.0
    DAY_LOOPBACK_DIRECT_MAX_M = 2500.0
    DAY_LOOPBACK_RATIO = 1.70
    DAY_LOOPBACK_PENALTY_PER_M = 0.10


    if _V65_44_HAS_NUMBA:
        @njit(cache=False)
        def _road_loopback_cost_numba_v65_44(arr, distance_matrix, window, min_excess_m, direct_max_m, ratio, penalty_per_m):
            n = arr.shape[0]
            if n < 4:
                return 0.0

            prefix = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for k in range(n - 1):
                e = distance_matrix[arr[k], arr[k + 1]]
                if not np.isfinite(e):
                    return np.inf
                prefix[k + 1] = prefix[k] + e

            total = 0.0
            win = int(window)
            min_ex = float(min_excess_m)
            direct_max = float(direct_max_m)
            ratio_f = float(ratio)
            penalty = float(penalty_per_m)

            for j in range(3, n):
                i0 = max(0, j - win)
                # Equivalent to Python: range(i0, j - 2)
                for i in range(i0, j - 2):
                    direct = distance_matrix[arr[i], arr[j]]
                    along = prefix[j] - prefix[i]
                    if (
                        np.isfinite(direct)
                        and direct > 0.0
                        and direct <= direct_max
                        and (along - direct) >= min_ex
                        and along >= direct * ratio_f
                    ):
                        total += (along - direct) * penalty
            return total
    else:
        _road_loopback_cost_numba_v65_44 = None


    def road_loopback_cost(order, distance_matrix, window, min_excess_m, direct_max_m, ratio, penalty_per_m):
        """使用道路距離矩陣，低權重抓局部無意義繞回；公式與舊版完全相同。

        v65.48：保留 v65.45 的 NumPy/Numba JIT 核心；Numba 不可用時，
        自動回退到原 v65.40 NumPy 實作。路線接受條件與公式均不變。
        """
        n = len(order)
        if n < 4:
            return 0.0

        arr = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64)
        if _V65_44_HAS_NUMBA:
            return float(_road_loopback_cost_numba_v65_44(
                arr,
                np.asarray(distance_matrix, dtype=np.float64),
                int(window),
                float(min_excess_m),
                float(direct_max_m),
                float(ratio),
                float(penalty_per_m),
            ))

        edge = np.asarray(distance_matrix[arr[:-1], arr[1:]], dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(edge)):
            return float("inf")
        prefix = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(edge)))
        total = 0.0
        for j in range(3, n):
            i0 = max(0, j - int(window))
            ii = np.arange(i0, j - 2, dtype=np.int64)
            if ii.size == 0:
                continue
            direct = np.asarray(distance_matrix[arr[ii], arr[j]], dtype=np.float64)
            along = prefix[j] - prefix[ii]
            mask = (
                np.isfinite(direct) & (direct > 0.0)
                & (direct <= float(direct_max_m))
                & ((along - direct) >= float(min_excess_m))
                & (along >= direct * float(ratio))
            )
            if np.any(mask):
                total += float(np.sum((along[mask] - direct[mask]) * float(penalty_per_m)))
        return float(total)


    # v65.48：預先編譯一次 NumPy/Numba loopback kernel，避免第一個實際候選承擔 JIT 編譯時間。
    if _V65_44_HAS_NUMBA:
        _v65_44_dummy_d = np.zeros((4, 4), dtype=np.float64)
        _v65_44_dummy_o = np.arange(4, dtype=np.int64)
        _road_loopback_cost_numba_v65_44(
            _v65_44_dummy_o, _v65_44_dummy_d, 2, 1000.0, 2500.0, 1.70, 0.10
        )
        print(" 每日往返路線計算：使用 Numba 加速")
    else:
        print(" 每日往返路線計算：使用一般 NumPy 計算（Numba 無法使用）")


    def closed_day_objective(order):
        """每日閉合路線：以實際旅行時間為主，空間繞回僅作次要懲罰。"""
        if not order or order[0] != start_idx:
            return float("inf")

        drive_min = closed_day_drive_minutes_from_order(order)
        if not np.isfinite(drive_min):
            return float("inf")

        score = drive_min

        loop_pen_m = road_loopback_cost(
            order,
            ROAD_D,
            DAY_LOOPBACK_WINDOW,
            DAY_LOOPBACK_MIN_EXCESS_M,
            DAY_LOOPBACK_DIRECT_MAX_M,
            DAY_LOOPBACK_RATIO,
            DAY_LOOPBACK_PENALTY_PER_M,
        )
        if not np.isfinite(loop_pen_m):
            return float("inf")
        score += distance_penalty_to_minutes(loop_pen_m)

        if len(order) >= 3:
            geom_m = (
                count_crossings(order) * _ROUTE_CROSSING_PENALTY_M
                + count_uturns(order) * _ROUTE_UTURN_PENALTY_M
            )
            score += distance_penalty_to_minutes(geom_m)

        # 空間連續性保留，但明確低於旅行時間。
        spread_m = centroid_spread(order) * (CLUSTER_SPREAD_PENALTY * 0.50)
        if batch_spread(order) > MAX_BATCH_SPREAD_M:
            spread_m += (batch_spread(order) - MAX_BATCH_SPREAD_M) * (LONG_BATCH_SPREAD_PENALTY * 0.50)
        score += distance_penalty_to_minutes(spread_m)

        return float(score)


    def _closed_edge_distance(a, b):
        d = float(ROAD_D[int(a), int(b)])
        return d if np.isfinite(d) else float("inf")


    def _closed_route_distance_fast(order):
        """固定起點閉合路線距離；只做 O(n) 邊掃描。"""
        if not order or order[0] != start_idx:
            return float("inf")
        if len(order) == 1:
            return 0.0
        total = 0.0
        for a, b in zip(order[:-1], order[1:]):
            d = _closed_edge_distance(a, b)
            if not np.isfinite(d):
                return float("inf")
            total += d
        back = _closed_edge_distance(order[-1], start_idx)
        if not np.isfinite(back):
            return float("inf")
        return total + back


    def _delta_relocate_closed(order, src, dst, matrix=ROAD_D):
        """O(1) relocate delta；與原 pop/insert 定義數學等價。

        正確的增量必須同時移除「被搬移點兩側的兩條 edge」以及「原插入位置 edge」，
        再加入 remove 後的連接 edge 與新的兩條插入 道路段：

            delta = (new_after_remove - old_remove) + (new_insert - old_insert)

        v65.48 正式保留 corrected relocate delta；已由診斷驗證與實際 pop→insert 距離差完全一致。
        """
        n = len(order)
        x = int(order[src])
        prev_src = int(order[src - 1])
        next_src = int(order[src + 1]) if src + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
        old_remove = float(matrix[prev_src, x]) + float(matrix[x, next_src])
        new_after_remove = float(matrix[prev_src, next_src])
        if not np.isfinite(old_remove) or not np.isfinite(new_after_remove):
            return float("inf")

        insert_at = dst - 1 if dst > src else dst
        if insert_at <= 0 or insert_at >= len(order):
            return float("inf")

        left = int(order[dst - 1])
        right = int(order[dst])
        old_insert = float(matrix[left, right])
        new_insert = float(matrix[left, x]) + float(matrix[x, right])
        if not np.isfinite(old_insert) or not np.isfinite(new_insert):
            return float("inf")

        return (new_after_remove - old_remove) + (new_insert - old_insert)


    def _best_relocate_closed_vectorized_v65_40(order, movable_lo, lock_ends, window, matrix=ROAD_D):
        """向量化掃描 relocate；保持原 src/dst 掃描順序與 1e-6 tie-break。"""
        n = len(order)
        arr = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64)
        best_delta = 0.0
        best_src = None
        best_dst = None
        for src in range(int(movable_lo), int(n if not lock_ends else n - 1)):
            lo = max(int(movable_lo), int(src - window))
            hi = min((n - 1) if lock_ends else n, int(src + window + 1))
            dst = np.arange(lo, hi, dtype=np.int64)
            dst = dst[(dst != int(src)) & (dst != int(src + 1))]
            if dst.size == 0:
                continue
            x = int(arr[src])
            prev_src = int(arr[src - 1])
            next_src = int(arr[src + 1]) if src + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
            old_remove = float(matrix[prev_src, x]) + float(matrix[x, next_src])
            new_after_remove = float(matrix[prev_src, next_src])
            if not np.isfinite(old_remove) or not np.isfinite(new_after_remove):
                continue
            left = arr[dst - 1]
            right = arr[dst]
            old_insert = np.asarray(matrix[left, right], dtype=np.float64)
            new_insert = np.asarray(matrix[left, x], dtype=np.float64) + np.asarray(matrix[x, right], dtype=np.float64)
            delta = (new_after_remove - old_remove) + (new_insert - old_insert)
            delta = np.where(np.isfinite(old_insert) & np.isfinite(new_insert), delta, np.inf)
            k = int(np.argmin(delta))
            local = float(delta[k])
            if local < best_delta - 1e-6:
                best_delta = local
                best_src = int(src)
                best_dst = int(dst[k])
        return best_delta, best_src, best_dst


    def _delta_swap_closed(order, a, b, matrix=ROAD_D):
        """O(1) swap delta，含相鄰節點與末端回起點。"""
        if a == b:
            return 0.0
        n = len(order)
        arr = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64)
        if a > b:
            a, b = b, a
        xa = int(arr[a]); xb = int(arr[b])
        pa = int(arr[a - 1]); sa = int(arr[a + 1]) if a + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
        pb = int(arr[b - 1]); sb = int(arr[b + 1]) if b + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
        if b == a + 1:
            old = float(matrix[pa, xa]) + float(matrix[xa, xb]) + float(matrix[xb, sb])
            new = float(matrix[pa, xb]) + float(matrix[xb, xa]) + float(matrix[xa, sb])
        else:
            old = float(matrix[pa, xa]) + float(matrix[xa, sa]) + float(matrix[pb, xb]) + float(matrix[xb, sb])
            new = float(matrix[pa, xb]) + float(matrix[xb, sa]) + float(matrix[pb, xa]) + float(matrix[xa, sb])
        if not np.isfinite(old) or not np.isfinite(new):
            return float("inf")
        return new - old


    def _best_swap_closed_vectorized_v65_40(order, movable_lo, lock_ends, window, matrix=ROAD_D):
        """向量化掃描 swap；保持原 a/b 掃描順序與 1e-6 tie-break。"""
        n = len(order)
        arr = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64)
        best_delta = 0.0
        best_pair = None
        for a in range(int(movable_lo), n - 1):
            hi = min((n - 1) if lock_ends else n, int(a + window + 1))
            b = np.arange(a + 1, hi, dtype=np.int64)
            if b.size == 0:
                continue
            xa = int(arr[a]); pa = int(arr[a - 1]); sa = int(arr[a + 1]) if a + 1 < n else int(start_idx)
            xb = arr[b]; pb = arr[b - 1]; sb = arr[b + 1]
            adjacent = b == a + 1
            old_non = (matrix[pa, xa] + matrix[xa, sa] + matrix[pb, xb] + matrix[xb, sb]).astype(float, copy=False)
            new_non = (matrix[pa, xb] + matrix[xb, sa] + matrix[pb, xa] + matrix[xa, sb]).astype(float, copy=False)
            old_adj = matrix[pa, xa] + matrix[xa, xb] + matrix[xb, sb]
            new_adj = matrix[pa, xb] + matrix[xb, xa] + matrix[xa, sb]
            delta = np.where(adjacent, new_adj - old_adj, new_non - old_non)
            delta = np.where(np.isfinite(delta), delta, np.inf)
            k = int(np.argmin(delta))
            local = float(delta[k])
            if local < best_delta - 1e-6:
                best_delta = local
                best_pair = (int(a), int(b[k]))
        return best_delta, best_pair


    # V65.48：零演算法變更的每日最佳化 profiler。
    # 只統計各段實際耗時，協助定位 V65.10 的真正瓶頸；不改任何接受條件。
    _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT = "other"
    _V65_44_OPT_STATS = {}

    def _v65_44_get_stats(name):
        d = _V65_44_OPT_STATS.setdefault(name, {
            "calls": 0, "total": 0.0, "relocate": 0.0, "swap": 0.0,
            "cleanup": 0.0, "check": 0.0, "cleanup_candidates": 0,
        })
        return d

    def optimize_closed_day(order, max_relocate_passes=4, max_swap_passes=2, window=16, lock_ends=True, day_no=None):
        """快速每日閉合路線最佳化。

        v16 的瓶頸是：每一個候選 relocate/swap 都重新執行完整 closed_day_objective，
        還包含 crossing / u-turn / spread 的 O(n²) 掃描；再乘上多個邊界候選，會把
        30~40 點的一天放大成數萬次完整評分。

        v17 改為：
          1) 候選搜尋主要使用道路距離的增量。
          2) 找到改善才真正修改 route。
          3) 每個 pass 結束才計一次完整診斷目標函數。
          4) 最後再用完整 closed_day_objective 做一次確認。
        """
        _opt_ctx = str(_V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT)
        _opt_stat = _v65_44_get_stats(_opt_ctx)
        _opt_stat["calls"] += 1
        _opt_t0 = time.perf_counter()
        best = list(order)
        if len(best) <= 3:
            _opt_stat["total"] += time.perf_counter() - _opt_t0
            return best

        # v30：每日切割完成後，固定「第一個查核點」與「最後一個查核點」。
        # 只允許中間查核點重新排序，避免每日獨立最佳化再次破壞日界。
        # order[0] 永遠是固定起點；若 lock_ends=True，位置 1 與 n-1 均視為日界錨點。
        movable_lo = 2 if lock_ends and len(best) >= 4 else 1
        movable_hi = len(best) - 1 if lock_ends and len(best) >= 4 else len(best)

        distance = _closed_route_distance_fast(best)
        if not np.isfinite(distance):
            return best

        # 小幅 penalty 只在 pass 結束後評估；候選搜尋以道路距離為主，
        # 保留合理回頭，不會把必要的回頭全部禁止。
        _rel_t0 = time.perf_counter()
        for _ in range(max_relocate_passes):
            improved = False
            best_delta, best_src, best_dst = _best_relocate_closed_vectorized_v65_40(
                best, movable_lo, lock_ends, window, ROAD_D
            )
            if best_src is None:
                break

            x = best.pop(best_src)
            insert_at = best_dst - 1 if best_dst > best_src else best_dst
            best.insert(insert_at, x)
            distance += best_delta
            improved = True

            # V65.48：原 closed_day_objective 在這裡只用來檢查是否有限，
            # 但它會額外掃描 loopback / crossing / U-turn / spread；這些量並不
            # 參與本 pass 的 relocate 選擇。改以同一套閉合工時成本做有限性檢查，
            # 最終日方案仍會照既有 actual_closed_day_metrics() 完整驗證。
            if improved:
                _check_t0 = time.perf_counter()
                _check_day_no = int(day_no) if day_no is not None else 1
                check_total = closed_day_total_minutes(best, _check_day_no)
                _opt_stat["check"] += time.perf_counter() - _check_t0
                if not np.isfinite(check_total):
                    break

        _opt_stat["relocate"] += time.perf_counter() - _rel_t0

        _swap_t0 = time.perf_counter()
        for _ in range(max_swap_passes):
            best_delta, best_pair = _best_swap_closed_vectorized_v65_40(
                best, movable_lo, lock_ends, window, ROAD_D
            )
            if best_pair is None:
                break
            a, b = best_pair
            best[a], best[b] = best[b], best[a]
            distance += best_delta
            _check_t0 = time.perf_counter()
            _check_day_no = int(day_no) if day_no is not None else 1
            if not np.isfinite(closed_day_total_minutes(best, _check_day_no)):
                _opt_stat["check"] += time.perf_counter() - _check_t0
                break
            _opt_stat["check"] += time.perf_counter() - _check_t0

        _opt_stat["swap"] += time.perf_counter() - _swap_t0

        # v30：距離已經收斂後，再做少量「無意義繞回」清理。
        # 允許極小道路距離增加，但不能突破 5 小時硬限制。
        LOOP_CLEANUP_PASSES = 2
        LOOP_CLEANUP_MAX_DISTANCE_INCREASE_M = 300.0
        LOOP_CLEANUP_MIN_SCORE_IMPROVEMENT_M = 10.0

        _cleanup_t0 = time.perf_counter()
        for _ in range(LOOP_CLEANUP_PASSES):
            current_loop = road_loopback_cost(
                best, ROAD_D, DAY_LOOPBACK_WINDOW, DAY_LOOPBACK_MIN_EXCESS_M,
                DAY_LOOPBACK_DIRECT_MAX_M, DAY_LOOPBACK_RATIO, DAY_LOOPBACK_PENALTY_PER_M
            )
            current_score = distance + current_loop
            best_candidate = None
            best_candidate_score = current_score

            # v65.48：cleanup 候選與既有 relocate 定義完全相同。
            # 不再對每一個候選呼叫 closed_day_distance() / closed_day_total_minutes()
            # 的 O(n) 完整掃描，而是用同一個 O(1) relocate delta 直接取得：
            #   1) ROAD_D 的距離變化
            #   2) TIME_D 的旅行時間變化
            # 服務時間固定，所以第 2 項加上固定 service 即與原公式等價。
            # 先通過這兩個便宜的硬條件，才建立 candidate route 並執行 loopback。
            current_drive_min = closed_day_drive_minutes_from_order(best)
            if not np.isfinite(current_drive_min):
                break
            _check_day_no = int(day_no) if day_no is not None else 1
            current_service_min = daily_service_minutes(_check_day_no, max(0, len(best) - 1))
            n = len(best)
            for src in range(movable_lo, n if not lock_ends else n - 1):
                lo = max(movable_lo, src - window)
                hi = min((n - 1) if lock_ends else n, src + window + 1)
                for dst in range(lo, hi):
                    if dst == src or dst == src + 1:
                        continue

                    # O(1) 候選距離；與原 cand_dist 完全相同的 relocate 定義。
                    delta_dist = _delta_relocate_closed(best, src, dst, ROAD_D)
                    if not np.isfinite(delta_dist):
                        continue
                    cand_dist = distance + delta_dist
                    if cand_dist > distance + LOOP_CLEANUP_MAX_DISTANCE_INCREASE_M + 1e-9:
                        continue

                    # O(1) 候選旅行時間；服務時間固定，因此這等價於
                    # closed_day_total_minutes(cand, day_no)。
                    delta_time = _delta_relocate_closed(best, src, dst, TIME_D)
                    if not np.isfinite(delta_time):
                        continue
                    cand_total_min = current_drive_min + delta_time + current_service_min
                    if cand_total_min > max_day_min + 1e-9:
                        continue

                    _opt_stat["cleanup_candidates"] += 1
                    cand = list(best)
                    x = cand.pop(src)
                    insert_at = dst - 1 if dst > src else dst
                    if insert_at <= 0 or insert_at >= len(cand):
                        continue
                    cand.insert(insert_at, x)

                    cand_loop = road_loopback_cost(
                        cand, ROAD_D, DAY_LOOPBACK_WINDOW, DAY_LOOPBACK_MIN_EXCESS_M,
                        DAY_LOOPBACK_DIRECT_MAX_M, DAY_LOOPBACK_RATIO, DAY_LOOPBACK_PENALTY_PER_M
                    )
                    if not np.isfinite(cand_loop):
                        continue
                    cand_score = cand_dist + cand_loop
                    if cand_score < best_candidate_score - LOOP_CLEANUP_MIN_SCORE_IMPROVEMENT_M:
                        best_candidate_score = cand_score
                        best_candidate = cand

            if best_candidate is None:
                break
            best = best_candidate
            distance = _closed_route_distance_fast(best)

        _opt_stat["cleanup"] += time.perf_counter() - _cleanup_t0
        _opt_stat["total"] += time.perf_counter() - _opt_t0
        return best


    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V55：先檢查「單一查核點＋固定起點往返」是否本身可安排。
    # 若 start→point→start 已超過每日上限，即使放到第 2 天也無法合法安排，
    # 必須立即指出點位，而不是讓 DP 最後只顯示「找不到切法」。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    max_day_min = DAILY_MAX_HOURS * 60.0
    impossible_single_points = []
    for p in visit_order:
        out_t = float(TIME_D[start_idx, int(p)])
        back_t = float(TIME_D[int(p), start_idx])
        base_service = float(POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES)
        one_day_min = out_t + base_service + back_t
        if not (np.isfinite(out_t) and np.isfinite(back_t)) or one_day_min > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            impossible_single_points.append({
                "idx": int(p),
                "name": str(work_df.iloc[int(p)]["名稱"]),
                "out_min": out_t,
                "back_min": back_t,
                "total_min": one_day_min,
            })

    if impossible_single_points:
        preview = impossible_single_points[:10]
        detail = "; ".join(
            f"{x['idx'] + 1}({x['name']})={x['total_min']:.1f} min"
            for x in preview
        )
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"有 {len(impossible_single_points)} 個單一查核點連同固定起點往返即超過每日 {DAILY_MAX_HOURS:.1f} 小時上限，"
            f"無法合法排入任何工作日。示例：{detail}"
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.9.5：每日分界依賴完整修正版
    #
    # 前版 v65.9.4 已恢復 _raw_boundary_penalty_at_cut()，但在重組
    # 每日分界區塊時遺漏了 Boundary 常數與配對懲罰函式，導致執行至
    # boundary_pen_min 快取時出現 NameError。
    #
    # 本版只恢復原有 Boundary 依賴，不修改 OSM、Dijkstra、全域排序、
    # visit_order 或 v65.9.2/v65.9.3 連續區段 DP 邏輯。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_THRESHOLD_M = 3000.0
    BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_COUNT = 5
    BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_PENALTY_PER_M = 0.02
    BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MIN_GAP_M = 5000.0
    BOUNDARY_VIA_START_PENALTY_PER_M = 0.08
    BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MAX_PAIR_M = 3000.0
    BOUNDARY_REVERSE_RATIO_MIN = 2.5
    BOUNDARY_JOINT_PASSES = 4

    # 保留既有 Boundary 設定介面；v65.9.5 的連續區段 DP 不執行跨日搬移／交換。
    BOUNDARY_TRANSFER_MAX_POINTS = 5
    BOUNDARY_TRANSFER_MAX_CANDIDATES_PER_SIDE = 15
    BOUNDARY_EXCHANGE_NEAR_M = 10000.0

    def _boundary_pair_weight(pos_a, pos_b):
        return 1.0 / (pos_a + pos_b - 1)

    def _boundary_penalty_pair(a, b, pos_a=1, pos_b=1):
        direct = float(ROAD_D[a, b])
        if not np.isfinite(direct) or direct < 0.0:
            return 0.0
        if direct > BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_THRESHOLD_M:
            return 0.0

        to_start = float(ROAD_D[a, start_idx])
        from_start = float(ROAD_D[start_idx, b])
        if not np.isfinite(to_start) or not np.isfinite(from_start):
            return 0.0

        # 0 m 代表兩筆地籍點落在同一 OSM snap node；以 1 m 作為數值基準，
        # 保留極近點意義，同時讓 via_start / direct 能安全計算。
        effective_direct = max(direct, 1.0)

        weight = _boundary_pair_weight(pos_a, pos_b)
        near_penalty = (
            (BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_THRESHOLD_M - effective_direct)
            * BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_PENALTY_PER_M
            * weight
        )

        via_start = to_start + from_start
        via_gap = via_start - effective_direct
        via_ratio = via_start / effective_direct
        reversal_penalty = 0.0
        if (
            direct <= BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MAX_PAIR_M
            and via_gap >= BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MIN_GAP_M
            and via_ratio >= BOUNDARY_REVERSE_RATIO_MIN
        ):
            reversal_penalty = via_gap * BOUNDARY_VIA_START_PENALTY_PER_M * weight

        # 同一 snap node 或幾乎同一點位跨日，提高分界修正優先程度；不硬禁止。
        zero_node_bonus = 0.0
        if direct <= 1.0 and via_gap >= BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MIN_GAP_M:
            zero_node_bonus = via_gap * 0.05 * weight

        return float(near_penalty + reversal_penalty + zero_node_bonus)

    def _raw_boundary_penalty_at_cut(cut_pos):
        if cut_pos <= 0 or cut_pos >= M:
            return 0.0
        tail_start = max(0, cut_pos - BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_COUNT)
        head_end = min(M, cut_pos + BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_COUNT)
        tail = visit_order[tail_start:cut_pos]
        head = visit_order[cut_pos:head_end]
        total = 0.0
        for pos_a, a in enumerate(reversed(tail), start=1):
            for pos_b, b in enumerate(head, start=1):
                total += _boundary_penalty_pair(a, b, pos_a, pos_b)
        return float(total)


    def boundary_continuity_penalty(day_a, day_b, return_detail=False):
        """以目前兩日實際順序檢查分界；一般跨日道路重複不處罰。"""
        if len(day_a) <= 1 or len(day_b) <= 1:
            return (0.0, []) if return_detail else 0.0

        tail = [int(x) for x in day_a[max(1, len(day_a) - BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_COUNT):]]
        head = [int(x) for x in day_b[1:1 + BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_COUNT]]
        total_penalty = 0.0
        details = []
        for pos_a, a in enumerate(reversed(tail), start=1):
            for pos_b, b in enumerate(head, start=1):
                direct = float(ROAD_D[a, b])
                if not np.isfinite(direct) or direct < 0.0 or direct > BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_THRESHOLD_M:
                    continue
                to_start = float(ROAD_D[a, start_idx])
                from_start = float(ROAD_D[start_idx, b])
                if not np.isfinite(to_start) or not np.isfinite(from_start):
                    continue
                weight = _boundary_pair_weight(pos_a, pos_b)
                near_penalty = (BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_THRESHOLD_M - direct) * BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_PENALTY_PER_M * weight
                via_start = to_start + from_start
                via_gap = via_start - direct
                via_ratio = via_start / max(direct, 1.0)
                reversal_penalty = 0.0
                if (
                    direct <= BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MAX_PAIR_M
                    and via_gap >= BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MIN_GAP_M
                    and via_ratio >= BOUNDARY_REVERSE_RATIO_MIN
                ):
                    reversal_penalty = via_gap * BOUNDARY_VIA_START_PENALTY_PER_M * weight
                pair_penalty = near_penalty + reversal_penalty
                total_penalty += pair_penalty
                if pair_penalty > 0:
                    details.append({
                        'a': a, 'b': b, 'pos_a': pos_a, 'pos_b': pos_b,
                        'direct_m': direct, 'a_to_start_m': to_start,
                        'start_to_b_m': from_start, 'via_start_m': via_start,
                        'via_gap_m': via_gap, 'via_ratio': via_ratio,
                        'near_penalty_m': near_penalty,
                        'reversal_penalty_m': reversal_penalty,
                        'total_penalty_m': pair_penalty,
                    })
        penalty_minutes = distance_penalty_to_minutes(total_penalty)
        if return_detail:
            details.sort(key=lambda x: x['total_penalty_m'], reverse=True)
            return float(penalty_minutes), details
        return float(penalty_minutes)



    def partition_score(day_batches):
        """完整評分：以每日實際總工時為主，空間連續性僅作次要懲罰。"""
        total = 0.0
        details = []
        for day_no, order in enumerate(day_batches, start=1):
            t = closed_day_total_minutes(order, day_no)
            if not np.isfinite(t) or t > max_day_min + 1e-9:
                return float('inf'), None
            dist = closed_day_distance(order)
            if not np.isfinite(dist):
                return float('inf'), None
            geom_pen_m = count_crossings(order) * _ROUTE_CROSSING_PENALTY_M + count_uturns(order) * _ROUTE_UTURN_PENALTY_M
            geom_pen_min = distance_penalty_to_minutes(geom_pen_m)
            total += t + geom_pen_min
            details.append({
                'day_no': day_no,
                'total_min': t,
