                'distance_m': dist,
                'geom_penalty_m': geom_pen_m,
                'geom_penalty_min': geom_pen_min,
            })

        boundary_details = []
        for k in range(len(day_batches) - 1):
            pen_min, pair_details = boundary_continuity_penalty(day_batches[k], day_batches[k + 1], return_detail=True)
            total += pen_min
            boundary_details.append({'boundary': k + 1, 'penalty_min': pen_min, 'pairs': pair_details})
        return float(total), {'days': details, 'boundaries': boundary_details}



    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.9.2-2：連續區段成本快取
    #
    # segment_day_stats_fast(i, j) 的 day_no 只有「第 1 天」與「後續天」
    # 兩種差異（固定起點服務時間），因此只需各快取一次。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.9.5：每日分界依賴完整性檢查。
    # 使用 locals()，因為這些函式／常數均定義在 run_vehicle_mode() 內。
    _required_daily_dependencies = [
        "daily_service_minutes",
        "segment_day_stats_fast",
        "closed_day_distance",
        "closed_day_drive_minutes",
        "closed_day_total_minutes",
        "distance_penalty_to_minutes",
        "_boundary_pair_weight",
        "_boundary_penalty_pair",
        "_raw_boundary_penalty_at_cut",
        "boundary_continuity_penalty",
        "partition_score",
        "BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_THRESHOLD_M",
        "BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_COUNT",
        "BOUNDARY_NEAR_POINT_PENALTY_PER_M",
        "BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MIN_GAP_M",
        "BOUNDARY_VIA_START_PENALTY_PER_M",
        "BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MAX_PAIR_M",
        "BOUNDARY_REVERSE_RATIO_MIN",
    ]
    _missing_daily_dependencies = [
        _name for _name in _required_daily_dependencies if _name not in locals()
    ]
    if _missing_daily_dependencies:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Cell 4 每日分界依賴缺失："
            + ", ".join(_missing_daily_dependencies)
        )

    _perf_daily_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    print("建立每日分界 DP 區段成本快取...")

    # V65.48：segment_day_stats_fast() 的數學式完全固定，
    # 原本逐段呼叫約 5,500 次、每段建立 dict；改為一次 NumPy 向量化。
    # 只改計算方式，不改任何成本定義。
    _seg_cache_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    seg_time_day1 = np.full((M + 1, M + 1), np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    seg_time_other = np.full((M + 1, M + 1), np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    seg_dist = np.full((M + 1, M + 1), np.inf, dtype=np.float64)

    if M > 0:
        _road_first = np.full(M, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
        _road_return = np.full(M, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
        _time_first = np.full(M, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
        _time_return = np.full(M, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)

        _visit_order_np = np.asarray(visit_order, dtype=np.int64)
        _road_first[:] = np.asarray(ROAD_D[int(start_idx), _visit_order_np], dtype=np.float64)
        _road_return[:] = np.asarray(ROAD_D[_visit_order_np, int(start_idx)], dtype=np.float64)
        _time_first[:] = np.asarray(TIME_D[int(start_idx), _visit_order_np], dtype=np.float64)
        _time_return[:] = np.asarray(TIME_D[_visit_order_np, int(start_idx)], dtype=np.float64)

        _rows = np.arange(M + 1, dtype=np.int64)[:, None]
        _cols = np.arange(M + 1, dtype=np.int64)[None, :]
        _valid = _rows < _cols

        _r = np.clip(_rows, 0, M - 1)
        _c_prev = np.clip(_cols - 1, 0, M - 1)

        _internal_dist = edge_prefix[_c_prev] - edge_prefix[_r]
        _internal_time = time_edge_prefix[_c_prev] - time_edge_prefix[_r]

        _seg_first_dist = _road_first[_r]
        _seg_return_dist = _road_return[_c_prev]
        _seg_first_time = _time_first[_r]
        _seg_return_time = _time_return[_c_prev]

        _seg_dist_raw = _seg_first_dist + _internal_dist + _seg_return_dist
        _seg_drive_raw = _seg_first_time + _internal_time + _seg_return_time

        _count = (_cols - _rows).astype(np.float64)
        _service_other = _count * float(POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES)
        _service_day1 = _service_other + (
            float(POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES) if COUNT_FIXED_START_AS_WORK_POINT else 0.0
        )

        _day1_total = _seg_drive_raw + _service_day1
        _other_total = _seg_drive_raw + _service_other

        seg_dist[_valid] = _seg_dist_raw[_valid]
        seg_time_day1[_valid] = _day1_total[_valid]
        seg_time_other[_valid] = _other_total[_valid]

    _seg_cache_sec = time.perf_counter() - _seg_cache_t0

    # 分界懲罰只依賴固定的 global visit_order；一次計算即可。
    boundary_pen_min = np.zeros(M + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    for cut in range(1, M):
        boundary_pen_min[cut] = distance_penalty_to_minutes(
            _raw_boundary_penalty_at_cut(cut)
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.9.2-3：先求「最少工作日」
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    min_day_dp = np.full(M + 1, 10**9, dtype=np.int32)
    min_day_dp[0] = 0
    min_prev = np.full(M + 1, -1, dtype=np.int32)

    for j in range(1, M + 1):
        best_days = 10**9
        best_i = -1
        for i in range(j - 1, -1, -1):
            t = seg_time_day1[i, j] if i == 0 else seg_time_other[i, j]
            if not np.isfinite(t) or t > max_day_min + 1e-9:
                continue
            cand_days = int(min_day_dp[i]) + 1
            if cand_days < best_days:
                best_days = cand_days
                best_i = i
        min_day_dp[j] = best_days
        min_prev[j] = best_i

    if min_day_dp[M] >= 10**9:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"依目前路網、速度與每日 {DAILY_MAX_HOURS:.1f} 小時上限，無法完成全部查核點。"
        )

    min_days = int(min_day_dp[M])

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.9.2-4：全域每日分界 DP
    #
    # 目標：固定最少工作日數後，讓所有工作日的實際總工時最小，
    # 並以分界連續性／反向重跑風險作為次要成本。
    # 這裡直接完成「跨日共同最佳化」，不再另外搬點／交換點。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    bal_dp = np.full((min_days + 1, M + 1), np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    bal_prev = np.full((min_days + 1, M + 1), -1, dtype=np.int32)
    bal_dp[0, 0] = 0.0

    for d_no in range(1, min_days + 1):
        for j in range(d_no, M + 1):
            best_val = np.inf
            best_i = -1
            for i in range(d_no - 1, j):
                prev_val = float(bal_dp[d_no - 1, i])
                if not np.isfinite(prev_val):
                    continue
                total_t = seg_time_day1[i, j] if d_no == 1 else seg_time_other[i, j]
                if not np.isfinite(total_t) or total_t > max_day_min + 1e-9:
                    continue

                val = prev_val + total_t
                if i > 0:
                    val += float(boundary_pen_min[i])

                if val < best_val - 1e-9:
                    best_val = val
                    best_i = i

            bal_dp[d_no, j] = best_val
            bal_prev[d_no, j] = best_i

    if not np.isfinite(bal_dp[min_days, M]):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "全域每日分界 DP 找不到符合每日時間上限的連續切法。"
        )

    cut_ranges = []
    j = M
    for d_no in range(min_days, 0, -1):
        i = int(bal_prev[d_no, j])
        if i < 0:
            raise RuntimeError("每日分界 DP 回溯失敗。")
        cut_ranges.append((i, j))
        j = i
    cut_ranges.reverse()

    raw_dp_score = float(bal_dp[min_days, M])
    print(
        f"最少工作日：{min_days} 天；固定天數後以『實際總工時最小』為主要目標。"
    )
    print(
        f"全域每日分界 DP：候選切點 1～{M - 1}；"
        f"DP 目標總工時={raw_dp_score:.2f} 分鐘等效值"
    )
    print(
        f"維持全域近→遠查核順序；"
        f"取消跨日 point transfer / swap {4} pass，改由全域 DP 一次共同決定分界。"
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.9.2-5：依 DP 分界建立每日原始方案，再各自最佳化一次
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    raw_batches = [
        [start_idx] + [int(x) for x in visit_order[i:j]]
        for i, j in cut_ranges
    ]

    def _safe_optimize_day(raw_order, day_no):
        """每日最佳化安全包裝：不可行時保留 DP 原始連續順序。"""
        raw_order = [int(x) for x in raw_order]
        raw_time = closed_day_total_minutes(raw_order, day_no)
        if not np.isfinite(raw_time) or raw_time > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"DP 產生第 {day_no} 日不可行方案：{raw_time:.2f} min。"
            )

        trial = optimize_closed_day(raw_order, lock_ends=True, day_no=day_no)
        trial_time = closed_day_total_minutes(trial, day_no)

        if not np.isfinite(trial_time) or trial_time > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            print(
                f"第 {day_no} 日：每日最佳化後變為不可行，"
                f"退回 DP 原始順序 ({raw_time:.2f} min)。"
            )
            return raw_order

        # V65.9：每日仍固定由較近查核點開始；只做整條序列的方向選擇，
        # 不改變該日查核點集合。
        oriented = orient_order_near_to_far(trial, label=f"第 {day_no} 日")
        oriented_time = closed_day_total_minutes(oriented, day_no)
        if np.isfinite(oriented_time) and oriented_time <= max_day_min + 1e-9:
            return oriented

        return trial

    _perf_daily_safe_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT = "initial"
    batches = [
        _safe_optimize_day(batch, day_no)
        for day_no, batch in enumerate(raw_batches, start=1)
    ]
    _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT = "other"

    # ============================================================
    # V65.14：跨日交換候選專用的輕量每日最佳化
    # 正式每日方案仍使用上面的 _safe_optimize_day，不改變最終品質。
    # ============================================================
    def _orient_order_near_to_far_quiet_v65_14(order):
        order = [int(x) for x in list(order)]
        if not PREFER_NEAR_TO_FAR_ORDER or len(order) <= 2:
            return order
        if order[0] != int(start_idx):
            return order
        forward = list(order)
        reverse = [int(start_idx)] + list(reversed(forward[1:]))
        f_first = int(forward[1])
        r_first = int(reverse[1])
        f_dist = float(ROAD_D[int(start_idx), f_first])
        r_dist = float(ROAD_D[int(start_idx), r_first])
        if np.isfinite(r_dist) and (not np.isfinite(f_dist) or r_dist < f_dist - 1e-6):
            return reverse
        return forward

    def _light_optimize_day_for_exchange_v65_14(raw_order, day_no):
        raw_order = [int(x) for x in raw_order]
        raw_time = closed_day_total_minutes(raw_order, day_no)
        if not np.isfinite(raw_time) or raw_time > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"候選第 {day_no} 日原始方案不可行：{raw_time:.2f} min。"
            )

        nonlocal _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT
        _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT = "crossday"
        trial = optimize_closed_day(
            raw_order,
            max_relocate_passes=int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_LIGHT_RELOCATE_PASSES),
            max_swap_passes=int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_LIGHT_SWAP_PASSES),
            window=int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_LIGHT_WINDOW),
            lock_ends=True,
            day_no=day_no,
        )
        _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT = "other"
        trial_time = closed_day_total_minutes(trial, day_no)
        if not np.isfinite(trial_time) or trial_time > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            return raw_order

        oriented = _orient_order_near_to_far_quiet_v65_14(trial)
        oriented_time = closed_day_total_minutes(oriented, day_no)
        if np.isfinite(oriented_time) and oriented_time <= max_day_min + 1e-9:
            return oriented
        return trial

    # V65.9.2-6：最終可行性／完整性檢查
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    for day_no, order in enumerate(batches, start=1):
        t = closed_day_total_minutes(order, day_no)
        if not np.isfinite(t) or t > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"第 {day_no} 日最終方案超過每日上限：{t:.2f} min。"
            )

    # 這裡仍使用完整 partition_score 做一次最終診斷；不再於候選搜尋期間反覆呼叫。
    best_partition_score, _best_details = partition_score(batches)
    if not np.isfinite(best_partition_score):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "最終每日方案無法通過完整 partition_score；"
            "已停止輸出，避免產生 inf 的每日方案。"
        )

    print(
        f"最終每日分界共同最佳化完成："
        f"{best_partition_score:.2f} 分鐘等效值"
    )

    # 顯示每日分界；僅診斷，不再進行跨日搬移／交換。
    for bno in range(len(batches) - 1):
        left_batch = batches[bno]
        right_batch = batches[bno + 1]
        pen, details = boundary_continuity_penalty(
            left_batch, right_batch, return_detail=True
        )
        if details:
            top = details[0]
            print(
                f"每日分界 {bno + 1}："
                f"前日末點→{top['a']} / 次日首點→{top['b']} | "
                f"直接={top['direct_m']/1000:.2f} km | "
                f"經起點={top['via_start_m']/1000:.2f} km | "
                f"額外往返={top['via_gap_m']/1000:.2f} km | "
                f"懲罰={pen:.2f} min"
            )
        else:
            print(
                f"每日分界 {bno + 1}：未發現明顯近點／反向重跑風險。"
            )

    # 嚴格檢查：105 個查核點必須全部且只出現一次；固定起點每日共用。
    assigned = [idx for batch in batches for idx in batch[1:]]
    if len(assigned) != M or len(set(assigned)) != M or set(assigned) != set(visit_order):
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"每日切分檢查失敗：應分配 {M} 個查核點，實際 {len(assigned)} 個。"
        )

    # v34：跨日點位共同最佳化完成後，不再無條件重新獨立最佳化每一天。
    # 若再單獨最佳化各天，可能重新破壞已找到的「前一天末點／隔天首點」連續性。
    # 僅做一次整體重新評分；若要再微調，必須連同日界目標一起評估。
    final_partition_score, _final_details = partition_score(batches)
    print(f"最終每日分界連續性檢查：{final_partition_score:.2f} 分鐘等效值")

    # 32. RoadIndex：進入真正 OSM geometry
    # ============================================================


    # ============================================================

    class RoadIndex:

        def __init__(
            self,
            grid_size_m=50,
            sample_spacing_m=30
        ):

            self.grid_size_m = (
                grid_size_m
            )

            self.sample_spacing_m = (
                sample_spacing_m
            )

            self.cells = defaultdict(
                float
            )


        def point_xy(
            self,
            lon,
            lat
        ):

            return lonlat_to_xy(
                lon,
                lat
            )


        def cell(
            self,
            x,
            y
        ):

            return (
                int(
                    math.floor(
                        x
                        /
                        self.grid_size_m
                    )
                ),
                int(
                    math.floor(
                        y
                        /
                        self.grid_size_m
                    )
                )
            )


        def sample(
            self,
            geometry
        ):

            if (
                geometry is None
                or
                len(geometry) < 2
            ):
                return []


            xy = [
                self.point_xy(
                    lon,
                    lat
                )
                for lon, lat
                in geometry
            ]


            samples = []


            for i in range(
                len(xy) - 1
            ):

                x1, y1 = xy[i]
                x2, y2 = xy[
                    i + 1
                ]

                dx = x2 - x1
                dy = y2 - y1

                seg_len = math.hypot(
                    dx,
                    dy
                )

                if seg_len <= 0:
                    continue

                n = max(
                    1,
                    int(
                        math.ceil(
                            seg_len
                            /
                            self.sample_spacing_m
                        )
                    )
                )

                for k in range(
                    1,
                    n + 1
                ):

                    t = k / n

                    samples.append(
                        (
                            x1 + dx * t,
                            y1 + dy * t
                        )
                    )

            return samples


        def overlap(
            self,
            geometry
        ):

            samples = (
                self.sample(
                    geometry
                )
            )

            if not samples:
                return 0.0

            repeated = 0

            for x, y in samples:

                gx, gy = (
                    self.cell(
                        x,
                        y
                    )
                )

                found = False

                for dx in (
                    -1,
                    0,
                    1
                ):

                    for dy in (
                        -1,
                        0,
                        1
                    ):

                        if (
                            self.cells.get(
                                (
                                    gx + dx,
                                    gy + dy
                                ),
                                0
                            )
                            > 0
                        ):

                            found = True
                            break

                    if found:
                        break

                if found:
                    repeated += 1

            return (
                repeated
                * self.sample_spacing_m
            )


        def add(
            self,
            geometry
        ):

            for x, y in (
                self.sample(
                    geometry
                )
            ):

                self.cells[
                    self.cell(
                        x,
                        y
                    )
                ] += (
                    self.sample_spacing_m
                )


    # ============================================================
    # V65.8：CAR 最終 OSM 路線水體鄰近 service/track 二次避讓
    # ============================================================

    # ============================================================
    # 第一階段的水利管理道路判斷仍維持 V65.3/V64 原規則，不全面封路。
    # _v65_8_is_soft_water_candidate 已提前定義；此處直接沿用。

    def _v65_8_edge_weight_for_path(weight_attr):
        def _weight(u, v, data):
            if G.is_multigraph():
                best = float("inf")
                found = False
                for attrs in data.values():
                    if _v65_8_is_soft_water_candidate(attrs):
                        continue
                    try:
                        w = float(attrs.get(weight_attr, attrs.get("length", float("inf"))))
                    except Exception:
                        w = float("inf")
                    if np.isfinite(w) and w < best:
                        best = w
                        found = True
                return best if found else None
            if _v65_8_is_soft_water_candidate(data):
                return None
            try:
                w = float(data.get(weight_attr, data.get("length", float("inf"))))
            except Exception:
                return None
            return w if np.isfinite(w) else None
        return _weight

    def _v65_8_select_edge_attrs(u, v, weight_attr):
        data = G.get_edge_data(int(u), int(v))
        if not data:
            return None
        best_attrs = None
        best_weight = float("inf")
        candidates = data.values() if G.is_multigraph() else [data]
        for attrs in candidates:
            if _v65_8_is_soft_water_candidate(attrs):
                continue
            try:
                w = float(attrs.get(weight_attr, attrs.get("length", float("inf"))))
            except Exception:
                w = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(w) and w < best_weight:
                best_weight = w
                best_attrs = attrs
        return best_attrs

    def _v65_8_build_pair_from_path(path, weight_attr):
        if not path:
            return {"distance": float("inf"), "path": [], "geometry": [], "travel_time_min": float("inf"), "rerouted": False}
        if len(path) == 1:
            node = int(path[0])
            nd = G.nodes[node]
            return {
                "distance": 0.0,
                "path": [node],
                "geometry": [(float(nd.get("x", 0.0)), float(nd.get("y", 0.0)))],
                "travel_time_min": 0.0,
                "rerouted": True,
            }

        geometry_coords = []
        total_distance = 0.0
        total_time = 0.0
        for u, v in zip(path[:-1], path[1:]):
            attrs = _v65_8_select_edge_attrs(u, v, weight_attr)
            if attrs is None:
                return {"distance": float("inf"), "path": [], "geometry": [], "travel_time_min": float("inf"), "rerouted": False}
            total_distance += float(attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
            total_time += float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", 0.0) or 0.0)
            edge_geom = attrs.get("geometry")
            if edge_geom is not None:
                edge_coords = [(float(lon), float(lat)) for lon, lat in edge_geom.coords]
            else:
                udata, vdata = G.nodes[int(u)], G.nodes[int(v)]
                edge_coords = [(float(udata["x"]), float(udata["y"])), (float(vdata["x"]), float(vdata["y"]))]
            if not geometry_coords:
                geometry_coords.extend(edge_coords)
            elif geometry_coords[-1] == edge_coords[0]:
                geometry_coords.extend(edge_coords[1:])
            else:
                geometry_coords.extend(edge_coords)

        return {
            "distance": float(total_distance),
            "path": [int(x) for x in path],
            "geometry": geometry_coords,
            "travel_time_min": float(total_time),
            "rerouted": True,
        }


    def route_pair_v65_8(a, b, weight_attr="route_weight"):
        base = route_pair(a, b, weight_attr=weight_attr)
        if VEHICLE_MODE != "CAR" or not CAR_WATER_REROUTE_ENABLED:
            return base
        base_path = base.get("path", [])
        soft_used = []
        soft_used_details = []
        for u, v in zip(base_path[:-1], base_path[1:]):
            data = G.get_edge_data(u, v)
            if not data:
                continue
            candidates = list(data.items()) if G.is_multigraph() else [(0, data)]
            best = None
            best_key = 0
            best_w = float("inf")
            for _key_i, attrs in candidates:
                try:
                    w = float(attrs.get(weight_attr, attrs.get("length", float("inf"))))
                except Exception:
                    w = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(w) and w < best_w:
                    best_w = w
                    best = attrs
                    best_key = _key_i
            if best is not None and _v65_8_is_soft_water_candidate(best):
                soft_used.append(best)
                def _water_diag_val(_v):
                    if _v is None:
                        return ""
                    if isinstance(_v, (list, tuple, set)):
                        return ";".join(map(str, _v))
                    return str(_v)
                try:
                    _wparsed = _car_tag_bundle(best)
                except Exception:
                    _wparsed = {}
                try:
                    _wspecial = _car_special_management_road_reason(best, _wparsed)
                except Exception:
                    _wspecial = ""
                soft_used_details.append({
                    "u": int(u), "v": int(v), "key": int(best_key),
                    "OSM_ID": _water_diag_val(best.get("osmid", "")),
                    "道路名稱": _water_diag_val(best.get("name", "")),
                    "highway": _water_diag_val(best.get("highway", "")),
                    "service": _water_diag_val(best.get("service", "")),
                    "surface": _water_diag_val(best.get("surface", "")),
                    "width": _water_diag_val(best.get("width", "")),
                    "narrow": _water_diag_val(best.get("narrow", "")),
                    "embankment": _water_diag_val(best.get("embankment", "")),
                    "motorcycle": _water_diag_val(best.get("motorcycle", "")),
                    "access": _water_diag_val(best.get("access", "")),
                    "vehicle": _water_diag_val(best.get("vehicle", "")),
                    "motor_vehicle": _water_diag_val(best.get("motor_vehicle", "")),
                    "motorcar": _water_diag_val(best.get("motorcar", "")),
                    "水體鄰近": bool(best.get("_car_v64_water_adjacent", False)),
                    "V65_特殊管理道路原因": str(_wspecial or ""),
                    "明確允許汽車": bool(_is_explicit_car_allowed(best, _wparsed)),
                })

        if not soft_used:
            base["rerouted"] = False
            base["water_soft_edges"] = 0
            base["water_soft_edge_details"] = []
            return base

        source = int(nearest_nodes[int(a)])
        target = int(nearest_nodes[int(b)])
        try:
            safe_path = nx.shortest_path(G, source, target, weight=_v65_8_edge_weight_for_path(weight_attr), method="dijkstra")
            alt = _v65_8_build_pair_from_path(safe_path, weight_attr)
        except (nx.NetworkXNoPath, nx.NodeNotFound):
            alt = {"distance": float("inf"), "path": [], "geometry": [], "travel_time_min": float("inf"), "rerouted": False}
        except Exception:
            alt = {"distance": float("inf"), "path": [], "geometry": [], "travel_time_min": float("inf"), "rerouted": False}

        base_distance = float(base.get("distance", float("inf")))
        base_time = float(base.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
        alt_distance = float(alt.get("distance", float("inf")))
        alt_time = float(alt.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
        ratio = alt_distance / max(base_distance, 1e-9) if np.isfinite(alt_distance) and np.isfinite(base_distance) else float("inf")

        _water_diag = {
            "water_base_distance_m": base_distance,
            "water_base_time_min": base_time,
            "water_alt_distance_m": alt_distance,
            "water_alt_time_min": alt_time,
            "water_distance_delta_m": (alt_distance - base_distance) if np.isfinite(alt_distance) and np.isfinite(base_distance) else float("inf"),
            "water_time_delta_min": (alt_time - base_time) if np.isfinite(alt_time) and np.isfinite(base_time) else float("inf"),
            "water_time_ratio": alt_time / max(base_time, 1e-9) if np.isfinite(alt_time) and np.isfinite(base_time) else float("inf"),
        }

        if np.isfinite(alt_distance) and ratio <= float(CAR_WATER_REROUTE_MAX_DETOUR_RATIO):
            alt["rerouted"] = True
            alt["water_soft_edges"] = len(soft_used)
            alt["water_soft_edge_details"] = soft_used_details
            alt["water_reroute_ratio"] = float(ratio)
            alt.update(_water_diag)
            return alt

        base["rerouted"] = False
        base["water_soft_edges"] = len(soft_used)
        base["water_soft_edge_details"] = soft_used_details
        base["water_reroute_ratio"] = float(ratio)
        base.update(_water_diag)
        return base

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # V65.64.23：Virtual anchor／全點一次專用隔離測試
    # ============================================================
    if False and V65_64_20_VA_ONLY_TEST and VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR":
        print()
        print("=" * 70)

        print("=" * 70)

        _va_test_weight_attr = "route_weight"

        _va_test_forward_count, _va_test_forward_hits = count_forward_virtual_anchor_crossings(_va_test_order)
        print(f"V65.64.23 Virtual anchor pair cache：{len(_VA_PAIR_INTERMEDIATE_CACHE)} 組")

        print(f"V65.64.23 全點一次 Virtual anchor forward-crossing：{_va_test_forward_count} 段")
        if _va_test_forward_count:
            for _hit in _va_test_forward_hits[:20]:
                print("  ", _hit)
            raise RuntimeError("V65.64.23 全點一次專用測試失敗：仍存在 Virtual anchor forward-crossing。")
        print("✅ V65.64.23：Virtual anchor 實際通過順序與全點一次路線驗證通過")

                "地段": _row["地段"] if "地段" in _row.index else "",
                "地號": _row["地號"] if "地號" in _row.index else "",
                "經度": float(coords[int(_idx), 0]),

        _va_test_order_df.to_csv(_va_test_prefix + "_route_order.csv", index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
        with open(_va_test_prefix + "_route.geojson", "w", encoding="utf-8") as _f:

    # ============================================================
    # V65.9.12：每日分界完成後的「自動最佳切換點＋最低改善門檻」
    #
    # 核心修正：候選切換方案不再使用 TIME_D 判斷最終成本。
    # 直接呼叫與第五階段相同的 route_pair_v65_8()，因此：
    #   - 路徑權重與第五階段一致；
    #   - CAR 水體二次避讓與第五階段一致；
    #   - 「最後一點 → 固定起點」明確納入；
    #   - 距離、旅行時間、5 小時上限採用最終會繪出的 OSM 路徑。
    #
    # 不做：
    #   - 不重新切日
    #   - 不跨日搬點
    #   - 不重新執行全域 Relocate / Swap
    #   - 不修改 OSM / Dijkstra / snap
    # ============================================================

    _V65_10_PAIR_CACHE = {}

    def _v65_10_pair_metrics(a, b, weight_attr="route_weight"):
        key = (int(a), int(b), str(weight_attr))
        cached = _V65_10_PAIR_CACHE.get(key)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached

        pair = route_pair_v65_8(int(a), int(b), weight_attr=weight_attr)
        distance_m = float(pair.get("distance", float("inf")))
        if not np.isfinite(distance_m):
            result = (float("inf"), float("inf"))
            _V65_10_PAIR_CACHE[key] = result
            return result

        drive_min = pair.get("travel_time_min", None)
        try:
            drive_min = float(drive_min)
        except Exception:
            drive_min = float("nan")

        # 舊的 route_pair 若遇到沒有回傳 travel_time_min 的情況，
        # 直接從同一路徑重新依「實際被選 edge」累計旅行時間。
        if not np.isfinite(drive_min):
            drive_min = 0.0
            path = pair.get("path", [])
            for u, v in zip(path[:-1], path[1:]):
                attrs = _v65_8_select_edge_attrs(int(u), int(v), weight_attr)
                if attrs is None:
                    drive_min = float("inf")
                    break
                try:
                    drive_min += float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", 0.0) or 0.0)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        result = (float(distance_m), float(drive_min))
        _V65_10_PAIR_CACHE[key] = result
        return result

    def actual_closed_day_metrics(order, day_no):
        """以第五階段同一套 OSM route_pair_v65_8 計算每日閉合路線。"""
        current = [int(x) for x in order]
        if not current or current[0] != int(start_idx):
            return {
                "distance_m": float("inf"),
                "drive_min": float("inf"),
                "service_min": float("inf"),
                "total_min": float("inf"),
                "outbound_distance_m": float("inf"),
                "return_distance_m": float("inf"),
            }

        if len(current) == 1:
            service_min = daily_service_minutes(day_no, 0)
            return {
                "distance_m": 0.0,
                "drive_min": 0.0,
                "service_min": float(service_min),
                "total_min": float(service_min),
                "outbound_distance_m": 0.0,
                "return_distance_m": 0.0,
            }

        outbound_distance = 0.0
        outbound_drive = 0.0
        for a, b in zip(current[:-1], current[1:]):
            d, t = _v65_10_pair_metrics(a, b, "route_weight")
            if not np.isfinite(d) or not np.isfinite(t):
                return {
                    "distance_m": float("inf"),
                    "drive_min": float("inf"),
                    "service_min": float("inf"),
                    "total_min": float("inf"),
                    "outbound_distance_m": float("inf"),
                    "return_distance_m": float("inf"),
                }
            outbound_distance += d
            outbound_drive += t

        return_distance_m, return_drive_min = _v65_10_pair_metrics(
            current[-1], int(start_idx), "route_weight"
        )
        if not np.isfinite(return_distance_m) or not np.isfinite(return_drive_min):
            return {
                "distance_m": float("inf"),
                "drive_min": float("inf"),
                "service_min": float("inf"),
                "total_min": float("inf"),
                "outbound_distance_m": outbound_distance,
                "return_distance_m": return_distance_m,
            }

        service_min = daily_service_minutes(day_no, len(current) - 1)
        total_distance = outbound_distance + return_distance_m
        drive_min = outbound_drive + return_drive_min
        total_min = drive_min + service_min
        return {
            "distance_m": float(total_distance),
            "drive_min": float(drive_min),
            "service_min": float(service_min),
            "total_min": float(total_min),
            "outbound_distance_m": float(outbound_distance),
            "return_distance_m": float(return_distance_m),
        }

    # ============================================================
    # V65.10：每日地理集中最佳化／跨日分界小幅調整
    #
    # 目的：
    #   目前每日分界是沿著全域近→遠順序切成連續區段；
    #   點數多寡本身不是問題，真正要降低的是「同一天空間過度分散」造成的跨區移動。
    #
    # 本版不做：
    #   - 不追求每日點數平均
    #   - 不任意把遠近點跨日洗牌
    #   - 不破壞全域近→遠母序列
    #   - 不移動固定起點
    #
    # 本版只做：
    #   - 對相鄰工作日的「分界位置」前後小幅移動
    #   - 每次只移動 1～N 個「連續母序列」點位
    #   - 候選日重新執行既有 optimize_closed_day()
    #   - 以與第五階段完全相同的 actual_closed_day_metrics() 評估
    #   - 兩個受影響工作日都必須 ≤ 5 小時
    #   - 只有兩日合計實際 OSM 工時改善達門檻才接受
    #
    # 因此第 3 天即使有很多點，只要彼此很集中，也不會因點數多而被硬拆；
    # 第 1 天若因分散造成道路成本過高，則可能從鄰近日重新調整少量分界點。
    # ============================================================

    DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_ENABLED = True
    DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MAX_SHIFT_POINTS = 6
    DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MAX_PASSES = 3
    DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN = 1.00

    def _rebuild_raw_batches_from_cut_ranges_v65_10(ranges):
        return [
            [int(start_idx)] + [int(x) for x in visit_order[i:j]]
            for i, j in ranges
        ]

    def _actual_batch_total_v65_10(order, day_no):
        m = actual_closed_day_metrics(order, day_no)
        return float(m.get("total_min", float("inf")))

    _V65_39_BOUNDARY_LB_CACHE = {}
    _v65_39_boundary_stats = {"checked": 0, "pruned": 0, "full": 0}

    def _tsp_assignment_lower_bound_minutes_v65_39(order):
        """固定起點閉合路線旅行時間的 assignment-relaxation 安全下限。

        將 start + 查核點視為有向 cycle-cover 問題：每個節點恰有一條
        outgoing 與一條 incoming 邊，禁止 self-loop。允許形成多個 subtour，
        所以 assignment 成本一定不高於真正 Hamiltonian closed route 的最短
        travel time，可安全用來判斷「不可能再改善 >= 1 分鐘」的候選。
        """
        nodes = tuple(int(x) for x in order)
        if len(nodes) <= 1:
            return 0.0
        cached = _V65_39_BOUNDARY_LB_CACHE.get(nodes)
        if cached is not None:
            return float(cached)

        idx = np.asarray(nodes, dtype=np.int64)
        cost = np.asarray(TIME_D[np.ix_(idx, idx)], dtype=np.float64).copy()
        np.fill_diagonal(cost, np.inf)

        finite_mask = np.isfinite(cost)
        if not np.all(np.any(finite_mask, axis=1)) or not np.all(np.any(finite_mask, axis=0)):
            _V65_39_BOUNDARY_LB_CACHE[nodes] = float('inf')
            return float('inf')

        finite = cost[finite_mask]
        if finite.size == 0:
            _V65_39_BOUNDARY_LB_CACHE[nodes] = float('inf')
            return float('inf')

        big = max(float(np.max(finite)) * 1e6, 1e12)
        work = np.where(finite_mask, cost, big)
        try:
            row_ind, col_ind = linear_sum_assignment(work)
        except Exception:
            _V65_39_BOUNDARY_LB_CACHE[nodes] = float('inf')
            return float('inf')

        chosen = work[row_ind, col_ind]
        if np.any(chosen >= big * 0.5):
            _V65_39_BOUNDARY_LB_CACHE[nodes] = float('inf')
            return float('inf')

        lb = float(np.sum(chosen))
        _V65_39_BOUNDARY_LB_CACHE[nodes] = lb
        return lb

    def _boundary_candidate_safe_lower_bound_v65_39(raw_left, raw_right, left_day_no, right_day_no):
        left_lb = _tsp_assignment_lower_bound_minutes_v65_39(raw_left)
        right_lb = _tsp_assignment_lower_bound_minutes_v65_39(raw_right)
        if not np.isfinite(left_lb) or not np.isfinite(right_lb):
            return float('inf')
        left_service = daily_service_minutes(left_day_no, max(0, len(raw_left) - 1))
        right_service = daily_service_minutes(right_day_no, max(0, len(raw_right) - 1))
        return float(left_lb + right_lb + left_service + right_service)

    def _evaluate_boundary_shift_v65_10(current_ranges, boundary_no, shift, current_pair_total=float("inf")):
        """測試單一相鄰日界前後移動；boundary_no 為 0-based。"""
        ranges = [tuple(map(int, x)) for x in current_ranges]
        left_i, left_j = ranges[boundary_no]
        right_i, right_j = ranges[boundary_no + 1]

        if shift == 0:
            return None

        # shift > 0：把右日前段連續點移到左日；分界往後移。
        # shift < 0：把左日尾端連續點移到右日；分界往前移。
        new_cut = left_j + int(shift)
        if new_cut <= left_i or new_cut >= right_j:
            return None

        # 限制一次不可跨越相鄰日的原始範圍。
        ranges[boundary_no] = (left_i, new_cut)
        ranges[boundary_no + 1] = (new_cut, right_j)

        # 僅要求受影響兩天的範圍仍非空。
        if ranges[boundary_no][0] >= ranges[boundary_no][1]:
            return None
        if ranges[boundary_no + 1][0] >= ranges[boundary_no + 1][1]:
            return None

        # 用既有 DP 快取先做便宜的「明顯不可行」排除。
        left_day_no = boundary_no + 1
        right_day_no = boundary_no + 2
        li, lj = ranges[boundary_no]
        ri, rj = ranges[boundary_no + 1]

        left_est = seg_time_day1[li, lj] if left_day_no == 1 else seg_time_other[li, lj]
        right_est = seg_time_day1[ri, rj] if right_day_no == 1 else seg_time_other[ri, rj]
        if not np.isfinite(left_est) or not np.isfinite(right_est):
            return None
        if left_est > max_day_min + 1e-9 or right_est > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            return None

        raw_left = [int(start_idx)] + [int(x) for x in visit_order[li:lj]]
        raw_right = [int(start_idx)] + [int(x) for x in visit_order[ri:rj]]

        _v65_39_boundary_stats["checked"] += 1
        _safe_lb = _boundary_candidate_safe_lower_bound_v65_39(
            raw_left, raw_right, left_day_no, right_day_no
        )
        _required_improvement = float(DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN)
        if not np.isfinite(_safe_lb) or _safe_lb >= float(current_pair_total) - _required_improvement - 1e-9:
            _v65_39_boundary_stats["pruned"] += 1
            return None
        _v65_39_boundary_stats["full"] += 1

        nonlocal _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT
        _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT = "boundary"
        try:
            opt_left = _safe_optimize_day(raw_left, left_day_no)
            opt_right = _safe_optimize_day(raw_right, right_day_no)
        except Exception:
            _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT = "other"
            return None
        _V65_44_OPT_CONTEXT = "other"

        left_metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(opt_left, left_day_no)
        right_metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(opt_right, right_day_no)
        left_total = float(left_metrics.get("total_min", float("inf")))
        right_total = float(right_metrics.get("total_min", float("inf")))

        if (
            not np.isfinite(left_total)
            or not np.isfinite(right_total)
            or left_total > max_day_min + 1e-9
            or right_total > max_day_min + 1e-9
        ):
            return None

        return {
            "ranges": ranges,
            "opt_left": opt_left,
            "opt_right": opt_right,
            "left_metrics": left_metrics,
            "right_metrics": right_metrics,
            "pair_total_min": left_total + right_total,
            "shift": int(shift),
        }

    def _apply_daily_boundary_rebalance_v65_10(initial_batches):
        """沿全域 visit_order 的日界做小幅 local search；不追求點數平均。"""
        if not DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_ENABLED:
            return initial_batches, cut_ranges, 0, 0.0

        if len(initial_batches) <= 1:
            return initial_batches, cut_ranges, 0, 0.0

        # cut_ranges 代表「點位歸屬」的母序列分界；initial_batches 只負責目前每日日內順序。
        current_ranges = [tuple(map(int, x)) for x in cut_ranges]
        current_batches = [list(map(int, x)) for x in initial_batches]
        total_improvement = 0.0
        accepted_moves = 0

        def current_day_actual_total(day_no, batch):
            return _actual_batch_total_v65_10(batch, day_no)

        for pass_no in range(1, int(DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MAX_PASSES) + 1):
            pass_improved = False

            for boundary_no in range(len(current_ranges) - 1):
                left_day_no = boundary_no + 1
                right_day_no = boundary_no + 2
                current_left_total = current_day_actual_total(left_day_no, current_batches[boundary_no])
                current_right_total = current_day_actual_total(right_day_no, current_batches[boundary_no + 1])
                current_pair_total = current_left_total + current_right_total
                if not np.isfinite(current_pair_total):
                    continue

                best_candidate = None
                best_improvement = 0.0

                max_shift = max(1, int(DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MAX_SHIFT_POINTS))
                for shift in list(range(1, max_shift + 1)) + list(range(-1, -max_shift - 1, -1)):
                    candidate = _evaluate_boundary_shift_v65_10(
                        current_ranges,
                        boundary_no,
                        shift,
                        current_pair_total=current_pair_total,
                    )
                    if candidate is None:
                        continue

                    improvement = current_pair_total - float(candidate["pair_total_min"])
                    if improvement < float(DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN) - 1e-9:
                        continue

                    if improvement > best_improvement + 1e-9:
                        best_improvement = improvement
                        best_candidate = candidate

                if best_candidate is None:
                    continue

                current_ranges = list(best_candidate["ranges"])
                current_batches[boundary_no] = list(best_candidate["opt_left"])
                current_batches[boundary_no + 1] = list(best_candidate["opt_right"])
                accepted_moves += 1
                total_improvement += best_improvement
                pass_improved = True

                shift = int(best_candidate["shift"])
                direction = "往後移" if shift > 0 else "往前移"
                print(
                    f"每日分界最佳化 pass {pass_no}："
                    f"Day {left_day_no} ↔ Day {right_day_no} 分界 {direction} {abs(shift)} 點 | "
                    f"兩日實際 OSM 工時改善 {best_improvement:.2f} min | "
                    f"Day {left_day_no}={best_candidate['left_metrics']['total_min']/60:.2f} hr | "
                    f"Day {right_day_no}={best_candidate['right_metrics']['total_min']/60:.2f} hr"
                )

            if not pass_improved:
                print(
                    f"每日分界最佳化 pass {pass_no}：無達到"
                    f" {DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN:.2f} 分鐘改善的分界調整。"
                )
                break

        if accepted_moves == 0:
            print(
                f"每日地理集中最佳化：未找到可接受的分界調整 | "
                f"門檻={DAILY_BOUNDARY_REBALANCE_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN:.2f} min"
            )
            return current_batches, current_ranges, 0, 0.0

        # 最終完整性檢查：每個點仍只出現一次，且每日日內皆有固定起點。
        assigned = [idx for batch in current_batches for idx in batch[1:]]
        if (
            len(assigned) != M
            or len(set(assigned)) != M
            or set(assigned) != set(visit_order)
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "每日分界調整後點位完整性檢查失敗。"
            )

        for day_no, batch in enumerate(current_batches, start=1):
            metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(batch, day_no)
            if (
                not np.isfinite(metrics["total_min"])
                or metrics["total_min"] > max_day_min + 1e-9
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"第 {day_no} 日分界調整後超過每日上限："
                    f"{metrics['total_min']:.2f} min。"
                )

        return current_batches, current_ranges, accepted_moves, total_improvement

    # V65.10 必須在 actual_closed_day_metrics() 定義之後執行，
    # 這樣 CAR 水體二次避讓與第五階段實際路徑成本會直接納入判斷。
    batches_before_v65_10 = [list(map(int, x)) for x in batches]
    _perf_boundary_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    batches, cut_ranges, boundary_move_count_v65_10, boundary_total_improvement_v65_10 = (
        _apply_daily_boundary_rebalance_v65_10(batches_before_v65_10)
    )
    print(
        f"每日地理集中最佳化完成：接受 {boundary_move_count_v65_10} 次分界調整 | "
        f"兩日合計實際 OSM 改善 {boundary_total_improvement_v65_10:.2f} min"
    )

    # ============================================================
    # V65.13：真正有限跨日點位交換（單次涉及查核點總數最多 5 點）
    #
    # V65.11.1：已確認 v65.10 每日分界最佳化可正常承接。
    # V65.13：將原本「單向搬點」升級為真正的跨日交換：
    #   - 只處理相鄰工作日。
    #   - 支援 1↔1、1↔2、2↔1、1↔3、3↔1……。
    #   - 單次交換／移轉涉及的查核點總數硬上限 = 5 點。
    #   - 候選池擴大，不再只看前 5 個候選點；以 TIME_D 先做候選篩選，
    #     再以實際 OSM 閉合路線判斷。
    #   - 每次候選方案兩側工作日都必須 ≤ 5 小時。
    #   - 每次只接受兩日合計實際 OSM 工時至少改善 1 分鐘的方案。
    #   - 每輪從全部相鄰工作日候選中選出「實際改善最大」方案。
    #   - 最多 3 輪；每次接受後重新建立下一輪候選。
    #   - 不追求每日點數平均，不改固定起點，不改已完成案件。
    #   - 先保留 v65.10 的每日分界微調，再執行本段。
    #
    # 與 v65.11 的差異：
    #   舊版只會 source → target 單向搬點。
    #   本版可同時從兩日拿點互換，因此可處理「A 日有一個不適合的點、
    #   B 日也有一個不適合的點」的真正跨日交換情況。
    # ============================================================

    # ================================================================
    # V65.14：快速篩選＋少量實際 OSM 驗證
    # ================================================================
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_ENABLED = True
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_MAX_POINTS = 5
    # v65.14：由 3 pass 降為 2 pass，避免邊際改善反覆重算。
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_MAX_PASSES = 2
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN = 1.00
    # 候選池保留 15 點，仍可涵蓋較不明顯的跨日候選。
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_CANDIDATE_POOL = 15
    # v65.14：每個相鄰日界只讓前 5 組進入真正 OSM 評估。
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_TOP_EVALUATIONS = 5
    # v65.14：5 組先進入候選排序；真正昂貴的實際 OSM＋輕量排序最多驗證 2 組。
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_ACTUAL_VALIDATION_CANDIDATES = 2
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_LIGHT_RELOCATE_PASSES = 1
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_LIGHT_SWAP_PASSES = 1
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_LIGHT_WINDOW = 8
    DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_USE_FAST_TIME_SCREEN = True

    def _cross_day_candidate_score_v65_14(point_idx, own_points, other_points):
        """以 TIME_D 判斷點位更適合哪一天；分數越高，越值得移往 other day。"""
        point_idx = int(point_idx)
        own_peers = [int(x) for x in own_points if int(x) != point_idx]
        other_peers = [int(x) for x in other_points if int(x) != int(start_idx)]

        def _nearest_mean(nodes, k=3):
            if not nodes:
                return float("inf")
            vals = []
            for x in nodes:
                try:
                    v = float(TIME_D[point_idx, int(x)])
                except Exception:
                    v = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(v):
                    vals.append(v)
            if not vals:
                return float("inf")
            vals.sort()
            return float(np.mean(vals[:min(k, len(vals))]))

        own_ref = own_peers + [int(start_idx)]
        other_ref = other_peers + [int(start_idx)]
        own_near = _nearest_mean(own_ref)
        other_near = _nearest_mean(other_ref)
        if not np.isfinite(own_near) and not np.isfinite(other_near):
            return float("-inf")
        if not np.isfinite(own_near):
            return float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(other_near):
            return float("-inf")
        return float(own_near - other_near)

    def _rank_cross_day_candidates_v65_14(source_batch, target_batch):
        """候選池最多 15 點；5 點是單次交換硬上限。"""
        source_points = [int(x) for x in source_batch[1:]]
        target_points = [int(x) for x in target_batch[1:]]
        scored = []
        for p in source_points:
            score = _cross_day_candidate_score_v65_14(
                p,
                source_points,
                target_points,
            )
            scored.append((float(score), int(p)))
        scored.sort(key=lambda z: (-z[0], z[1]))
        pool_n = min(
            len(scored),
            max(1, int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_CANDIDATE_POOL)),
        )
        return [p for _, p in scored[:pool_n]]

    def _cross_day_group_score_v65_14(group_points, source_points, target_points):
        """TIME_D 鄰近度粗篩，僅作候選排序，不作最終採納依據。"""
        score = 0.0
        for p in group_points:
            score += _cross_day_candidate_score_v65_14(
                int(p),
                source_points,
                target_points,
            )
        return float(score)

    def _make_cross_day_fast_route_context_v65_20(batch, day_no):
        """V65.22：建立跨日候選快速評估的批次上下文；不改變任何成本定義。"""
        points = [int(x) for x in batch[1:]]
        n = len(points)
        edge_times = np.zeros(max(n + 1, 1), dtype=np.float64)
        edge_times[0] = float(TIME_D[int(start_idx), points[0]]) if n else 0.0
        if n:
            for i in range(n - 1):
                edge_times[i + 1] = float(TIME_D[points[i], points[i + 1]])
            edge_times[n] = float(TIME_D[points[-1], int(start_idx)])
        prefix = np.zeros(max(n + 2, 1), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n + 1):
            prefix[i + 1] = prefix[i] + edge_times[i]
        pos = {int(p): i for i, p in enumerate(points)}
        return {
            "points": points,
            "n": n,
            "edge_times": edge_times,
            "prefix": prefix,
            "pos": pos,
            "day_no": int(day_no),
            "service_per_point": float(POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES),
            "fixed_start_service": float(POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES if int(day_no) == 1 and COUNT_FIXED_START_AS_WORK_POINT else 0.0),
        }

    def _cross_day_fast_route_time_after_move_v65_20(ctx, remove_points, append_points):
        """V65.22：精確計算「原順序刪除＋尾端追加」後閉合 TIME_D。

        與舊版 closed_day_total_minutes() 完全相同的數學結果，
        但不再逐候選掃描整條路線；刪除點最多 5 個，因此改為依連續刪除區段 O(k) 更新。
        """
        points = ctx["points"]
        n = int(ctx["n"])
        if n == 0:
            remaining_time = 0.0
        else:
            remove_set = {int(x) for x in remove_points}
            remove_pos = sorted(ctx["pos"][x] for x in remove_set if x in ctx["pos"])
            if len(remove_pos) >= n:
                remaining_time = 0.0
            elif not remove_pos:
                remaining_time = float(ctx["prefix"][n + 1])
            else:
                # 原閉合路線 edge 總時間。
                remaining_time = float(ctx["prefix"][n + 1])
                # 找出 maximal consecutive removed-position runs。
                runs = []
                rs = prev = remove_pos[0]
                for rp in remove_pos[1:]:
                    if rp == prev + 1:
                        prev = rp
                    else:
                        runs.append((rs, prev))
                        rs = prev = rp
                runs.append((rs, prev))

                edge_times = ctx["edge_times"]
                for l, r in runs:
                    # 原路線從 prev_node 經 removed run 到 next_node 的所有 edge。
                    # edge index l..r+1；以 prefix O(1) 取出。
                    removed_edge_sum = float(ctx["prefix"][r + 2] - ctx["prefix"][l])
                    if l == 0:
                        prev_node = int(start_idx)
                    else:
                        prev_node = int(points[l - 1])
                    if r == n - 1:
                        next_node = int(start_idx)
                    else:
                        next_node = int(points[r + 1])
                    bridge = float(TIME_D[prev_node, next_node])
                    if not np.isfinite(bridge):
                        return float("inf")
                    remaining_time += bridge - removed_edge_sum

        append_points = [int(x) for x in append_points]
        if append_points:
            # 與舊版完全一致：刪除後的剩餘順序不變，跨日點依 set iteration 順序追加。
            # 找出剩餘序列最後一點；若無剩餘則由固定起點直接進入追加點。
            remove_set = {int(x) for x in remove_points}
            last_remaining = None
            for p in reversed(points):
                if int(p) not in remove_set:
                    last_remaining = int(p)
                    break

            if last_remaining is None:
                # 目前 remaining_time 已包含原路線成本，但全部點被移除時應為 0。
                append_time = float(TIME_D[int(start_idx), append_points[0]])
            else:
                old_return = float(TIME_D[last_remaining, int(start_idx)])
                append_time = float(TIME_D[last_remaining, append_points[0]])
                remaining_time -= old_return

            if not np.isfinite(append_time):
                return float("inf")
            for a, b in zip(append_points[:-1], append_points[1:]):
                d = float(TIME_D[a, b])
                if not np.isfinite(d):
                    return float("inf")
                append_time += d
            back = float(TIME_D[append_points[-1], int(start_idx)])
            if not np.isfinite(back):
                return float("inf")
            append_time += back
            remaining_time += append_time

        if not np.isfinite(remaining_time):
            return float("inf")
        visit_count = n - len(set(int(x) for x in remove_points)) + len(append_points)
        service = float(visit_count * ctx["service_per_point"] + ctx["fixed_start_service"])
        return float(remaining_time + service)

    from functools import lru_cache

    @lru_cache(maxsize=None)
    def _cross_day_append_extra_travel_v65_21(predecessor_node, append_group_key):
        """V65.22：快取「由既有最後節點接到追加點群再回固定起點」的純行駛時間差。

        append_group_key 保持為 tuple(sorted(group))；實際追加順序仍沿用 v65.21
        的 list(set(group))，因此不改變既有候選的數學定義。
        """
        append_group = list(set(int(x) for x in append_group_key))
        if not append_group:
            return 0.0

        pred = None if predecessor_node is None else int(predecessor_node)
        first = int(append_group[0])
        extra = 0.0
        if pred is None:
            extra += float(TIME_D[int(start_idx), first])
        else:
            extra -= float(TIME_D[pred, int(start_idx)])
            extra += float(TIME_D[pred, first])

        for a, b in zip(append_group[:-1], append_group[1:]):
            extra += float(TIME_D[int(a), int(b)])
        extra += float(TIME_D[int(append_group[-1]), int(start_idx)])
        if not np.isfinite(extra):
            return float('inf')
        return float(extra)

    def _build_cross_day_group_fast_info_v65_21(ctx, pool, max_total):
        """V65.22：一次建立候選群組的刪點後基礎路線資訊。

        每個 group 只做一次刪點後閉合路線計算，之後所有雙向交換候選都直接查表；
        數學結果等價於 v65.20 的逐候選 TIME_D 精確計算。
        """
        from itertools import combinations

        points = ctx["points"]
        n = int(ctx["n"])
        edge_times = ctx["edge_times"]
        prefix = ctx["prefix"]
        pos = ctx["pos"]
        service = float(ctx["service_per_point"])
        fixed_start_service = float(ctx["fixed_start_service"])
        original_travel = float(prefix[n + 1]) if n else 0.0

        info = {}

        for k in range(0, min(int(max_total), len(pool)) + 1):
            for group in combinations(pool, k):
                group = tuple(sorted(int(x) for x in group))
                remove_set = set(group)
                remove_pos = sorted(pos[int(x)] for x in remove_set if int(x) in pos)

                if len(remove_pos) >= n:
                    base_travel = 0.0
                    last_remaining = None
                elif not remove_pos:
                    base_travel = original_travel
                    last_remaining = int(points[-1]) if points else None
                else:
                    base_travel = original_travel
                    runs = []
                    rs = prev = remove_pos[0]
                    for rp in remove_pos[1:]:
                        if rp == prev + 1:
                            prev = rp
                        else:
                            runs.append((rs, prev))
                            rs = prev = rp
                    runs.append((rs, prev))

                    for l, r in runs:
                        removed_edge_sum = float(prefix[r + 2] - prefix[l])
                        if l == 0:
                            prev_node = int(start_idx)
                        else:
                            prev_node = int(points[l - 1])
                        if r == n - 1:
                            next_node = int(start_idx)
                        else:
                            next_node = int(points[r + 1])
                        bridge = float(TIME_D[prev_node, next_node])
                        if not np.isfinite(bridge):
                            base_travel = float('inf')
                            break
                        base_travel += bridge - removed_edge_sum

                    last_remaining = None
                    for p in reversed(points):
                        if int(p) not in remove_set:
                            last_remaining = int(p)
                            break

                visit_count = n - len(group)
                base_total_min = (
                    float(base_travel)
                    + float(visit_count * service)
                    + fixed_start_service
                    if np.isfinite(base_travel)
                    else float('inf')
                )
                info[group] = {
                    "remove_group": group,
                    "base_travel_min": float(base_travel),
                    "base_total_min": float(base_total_min),
                    "last_remaining": last_remaining,
                    "visit_count_after_remove": int(visit_count),
                }

        return info

    def _fast_candidate_pair_time_v65_21(left_info, right_info, left_group, right_group, service_per_point):
        """V65.22：透過預先計算的 group info＋append cache，O(1) 組合候選 TIME_D。"""
        left_group = tuple(sorted(int(x) for x in left_group))
        right_group = tuple(sorted(int(x) for x in right_group))
        li = left_info[left_group]
        ri = right_info[right_group]

        left_fast = float(li["base_total_min"])
        right_fast = float(ri["base_total_min"])
        if not np.isfinite(left_fast) or not np.isfinite(right_fast):
            return float('inf'), float('inf'), float('inf')

        left_extra = _cross_day_append_extra_travel_v65_21(
            li["last_remaining"], right_group
        )
        right_extra = _cross_day_append_extra_travel_v65_21(
            ri["last_remaining"], left_group
        )
        if not np.isfinite(left_extra) or not np.isfinite(right_extra):
            return float('inf'), float('inf'), float('inf')

        left_fast += float(left_extra) + float(len(right_group) * service_per_point)
        right_fast += float(right_extra) + float(len(left_group) * service_per_point)
        return float(left_fast), float(right_fast), float(left_fast + right_fast)

    def _build_cross_day_exchange_candidates_v65_14(
        left_batch,
        right_batch,
        left_day_no,
        right_day_no,
        current_pair_fast_total,
    ):
        """
        V65.48：保留 v65.22 的候選集合與排序定義，
        但把 15 點候選池最多 174,436 組的 Python 逐組 fast-score，
        改成 NumPy 向量化計算。

        數學定義完全沿用：
          - 單向搬移 1～5 點
          - 雙向不等量交換，左右總涉及點數 <= 5
          - TIME_D 精確快速成本
          - fast_improvement → approx_score → 涉及點數 → tuple lexicographic
            的原排序規則
        不改實際 OSM 驗證、不改採納門檻、不改每日路線結果。
        """
        from itertools import combinations

        left_points = [int(x) for x in left_batch[1:]]
        right_points = [int(x) for x in right_batch[1:]]
        if not left_points or not right_points:
            return []

        left_pool = _rank_cross_day_candidates_v65_14(left_batch, right_batch)
        right_pool = _rank_cross_day_candidates_v65_14(right_batch, left_batch)
        max_total = int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_MAX_POINTS)

        left_score_map = {
            int(p): float(_cross_day_candidate_score_v65_14(int(p), left_points, right_points))
            for p in left_pool
        }
        right_score_map = {
            int(p): float(_cross_day_candidate_score_v65_14(int(p), right_points, left_points))
            for p in right_pool
        }

        left_ctx = _make_cross_day_fast_route_context_v65_20(left_batch, left_day_no)
        right_ctx = _make_cross_day_fast_route_context_v65_20(right_batch, right_day_no)

        left_group_info = _build_cross_day_group_fast_info_v65_21(
            left_ctx, left_pool, max_total
        )
        right_group_info = _build_cross_day_group_fast_info_v65_21(
            right_ctx, right_pool, max_total
        )

        service = float(POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES)

        def _group_arrays(group_info, score_map):
            groups = list(group_info.keys())
            # dict insertion order matches combinations() generation in the
            # original implementation: size 0, then size 1..max_total.
            group_index = {g: i for i, g in enumerate(groups)}
            m = len(groups)
            base_total = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
            last_pred = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int64)
            group_sizes = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int16)
            approx_score = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            append_first = np.full(m, -1, dtype=np.int64)
            append_tail = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

            for i, g in enumerate(groups):
                info = group_info[g]
                base_total[i] = float(info["base_total_min"])
                lp = info.get("last_remaining", None)
                last_pred[i] = -1 if lp is None else int(lp)
                group_sizes[i] = int(len(g))
                if g:
                    approx_score[i] = float(sum(score_map[int(p)] for p in g))
                    # Preserve the exact historical append order:
                    # list(set(group)).
                    append_order = list(set(int(x) for x in g))
                    append_first[i] = int(append_order[0])
                    total = 0.0
                    for a, b in zip(append_order[:-1], append_order[1:]):
                        total += float(TIME_D[int(a), int(b)])
                    total += float(TIME_D[int(append_order[-1]), int(start_idx)])
                    append_tail[i] = total
                else:
                    approx_score[i] = 0.0
                    append_first[i] = -1
                    append_tail[i] = 0.0

            return {
                "groups": groups,
                "group_index": group_index,
                "base_total": base_total,
                "last_pred": last_pred,
                "group_sizes": group_sizes,
                "approx_score": approx_score,
                "append_first": append_first,
                "append_tail": append_tail,
            }

        left_arr = _group_arrays(left_group_info, left_score_map)
        right_arr = _group_arrays(right_group_info, right_score_map)

        def _build_append_extra_table(pred_arr, append_arr):
            """pred node × append-group exact v65.21 append-extra travel table."""
            pred_nodes = sorted(set(int(x) for x in pred_arr["last_pred"].tolist()))
            pred_to_row = {p: i for i, p in enumerate(pred_nodes)}
            g_first = append_arr["append_first"]
            g_tail = append_arr["append_tail"]
            table = np.full((len(pred_nodes), len(append_arr["groups"])), np.inf, dtype=np.float64)

            nonempty = g_first >= 0
            if np.any(nonempty):
                first_idx = g_first[nonempty]
                tail = g_tail[nonempty]
                for pred in pred_nodes:
                    row = pred_to_row[pred]
                    if pred < 0:
                        vals = TIME_D[int(start_idx), first_idx] + tail
                    else:
                        vals = TIME_D[int(pred), first_idx] - float(TIME_D[int(pred), int(start_idx)]) + tail
                    table[row, nonempty] = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
            # Empty append group has exact extra travel = 0.
            empty_idx = append_arr["group_index"].get(tuple(), None)
            if empty_idx is not None:
                table[:, int(empty_idx)] = 0.0
            return pred_to_row, table

        # left_pred -> right_group, and right_pred -> left_group.
        left_pred_to_row, left_to_right_extra_table = _build_append_extra_table(left_arr, right_arr)
        right_pred_to_row, right_to_left_extra_table = _build_append_extra_table(right_arr, left_arr)

        left_by_size = {
            k: np.array(
                [i for i, sz in enumerate(left_arr["group_sizes"]) if int(sz) == k],
                dtype=np.int32,
            )
            for k in range(1, max_total + 1)
        }
        right_by_size = {
            k: np.array(
                [i for i, sz in enumerate(right_arr["group_sizes"]) if int(sz) == k],
                dtype=np.int32,
            )
            for k in range(1, max_total + 1)
        }

        empty_left = int(left_arr["group_index"][tuple()])
        empty_right = int(right_arr["group_index"][tuple()])

        left_ids_parts = []
        right_ids_parts = []

        # Transfer left -> right; transfer right -> left.
        for k in range(1, min(max_total, len(left_pool)) + 1):
            li = left_by_size[k]
            if li.size:
                left_ids_parts.append(li)
                right_ids_parts.append(np.full(li.size, empty_right, dtype=np.int32))
        for k in range(1, min(max_total, len(right_pool)) + 1):
            ri = right_by_size[k]
            if ri.size:
                left_ids_parts.append(np.full(ri.size, empty_left, dtype=np.int32))
                right_ids_parts.append(ri)

        # True two-way exchanges. Generate all combinations exactly as the
        # original nested combinations() loops did.
        for left_k in range(1, max_total):
            for right_k in range(1, max_total - left_k + 1):
                if left_k + right_k > max_total:
                    continue
                li = left_by_size[left_k]
                ri = right_by_size[right_k]
                if li.size == 0 or ri.size == 0:
                    continue
                left_ids_parts.append(np.repeat(li, ri.size))
                right_ids_parts.append(np.tile(ri, li.size))

        if not left_ids_parts:
            return []

        left_ids = np.concatenate(left_ids_parts)
        right_ids = np.concatenate(right_ids_parts)

        left_pred = left_arr["last_pred"][left_ids]
        right_pred = right_arr["last_pred"][right_ids]
        left_pred_rows = np.array(
            [left_pred_to_row[int(x)] for x in left_pred], dtype=np.int32
        )
        right_pred_rows = np.array(
            [right_pred_to_row[int(x)] for x in right_pred], dtype=np.int32
        )

        left_base = left_arr["base_total"][left_ids]
        right_base = right_arr["base_total"][right_ids]
        left_sizes = left_arr["group_sizes"][left_ids].astype(np.float64)
        right_sizes = right_arr["group_sizes"][right_ids].astype(np.float64)

        # Exact v65.21 candidate pair fast costs, vectorized.
        left_extra = left_to_right_extra_table[left_pred_rows, right_ids]
        right_extra = right_to_left_extra_table[right_pred_rows, left_ids]
        fast_left = left_base + left_extra + right_sizes * service
        fast_right = right_base + right_extra + left_sizes * service
        fast_pair = fast_left + fast_right
        fast_improvement = float(current_pair_fast_total) - fast_pair
        approx_score = (
            left_arr["approx_score"][left_ids]
            + right_arr["approx_score"][right_ids]
        )
        total_involved = left_sizes.astype(np.int16) + right_sizes.astype(np.int16)

        finite = (
            np.isfinite(fast_pair)
            & np.isfinite(fast_left)
            & np.isfinite(fast_right)
        )
        if not np.any(finite):
            return []

        left_ids = left_ids[finite]
        right_ids = right_ids[finite]
        fast_left = fast_left[finite]
        fast_right = fast_right[finite]
        fast_pair = fast_pair[finite]
        fast_improvement = fast_improvement[finite]
        approx_score = approx_score[finite]
        total_involved = total_involved[finite]

        # Exact lexicographic tuple tie-break encoding.
        # Visit point indices are non-negative; sentinel 0 is reserved for
        # tuple padding, so tuple() < (x, ...), and Python tuple ordering is
        # preserved by the fixed-width base representation.
        base_code = int(max(max(left_points, default=0), max(right_points, default=0)) + 2)
        powers = np.array(
            [base_code ** (max_total - 1 - i) for i in range(max_total)],
            dtype=np.int64,
        )

        def _tuple_codes(arr, ids):
            codes = np.zeros(len(ids), dtype=np.int64)
            # Only the first max_total entries exist by construction.
            for pos in range(max_total):
                vals = []
                any_mask = np.zeros(len(ids), dtype=bool)
                for j, gid in enumerate(ids.tolist()):
                    g = arr["groups"][int(gid)]
                    if pos < len(g):
                        vals.append(int(g[pos]) + 1)
                        any_mask[j] = True
                    else:
                        vals.append(0)
                codes += np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.int64) * int(powers[pos])
            return codes

        left_codes = _tuple_codes(left_arr, left_ids)
        right_codes = _tuple_codes(right_arr, right_ids)

        # np.lexsort: last key is primary. This matches the historical
        # Python sorted() key exactly:
        #   -fast_improvement, -approx_score, involved_count, left_tuple, right_tuple
        order = np.lexsort(
            (
                right_codes,
                left_codes,
                total_involved,
                -approx_score,
                -fast_improvement,
            )
        )
        top_n = min(max(1, int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_TOP_EVALUATIONS)), len(order))
        selected = order[:top_n]

        candidates = []
        for idx in selected.tolist():
            lg = tuple(left_arr["groups"][int(left_ids[idx])])
            rg = tuple(right_arr["groups"][int(right_ids[idx])])
            candidates.append({
                "left_remove": lg,
                "right_remove": rg,
                "approx_score": float(approx_score[idx]),
                "fast_left_min": float(fast_left[idx]),
                "fast_right_min": float(fast_right[idx]),
                "fast_pair_total_min": float(fast_pair[idx]),
                "fast_improvement_min": float(fast_improvement[idx]),
                "kind": "swap" if lg and rg else "transfer",
            })

        return candidates

    def _evaluate_cross_day_exchange_v65_14(current_batches, boundary_no):
        """相鄰兩日真正交換／搬移；先 TIME_D 快速篩選，再只驗證前 5 組實際 OSM。"""
        left_day_no = int(boundary_no) + 1
        right_day_no = int(boundary_no) + 2
        left_batch = [int(x) for x in current_batches[boundary_no]]
        right_batch = [int(x) for x in current_batches[boundary_no + 1]]

        if len(left_batch) <= 2 or len(right_batch) <= 2:
            return None

        current_left_metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(left_batch, left_day_no)
        current_right_metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(right_batch, right_day_no)
        current_pair_total = (
            float(current_left_metrics["total_min"])
            + float(current_right_metrics["total_min"])
        )
        if not np.isfinite(current_pair_total):
            return None

        try:
            current_pair_fast_total = (
                float(closed_day_total_minutes(left_batch, left_day_no))
                + float(closed_day_total_minutes(right_batch, right_day_no))
            )
        except Exception:
            current_pair_fast_total = float("inf")

        _v65_21_candidate_t0 = time.perf_counter()
        candidates = _build_cross_day_exchange_candidates_v65_14(
            left_batch,
            right_batch,
            left_day_no,
            right_day_no,
            current_pair_fast_total,
        )
        _v65_21_candidate_sec = time.perf_counter() - _v65_21_candidate_t0
        if not candidates:
            return None

        best = None
        actual_evaluated = 0
        actual_feasible = 0
        actual_limit = max(1, int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_ACTUAL_VALIDATION_CANDIDATES))

        # v65.14：候選已依快速 TIME_D 改善排序；實際昂貴驗證只取前 N 組。
        for c in candidates[:actual_limit]:
            left_remove = set(int(x) for x in c["left_remove"])
            right_remove = set(int(x) for x in c["right_remove"])
            total_involved = len(left_remove) + len(right_remove)
            if total_involved < 1 or total_involved > int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_MAX_POINTS):
                continue

            # v65.14：若快速估算已經完全沒有改善，直接不進昂貴 OSM 驗證。
            if (
                DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_USE_FAST_TIME_SCREEN
                and float(c.get("fast_improvement_min", 0.0)) < 0.0
            ):
                continue

            new_left_points = [
                int(x) for x in left_batch[1:] if int(x) not in left_remove
            ] + [int(x) for x in right_remove]
            new_right_points = [
                int(x) for x in right_batch[1:] if int(x) not in right_remove
            ] + [int(x) for x in left_remove]

            if not new_left_points or not new_right_points:
                continue

            raw_left = [int(start_idx)] + new_left_points
            raw_right = [int(start_idx)] + new_right_points
            actual_evaluated += 1
            try:
                # v65.14：候選測試只做 1 relocate + 1 swap、window=8，避免每組候選
                # 都完整跑一次每日最佳化。接受後，後續正式流程仍會再用完整 _safe_optimize_day。
                left_opt = _light_optimize_day_for_exchange_v65_14(raw_left, left_day_no)
                right_opt = _light_optimize_day_for_exchange_v65_14(raw_right, right_day_no)
            except Exception:
                continue

            left_metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(left_opt, left_day_no)
            right_metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(right_opt, right_day_no)
            left_total = float(left_metrics.get("total_min", float("inf")))
            right_total = float(right_metrics.get("total_min", float("inf")))

            if (
                not np.isfinite(left_total)
                or not np.isfinite(right_total)
                or left_total > max_day_min + 1e-9
                or right_total > max_day_min + 1e-9
            ):
                continue
            actual_feasible += 1

            pair_total = left_total + right_total
            improvement = current_pair_total - pair_total
            if improvement <= 0.0:
                continue

            if best is None or improvement > float(best["improvement_min"]) + 1e-9:
                best = {
                    "left_batch": left_opt,
                    "right_batch": right_opt,
                    "left_metrics": left_metrics,
                    "right_metrics": right_metrics,
                    "left_remove": tuple(sorted(left_remove)),
                    "right_remove": tuple(sorted(right_remove)),
                    "move_count": total_involved,
                    "kind": c["kind"],
                    "pair_total_min": float(pair_total),
                    "improvement_min": float(improvement),
                    "fast_improvement_min": float(c.get("fast_improvement_min", 0.0)),
                }

        if best is not None:
            best["actual_evaluated"] = int(actual_evaluated)
            best["actual_feasible"] = int(actual_feasible)
        return best

    def _validate_cross_day_batches_v65_14(test_batches):
        """確認點位無遺漏、無重複，且所有工作日均符合 5 小時上限。"""
        assigned = [int(idx) for batch in test_batches for idx in batch[1:]]
        if (
            len(assigned) != M
            or len(set(assigned)) != M
            or set(assigned) != set(visit_order)
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("跨日點位交換後完整性檢查失敗。")

        for day_no, batch in enumerate(test_batches, start=1):
            metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(batch, day_no)
            total_min = float(metrics.get("total_min", float("inf")))
            if not np.isfinite(total_min) or total_min > max_day_min + 1e-9:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"第 {day_no} 日交換後超過每日上限：{total_min:.2f} min。"
                )

    def _apply_daily_cross_day_exchange_v65_14(initial_batches):
        """
        相鄰工作日快速篩選＋實際 OSM 驗證：
          - 單次涉及點數總數 <= 5
          - 支援單向搬移及雙向不等量交換
          - 每個日界先用 TIME_D 粗篩
          - 每個日界只驗證前 N 組實際 OSM
          - 每輪選擇實際 OSM 改善最大者
        """
        if not DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_ENABLED or len(initial_batches) <= 1:
            return initial_batches, 0, 0.0

        current_batches = [list(map(int, x)) for x in initial_batches]
        accepted_moves = 0
        total_improvement = 0.0
        max_passes = int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_MAX_PASSES)
        min_improvement = float(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN)
        max_points = int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_MAX_POINTS)

        for pass_no in range(1, max_passes + 1):
            pass_best = None

            for boundary_no in range(len(current_batches) - 1):
