                candidate = _evaluate_cross_day_exchange_v65_14(
                    current_batches, boundary_no
                )
                if candidate is None:
                    continue
                improvement = float(candidate["improvement_min"])
                if improvement < min_improvement - 1e-9:
                    continue
                candidate["boundary_no"] = int(boundary_no)
                if pass_best is None or improvement > float(pass_best["improvement_min"]) + 1e-9:
                    pass_best = candidate

            if pass_best is None:
                print(
                    f"跨日點位交換 pass {pass_no}："
                    f"未找到達到 {min_improvement:.2f} 分鐘改善門檻的方案。"
                )
                break

            boundary_no = int(pass_best["boundary_no"])
            current_batches[boundary_no] = list(pass_best["left_batch"])
            current_batches[boundary_no + 1] = list(pass_best["right_batch"])
            accepted_moves += 1
            total_improvement += float(pass_best["improvement_min"])

            left_remove = list(pass_best["left_remove"])
            right_remove = list(pass_best["right_remove"])
            if left_remove and right_remove:
                direction_text = f"Day {boundary_no + 1} ↔ Day {boundary_no + 2}"
                detail_text = f"交換 {len(left_remove)}↔{len(right_remove)} 點"
            elif left_remove:
                direction_text = f"Day {boundary_no + 1} → Day {boundary_no + 2}"
                detail_text = f"移轉 {len(left_remove)} 點"
            else:
                direction_text = f"Day {boundary_no + 2} → Day {boundary_no + 1}"
                detail_text = f"移轉 {len(right_remove)} 點"

            print(
                f"跨日點位交換 pass {pass_no}：{direction_text} | "
                f"{detail_text} | 涉及 {int(pass_best['move_count'])} 點（上限 {max_points}） | "
                f"左側取出={left_remove} | 右側取出={right_remove} | "
                f"快速估算改善 {float(pass_best.get('fast_improvement_min', 0.0)):.2f} min | "
                f"實際 OSM 工時改善 {float(pass_best['improvement_min']):.2f} min | "
                f"Day {boundary_no + 1}={float(pass_best['left_metrics']['total_min'])/60:.2f} hr | "
                f"Day {boundary_no + 2}={float(pass_best['right_metrics']['total_min'])/60:.2f} hr | "
                f"實際驗證候選={int(pass_best.get('actual_evaluated', 0))} 組"
            )

        _validate_cross_day_batches_v65_14(current_batches)
        if accepted_moves == 0:
            print(
                f"快速跨日點位交換：未找到可接受方案 | "
                f"單次最多涉及 {max_points} 點 | "
                f"候選池={int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_CANDIDATE_POOL)} 點/日 | "
                f"實際 OSM 驗證上限={int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_ACTUAL_VALIDATION_CANDIDATES)} 組/日界 | "
                f"最多 pass={max_passes} | 門檻={min_improvement:.2f} min"
            )
        else:
            print(
                f"快速跨日點位交換完成：接受 {accepted_moves} 次 | "
                f"單次最多涉及 {max_points} 點 | "
                f"實際 OSM 驗證上限={int(DAILY_CROSS_DAY_EXCHANGE_ACTUAL_VALIDATION_CANDIDATES)} 組/日界 | "
                f"累計兩日實際 OSM 改善 {total_improvement:.2f} min"
            )
        return current_batches, accepted_moves, total_improvement

    # V65.14：在 v65.10 分界微調後，執行真正的跨日點位交換。
    def _print_daily_exchange_baseline_v65_13(label, day_batches):
        vals = []
        for _day_no, _batch in enumerate(day_batches, start=1):
            _m = actual_closed_day_metrics(_batch, _day_no)
            vals.append(float(_m["total_min"]))
        _txt = " / ".join(f"{v/60:.2f} hr" for v in vals)
        _sum = sum(vals)
        print(f"每日跨日點位交換{label}：每日總工時={_txt} | 全日合計={_sum/60:.2f} hr")
        return vals, _sum

    _v65_13_before_vals, _v65_13_before_sum = _print_daily_exchange_baseline_v65_13(
        "跨日交換前基準", batches
    )

    _perf_crossday_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    batches, cross_day_exchange_count_v65_13, cross_day_exchange_improvement_v65_13 = (
        _apply_daily_cross_day_exchange_v65_14(batches)
    )
    _validate_cross_day_batches_v65_14(batches)
    _v65_13_after_vals, _v65_13_after_sum = _print_daily_exchange_baseline_v65_13(
        "跨日交換後", batches
    )
    _global_improvement_after_exchange_v65_13 = _v65_13_before_sum - _v65_13_after_sum
    print(
        f"跨日交換全日合計改善：{_global_improvement_after_exchange_v65_13:.2f} min"
    )
    if _global_improvement_after_exchange_v65_13 < -1e-9:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "跨日交換後全日總工時反而增加，已拒絕該交換結果。"
        )


    DAILY_DIRECTION_SWITCH_MIN_HEAD_POINTS = 5
    DAILY_DIRECTION_SWITCH_MIN_TAIL_POINTS = 5
    # 只有實際 OSM 閉合總工時至少改善 2 分鐘，才接受方向切換。
    # 避免僅改善幾十秒就改變整天路線方向。
    DAILY_DIRECTION_SWITCH_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN = 2.0

    def _apply_daily_best_direction_switch_v65_10(order, day_no):
        current = [int(x) for x in order]
        if len(current) <= 2:
            return current, False, 0, 0.0, 0
        if current[0] != int(start_idx):
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"第 {day_no} 日固定起點錯誤：第一筆={current[0]}、預期={int(start_idx)}。"
            )

        points = current[1:]
        point_count = len(points)
        min_head = max(1, int(DAILY_DIRECTION_SWITCH_MIN_HEAD_POINTS))
        min_tail = max(1, int(DAILY_DIRECTION_SWITCH_MIN_TAIL_POINTS))
        first_k = min_head
        last_k = point_count - min_tail
        base_metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(current, day_no)
        base_time = float(base_metrics["total_min"])
        if not np.isfinite(base_time):
            return current, False, 0, 0.0, 0
        if first_k > last_k:
            return current, False, 0, 0.0, 0

        best_order = current
        best_time = base_time
        best_k = 0
        feasible_candidates = 0

        for k in range(first_k, last_k + 1):
            candidate = [int(start_idx)] + [int(x) for x in points[:k]] + [int(x) for x in reversed(points[k:])]
            metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(candidate, day_no)
            candidate_time = float(metrics["total_min"])
            if not np.isfinite(candidate_time):
                continue
            if candidate_time > max_day_min + 1e-9:
                continue
            feasible_candidates += 1
            if candidate_time < best_time - 0.01:
                best_time = candidate_time
                best_order = candidate
                best_k = k

        improvement = base_time - best_time
        min_improvement = float(DAILY_DIRECTION_SWITCH_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN)
        applied = bool(best_k > 0 and improvement >= min_improvement)
        if not applied:
            # 找到候選但改善不足門檻時，回到原順序。
            best_order = current
            best_k = 0
            best_time = base_time
        return best_order, applied, best_k, improvement, feasible_candidates

    switched_batches = []
    for day_no, batch in enumerate(batches, start=1):
        before = actual_closed_day_metrics(batch, day_no)
        switched, applied, switch_k, delta_min, feasible_n = _apply_daily_best_direction_switch_v65_10(batch, day_no)
        switched_batches.append(switched)
        after = actual_closed_day_metrics(switched, day_no)
        point_count = max(0, len(switched) - 1)

        if applied:
            tail_n = point_count - switch_k
            print(
                f"第 {day_no} 日最佳切換點：第 {switch_k} 點後切換 | "
                f"前 {switch_k} 點近→遠，後 {tail_n} 點遠→近 | "
                f"候選可行={feasible_n} 組 | "
                f"實際 OSM 工時 {before['total_min']/60:.2f} → {after['total_min']/60:.2f} hr | "
                f"改善 {delta_min:.2f} min"
            )
        else:
            print(
                f"第 {day_no} 日：未達最低改善門檻 | "
                f"門檻={DAILY_DIRECTION_SWITCH_MIN_IMPROVEMENT_MIN:.2f} min | "
                f"候選可行={feasible_n} 組 | 維持原順序 | "
                f"實際 OSM 工時={after['total_min']/60:.2f} hr"
            )

    batches = switched_batches

    # 最終實際 OSM 閉合工時驗證。這裡才是每日 5 小時上限的最終依據。
    for day_no, order in enumerate(batches, start=1):
        metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(order, day_no)
        if not np.isfinite(metrics["total_min"]) or metrics["total_min"] > max_day_min + 1e-9:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"第 {day_no} 日最終實際 OSM 路線超過每日上限："
                f"{metrics['total_min']:.2f} min > {max_day_min:.2f} min。"
            )

    # 每日最終方案摘要：此時 actual_closed_day_metrics 已完成定義，
    # 且 v65.9.12 自動切換點已套用，故摘要與最終 OSM 路徑一致。
    for day_no, order in enumerate(batches, start=1):
        metrics = actual_closed_day_metrics(order, day_no)
        total_m = float(metrics["distance_m"])
        drive_min = float(metrics["drive_min"])
        service_min = float(metrics["service_min"])
        total_min = float(metrics["total_min"])
        outbound = float(metrics["outbound_distance_m"])
        return_leg = float(metrics["return_distance_m"])
        visits = order[1:]
        print(
            f"Day {day_no:02d}：{len(visits):2d} 點 | "
            f"駕駛={drive_min/60:.2f} hr | "
            f"處理={service_min/60:.2f} hr | "
            f"總工時={total_min/60:.2f} hr | "
            f"往返距離={total_m/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"去程+點間={outbound/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"回起點={return_leg/1000:.2f} km"
        )

    # ============================================================
    # V65.7：單日兩方案改以「最終實際 OSM 路線」比較距離。
    # 前版 V65.7 以矩陣 ROAD_D / unrestricted_roundtrip_distance 比較，
    # 但每日方案與一次完成方案的 edge weight 不同，可能出現「比較距離」
    # 與最後繪製 geometry 距離不一致的情況。現在兩者都先透過 route_pair()
    # 取得各自最終會繪製的實際道路距離，再決定採用哪一套。
    def actual_roundtrip_distance_for_order(order, weight_attr):
        if not order or int(order[0]) != int(start_idx):
            return float("inf")
        total = 0.0
        for a, b in zip(order[:-1], order[1:]):
            pair = route_pair_v65_8(int(a), int(b), weight_attr=weight_attr)
            d = float(pair.get("distance", float("inf")))
            if not np.isfinite(d):
                return float("inf")
            total += d
        pair = route_pair_v65_8(int(order[-1]), int(start_idx), weight_attr=weight_attr)
        d = float(pair.get("distance", float("inf")))
        if not np.isfinite(d):
            return float("inf")
        total += d
        return float(total)

    # V65.7：單日真正比較。
    single_day_preferred = "daily"
    single_day_daily_distance_m = 0.0
    single_day_daily_drive_min = 0.0
    single_day_all_in_one_distance_m = 0.0
    single_day_all_in_one_drive_min = float(all_in_one_time_min)
    # V65.63.9：MOTOR 的最終幾何沿用 route_weight，讓台66平面側車道優先
    # 真正作用到第五階段繪製的道路；CAR 維持 travel_time_min。
    single_day_selected_weight_attr = ("route_weight" if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else "travel_time_min")
    if len(batches) == 1:
        daily_candidate_order = [int(x) for x in batches[0]]
        all_candidate_order = [int(x) for x in all_in_one_order]

        single_day_daily_distance_m = actual_roundtrip_distance_for_order(
            daily_candidate_order, "route_weight"
        )
        _all_in_one_compare_weight_attr = (
            "route_weight" if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else "travel_time_min"
        )
        single_day_all_in_one_distance_m = actual_roundtrip_distance_for_order(
            all_candidate_order, _all_in_one_compare_weight_attr
        )

        single_day_daily_drive_min = float(
            closed_day_drive_minutes_from_order(daily_candidate_order)
        )
        daily_total_min = single_day_daily_drive_min + float((N - 1) * POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES)
        all_total_min = float(single_day_all_in_one_drive_min) + float(all_in_one_service_min)
        all_in_one_feasible = all_total_min <= float(DAILY_MAX_HOURS) * 60.0 + 1e-9

        if (
            all_in_one_feasible
            and np.isfinite(single_day_all_in_one_distance_m)
            and single_day_all_in_one_distance_m < single_day_daily_distance_m - 1e-6
        ):
            batches[0] = all_candidate_order
            single_day_preferred = "all_in_one"
            single_day_selected_weight_attr = (
                "route_weight" if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else "travel_time_min"
            )

        print()
        print(
            "單日實際路線比較："
            f"分日={single_day_daily_distance_m/1000:.2f} km / {daily_total_min/60:.2f} hr；"
            f"一次完成={single_day_all_in_one_distance_m/1000:.2f} km / {all_total_min/60:.2f} hr；"
            f"採用={'一次完成' if single_day_preferred == 'all_in_one' else '分日'}。"
        )
    else:
        # 多日維持原本每日 route_weight 的 geometry。
        single_day_selected_weight_attr = "route_weight"

    # ============================================================
    # 33. 建立「每日」最終 OSM 實際道路路線
    #
    # 每一天：固定起點 → 當日點位 → 固定起點。
    # 不再使用「上一批 → 下一批」接線。
    # ============================================================

    # V45：統計 CAR 實際繪製路線使用到的道路適用性等級。
    _v64_water_adjacent_used_rows = []
    _v65_8_water_reroute_rows = []

    car_route_used = {
        "grade_counts": defaultdict(int),
        "grade_length_m": defaultdict(float),
        "reason_counts": defaultdict(int),
        "edges": 0,
        "non_a_edges": 0,
        "d_edges": 0,
        "speed_by_day": defaultdict(lambda: {
            "surface_m": 0.0,
            "motorway_m": 0.0,
            "expressway_m": 0.0,
            "surface_min": 0.0,
            "motorway_min": 0.0,
            "expressway_min": 0.0,
        }),
    }

    def _accumulate_car_route_suitability(path, day_no=None):
        if VEHICLE_MODE != "CAR" or not path or len(path) < 2:
            return
        for u, v in zip(path[:-1], path[1:]):
            edge_data = G.get_edge_data(u, v)
            if not edge_data:
                continue
            best_attrs = None
            best_weight = float("inf")
            candidates = edge_data.values() if G.is_multigraph() else [edge_data]
            for attrs in candidates:
                try:
                    w = float(attrs.get("route_weight", attrs.get("length", float("inf"))))
                except Exception:
                    w = float("inf")
                if w < best_weight:
                    best_weight = w
                    best_attrs = attrs
            if best_attrs is None:
                continue
            if _v65_8_is_soft_water_candidate(best_attrs):
                _v64_water_adjacent_used_rows.append({
                    "OSM_ID": ";".join(map(str, sorted(_edge_osmid_set(best_attrs)))) if _edge_osmid_set(best_attrs) else "",
                    "道路名稱": _edge_name_text(best_attrs),
                    "highway": tag_string(best_attrs.get("highway")),
                    "service": tag_string(best_attrs.get("service")),
                    "access": tag_string(best_attrs.get("access")),
                    "vehicle": tag_string(best_attrs.get("vehicle")),
                    "motor_vehicle": tag_string(best_attrs.get("motor_vehicle")),
                    "motorcar": tag_string(best_attrs.get("motorcar")),
                    "motorcycle": tag_string(best_attrs.get("motorcycle")),
                    "length_m": float(best_attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0),
                })
            grade = str(best_attrs.get("car_suitability_grade", "A"))
            length = float(best_attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
            reason = str(best_attrs.get("car_suitability_reason", ""))
            car_route_used["grade_counts"][grade] += 1
            car_route_used["grade_length_m"][grade] += length
            car_route_used["reason_counts"][reason] += 1
            car_route_used["edges"] += 1
            if grade != "A":
                car_route_used["non_a_edges"] += 1
            if grade == "D":
                car_route_used["d_edges"] += 1

            # V49：同時累計「實際被 route_pair 選中的 edge」之平面／高速／快速里程與時間。
            if day_no is not None:
                speed = float(best_attrs.get("travel_speed_kmh", CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH))
                edge_min = length / 1000.0 / max(speed, 1e-9) * 60.0
                if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
                    speed_class = _car_road_speed_class(best_attrs)
                    meter_key, min_key = {
                        "MOTORWAY": ("motorway_m", "motorway_min"),
                        "EXPRESSWAY": ("expressway_m", "expressway_min"),
                        "SURFACE": ("surface_m", "surface_min"),
                    }[speed_class]
                    car_route_used["speed_by_day"][int(day_no)][meter_key] += length
                    car_route_used["speed_by_day"][int(day_no)][min_key] += edge_min




    # 使用兩個 RoadIndex：
    #   day_road_index：只計算「同一天內」的重複道路
    #   all_days_road_index：計算與前面工作日已走道路的跨日重複
    day_road_index = None
    all_days_road_index = RoadIndex()


    actual_batches = []
    actual_connectors = []  # v12：每日皆從固定起點出發，不需要跨日接線
    return_infos = []

    total_batch_distance = 0.0
    return_distance = 0.0
    total_repeat_within_day = 0.0
    total_repeat_cross_day = 0.0

    day_times = []

    # V53：geometry 不再重新執行 NetworkX shortest_path，直接回溯前段
    # SciPy Dijkstra 的 predecessor；travel_time_min 使用 TIME_PREDECESSOR，
    # 每日方案使用 route_weight 的 ROUTE_PREDECESSOR。

    # V50/V65.63.12：全點一次跑完的最終 OSM 實際道路路線。
    # MOTOR 使用 route_weight；CAR 使用 travel_time_min。
    all_in_one_pair_geometries = []
    all_in_one_actual_distance = 0.0
    all_in_one_actual_drive_min = 0.0
    all_in_one_speed_diag = {
        "surface_m": 0.0,
        "motorway_m": 0.0,
        "expressway_m": 0.0,
        "surface_min": 0.0,
        "motorway_min": 0.0,
        "expressway_min": 0.0,
    }

    # V65.64.23：第五階段建立大量 route geometry 前，回收全點一次候選計算
    # 所產生的短命 Python 物件；不改任何路線結果。
    gc.collect()
    print("V65.64.23：第五階段前 Python 暫存物件回收完成")

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    _perf_mark("daily_partition_and_cross_day_optimization", _perf_daily_t0, emit=True)
    _perf_geom_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    print("第五階段：本機 OSM 實際道路路線（每日往返固定起點＋箭頭）")
    print("=" * 70)

    for day_no, order in enumerate(batches, start=1):

        _day_geom_t0 = time.perf_counter()

        # 每天重新建立自己的 RoadIndex，讓「日內重複」不會把前幾天的道路算進來。
        day_road_index = RoadIndex()

        pair_geometries = []
        day_distance = 0.0
        day_actual_drive_min = 0.0

        day_weight_attr = (
            single_day_selected_weight_attr
            if len(batches) == 1
            else "route_weight"
        )
        for a, b in zip(order[:-1], order[1:]):
            pair = route_pair_v65_8(a, b, weight_attr=day_weight_attr)
            if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and int(pair.get("water_soft_edges", 0) or 0) > 0:
                _v65_8_water_reroute_rows.append({
                    "工作日": day_no,
                    "起點索引": int(a),
                    "終點索引": int(b),
                    "起點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[int(a)]["名稱"]),
                    "終點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[int(b)]["名稱"]),
                    "原先鄰水service/track edge數": int(pair.get("water_soft_edges", 0) or 0),
                    "水體避讓是否改路": bool(pair.get("rerouted", False)),
                    "避讓前距離_km": float(pair.get("water_base_distance_m", float("nan"))) / 1000.0,
                    "避讓後距離_km": float(pair.get("water_alt_distance_m", pair.get("distance", float("nan")))) / 1000.0,
                    "距離增加_km": float(pair.get("water_distance_delta_m", float("nan"))) / 1000.0,
                    "避讓前時間_min": float(pair.get("water_base_time_min", float("nan"))),
                    "避讓後時間_min": float(pair.get("water_alt_time_min", pair.get("travel_time_min", float("nan")))),
                    "時間增加_min": float(pair.get("water_time_delta_min", float("nan"))),
                    "距離倍率": float(pair.get("water_reroute_ratio", 1.0) or 1.0),
                    "時間倍率": float(pair.get("water_time_ratio", float("nan"))),
                })
            _accumulate_car_route_suitability(pair.get("path", []), day_no)
            pair_geometries.append(pair["geometry"])
            day_distance += pair["distance"]
            _pair_t = pair.get("travel_time_min", float("nan"))
            if not np.isfinite(_pair_t):
                _, _pair_t = _v65_10_pair_metrics(int(a), int(b), day_weight_attr)
            day_actual_drive_min += float(_pair_t)

        geometry = []
        for segment in pair_geometries:
            if not segment:
                continue
            if not geometry:
                geometry.extend(segment)
            elif geometry[-1] == segment[0]:
                geometry.extend(segment[1:])
            else:
                geometry.extend(segment)

        # 日內重複：主路線先加入，再檢查回程是否與當日已走道路重疊。
        day_repeat_out = day_road_index.overlap(geometry)
        day_road_index.add(geometry)

        last_idx = order[-1]
        return_pair = route_pair_v65_8(last_idx, start_idx, weight_attr=day_weight_attr)
        if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and int(return_pair.get("water_soft_edges", 0) or 0) > 0:
            _v65_8_water_reroute_rows.append({
                "工作日": day_no,
                "起點索引": int(last_idx),
                "終點索引": int(start_idx),
                "起點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[int(last_idx)]["名稱"]),
                "終點名稱": str(work_df.iloc[int(start_idx)]["名稱"]),
                "原先鄰水service/track edge數": int(return_pair.get("water_soft_edges", 0) or 0),
                "水體避讓是否改路": bool(return_pair.get("rerouted", False)),
                "避讓前距離_km": float(return_pair.get("water_base_distance_m", float("nan"))) / 1000.0,
                "避讓後距離_km": float(return_pair.get("water_alt_distance_m", return_pair.get("distance", float("nan")))) / 1000.0,
                "距離增加_km": float(return_pair.get("water_distance_delta_m", float("nan"))) / 1000.0,
                "避讓前時間_min": float(return_pair.get("water_base_time_min", float("nan"))),
                "避讓後時間_min": float(return_pair.get("water_alt_time_min", return_pair.get("travel_time_min", float("nan")))),
                "時間增加_min": float(return_pair.get("water_time_delta_min", float("nan"))),
                "距離倍率": float(return_pair.get("water_reroute_ratio", 1.0) or 1.0),
                "時間倍率": float(return_pair.get("water_time_ratio", float("nan"))),
            })
        _accumulate_car_route_suitability(return_pair.get("path", []), day_no)
        return_geometry = return_pair["geometry"]
        return_dist = float(return_pair["distance"])
        _return_t = return_pair.get("travel_time_min", float("nan"))
        if not np.isfinite(_return_t):
            _, _return_t = _v65_10_pair_metrics(int(last_idx), int(start_idx), day_weight_attr)
        day_actual_drive_min += float(_return_t)
        day_repeat_return = day_road_index.overlap(return_geometry)
        day_road_index.add(return_geometry)
        day_repeat = day_repeat_out + day_repeat_return

        # 跨日重複：與前面所有工作日已使用道路比較。
        cross_repeat_out = all_days_road_index.overlap(geometry)
        all_days_road_index.add(geometry)
        cross_repeat_return = all_days_road_index.overlap(return_geometry)
        all_days_road_index.add(return_geometry)
        cross_day_repeat = cross_repeat_out + cross_repeat_return

        actual_batches.append({
            "distance": day_distance,
            "return_distance": return_dist,
            "duration": float(day_actual_drive_min),
            "geometry": geometry,
            "return_geometry": return_geometry,
            "repeat": day_repeat,
            "return_repeat": day_repeat_return,
            "within_day_repeat": day_repeat,
            "cross_day_repeat": cross_day_repeat,
            "source": "Local OSM / SciPy predecessor + OSM graph edge",
        })

        return_infos.append(return_pair)

        total_batch_distance += day_distance
        return_distance += return_dist
        total_repeat_within_day += day_repeat
        total_repeat_cross_day += cross_day_repeat

        visits = order[1:]
        service_min = len(visits) * POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES
        if day_no == 1 and COUNT_FIXED_START_AS_WORK_POINT:
            service_min += POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES

        drive_min = float(day_actual_drive_min)
        total_min = drive_min + service_min
        day_times.append({
            "day": day_no,
            "points": len(visits),
            "drive_min": drive_min,
            "service_min": service_min,
            "total_min": total_min,
            "distance_m": day_distance + return_dist,
            "outbound_distance_m": day_distance,
            "return_distance_m": return_dist,
        })

        _day_geom_sec = time.perf_counter() - _day_geom_t0

        print(
            f"Day {day_no:02d} | "
            f"{len(visits):2d} 點 | "
            f"drive={drive_min/60:.2f} hr | "
            f"service={service_min/60:.2f} hr | "
            f"total={total_min/60:.2f} hr | "
            f"road={(day_distance+return_dist)/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"out={day_distance/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"return={return_dist/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"日內repeat={day_repeat/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"跨日repeat={cross_day_repeat/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"cross={count_crossings(visits) if len(visits)>1 else 0} | "
            f"U-turn={count_uturns(visits) if len(visits)>1 else 0}"
        )

    _perf_mark("daily_final_osm_geometry", _perf_geom_t0, emit=True)
    _perf_all_geom_t0 = time.perf_counter()

    # ============================================================
    # 35A. V50：所有點位一次跑完的實際 OSM geometry
    # ============================================================
    all_in_one_pair_geometries = []
    all_in_one_actual_distance = 0.0
    all_in_one_actual_drive_min = 0.0

    _all_in_one_geometry_weight_attr = ("route_weight" if VEHICLE_MODE == "MOTOR" else "travel_time_min")
    print(f"全點一次跑完最終幾何權重：{_all_in_one_geometry_weight_attr}")
    for a, b in zip(all_in_one_order[:-1], all_in_one_order[1:]):
        pair = route_pair_v65_8(a, b, weight_attr=_all_in_one_geometry_weight_attr)
        if not np.isfinite(pair.get("distance", np.inf)):
            raise RuntimeError(f"V50 全點一次跑完 geometry：{a} → {b} 無可達路徑。")
        all_in_one_pair_geometries.append(pair["geometry"])
        all_in_one_actual_distance += float(pair["distance"])

        path = pair.get("path", [])
        for u, v in zip(path[:-1], path[1:]):
            edge_data = G.get_edge_data(u, v)
            if not edge_data:
                continue
            best_attrs = None
            best_w = float("inf")
            candidates = edge_data.values() if G.is_multigraph() else [edge_data]
            for attrs in candidates:
                try:
                    w = float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
                except Exception:
                    w = float("inf")
                if w < best_w:
                    best_w = w
                    best_attrs = attrs
            if best_attrs is None:
                continue
            edge_min = float(best_attrs.get("travel_time_min", 0.0) or 0.0)
            edge_len = float(best_attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
            all_in_one_actual_drive_min += edge_min
            if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
                speed_class = _car_road_speed_class(best_attrs)
                meter_key, min_key = {
                    "MOTORWAY": ("motorway_m", "motorway_min"),
                    "EXPRESSWAY": ("expressway_m", "expressway_min"),
                    "SURFACE": ("surface_m", "surface_min"),
                }[speed_class]
                all_in_one_speed_diag[meter_key] += edge_len
                all_in_one_speed_diag[min_key] += edge_min

    all_in_one_geometry = []
    for segment in all_in_one_pair_geometries:
        if not segment:
            continue
        if not all_in_one_geometry:
            all_in_one_geometry.extend(segment)
        elif all_in_one_geometry[-1] == segment[0]:
            all_in_one_geometry.extend(segment[1:])
        else:
            all_in_one_geometry.extend(segment)

    # 最後一個查核點 → 固定起點，作為獨立回程段。
    all_in_one_last = int(all_in_one_order[-1])
    all_in_one_return_pair = route_pair_v65_8(
        all_in_one_last, start_idx, weight_attr=_all_in_one_geometry_weight_attr
    )
    if not np.isfinite(all_in_one_return_pair.get("distance", np.inf)):
        raise RuntimeError("V50 全點一次跑完：回固定起點無可達路徑。")
    all_in_one_return_geometry = all_in_one_return_pair["geometry"]
    all_in_one_actual_distance += float(all_in_one_return_pair["distance"])
    for u, v in zip(all_in_one_return_pair.get("path", [])[:-1], all_in_one_return_pair.get("path", [])[1:]):
        edge_data = G.get_edge_data(u, v)
        if not edge_data:
            continue
        best_attrs = None
        best_w = float("inf")
        candidates = edge_data.values() if G.is_multigraph() else [edge_data]
        for attrs in candidates:
            try:
                w = float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
            except Exception:
                w = float("inf")
            if w < best_w:
                best_w = w
                best_attrs = attrs
        if best_attrs is None:
            continue
        edge_min = float(best_attrs.get("travel_time_min", 0.0) or 0.0)
        edge_len = float(best_attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
        all_in_one_actual_drive_min += edge_min
        if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
            speed_class = _car_road_speed_class(best_attrs)
            meter_key, min_key = {
                "MOTORWAY": ("motorway_m", "motorway_min"),
                "EXPRESSWAY": ("expressway_m", "expressway_min"),
                "SURFACE": ("surface_m", "surface_min"),
            }[speed_class]
            all_in_one_speed_diag[meter_key] += edge_len
            all_in_one_speed_diag[min_key] += edge_min

    all_in_one_total_actual_min = all_in_one_actual_drive_min + all_in_one_service_min
    daily_flat_order = [int(x) for batch in batches for x in batch[1:]]
    all_in_one_visit_order = [int(x) for x in all_in_one_order[1:]]
    all_in_one_same_as_daily = (all_in_one_visit_order == daily_flat_order)
    print(f"全部點一次跑完的實際道路路線：{all_in_one_actual_distance/1000:.2f} km")
    print(f"全點一次跑完實際駕駛時間：{all_in_one_actual_drive_min/60:.2f} hr")
    print(f"全點一次跑完實際總外勤時間：{all_in_one_total_actual_min/60:.2f} hr")
    print(f"全點一次跑完順序與分日方案完全相同：{'是' if all_in_one_same_as_daily else '否'}")

    total_distance = total_batch_distance + return_distance
    repeat_rate_within_day = (
        total_repeat_within_day / total_distance * 100.0
        if total_distance > 0 else 0.0
    )

    # ============================================================
    # 36. Route records（每日工作點，不重複列出每日固定起點）
    # ============================================================

    route_records = []
    global_sequence = 1

    # 固定起點只在第 1 天的路線表顯示一次。
    start_row = work_df.iloc[start_idx]
    route_records.append({
        "總路線序號": global_sequence,
        "工作日": 1,
        "批次": 1,
        "日內序號": 0,
        "角色": "固定起點",
        "點索引": start_idx,
        "原始_df_index": int(start_row["_原始_df_index"]),
        "原始序號": start_idx + 1,
        "Excel列": int(start_row["_原始_df_index"]) + 2,
        "名稱": start_row["名稱"],
        "縣市": start_row["縣市"] if "縣市" in start_row.index else "",
        "鄉鎮市區": start_row["鄉鎮市區"] if "鄉鎮市區" in start_row.index else "",
        "地段": start_row["地段"] if "地段" in start_row.index else "",
        "地號": start_row["地號"] if "地號" in start_row.index else "",
        "經度": float(coords[start_idx, 0]),
        "緯度": float(coords[start_idx, 1]),
        "OSM_Snap距離_m": float(snap_distances[start_idx]),
    })

    global_sequence = 2

    for day_no, order in enumerate(batches, start=1):
        for seq, idx in enumerate(order[1:], start=1):
            row = work_df.iloc[idx]
            route_records.append({
                "總路線序號": global_sequence,
                "工作日": day_no,
                "批次": day_no,
                "日內序號": seq,
                "角色": "查核點",
                "點索引": idx,
                "原始_df_index": int(row["_原始_df_index"]),
                "原始序號": idx + 1,
                "Excel列": int(row["_原始_df_index"]) + 2,
                "名稱": row["名稱"],
                "縣市": row["縣市"] if "縣市" in row.index else "",
                "鄉鎮市區": row["鄉鎮市區"] if "鄉鎮市區" in row.index else "",
                "地段": row["地段"] if "地段" in row.index else "",
                "地號": row["地號"] if "地號" in row.index else "",
                "經度": float(coords[idx, 0]),
                "緯度": float(coords[idx, 1]),
                "OSM_Snap距離_m": float(snap_distances[idx]),
            })
            global_sequence += 1

    route_order_df = pd.DataFrame(route_records)


    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # v44 修正：從 OSM MultiDiGraph 取得與 TIME_Dijkstra 相同的最佳時間 edge。
    # scipy predecessor 只記錄前一個 node，因此這裡需要在平行 edge 中
    # 找出 travel_time_min 最小者，才能正確回溯路段的道路型態。
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def _best_travel_edge(u, v):
        """取得 u -> v 的最短旅行時間 edge，回傳 (attrs, time_min)。"""
        try:
            edge_data = G.get_edge_data(u, v)
        except Exception:
            edge_data = None

        if not edge_data:
            return None, float("inf")

        best_attrs = None
        best_time = float("inf")
        best_length = float("inf")

        # MultiDiGraph：{key: attrs}
        if G.is_multigraph():
            candidates = edge_data.values()
        else:
            candidates = [edge_data]

        for attrs in candidates:
            if attrs is None:
                continue

            try:
                tmin = float(attrs.get("travel_time_min", float("inf")))
            except Exception:
                tmin = float("inf")

            # 若舊 edge 沒有 travel_time_min，依目前交通工具補算，
            # 讓診斷函式不會因單一 edge 缺欄位而整段失敗。
            if not np.isfinite(tmin):
                try:
                    length = float(attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
                except Exception:
                    length = 0.0
                if length > 0.0:
                    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
                        speed = _car_road_speed_kmh(
                            attrs,
                            CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH,
                            CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH,
                            CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH,
                        )
                    else:
                        speed = MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH
                    tmin = length / 1000.0 / float(speed) * 60.0

            if not np.isfinite(tmin):
                continue

            try:
                length = float(attrs.get("length", 0.0) or 0.0)
            except Exception:
                length = 0.0

            # 與 _build_sparse_weight_matrix 的邏輯一致：
            # 旅行時間最短優先；若完全相同則採較短 edge。
            if (tmin < best_time - 1e-12) or (
                abs(tmin - best_time) <= 1e-12 and length < best_length
            ):
                best_time = tmin
                best_length = length
                best_attrs = attrs

        if best_attrs is None:
            return None, float("inf")

        return best_attrs, float(best_time)


    _perf_mark("all_in_one_final_osm_geometry", _perf_all_geom_t0, emit=True)


    # ============================================================
    # 36A. 實際最終路線速度分類診斷（V48）
    #
    # CAR：直接統計第五階段 route_pair() 實際選中的 edge，
    # 因此地圖上的路線與速度診斷完全一致。
    # MOTOR：沿用當日實際駕駛時間作為總時間，不做高速／快速分類。
    # ============================================================

    speed_diagnostic_records = []
    total_speed_surface_m = 0.0
    total_speed_motorway_m = 0.0
    total_speed_expressway_m = 0.0
    total_speed_surface_min = 0.0
    total_speed_motorway_min = 0.0
    total_speed_expressway_min = 0.0

    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
        for day_no in range(1, len(batches) + 1):
            stats = car_route_used["speed_by_day"].get(day_no, {})
            day_surface_m = float(stats.get("surface_m", 0.0))
            day_motorway_m = float(stats.get("motorway_m", 0.0))
            day_expressway_m = float(stats.get("expressway_m", 0.0))
            day_surface_min = float(stats.get("surface_min", 0.0))
            day_motorway_min = float(stats.get("motorway_min", 0.0))
            day_expressway_min = float(stats.get("expressway_min", 0.0))
            total_speed_surface_m += day_surface_m
            total_speed_motorway_m += day_motorway_m
            total_speed_expressway_m += day_expressway_m
            total_speed_surface_min += day_surface_min
            total_speed_motorway_min += day_motorway_min
            total_speed_expressway_min += day_expressway_min
            total_speed_min = day_surface_min + day_motorway_min + day_expressway_min
            total_class_m = day_surface_m + day_motorway_m + day_expressway_m
            speed_diagnostic_records.append({
                "工作日": day_no,
                "平面道路里程_km": day_surface_m / 1000.0,
                "高速公路里程_km": day_motorway_m / 1000.0,
                "高速公路里程占比_percent": day_motorway_m / max(total_class_m, 1e-9) * 100.0,
                "快速公路里程_km": day_expressway_m / 1000.0,
                "快速公路里程占比_percent": day_expressway_m / max(total_class_m, 1e-9) * 100.0,
                "平面道路行駛時間_hr": day_surface_min / 60.0,
                "高速公路行駛時間_hr": day_motorway_min / 60.0,
                "高速公路時間占比_percent": day_motorway_min / max(total_speed_min, 1e-9) * 100.0,
                "快速公路行駛時間_hr": day_expressway_min / 60.0,
                "快速公路時間占比_percent": day_expressway_min / max(total_speed_min, 1e-9) * 100.0,
                "速度診斷行駛時間_hr": total_speed_min / 60.0,
            })

        if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
            _water_reroute_count = sum(1 for _r in _v65_8_water_reroute_rows if _r.get("水體避讓是否改路"))
            if _v65_8_water_reroute_rows:
                print(
                    f"汽車水體二次避讓：檢查={len(_v65_8_water_reroute_rows):,} 段｜"
                    f"採用替代道路={_water_reroute_count:,} 段"
                )
            if _v64_water_adjacent_used_rows:
                print(
                    f"⚠️ 汽車最終路線仍使用水體鄰近 service/track：{len(_v64_water_adjacent_used_rows):,} edge"
                )
            else:
                print("汽車水體二次避讓：最終路線未使用水體鄰近 service/track。")
        print("汽車道路速度分類：")
        print(f"  平面道路：{total_speed_surface_m/1000:.2f} km | {total_speed_surface_min/60:.2f} hr")
        print(f"  高速公路：{total_speed_motorway_m/1000:.2f} km | {total_speed_motorway_min/60:.2f} hr")
        print(f"  快速公路：{total_speed_expressway_m/1000:.2f} km | {total_speed_expressway_min/60:.2f} hr")
        total_class_m = total_speed_surface_m + total_speed_motorway_m + total_speed_expressway_m
        total_class_min = total_speed_surface_min + total_speed_motorway_min + total_speed_expressway_min
        motorway_dist_pct = total_speed_motorway_m / max(total_class_m, 1e-9) * 100.0
        expressway_dist_pct = total_speed_expressway_m / max(total_class_m, 1e-9) * 100.0
        motorway_time_pct = total_speed_motorway_min / max(total_class_min, 1e-9) * 100.0
        expressway_time_pct = total_speed_expressway_min / max(total_class_min, 1e-9) * 100.0
        print(f"  高速公路占比：{motorway_dist_pct:.2f}%（里程）")
        print(f"  快速公路占比：{expressway_dist_pct:.2f}%（里程）")
        print(f"  高速公路時間占比：{motorway_time_pct:.2f}%（時間）")
        print(f"  快速公路時間占比：{expressway_time_pct:.2f}%（時間）")
    else:
        for row in day_times:
            speed_diagnostic_records.append({
                "工作日": int(row["day"]),
                "平面道路里程_km": float(row["distance_m"]) / 1000.0,
                "高速公路里程_km": np.nan,
                "高速公路里程占比_percent": np.nan,
                "快速公路里程_km": np.nan,
                "快速公路里程占比_percent": np.nan,
                "平面道路行駛時間_hr": float(row["drive_min"]) / 60.0,
                "高速公路行駛時間_hr": np.nan,
                "高速公路時間占比_percent": np.nan,
                "快速公路行駛時間_hr": np.nan,
                "快速公路時間占比_percent": np.nan,
                "速度診斷行駛時間_hr": float(row["drive_min"]) / 60.0,
            })

    speed_diagnostic_df = pd.DataFrame(speed_diagnostic_records)

    # ============================================================
    # V45：汽車實際使用路徑適用性診斷
    # ============================================================

    car_route_used_rows = []
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
        total_used_edges = max(int(car_route_used["edges"]), 1)
        total_used_length_m = sum(car_route_used["grade_length_m"].values())
        for grade in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]:
            cnt = int(car_route_used["grade_counts"].get(grade, 0))
            km = float(car_route_used["grade_length_m"].get(grade, 0.0) / 1000.0)
            car_route_used_rows.append({
                "等級": grade,
                "說明": {
                    "A": "一般汽車道路",
                    "B": "疑似低汽車適用／服務／未鋪面等道路",
                    "C": "conditional 汽車限制",
                    "D": "汽車明確禁止（理論上不應出現在 CAR 實際路線）",
                }[grade],
                "實際路徑edge數": cnt,
                "edge數占比_percent": cnt / total_used_edges * 100.0,
                "實際路徑道路長度_km": km,
                "道路長度占比_percent": km / max(total_used_length_m / 1000.0, 1e-9) * 100.0,
            })
    car_route_used_df = pd.DataFrame(car_route_used_rows, columns=[
        "等級", "說明", "實際路徑edge數", "edge數占比_percent",
        "實際路徑道路長度_km", "道路長度占比_percent"
    ])

    # ============================================================
    # 36A-1. 汽車道路適用性診斷
    # ============================================================

    car_suitability_rows = []
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
        total_edges_seen = int(_car_diag_total_edges_seen)
        grade_counts = _car_diag_grade_counts
        grade_length = _car_diag_grade_length
        reason_counts = _car_diag_reason_counts

        finite_graph_lengths = sum(grade_length.values())
        for grade in ["A", "B", "C"]:
            count = int(grade_counts.get(grade, 0))
            km = float(grade_length.get(grade, 0.0) / 1000.0)
            pct_edges = count / max(total_edges_seen, 1) * 100.0
            pct_length = grade_length.get(grade, 0.0) / max(finite_graph_lengths, 1e-9) * 100.0
            car_suitability_rows.append({
                "等級": grade,
                "說明": {
                    "A": "一般汽車道路",
                    "B": "疑似低汽車適用／服務／未鋪面等道路，保留但提高成本",
                    "C": "conditional 汽車限制，無法確認實際時段是否可通行，保留但大幅提高成本",
                }[grade],
                "道路edge數": count,
                "edge數占比_percent": pct_edges,
                "道路長度_km": km,
                "道路長度占比_percent": pct_length,
            })

        # D 級已在建圖時排除，因此另外以「已排除」列示，不讓使用者誤以為 D=0。
        d_count = int(car_removed_grade_counts.get("D", 0))
        d_km = float(car_removed_length_m.get("D", 0.0) / 1000.0)
        car_suitability_rows.insert(0, {
            "等級": "D（已排除）",
            "說明": "汽車明確禁止；已從汽車路網移除",
            "道路edge數": d_count,
            "edge數占比_percent": np.nan,
            "道路長度_km": d_km,
            "道路長度占比_percent": np.nan,
        })

        car_suitability_rows.insert(1, {
            "等級": "D-V64（已排除）",
            "說明": "疑似水利／防汛／堤防管理道路；缺乏明確汽車允許標示時採保守排除",
            "道路edge數": int(car_removed_v64_special_count),
            "edge數占比_percent": np.nan,
            "道路長度_km": float(car_removed_v64_special_length_m / 1000.0),
            "道路長度占比_percent": np.nan,
        })

        # V65.17：原因統計在主要 edge pass 已完成，不再重新掃 G。
        for reason, cnt in sorted(reason_counts.items(), key=lambda x: (-x[1], x[0])):
            if not reason:
                continue
            car_suitability_rows.append({
                "等級": "原因",
                "說明": reason,
                "道路edge數": int(cnt),
                "edge數占比_percent": int(cnt) / max(total_edges_seen, 1) * 100.0,
                "道路長度_km": np.nan,
                "道路長度占比_percent": np.nan,
            })

        car_suitability_df = pd.DataFrame(car_suitability_rows)
    else:
        car_suitability_df = pd.DataFrame(columns=[
            "等級", "說明", "道路edge數", "edge數占比_percent",
            "道路長度_km", "道路長度占比_percent"
        ])

    # ============================================================
    # 36B. 每日分界診斷表
    # ============================================================

    boundary_diagnostic_records = []

    for bno in range(len(batches) - 1):
        left_batch = batches[bno]
        right_batch = batches[bno + 1]
        pen, details = boundary_continuity_penalty(left_batch, right_batch, return_detail=True)

        last_idx = int(left_batch[-1])
        first_idx = int(right_batch[1])
        direct_m = float(ROAD_D[last_idx, first_idx])
        via_start_m = float(ROAD_D[last_idx, start_idx]) + float(ROAD_D[start_idx, first_idx])
        via_gap_m = via_start_m - direct_m if np.isfinite(direct_m) and np.isfinite(via_start_m) else float("inf")
        via_ratio = via_start_m / max(direct_m, 1.0) if np.isfinite(direct_m) and np.isfinite(via_start_m) else float("inf")

        severe = bool(
            np.isfinite(direct_m)
            and np.isfinite(via_gap_m)
            and direct_m <= BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MAX_PAIR_M
            and via_gap_m >= BOUNDARY_VIA_START_MIN_GAP_M
            and via_ratio >= BOUNDARY_REVERSE_RATIO_MIN
        )

        top = details[0] if details else None
        boundary_diagnostic_records.append({
            "每日分界": f"Day {bno + 1} ↔ Day {bno + 2}",
            "前日末點": last_idx,
            "隔日首點": first_idx,
            "末點↔首點直接道路_km": direct_m / 1000.0 if np.isfinite(direct_m) else np.nan,
            "末點→起點→首點道路_km": via_start_m / 1000.0 if np.isfinite(via_start_m) else np.nan,
            "額外繞行_km": via_gap_m / 1000.0 if np.isfinite(via_gap_m) else np.nan,
            "經起點／直接道路倍數": via_ratio if np.isfinite(via_ratio) else np.nan,
            "分界懲罰_eqkm": pen / 1000.0,
            "3x3最高風險前日點": top["a"] if top else np.nan,
            "3x3最高風險隔日點": top["b"] if top else np.nan,
            "3x3最高風險直接道路_km": top["direct_m"] / 1000.0 if top else np.nan,
            "3x3最高風險額外繞行_km": top["via_gap_m"] / 1000.0 if top else np.nan,
            "3x3最高風險懲罰_eqkm": top["total_penalty_m"] / 1000.0 if top else np.nan,
            "嚴重反向重跑風險": "是" if severe else "否",
        })

    boundary_diagnostic_df = pd.DataFrame(boundary_diagnostic_records)

    # ============================================================
    # 37. 每日摘要
    # ============================================================

    daily_summary_records = []

    for day_no, order in enumerate(batches, start=1):
        info = actual_batches[day_no - 1]
        timing = day_times[day_no - 1]
        visits = order[1:]

        total_hours = timing["total_min"] / 60.0
        if total_hours > DAILY_MAX_HOURS + 1e-9:
            daily_status = "超過5小時（不可安排）"
        else:
            daily_status = "符合5小時上限"

        daily_summary_records.append({
            "工作日": day_no,
            "點數": len(visits),
            "起點": start_name,
            "第一個查核點": work_df.iloc[visits[0]]["名稱"] if visits else "",
            "最後一個查核點": work_df.iloc[visits[-1]]["名稱"] if visits else "",
            "駕駛距離_km": timing["distance_m"] / 1000.0,
            "去程與點間道路_km": timing["outbound_distance_m"] / 1000.0,
            "回起點距離_km": timing["return_distance_m"] / 1000.0,
            "預估駕駛時間_hr": timing["drive_min"] / 60.0,
            "點位處理時間_hr": timing["service_min"] / 60.0,
            "總工時_hr": total_hours,
            "工時狀態": daily_status,
            "剩餘時間至5小時_hr": DAILY_MAX_HOURS - timing["total_min"] / 60.0,
            "日內重複道路_km": info["within_day_repeat"] / 1000.0,
            "跨日重複道路_km": info["cross_day_repeat"] / 1000.0,
            "Crossing": count_crossings(visits) if len(visits) > 1 else 0,
            "U-turn": count_uturns(visits) if len(visits) > 1 else 0,
            "空間展開_km": batch_spread(visits) / 1000.0 if len(visits) > 1 else 0.0,
            "中心平均距離_km": centroid_spread(visits) / 1000.0 if len(visits) > 1 else 0.0,
        })

    daily_summary_df = pd.DataFrame(daily_summary_records)
    # backward-compatible alias for downstream display/export code

    # ============================================================
    # 37A. V50：全點一次跑完輸出資料
    # ============================================================
    all_in_one_order_records = []
    start_row = work_df.iloc[start_idx]
    all_in_one_order_records.append({
        "全點一次跑完序號": 0,
        "角色": "固定起點",
        "點索引": start_idx,
        "原始_df_index": int(start_row["_原始_df_index"]),
        "原始序號": start_idx + 1,
        "Excel列": int(start_row["_原始_df_index"]) + 2,
        "名稱": start_row["名稱"],
        "縣市": start_row["縣市"] if "縣市" in start_row.index else "",
        "鄉鎮市區": start_row["鄉鎮市區"] if "鄉鎮市區" in start_row.index else "",
        "地段": start_row["地段"] if "地段" in start_row.index else "",
        "地號": start_row["地號"] if "地號" in start_row.index else "",
        "經度": float(coords[start_idx, 0]),
        "緯度": float(coords[start_idx, 1]),
        "OSM_Snap距離_m": float(snap_distances[start_idx]),
    })

    for seq, idx in enumerate(all_in_one_order[1:], start=1):
        row = work_df.iloc[int(idx)]
        all_in_one_order_records.append({
            "全點一次跑完序號": seq,
            "角色": "查核點",
            "點索引": int(idx),
            "原始_df_index": int(row["_原始_df_index"]),
            "原始序號": int(row["_原始_df_index"]) + 1,
            "Excel列": int(row["_原始_df_index"]) + 2,
            "名稱": row["名稱"],
            "縣市": row["縣市"] if "縣市" in row.index else "",
            "鄉鎮市區": row["鄉鎮市區"] if "鄉鎮市區" in row.index else "",
            "地段": row["地段"] if "地段" in row.index else "",
            "地號": row["地號"] if "地號" in row.index else "",
            "經度": float(coords[int(idx), 0]),
            "緯度": float(coords[int(idx), 1]),
            "OSM_Snap距離_m": float(snap_distances[int(idx)]),
        })

    all_in_one_order_df = pd.DataFrame(all_in_one_order_records)

    all_in_one_summary_df = pd.DataFrame({
        "項目": [
            "路線類型",
            "有效點數",
            "查核點數",
            "工作日時間限制",
            "每點處理時間_min",
            "起點",
            "全部查核點各一次",
            "最後返回固定起點",
            "矩陣最佳化旅行時間_hr",
            "矩陣診斷道路距離_km",
            "實際OSM幾何道路距離_km",
            "實際OSM駕駛時間_hr",
            "點位處理時間_hr",
            "實際總外勤時間_hr",
            "與分日方案順序不同",
            "平面道路里程_km" if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else "機車道路里程_km",
            "高速公路里程_km" if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else "高速公路里程_km",
            "快速公路里程_km" if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else "快速公路里程_km",
        ],
        "數值": [
            "所有點位一次跑完",
            N,
            M,
            "不受每日5小時限制",
            POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES,
            start_name,
            True,
            True,
            all_in_one_time_min / 60.0,
            all_in_one_distance_m / 1000.0,
            all_in_one_actual_distance / 1000.0,
            all_in_one_actual_drive_min / 60.0,
            all_in_one_service_min / 60.0,
            all_in_one_total_actual_min / 60.0,
            bool(not all_in_one_same_as_daily),
            (all_in_one_speed_diag["surface_m"] / 1000.0),
            (all_in_one_speed_diag["motorway_m"] / 1000.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            (all_in_one_speed_diag["expressway_m"] / 1000.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
        ],
    })

    all_in_one_geojson = {
        "type": "FeatureCollection",
        "features": [],
    }
    if all_in_one_geometry and len(all_in_one_geometry) >= 2:
        all_in_one_geojson["features"].append({
            "type": "Feature",
            "geometry": {
                "type": "LineString",
                "coordinates": all_in_one_geometry,
            },
            "properties": {

                "距離_m": float(all_in_one_actual_distance),
                "駕駛時間_min": float(all_in_one_actual_drive_min),
            },
        })
    if all_in_one_return_geometry and len(all_in_one_return_geometry) >= 2:
        all_in_one_geojson["features"].append({
            "type": "Feature",
            "geometry": {
                "type": "LineString",
                "coordinates": all_in_one_return_geometry,
            },
            "properties": {
                "type": "全點一次跑完回固定起點",
                "距離_m": float(all_in_one_return_pair["distance"]),
            },
        })
    for row in all_in_one_order_records:
        all_in_one_geojson["features"].append({
            "type": "Feature",
            "geometry": {
                "type": "Point",
                "coordinates": [float(row["經度"]), float(row["緯度"])],
            },
            "properties": {
                "type": "全點一次跑完地籍點位",
                "全點一次跑完序號": row["全點一次跑完序號"],
                "角色": row["角色"],
                "名稱": row["名稱"],
                "地段": row["地段"],
                "地號": row["地號"],
            },
        })

    # ============================================================
    # 38. GeoJSON
    # ============================================================

    features = []


    def add_line_feature(
        geometry,
        properties
    ):

        if (
            geometry is None
            or
            len(geometry) < 2
        ):
            return

        features.append({
            "type":
                "Feature",

            "geometry": {
                "type":
                    "LineString",

                "coordinates":
                    geometry
            },

            "properties":
                properties
        })


    def add_point_feature(
        lon,
        lat,
        properties
    ):

        features.append({
            "type":
                "Feature",

            "geometry": {
                "type":
                    "Point",

                "coordinates": [
                    float(lon),
                    float(lat)
                ]
            },

            "properties":
                properties
        })


    for day_no, info in enumerate(
        actual_batches,
        start=1
    ):

        add_line_feature(
            info[
                "geometry"
            ],
            {
                "type":
                    "每日主路線",

                "工作日":
                    day_no,

                "距離_m":
                    info[
                        "distance"
                    ]
            }
        )


    for day_no, return_info in enumerate(return_infos, start=1):
        if return_info is None:
            continue
        add_line_feature(
            return_info["geometry"],
            {
                "type": "回起點",
                "工作日": day_no,
                "距離_m": return_info["distance"]
            }
        )


    for row in route_records:

        add_point_feature(
            row["經度"],
            row["緯度"],
            {
                "type":
                    "地籍點位",

                "總路線序號":
                    row[
                        "總路線序號"
                    ],

                "批次":
                    row["工作日"],

                "名稱":
                    row["名稱"],

                "地段":
                    row["地段"],

                "地號":
                    row["地號"]
            }
        )


    geojson = {
        "type":
            "FeatureCollection",

        "features":
            features
    }


    with open(
        OUTPUT_GEOJSON,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            geojson,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2
        )


    # ============================================================
    # 39. Summary
    # ============================================================

    total_crossings = sum(
        count_crossings(order[1:])
        for order in batches
        if len(order) > 2
    )

    total_uturns = sum(
        count_uturns(order[1:])
        for order in batches
        if len(order) > 2
    )

    summary_df = pd.DataFrame({
        "項目": [

            "道路模式",

            "跨日重複道路估計_km",

            "汽車明確禁止已排除edge數",
            "明確汽車禁止＋機車明確允許樣式已排除edge數",
            "V64疑似水利／防汛／堤防管理道路已排除edge數",
            "V64疑似水利／防汛／堤防管理道路已排除長度_km",
            "CAR實際路徑非A級edge數",
            "CAR實際路徑D級edge數",
            "Matrix非對角可達率_percent",

            "快速公路里程占比_percent",
            "平面道路行駛時間_速度診斷_hr",
            "高速公路行駛時間_速度診斷_hr",
            "高速公路時間占比_percent",
            "快速公路行駛時間_速度診斷_hr",
            "快速公路時間占比_percent",

        ],
        "數值": [

            VEHICLE_LABEL,
            "OpenStreetMap / OSMnx / NetworkX",
            OSM_BUFFER_M,

            total_repeat_within_day / 1000.0,
            repeat_rate_within_day,
            total_repeat_cross_day / 1000.0,
            total_crossings,

            float(np.mean(snap_distances)),
            float(np.max(snap_distances)),
            int(np.sum(snap_distances > 500)),
            len(G.nodes),
            len(G.edges),
            removed_edges,
            int((car_suitability_df.loc[car_suitability_df["等級"] == "A", "道路edge數"].sum()) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and not car_suitability_df.empty else 0),
            int((car_suitability_df.loc[car_suitability_df["等級"] == "B", "道路edge數"].sum()) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and not car_suitability_df.empty else 0),
            int((car_suitability_df.loc[car_suitability_df["等級"] == "C", "道路edge數"].sum()) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and not car_suitability_df.empty else 0),
            int(car_removed_grade_counts.get("D", 0)) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else 0,
            int(car_removed_motorcycle_only_count) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else 0,
            int(car_removed_v64_special_count) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else 0,
            (car_removed_v64_special_length_m / 1000.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            int(car_route_used["non_a_edges"]) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else 0,
            int(car_route_used["d_edges"]) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else 0,
            non_diag_ratio * 100.0,
            start_name,
            float(coords[start_idx, 0]),

            "OSM geometry採樣 + 50m附近網格近似",
            (total_speed_surface_m / 1000.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            (total_speed_motorway_m / 1000.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            (total_speed_motorway_m / max(total_speed_surface_m + total_speed_motorway_m + total_speed_expressway_m, 1e-9) * 100.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            (total_speed_expressway_m / 1000.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            (total_speed_expressway_m / max(total_speed_surface_m + total_speed_motorway_m + total_speed_expressway_m, 1e-9) * 100.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            total_speed_surface_min / 60.0,
            (total_speed_motorway_min / 60.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            (total_speed_motorway_min / max(total_speed_surface_min + total_speed_motorway_min + total_speed_expressway_min, 1e-9) * 100.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            (total_speed_expressway_min / 60.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            (total_speed_expressway_min / max(total_speed_surface_min + total_speed_motorway_min + total_speed_expressway_min, 1e-9) * 100.0) if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" else np.nan,
            all_in_one_actual_distance / 1000.0,
            all_in_one_actual_drive_min / 60.0,
            all_in_one_total_actual_min / 60.0,
            bool(all_in_one_same_as_daily),
        ]
    })

    car_manual_diagnostic_df = pd.DataFrame(CAR_MANUAL_DIAGNOSTICS)
    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR" and car_manual_diagnostic_df.empty:

    # ============================================================

    # ============================================================
    # CELL4 → CELL5 資料交接

    # Cell 4 負責：資料整理、OSM 路網、路徑最佳化、每日分配及輸出表格。
    # Cell 4 負責：Leaflet/Folium 地圖製作。
    # 將地圖所需資料集中交給 Cell 4，避免重新下載 OSM 或重新計算路徑。
    map_payload = {
        "VEHICLE_MODE": VEHICLE_MODE,

        "nearest_edge_distances": np.asarray(nearest_edge_distances),
        "vehicle_ingress_distances": np.asarray(vehicle_ingress_distances),
        "vehicle_ingress_road_distances": np.asarray(vehicle_ingress_road_distances),
        "vehicle_ingress_nodes": np.asarray(nearest_nodes, dtype=np.int64),
        "start_name": start_name,
        "start_lat": float(start_lat),
        "start_lon": float(start_lon),
        "route_order_df": route_order_df,
        "actual_batches": actual_batches,
        "return_infos": return_infos,
        "actual_connectors": actual_connectors,
        "all_in_one_order": all_in_one_order,
        "virtual_anchor_traversal_repair_count": int(_all_in_one_va_move_count if "_all_in_one_va_move_count" in locals() else 0),
        "all_in_one_geometry": all_in_one_geometry,
        "all_in_one_return_geometry": all_in_one_return_geometry,
        "all_in_one_return_pair": all_in_one_return_pair,
        "all_in_one_actual_distance": float(all_in_one_actual_distance),
        "all_in_one_actual_drive_min": float(all_in_one_actual_drive_min),
        "single_day_preferred": str(single_day_preferred),
        "single_day_daily_distance_m": float(single_day_daily_distance_m),
        "single_day_all_in_one_distance_m": float(single_day_all_in_one_distance_m),
        "single_day_selected_weight_attr": str(single_day_selected_weight_attr),
    }

    return map_payload

    # ============================================================
    # 49. Final
    # ============================================================

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("完成")
    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        f"有效資料點數：{N}"
    )
    print(
        f"固定起點：1 點"
    )
    print(
        f"查核點數：{N - 1}"
    )
    print(
        f"查核點每日分配總數：{sum(len(order) - 1 for order in batches)}"
    )
    print(
        f"工作日數：{len(batches)}"
    )

    print(
        f"交通工具：{VEHICLE_LABEL}"
    )
    print(

    )

    print(

    )

    print(
        f"Snap > 500m："
        f"{np.sum(snap_distances > 500)}"
    )

    print(
        f"OSM Matrix 非對角可達率："
        f"{non_diag_ratio * 100:.2f}%"
    )

    print()
    print("每日摘要：")

    display(
        daily_summary_df
    )

    if VEHICLE_MODE == "CAR":
        print()
        print("汽車實際路徑道路適用性：")

            f"  實際使用道路段：{int(car_route_used['edges']):,} | "

        )
        if car_route_used["edges"] > 0:
            non_a_pct = car_route_used["non_a_edges"] / car_route_used["edges"] * 100.0
            print(f"  CAR 實際路線非A級比例：{non_a_pct:.2f}%")
        print(
            f"  平面道路：{total_speed_surface_m/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"高速公路：{total_speed_motorway_m/1000:.2f} km | "
            f"快速公路：{total_speed_expressway_m/1000:.2f} km"
        )

    print()
    print("每日出門時間檢查（5小時上限）：")
    display(daily_summary_df[[
        "工作日", "點數", "駕駛距離_km", "預估駕駛時間_hr",
        "點位處理時間_hr", "總工時_hr", "工時狀態", "剩餘時間至5小時_hr",
        "日內重複道路_km", "跨日重複道路_km"
    ]])

    print()
    print("全點一次跑完摘要（不受每日5小時限制）：")
    display(all_in_one_summary_df)

    print()
    print("輸出檔案：")

    for path in [
        OUTPUT_ROUTE_ORDER,

# ============================================================
# v65.48 RAM 安全模式
# ============================================================

def _cell4_memory_snapshot_gb():
    total_gb = None
    available_gb = None
    try:
        with open("/proc/meminfo", "r", encoding="utf-8") as _f:
            _meminfo = {}
            for _line in _f:
                _parts = _line.split()
                if len(_parts) >= 2:
                    _meminfo[_parts[0].rstrip(":")] = float(_parts[1]) / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
        total_gb = _meminfo.get("MemTotal")
        available_gb = _meminfo.get("MemAvailable")
    except Exception:
        pass
    return total_gb, available_gb


_total_ram_gb, _available_ram_gb = _cell4_memory_snapshot_gb()

if _total_ram_gb is not None:
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("Cell 4 RAM 安全檢查")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"RAM 總量：{_total_ram_gb:.2f} GB")
    if _available_ram_gb is not None:
        print(f"RAM 可用：{_available_ram_gb:.2f} GB")

if AUTO_SERIALIZE_LOW_RAM and _total_ram_gb is not None:
    _USE_PARALLEL_VEHICLE_RUNS = (
        PARALLEL_VEHICLE_RUNS
        and _total_ram_gb >= PARALLEL_MIN_TOTAL_RAM_GB
    )
else:
    _USE_PARALLEL_VEHICLE_RUNS = bool(PARALLEL_VEHICLE_RUNS)

if _USE_PARALLEL_VEHICLE_RUNS:
    print("BOTH 模式執行方式：平行（目前 RAM 足夠）")
else:
    print(
        "BOTH 模式執行方式：逐模式（RAM 安全模式；"
        f"平行門檻={PARALLEL_MIN_TOTAL_RAM_GB:g} GB）"
    )

_runs = list(_route_cfg["VEHICLE_RUNS"])

# V24.80.9.11.24：完整流程候選版；關閉全點一次測試後的早退，恢復每日排程與 Cell5 handoff。
V65_64_20_VA_ONLY_TEST = False

# V24.80.9.11.24：依本輪人工驗收範圍只執行 MOTOR，不執行 CAR。
V65_64_18_VA_MOTOR_ONLY_TEST = True
if V65_64_18_VA_MOTOR_ONLY_TEST:
    _runs = [("MOTOR", "機車")]
    print("V24.80.9.11.24 完整流程候選：只執行 MOTOR；CAR 暫不計算")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# v65.16：本次輸入／設定簽章
# ------------------------------------------------------------
_CELL4_RUN_SIGNATURE, _CELL4_SIGNATURE_PAYLOAD = _build_cell4_run_signature(_route_cfg)
print()
print("Cell 4 執行簽章：", _CELL4_RUN_SIGNATURE)
print("Cell 4 交通工具分模式續跑進度：", CELL4_CHECKPOINT_DIR)

# 先載入同一份 result.xlsx／路線設定／Cell4 版本下已完成的模式。
CELL4_RESULTS = {}
_remaining_runs = []

for _mode, _label in _runs:
    _cached_payload = _load_vehicle_checkpoint(_mode, _CELL4_RUN_SIGNATURE)
    if _cached_payload is not None:
        CELL4_RESULTS[_mode] = _cached_payload
        print(f"使用既有 {_label}（{_mode}）完成續跑進度：{_checkpoint_path(_mode)}")
    else:
        _remaining_runs.append((_mode, _label))

if CELL4_RESULTS:
    print(
        "已由續跑進度載入：" + ", ".join(sorted(CELL4_RESULTS.keys()))
    )
if _remaining_runs:
    print(
        "需要重新計算：" + ", ".join(
            f"{_label}（{_mode}）" for _mode, _label in _remaining_runs
        )
    )
else:
    print("所有指定的交通工具模式都有相同簽章的既有成果，不重新計算。")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 只有仍需要計算時，才建立 BOTH 共用 OSM / 水路前置資料。
# ------------------------------------------------------------
if (
    _USE_PARALLEL_VEHICLE_RUNS
    and len(_remaining_runs) == 2
    and _route_cfg["VEHICLE_SELECTION"] == "BOTH"
):
    _shared_prepare_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    _shared_context = _prepare_shared_osm_context()
    _shared_prepare_sec = time.perf_counter() - _shared_prepare_t0
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("BOTH 模式：MOTOR / CAR 專屬後段平行運算（記憶體安全模式）")
    print("=" * 70)

    def _run_mode_thread(mode_label_pair):
        _mode, _label = mode_label_pair
        print("\n" + "#" * 80)
        print(f"開始平行執行：{_label}（{_mode}）")
        print("#" * 80)
        _payload = run_vehicle_mode(
            vehicle_mode=_mode,
            vehicle_label=_label,
            vehicle_suffix=_mode,
            point_service_minutes=_route_cfg["POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES"],
            daily_max_hours=_route_cfg["DAILY_MAX_HOURS"],
            motor_avg_speed_kmh=_route_cfg["MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH"],
            car_surface_speed_kmh=_route_cfg["CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH"],
            car_motorway_speed_kmh=_route_cfg["CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH"],
            car_expressway_speed_kmh=_route_cfg["CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH"],
            shared_context=_shared_context,
        )
        _checkpoint = _save_vehicle_checkpoint(
            _payload,
            _CELL4_RUN_SIGNATURE,
            _CELL4_SIGNATURE_PAYLOAD,
        )
        print(f"{_label}（{_mode}）完成，已立即儲存交通工具續跑進度：{_checkpoint}")
        return _payload

    _parallel_t0 = time.perf_counter()
    _executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2, thread_name_prefix="Cell4")
    _future_to_mode = {}
    _interrupted = False
    try:
        for _item in _remaining_runs:
            _future = _executor.submit(_run_mode_thread, _item)
            _future_to_mode[_future] = _item[0]

        # 不再用 [future.result() for future in futures]。
        # 改用 as_completed，哪一個模式完成就立即回收哪一個結果。
        for _future in as_completed(_future_to_mode):
            _mode = _future_to_mode[_future]
            _payload = _future.result()
            CELL4_RESULTS[_mode] = _payload
            print(f"已回收 {_mode} 結果：CELL4_RESULTS['{_mode}']")

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        _interrupted = True
        print()
        print("=" * 70)
        print("偵測到 Colab 中斷操作。")
        print("已完成的交通工具模式會保留於分模式續跑進度檔；")
        print("未完成模式不會誤寫成完成成果。")
        print("下一次重新執行 Cell 4 時，會自動載入相同簽章的完成模式。")
        print("=" * 70)
        # 不 re-raise，避免再次出現 traceback。
    finally:
        if _interrupted:
            # 不等待尚未完成的背景工作；已完成的模式已在 worker 內立即儲存。
            try:
                _executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=False)
            except TypeError:
                _executor.shutdown(wait=False)
        else:
            _executor.shutdown(wait=True)

    if not _interrupted:
        print(
            f"MOTOR / CAR 後段平行運算完成：{time.perf_counter() - _parallel_t0:.1f} 秒"
        )

elif _remaining_runs:
    # v65.48：
    # 只有一種模式需要重算，或低 RAM 的 BOTH 模式；
    # 逐模式執行，避免 MOTOR / CAR 同時持有兩份大型 OSM graph。
    for _mode, _label in _remaining_runs:
        print("\n" + "#" * 80)
        print(f"開始執行：{_label}（{_mode}）")
        print("#" * 80)
        _payload = run_vehicle_mode(
            vehicle_mode=_mode,
            vehicle_label=_label,
            vehicle_suffix=_mode,
            point_service_minutes=_route_cfg["POINT_SERVICE_MINUTES"],
            daily_max_hours=_route_cfg["DAILY_MAX_HOURS"],
            motor_avg_speed_kmh=_route_cfg["MOTOR_AVG_SPEED_KMH"],
            car_surface_speed_kmh=_route_cfg["CAR_SURFACE_SPEED_KMH"],
            car_motorway_speed_kmh=_route_cfg["CAR_MOTORWAY_SPEED_KMH"],
            car_expressway_speed_kmh=_route_cfg["CAR_EXPRESSWAY_SPEED_KMH"],
            shared_context=None,
        )
        CELL4_RESULTS[_mode] = _payload
        _checkpoint = _save_vehicle_checkpoint(
            _payload,
            _CELL4_RUN_SIGNATURE,
            _CELL4_SIGNATURE_PAYLOAD,
        )
        print(f"{_label}（{_mode}）完成，已立即儲存交通工具續跑進度：{_checkpoint}")

        # v65.48：低 RAM 逐模式完成後，主動回收本模式局部大型物件。
        gc.collect()

# V65.64.23：專用 Virtual anchor／全點一次測試只產生隔離輸出，不覆蓋正常 Cell 4 → Cell 5 交接檔。
if V65_64_20_VA_ONLY_TEST:
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("V65.64.23 TEST 完成：專用隔離測試已結束；未寫入正常 /content/cell4_map_data.pkl")
    print("=" * 70)
    raise SystemExit(0)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 正常完成：兩模式都具備時，寫入 Cell 4 → Cell 5 共同交接檔。
# 中斷：只儲存已完成模式的分模式續跑進度，不覆蓋舊的完整交接檔。
# ------------------------------------------------------------
_expected_modes = [mode for mode, _label in _runs]
_missing_modes = [mode for mode in _expected_modes if mode not in CELL4_RESULTS]

if _missing_modes:
    print()
    print("目前尚未完成的交通工具模式：", ", ".join(_missing_modes))
    print("本次不寫入新的 /content/cell4_map_data.pkl 完整交接檔。")
    print("可直接重新執行本 CELL 4，系統會從分模式續跑進度繼續執行。")
else:
    print()
    print("所有指定的交通工具模式均已完成。")

    try:
        _map_path = _save_combined_cell4_results(CELL4_RESULTS)
        print(f"Cell 4 → Cell 5 地圖資料已交接：{_map_path}")
    except Exception as _e:
        print(f"Cell 4 → Cell 5 地圖資料 pickle 儲存失敗：{_e!r}")

    # 再做一次最基本的欄位確認，確保統一顯示名稱已帶入 Cell 5 handoff。
    for _mode, _payload in CELL4_RESULTS.items():
        _df_check = _payload.get("work_df")
        if isinstance(_df_check, pd.DataFrame):
            print(
                f"{_mode}：work_df={len(_df_check)} 筆 | "
                f"顯示名稱欄位={'顯示名稱' in _df_check.columns}"
            )
            if "顯示名稱" in _df_check.columns:
                _sample_names = (
                    _df_check["顯示名稱"]
                    .dropna()
                    .astype(str)
                    .head(3)
                    .tolist()
                )
                print(f"{_mode} 顯示名稱範例：{_sample_names}")
